# 语音台词

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_wait_1

Say, why don't you join a party? If you can't find anyone else... I wouldn't mind helping out.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_wait_2

Asuna：Cooking always makes me forget my troubles. And the tasty meal at the end is a nice bonus.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_wait_3

Asuna：I used to prioritize raids above all else, but that's changed recently. Maybe you played a hand in that...  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_wait_4

Asuna：Would you mind changing up that all-black look of yours? At least when we're in town...  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_wait_5

Asuna：Smile! I brought us lunch today. Want to go on a picnic with me?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_wait_6

Asuna：You're really strong, but you can be a loose cannon sometimes. You'd be hopeless without me.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_wait_7

Asuna：It's sad enough that we're in this death game—why must we kill each other, too? We should all be friends.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_wait_8

Asuna：You may act like a loner, but between that bandana guy and the informant, you actually do have a few friends.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_wait_9

Asuna：Is something wrong? Oh, I know. You're still sleepy. Jeez, serves you right for staying up so late.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_touch_1

Asuna：Stop that! This is neither the time nor the place! Goodness. People are watching...  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_touch_2

Asuna：Sometimes my hair does get in the way. But I want to keep it like this... how it was when I started the game.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_touch_3

Asuna：Um...friendly advice, don't touch a girl's face like that unless you want to get on her bad side.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_touch_4

Asuna：If you keep your hand there one second longer, the moral code will activate!  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_touch_5

Asuna：You insist it's an "accident," but would you mind explaining exactly how both your hands got there?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_touch_6

Asuna：I also like to dress light. And I use a rapier because I value swiftness over all else.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_touch_7

Asuna：H-Hey, what if the informant sees us like this?!  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_touch_8

Asuna：Again, this is neither the time nor the place! I mean, if we at least had... N-Never mind!  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_time_1

Asuna：I'm surprised to see you up so early. Is it gonna snow today or something?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_time_2

Asuna：Come on, at least go wash your face. Then I'll cook a special breakfast just for you.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_time_3

Asuna：Wait, I hope you're not just going to bed now? Goodness... Don't cry to me when you get sick.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_time_4

Asuna：Good afternoon, sleepy-head! You know, I'm almost impressed by how late you can sleep in.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_time_5

Asuna：With the raid out of the way, looks like I finally get an afternoon off.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_time_6

Asuna：I hate that I have a guild meeting today when the weather's so nice. We should at least have it outside.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_time_7

Asuna：I try not to go out at night. I m-mean...there might be... ghosts and stuff.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_time_8

Asuna：I always feel a sense of calm when I see a bustling shop. It means everyone in there is happy.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_time_9

Asuna：Yawwwn... Oh, my. I'm sleepy. I guess I'll turn in early, then. Goodnight.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_time_10

Asuna：I always feel so lonely, waking up alone in the middle of the night. Wait...do you feel lonely, too?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_time_11

Asuna：Looks like that nap I took with you made me wake up too early. You'd better keep me entertained!  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_time_12

Asuna：You're going out? This late at night? Oh, fine...I'll keep you company.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_day_1

Asuna：I try to be extra-sharp on Mondays... so I'll be prepared for the real world if we ever make it back.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_day_2

Asuna：Between guild meetings, stocking supplies, and prepping for raids... this doesn't feel at all like a game.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_day_3

Asuna：I've synced up my schedule so we can take the day off together. Let's totally spread our wings!  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_day_4

Asuna：Sunday evenings always come too fast—even in-game. I guess that means we had a great time.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_event_1

Asuna：Happy New Year. I suppose spending New Year's in a game isn't all bad.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_event_2

Asuna：I wonder if Setsubun beans require the "Throw" skill? If so, I guess I'll need Martial Arts as a prerequisite?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_event_3

Asuna：Stop staring. I can take a hint. Here. Happy Valentine's Day. And yes, they are homemade.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_event_4

Asuna：I made some Hinamatsuri crackers, and everyone actually loved them. Even the boys!  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_event_5

Asuna：Is that...for me? Oh, thank you! That's so sweet. Wow...a gift from you.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_event_6

Asuna：Don't you dare lie today. I'm going to stay with you all day to make sure you're honest!  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_event_7

Asuna：I'd always spent my vacations studying, so playing a game like this feels rather surreal.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_event_8

Asuna：Never thought I'd feel so sticky from rainy season in a virtual world... Oh, I wish I could take a shower!  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_event_9

Asuna：Tanabata sure is strange, isn't it? What does a celestial love story have to do with granting wishes?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_event_10

Asuna：Haha, can you believe it? Festival stalls in this world! They do make me smile, though.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_event_11

Asuna：I think I always spent the last day of summer break in study camp with friends. I was the teacher, of course.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_event_12

Asuna：I heard Moon lore is different all over the world...but I wonder if there's life on the Moon in SAO lore?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_event_13

Asuna：I usually don't get VR costumes... but I bought one. Oh, nooo, not yet. I'll show it to you later.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_event_14

Asuna：I'll never forget that first day. But I survived a year! That makes me feel a sense of pride...and joy.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_event_15

Asuna：Since it's Christmas, we might as well make the most of it and live a little!  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_season_1

Asuna：I wish this world had cherry groves. Then we could have a spring picnic under the cherry blossoms.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_season_2

Asuna：If it's going to be so humid, I'd almost rather the weather just be super hot and sunny.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_season_3

Asuna：In the real world, it's feeling less and less like autumn, but that doesn't seem to be the case in SAO.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_season_4

Asuna：I always feel like staying in on snowy days like this. Would you like to just do that?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_bd_2

Asuna：What? How did you know...? Well, thank you. That's so sweet of you!  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_bd_3

Asuna：It's Kirito's birthday. I know— the informant told me so. I hope he likes my present.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_bd_5

Asuna：Yay, it's Alice's birthday. I'll do the cooking, so you set up for the party, okay?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_bd_7

Asuna：Oh, it's your little sister's birthday! Yeah, I'll help you throw a party. I'm sure she'll love that.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_bd_8

Asuna：Hey, it's Shino-non's birthday. I think she'd like it best if we picked her present together.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_bd_9

Asuna：Today's Yuuki's birthday and I actually had a favor to ask... She says she wants a proper fight.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_bd_14

Asuna：I was thinking of getting Liz some blacksmith tools for her birthday. I figured she'd like that the most.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_bd_15

Asuna：It's Silica's birthday today, isn't it? I'm thinking of getting her something cute for Pina to wear.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_home_etc_1

Asuna：Wow, you already cleared the quest? Haha, you sure are eager.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_home_etc_2

Asuna：A gift arrived for you. Come on, open it! It might be something great.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_2nd_home_etc_3

Asuna：Are you ready for the next raid? If you are, let's head right out!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_alo_1

Asuna：Those Sleeping Knights are truly great. They're nice and so easygoing!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_alo_2

Asuna：I was surprised at first. I would have never had thought that the Absolute Sword was a woman!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_alo_1

Asuna：It doesn't bother me when you do it... Could I also touch it?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_alo_4

Asuna：What happened? I got it, you want to cuddle, don't you? It's ok, come on over!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_alo_5

Asuna：For some reason... When I feel your warmth like this, all my worries go away.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_alo_1

Asuna：Morning! Looks like we'll be together all day today!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_alo_4

Asuna：Since the weather is so nice, how about we skip on hunting and just relax today?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_alo_7

Asuna：I found a very interesting recipe over there, so I'll try to reproduce it here.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_alo_10

Asuna：I'm tired but don't want to sleep just yet. Because I want to spend a little more time with you.   

## 场景 novel.home_day_asuna_alo_1

Asuna：Monday mornings are a real slog, but if you do your best on Monday, then the rest of the week should be a breeze!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_asuna_alo_2

Asuna：If my grades fall behind I won't be able to log onto ALO... I have to try hard for my studies too!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_asuna_alo_3

Asuna：Looks like we'll be together all day today! What do you want to do?  

## 场景 novel.home_day_asuna_alo_4

Asuna：There goes another fun vacation. And now the long wait for the next.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_asuna_alo_1

Asuna：When the snow melts, that means spring has finally arrived. Shall we go somewhere for the occasion?  

## 场景 novel.home_season_asuna_alo_2

Asuna：Should we go to the beach? There's not that many people around and it's quiet so we could talk on the shore.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_asuna_alo_3

Asuna：On long autumn nights, I'm looking forward to spending time together reading in the same room.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_asuna_alo_4

Asuna：I remember clearly whenever it's winter. It's a special season since that's when I met you.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_alo_swim_wait_1

Asuna：I wonder if this swimsuit was a little too bold... Hehe. It should be fine, once in awhile.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_alo_swim_touch_1

Asuna：I need to dry my hair! Otherwise, I'll catch a cold!  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_alo_swim_touch_4

Asuna：Kyaaa! D-Did a hand just... My chest... I've just about had it!  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_student_wait_1

Asuna：I made a lunch box for you! Hehe, I poured my heart and soul into it!  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_student_wait_2

Asuna：Any plans after school? We could meet on ALO...But a date in our uniforms would be nice too!  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_student_wait_3

Asuna：I'm so happy I get to go to this school. I get to meet everyone every day...And you.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_student_touch_1

Asuna：...I like it when you pet me like that!  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_student_touch_2

Asuna：W-wait...I can't get my uniform dirty in a place like this!  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_student_time_1

Asuna：Hey sleepyhead! You better not take a nap during class!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_alo_wedding_1

Asuna：What do you think of this dress? I feel a little silly in it, but I'll wear it if it makes you happy.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_alo_wedding_2

Asuna：Marriage... I'd never considered it before, but...I might change my mind for you.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_alo_wedding_3

Asuna：Wanna go shopping? Let's get some upgrades to celebrate.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_alo_wedding_4

Asuna：Maybe this is the sort of thing we should announce to people? It just feels...kinda embarrassing.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_alo_wedding_5

Asuna：Western-style weddings are great and all, but I've always kinda liked Japanese wedding kimono.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_alo_wedding_6

Asuna：Today is just as special to me as my birthday... No, it's even more special.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_alo_wedding_7

Asuna：If I do have a wedding, I'd rather have it in VR with my friends than in the real world, with my family.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_alo_wedding_8

Asuna：You and I are...newlyweds...right? Hahaha... Newlyweds has such a lovely ring to it.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_alo_wedding_9

Asuna：Sorry, this dress is a real hassle to get changed into... I hope I didn't hold you up too long?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_alo_wedding_1

Asuna：I wish I could've done something more complex with my hairstyle... but games have their limits.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_alo_wedding_2

Asuna：After you lift a bride's veil, there's only one thing left to do... Get it?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_alo_wedding_3

Asuna：Don't touch my hair, okay? I just had it styled.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_alo_wedding_4

Asuna：Hey, you're making my dress slip! Oh, you... Be a little more patient, okay?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_alo_wedding_5

Asuna：...Please, hug me tight. I want to feel you, to feel that this is real.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_alo_wedding_6

Asuna：I pledge my eternal love to you. I will be by your side forever, no matter what world you are in.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_alo_wedding_7

Asuna：So, how do I look? Thank you. I knew you would say something like that!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_alo_wedding_8

Asuna：Your loyal touch saved my life. And I'll live the rest of my life with your hand in mine.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_alo_wedding_1

Asuna：I'm so glad it's a nice day. Perfect weather for us to set sail into the next chapter of our lives.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_alo_wedding_2

Asuna：Come on, wake up! It's a special day today. You don't want to sleep through it.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_alo_wedding_4

Asuna：I cooked all the wedding food myself! It's our wedding after all.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_alo_wedding_5

Asuna：I'm a little too embarrassed to go out in public dressed like this.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_alo_wedding_7

Asuna：Today was pretty exhausting... But I'm glad we got to celebrate with all our friends.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_alo_wedding_8

Asuna：Let's have a light supper today. We did gorge at the reception earlier.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_alo_wedding_10

Asuna：I just...can't sleep. I'm so wired that my heart won't stop pounding.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_alo_wedding_11

Asuna：Can we hold hands? I want to hold hands all night while we sleep.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_asuna_alo_wedding_1

Asuna：Another week starts today. Let's crush it together again!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_asuna_alo_wedding_2

Asuna：Knowing I'll see you when I get home at night gives me all the energy I need for the day's work.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_asuna_alo_wedding_3

Asuna：I want to spend the whole day together doing nothing. How does that sound to you?  

## 场景 novel.home_day_asuna_alo_wedding_4

Asuna：It's actually already night. Hard to believe, since it's so light... Oh well. See you tomorrow.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_alo_wedding_1

Asuna：Happy New Year. I'm excited to spend another year together.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_alo_wedding_2

Asuna：You're supposed to decorate with sardine heads on Setsubun. Does this world have sardines?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_alo_wedding_3

Asuna：I assume many girls will give you chocolate, but I'm sure mine will be the tastiest.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_alo_wedding_4

Asuna：Don't you think this doll kind of looks like Yui? Gosh, she's so cute.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_alo_wedding_5

Asuna：What would I like for White Day? Let's see... How about, we spend the whole day together?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_alo_wedding_6

Asuna：Okay, what joke did you just think of? Come on, I've known you for years. You can't hide it from me.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_alo_wedding_7

Asuna：Spending a week-long vacation inside a VR game... Yeah, that's pretty "us."  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_alo_wedding_8

Asuna：I wonder why June brides are a thing? That's the rainy season in Japan.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_alo_wedding_9

Asuna：Poor Orihime and Hikoboshi were forced apart. I would never let that happen to us.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_alo_wedding_10

Asuna：Private beaches are nice and all, but I would love to take Yui to a waterpark someday.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_alo_wedding_11

Asuna：Summer vacation was so much fun this year! Probably...because I was with you the whole time.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_alo_wedding_12

Asuna：It's the moon-viewing festival today! Our friends are coming over tonight, so we'd better tidy up.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_alo_wedding_13

Asuna：If I wear a dress like this today, will people think it's a costume?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_alo_wedding_14

Asuna：I know I'll never forget this day. I get the feeling it'll become a really fond memory for me.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_alo_wedding_15

Asuna：Christmas is so much more special when it's just you and me. Let's do this again next year...  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_asuna_alo_wedding_3

Asuna：Think about what you want for your birthday, okay? And, "I'm fine with whatever" is not an option!  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_asuna_alo_wedding_5

Asuna：Alice would probably rather we celebrate in VR instead of IRL.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_asuna_alo_wedding_7

Asuna：I can never thank Leafa enough. So I want to give her a big party with all her friends today.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_asuna_alo_wedding_8

Asuna：Why don't we play GGO today for Shino-non's birthday? She'd probably love that.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_asuna_alo_wedding_11

Asuna：It's Ronie's birthday today. I'd love to go to her world again sometime and throw her a party.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_asuna_alo_wedding_12

Asuna：It's Tiese's birthday today. When we finally get to see her again, let's have a big party.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_asuna_alo_wedding_14

Asuna：We should head to Liz's birthday party soon. I want to get there early so we can help set up.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_asuna_alo_wedding_15

Asuna：I'm thinking of giving Silica some jewelry, since she's so cute. I've actually got Liz on the case.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_asuna_alo_wedding_1

Asuna：You've reached your goal! Have you been improving while I wasn't looking?  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_asuna_alo_wedding_2

Asuna：You got a gift, right? Can I watch you open it?  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_asuna_alo_wedding_3

Asuna：There's a new quest out. Want to try it out together?  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_1st_1

Asuna：I've gotten so used to being with you...that I sometimes forget how happy I am.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_1st_2

Asuna：No matter where you go, I'm coming with you. Always. Anywhere and everywhere.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_1st_3

Asuna：I just mastered a new recipe! Get your taste buds ready.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_1st_4

Asuna：No matter where I go, there's always so many rivals.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_1st_9

Asuna：I love the way you look when you're deep in thought... Ack, you heard that? F-Forget it!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_1st_1

Asuna：You've not changed your hairstyle all this time. You know, since it's VR, you could change it.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_1st_2

Asuna：I smell good? Hmmm... It's probably my shampoo.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_1st_4

Asuna：If you're gonna touch me, don't be so timid. Just go in for a hug!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_1st_5

Asuna：Hey, consider our surroundings, will you? This isn't good for Yui's upbringing...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_1st_6

Asuna：I wish I had a nicer figure...  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_1st_1

Asuna：I love soup in the morning. It's so warm and comforting.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_1st_4

Asuna：We're eating lunch outside today, right? Don't worry, I'm going all out with our bento!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_1st_7

Asuna：Another hard day's work in the books. Anything fun happen today?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_1st_10

Asuna：The moon is so pretty tonight... Wanna go for a walk?  

## 场景 novel.home_day_asuna_1st_1

Asuna：If you're already exhausted on Monday, then there goes your whole week.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_asuna_1st_2

Asuna：You're probably feeling pretty tired by now. How about a nice warm bath and then bedtime?  

## 场景 novel.home_day_asuna_1st_3

Asuna：Want to go out today? Unless you'd rather just stay in?  

## 场景 novel.home_day_asuna_1st_4

Asuna：All rested up for tomorrow? Hehe. Good. Now let's get some sleep.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_asuna_1st_1

Asuna：Wild plants are so yummy in early spring. We should go foraging sometime.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_asuna_1st_2

Asuna：Food goes bad really easily in the summer, so we need to be careful.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_asuna_1st_3

Asuna：Autumn's chill just sneaks up on you, doesn't it? I always regret not bringing a jacket.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_asuna_1st_4

Asuna：I love how winter is full of fun events. Hehe, I'm so excited.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_sao_sleeping_1

Asuna：I love how quiet and relaxing this place is. It's completely unlike the front line floors.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_sao_sleeping_2

Asuna：Hey... Can we just stay like this a little bit longer?  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_sao_sleeping_3

Asuna：The safest place in this world is by your side. That's how it is for me, at least.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_sao_sleeping_4

Asuna：Your eyes are so beautiful...black as the night sky. When I first met you, I almost lost myself in them.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_sao_sleeping_5

Asuna：Just for today...will you come just a bit closer to me?  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_sao_sleeping_6

Asuna：If only we could just hang out and relax like this every day... How happy our lives would be.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_sao_sleeping_7

Asuna：I suppose we really have to get breakfast now. I feel like I could stay in bed with you all day.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_sao_sleeping_8

Asuna：I've had my share of pain in the past, but I'm very happy now... because I have you.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_asuna_sao_sleeping_9

Asuna：I love the look of your sleeping face. I could stare at it all night.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_sao_sleeping_1

Asuna：One of the few pluses about SAO is how easy haircare is. Though I guess a boy wouldn't understand.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_sao_sleeping_2

Asuna：Having my hair touched...it feels good, but it also makes me feel... kind of weird.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_sao_sleeping_3

Asuna：Hey, come closer... So close that our foreheads are almost touching.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_sao_sleeping_4

Asuna：Oh, now you've done it... Well, two can play at that game!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_sao_sleeping_5

Asuna：I can still feel your warmth... Even though this is a virtual world, it still comes through.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_sao_sleeping_6

Asuna：Hey! Just because nobody's watching, that doesn't... Do you really want to touch me that badly?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_sao_sleeping_7

Asuna：Hey, can you stay turned away for a bit longer? Heehee... Your shoulders are so...broad.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_asuna_sao_sleeping_8

Asuna：This feels like a dream. Just, sharing space with you and touching you like this.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_sao_sleeping_1

Asuna：Um...when it's light, I feel kind of self-conscious.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_sao_sleeping_2

Asuna：Just for today, I'll let you sleep in. So, hey come...closer.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_sao_sleeping_4

Asuna：Hey, wanna go out somewhere? I'll even make one of my famous picnic lunches.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_sao_sleeping_5

Asuna：Look at us, lying in bed on such a nice day... I kind of feel like royalty.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_sao_sleeping_7

Asuna：Go ahead and shower ahead of me. I'm going to clean the dishes first.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_sao_sleeping_8

Asuna：It's still a bit early... but wanna just turn in?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_sao_sleeping_10

Asuna：Hey...are you still awake? Haha... Me too.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_asuna_sao_sleeping_11

Asuna：The moonlight through the curtain always looks so magical... Maybe it's that you've cast a spell on me.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_asuna_sao_sleeping_1

Asuna：Starting today, we have to go on raids again. But I'll be fine, now that I have you by my side.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_asuna_sao_sleeping_2

Asuna：Hey, it's okay to rest if you're tired. Let me take care of you.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_asuna_sao_sleeping_3

Asuna：Making plans is nice and all, but as long as I'm with you, I don't really care what we do.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_asuna_sao_sleeping_4

Asuna：Hey...can you help me recharge, so I can make it to my next day off? Just hold me tight.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_sao_sleeping_1

Asuna：Happy New Year! I hope you're by my side just as much as you were last year.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_sao_sleeping_2

Asuna：You're supposed to eat your age in beans, right? I'll feed you. Say ahh.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_sao_sleeping_3

Asuna：Haha, yes, don't worry, I got you some chocolates. For my true valentine, of course.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_sao_sleeping_4

Asuna：Hinamatsuri is a festival for girls, so Yui might like it. Shall we celebrate with her?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_sao_sleeping_5

Asuna：Yeah...I was actually hoping you'd get me a White Day gift. I kept guessing what it might be.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_sao_sleeping_6

Asuna：Lie to your heart's content! If I look in your eyes, I'll know the whole truth anyway.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_sao_sleeping_7

Asuna：Are there week-long holidays in this world? If so...then I'd love to go traveling with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_sao_sleeping_8

Asuna：It's raining today...so why don't we relax at home? We deserve a break, don't we?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_sao_sleeping_9

Asuna：If you're ever in trouble, I'll fly to your rescue, even if I have to cross the Milky Way.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_sao_sleeping_10

Asuna：Since it's street festival season in Japan right now, I made us lots of grilled food.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_sao_sleeping_11

Asuna：We made so many memories during this vacation. Each and every one of them is a treasure to me.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_sao_sleeping_12

Asuna：What a beautiful moon! Hard to believe we're in VR... I hope I can see the real thing again someday.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_sao_sleeping_13

Asuna：Since it's Halloween, I made pumpkin soup. Want to have a taste? It's my own recipe.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_sao_sleeping_14

Asuna：Whenever this day comes, I always remember my initial despair...and how you rescued me from it.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_asuna_sao_sleeping_15

Asuna：I hear that in the West, Christmas is a family holiday. Well, I guess we're also family...right?  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_asuna_sao_sleeping_3

Asuna：Today is a special day, so please come back early. I'll cook you a big feast.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_asuna_sao_sleeping_14

Asuna：Should we give Liz a blacksmithing tool for her birthday? I'm sure she'd love that.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_asuna_sao_sleeping_15

Asuna：Since it's Silica's birthday, Agil and I worked together to cook her a big feast.  

## 场景 novel.home_rank_asuna_sao_sleeping_past_1

Asuna：Did you rank up already? I've got some catching up to do! I don't want to be left behind.  

## 场景 novel.home_rank_asuna_sao_sleeping_past_2

Asuna：I think you've bulked up a little. Strange...this is a VR, so your appearance shouldn't change...  

## 场景 novel.home_rank_asuna_sao_sleeping_past_3

Asuna：You're always coming to my rescue. I need to get stronger so that I can rescue you back.  

## 场景 novel.home_rank_asuna_sao_sleeping_past_4

Asuna：I know just how strong you are— I've seen how hard you had to work for it.  

## 场景 novel.home_rank_asuna_sao_sleeping_past_5

Asuna：If anyone can clear Aincrad, it's you. And I'll be with you every step of the way.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_asuna_sao_sleeping_1

Asuna：Congratulations on clearing the quest! So, what was your reward?  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_asuna_sao_sleeping_2

Asuna：You got a present! Aren't you going to open it?  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_asuna_sao_sleeping_3

Asuna：Oh dear, it's time for the next raid. Before we have to go... can we snuggle a bit longer?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_wait_1

Leafa：At last, I have a pupil of my own. I am honored to help pass on our traditions to future generations.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_wait_2

Leafa：When I watch the dragons at play, I wish they could remain innocent and be spared from dangerous wars.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_wait_3

Alice：I'm always more childish when I'm near the Commander. I know it's not very valiant of me, though.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_wait_4

Alice：Integrity Knights are very powerful, but we cannot deny that we are shorthanded to protect this world.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_wait_5

Alice：Your sword was once a tree, I see. Well, well... Looks like you and I have something in common.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_wait_6

Alice：Someday, I would love to see an honest duel between you and the Commander.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_wait_7

Alice：The High Pontifex is indeed a woman of many mysteries, but we mustn't try to uncover those secrets.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_wait_8

Alice：How can you be so devoted to helping others? Surely you know of the risk to your own life...  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_wait_9

Alice：Are you hungry? Or perhaps tired? In either case, you mustn't be so sluggish in a Knight's presence.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_touch_1

Alice：I should probably wear a helmet for proper defense, but I hate fighting with such a narrow field of vision.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_touch_2

Alice：You like the feel...of my hair? W-Well, I suppose you would! I just washed it...  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_touch_3

Alice：H-How dare you touch my cheek! How am I supposed to respond? This's n-neither the time nor place...  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_touch_4

Alice：Why do you even have hands? To swing a sword, I assume?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_touch_5

Alice：Well, I do train hard every day... so I'm sure my body is more muscular than other girls...  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_touch_6

Alice：You wish to touch my sword? Sorry, but I do not allow anyone other than myself to handle it.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_touch_7

Alice：A Knight must always be prepared. That is why I always wear armor.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_touch_8

Alice：Hey! S-Stop that at once! How dare you tickle a Knight's body...  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_time_1

Alice：You really do look cozy when you sleep... It's rather infectious.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_time_2

Alice：I am leaving for an assignment today. ...Aren't you going to send me off?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_time_3

Alice：A sluggish start in the morning leads to a sluggish day overall. Come on! Sharpen up!  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_time_4

Alice：It's so clear today that even the distant mountains are visible. Shall we fly the dragons out there?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_time_5

Alice：The snacks you bring me are always so tasty. Where do you find them?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_time_6

Alice：Come to think of it, we've never seen a duel through to the end. I'd like to settle the score someday.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_time_7

Alice：I used to feel lonely and anxious at night...but ever since I met you, nights have been easier.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_time_8

Alice：I haven't seen you all day— what were you up to? If you want to talk, I'll listen.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_time_9

Alice：The stars are so lovely tonight. And Lunaria is so bright... I've never seen a night so beautiful.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_time_10

Alice：I always find myself staying up too late when I'm with you... Please be sure to get up on time tomorrow.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_time_11

Alice：As much as I enjoy chatting with you, I've gotten sleepy. Let us... continue this conversation tomorrow.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_time_12

Alice：Sneaking into a lady's room at night is a grave sin. As penance... let me get a good look at your face.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_day_1

Alice：With the day of rest at end, the citizens will get back to work... The sight of it pleases me somehow.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_day_2

Alice：Don't hide it—I know you are tired. I assure you, resting itself is a vital part of work.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_day_3

Alice：While I do feel that working on a day of rest is unfortunate, it is all part of a Knight's solemn duty.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_day_4

Alice：Today's merriment brings tomorrow's productivity. I suppose this is the "recharge" concept you speak of?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_event_1

Alice：I wish you a Happy Birthday. May you have a year of peace and comfort.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_event_2

Alice：Decorating with fish heads is an amusing custom. Tales of your homeland always fascinate me.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_event_3

Alice：I think giving candy to one's beloved is a noble custom. Who's my Valentine, you ask? N-Not certain yet!  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_event_4

Alice：Since this is a festival for girls, I must honor Selka. I wish her all the happiness in the world.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_event_5

Alice：It would be rude not to accept a thank you gift... Now, whom is that other gift for?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_event_6

Alice：I can never tell when someone is lying. So I ask that you please never tell lies to me.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_event_7

Alice：Using this time off for rest is a valid suggestion, but I would rather practice my sword and Sacred Arts.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_event_8

Alice：As dreary as the rain is, our crops will suffer without it. Consider this a blessing from Solus.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_event_9

Alice：If you ever went far, far away... I would run to your side. No matter what the obstacles.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_event_10

Alice：What is this "beach" place you keep speaking of? I would like to visit it someday.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_event_11

Alice：Just as you say, being assigned piles of tasks during a time of rest is indeed unfair.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_event_12

Alice：Unlike daytime festivals, Lunaria's has a tranquil atmosphere to it.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_event_13

Alice：If I take on a festive appearance, then I shall be able to give food and sweets to children in need, yes?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_event_15

Alice：A saint who gives children gifts in winter? Your world surely celebrates children a lot. I love it.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_season_1

Alice：The flying foxes teach their young to hunt in the springtime. I simply adore watching them.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_season_2

Alice：You mustn't stay in this inferno much longer—you'll hurt yourself. Come, let's rest in the shade.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_season_3

Alice：As I watch the falling blossoms, though I know it is Nature's will, I cannot help but feel sadness.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_season_4

Alice：Ooh, your hands are cold as ice. So are mine. Just feel how cold they are...  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_bd_2

Alice：A fleeting memory visits me at times...a memory of a childhood birthday. And the person I was with...  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_bd_3

Alice：I plan on getting Kirito something to wear for his birthday. That boy has no variety in his wardrobe.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_bd_4

Alice：Let us plan a celebration for Eugeo's birthday. I wish to give him an extravagant party.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_bd_6

Alice：What do you suppose Asuna would like for her birthday? Wouldn't you know best?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_bd_7

Alice：The Green Swordswoman, savior of the world... We truly owe her a huge celebration on her birthday.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_bd_8

Alice：Sinon never seems to put her own desires into words. That makes gift-giving a real puzzle.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_bd_14

Alice：You are also friends with Lisbeth, correct? Have you planned a birthday celebration for her?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_bd_15

Alice：Silica is indeed a sweet girl. I'm considering getting something for her pet dragon. What do you think?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_home_etc_1

Alice：I had every confidence in you. Now rest well until the next mission arrives.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_home_etc_2

Alice：A gift's arrived for you. Seeing how loved you are brings me such joy.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_2nd_home_etc_3

Alice：A new task has arrived. Come, swing your sword with me! Together, we cannot be defeated.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_wait_1

Alice：I find this sort of clothing rather uncomfortable... P-Please don't stare!  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_wait_2

Alice：You too are dressed differently than usual. So I suppose... that shall relax me a little.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_wait_3

Alice：I am only fine with you seeing me dressed like this. Not anyone else. It is f-far too embarrassing.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_wait_4

Alice：There is never a dull moment when I am with you. And I do not mean that as praise.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_wait_5

Alice：Though I am unfamiliar with this garment, it is easy to move in. Ideal for swinging my sword.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_wait_6

Alice：Keep memories of this day locked inside your mind. Not a word to anyone else...understood?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_wait_7

Alice：I don't particularly wish to see anyone else today... So I shall stay by your side at all times.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_wait_8

Alice：Do the other ladies all dress like this?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_wait_9

Alice：Why are you silent? If you wish to say something about my appearance, then speak up.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_touch_1

Alice：This hair ornament is unlike what I usually wear... What do you think of it?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_touch_2

Alice：You need not touch me so— you know it is I, do you not?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_touch_4

Alice：N-Now, now! My garment is flimsier than usual. D-Do not touch me in that manner...  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_touch_5

Alice：I see that my unusual garb interests you. However, could you keep your urges in check?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_time_1

Alice：Good morning. What? Why are you staring at me like that? Well, stop it.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_time_4

Alice：Let us stay indoors all day. That is indeed a great idea.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_time_7

Alice：Spending the day in these clothes did acclimate me to them. However, I do not wish to make a habit of it.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_time_10

Alice：Please, be honest. Me...in these clothes... What is your honest opinion of them?!  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_day_1

Alice：Everyone goes to school on Monday, I see. Learning with a big group sounds rather fun.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_day_2

Alice：People in this world rest even when it isn't a day of rest. Is there really such variety here?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_day_3

Alice：I love how lively weekends are. I can inconspicuously blend in with the crowd as a normal girl.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_day_4

Alice：I am a little tired, but this day of rest was enjoyable. I am sad to see it end.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_season_1

Alice：A picnic? Well...I do want to see the cherry blossoms, but for others to see me dressed like this...  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_season_2

Alice：This garment breathes much better than my armor. Perhaps this is ideal wear for a day of rest.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_season_3

Alice：If there is a festival where people wear costumes, then I suppose I won't stand out in these clothes.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_season_4

Alice：Winter's breeze feels colder in these clothes. That is why I am sticking so close to you.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_home_etc_1

Alice：I see you have completed a mission. I knew you could do it.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_home_etc_2

Alice：A gift has arrived for you. Now... who in the world sent it to you?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_tales_home_etc_3

Alice：The next mission awaits. Working with you as a team, I have every confidence in our success.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_integrator_enhance_wait_1

Alice：From now on, I'm going to fight for what I want to.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_integrator_enhance_touch_1

Alice：...I'm fine I will press forward. For what you and I desire.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_integrator_enhance_touch_4

Alice：Will you listen to my resolve?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_real_student_wait_1

Alice：With these clothes, I can be a knight here as well... how reassuring.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_real_student_wait_2

Alice：I feel a little tense without my armor, but these clothes give me confidence.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_real_student_wait_3

Alice：It won't be weird walking around town in this outfit, will it? If not, let's get going.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_alo_wait_1

Alice：The real test of being a knight is when you're in a pinch!  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_alo_wait_2

Alice：I want to hurry up and become a dragon knight so I can soar the skies!  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_alo_wait_3

Alice：This world is similar to my world. Like it's beautiful forests and blue skies.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_alo_wait_4

Alice：They also have plenty of skilled knights and Sacred Arts users. I must work harder!  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_alo_touch_1

Alice：I feel like a big-eared golden flying fox!  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_alo_touch_2

Alice：In my world, an Integrity Knight's rank is inscribed right around there.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_swimsuit_wait_1

Alice：It's amazing how exposed this outfit is...  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_swimsuit_touch_1

Alice：I'm f-fine! I can do my own hair...  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_swimsuit_touch_2

Alice：S-Stop! It's because of people like you this outfit is dangerous!  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_edith_cos_wait_1

Alice：Ahhhh... Where did I put my armor?!  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_edith_cos_wait_2

Alice：This sure is breezier than my regular clothes. I guess Eydis doesn't like the heat?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_edith_cos_touch_1

Alice：To think I'm going to mimic Eydis down to her haircut...  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_edith_cos_touch_2

Alice：My head feels a bit heavier than usual. I wonder if it's because I tied this up too high?  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_edith_cos_touch_3

Alice：Why are you looking at me like that?! Grr...the nerve...  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_edith_cos_touch_4

Alice：Don't you dare touch me like that...! You better back off or I'll tell uncle!  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_halloween_wait_1

Alice：T-This short costume wasn't my idea!  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_halloween_touch_1

Alice：Y-You want me to fight in this?! You must be an idiot!  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_halloween_touch_2

Alice：N-No tricks! I've got your treats right here!   

## 场景 novel.home_alice_uw_2022_new_year_wait_1

Alice：May not look it, but weirdly enough, these clothes are really  comfortable.  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_uw_2022_new_year_wait_2

Alice：Just dropped by to wish you a happy new year!  

## 场景 novel.home_alice_uw_2022_new_year_wait_3

Alice：Hmm... I could use a hot cup  of tea right now.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_alice_1st_1

Alice：On clear days like this, Amayori likes to sunbathe. Reminds me of someone I know.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_alice_1st_2

Alice：I wish I had someone to teach me to cook, just as I do for the sword and Sacred Arts.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_alice_1st_3

Alice：You look bored. Fancy a duel? Come on then, draw your sword.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_alice_1st_4

Alice：I have the honor of being an Integrity Knight. I will protect the Human Empire with my life.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_alice_1st_9

Alice：Please...help me hide! Eydis is pressuring me to bathe with her again!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_alice_1st_1

Alice：Just this once, I'll allow it... But treat me like a child again, and you'll pay dearly.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_alice_1st_2

Alice：Long hair is a liability in combat... That is what some people say. But I don't want to cut my hair.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_alice_1st_4

Alice：You just don't know when to quit, do you...? Clearly you don't care if you lose your hands.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_alice_1st_5

Alice：Do you really think I would let you touch me so easily? Who do you think I am?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_alice_1st_6

Alice：Ohh...now I'm feeling melancholy. Why must I feel so vulnerable around the likes of...  

## 场景 novel.home_time_alice_1st_1

Alice：Solus's light never fails to wake me early. Come, let's go outside.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_alice_1st_4

Alice：Seeing you napping like that is making me sleepy. If you must nap, do so indoors.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_alice_1st_7

Alice：Hey! No dinner until it's ready! I will not tolerate sneaking bites off the plate!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_alice_1st_10

Alice：On nights like this, I can understand the desire to have a drink of wine.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_alice_1st_1

Alice：Our next mission is about to start. Let's give it our all.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_alice_1st_2

Alice：Doing mission after mission really takes its toll on the body. Be sure to rest well at night.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_alice_1st_3

Alice：Don't think you can get away with napping all day just because it's a Day of Rest.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_alice_1st_4

Alice：That was a fun Day of Rest. Thank you for making it so.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_alice_1st_1

Alice：The days have been getting warmer. I only hope this won't make you even lazier.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_alice_1st_2

Alice：No matter how hot the weather, an esteemed I-Integrity Knight mustn't dress sk-skimpily.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_alice_1st_3

Alice：The fragrant olives bloom this time of year. Seeing the tiny buds makes my heart flutter.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_alice_1st_4

Alice：I see ice in the rivers now. I have the feeling...you and I beheld this scene long ago.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_alice_uw_sleeping_1

Alice：I will not tolerate rudeness. But...if you're only standing next to me...  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_alice_uw_sleeping_2

Alice：It's strange whenever I am with you. I feel calm and can just fall asleep at any moment.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_alice_uw_sleeping_3

Alice：Where do you think you're going? Don't just wander around! I'll teach you for worrying me!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_alice_uw_sleeping_4

Alice：Don't worry. Even if you fall asleep, I'll be here to protect you.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_alice_uw_sleeping_5

Alice：If someone was to see us like this...  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_alice_uw_sleeping_6

Alice：You know, it's good to be able to forget about fighting and just take it easy from time to time.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_alice_uw_sleeping_7

Alice：Whenever I see your smile... I feel a sense of ease and calm within me.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_alice_uw_sleeping_8

Alice：Hey! Put some clothes on! You'll catch a cold if you sleep with your shoulders out like that.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_alice_uw_sleeping_9

Alice：Did you fall asleep? You're a man of many weaknesses... Hehe, only weaknesses.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_alice_uw_sleeping_1

Alice：My right eye is already better. With sacred power, even wounds like this can be treated.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_alice_uw_sleeping_2

Alice：...What are your thoughts on women's hair? If we were to talk casually...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_alice_uw_sleeping_3

Alice：Getting a little comfortable are we? Don't think you can do as you please...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_alice_uw_sleeping_4

Alice：Agh?! Stop that this instant! I'm not wearing any armor!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_alice_uw_sleeping_5

Alice：Don't try to get any closer... But don't leave... Stay right there...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_alice_uw_sleeping_6

Alice：Don't think just because we're sharing a bed together doesn't mean I 100% forgive you!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_alice_uw_sleeping_7

Alice：With these hands you changed the fate of the world... and me...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_alice_uw_sleeping_8

Alice：Don't move around so much! If you're cold... we can hold hands.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_alice_uw_sleeping_1

Alice：Looks like it's morning already... I wish I could stay a little longer, but let's get up.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_alice_uw_sleeping_2

Alice：Are you still asleep? ...Hehe, you look sound asleep.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_alice_uw_sleeping_4

Alice：You know, taking a nap with you from time to time isn't so bad.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_alice_uw_sleeping_5

Alice：This heat is sure to make anyone drowsy. But even if you weren't here, I wouldn't succumb to it.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_alice_uw_sleeping_7

Alice：Shall we start preparing dinner? ...What's with that look? Listen, I've gotten better!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_alice_uw_sleeping_8

Alice：I often got nightmares whenever I slept at night. But with you, I've seen nothing but pleasant dreams.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_alice_uw_sleeping_10

Alice：Being with you in this silence feels like we're the only ones in this world...  

## 场景 novel.home_time_alice_uw_sleeping_11

Alice：Still awake, are we? Hurry up and get some shuteye. I won't let you see me sleeping.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_alice_uw_sleeping_1

Alice：Don't go on sleeping forever! Time to wake up. ...Don't look at me like that!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_alice_uw_sleeping_2

Alice：We'll be able to meet again like this when you go back. So let's finish up what we need to do.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_alice_uw_sleeping_3

Alice：I do like the calmness on my days off, but being with you is something I can't toss away either.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_alice_uw_sleeping_4

Alice：Ever since I met you, I feel lonely when my days off come to an end.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_alice_uw_sleeping_1

Alice：Looks like it's the start of a new year. I'm glad I get to see you during this time.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_alice_uw_sleeping_2

Alice：Beans are a great source for nutrients. They're the perfect food if you want to be healthy!  

## 场景 novel.home_event_alice_uw_sleeping_3

Alice：In your world, you hand sweets to those who you care for, correct? ...Well, here you go...  

## 场景 novel.home_event_alice_uw_sleeping_4

Alice：A ceremony to celebrate a girl growing up... How lovely. There's nothing happier than that.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_alice_uw_sleeping_5

Alice：Is this for me?! ...Thank you! I will never forget this.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_alice_uw_sleeping_6

Alice：Let's stop with the lies. Not because it's taboo, but because we trust each other.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_alice_uw_sleeping_7

Alice：I haven't gotten used to having long breaks. So...let's think of something to do. Just us.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_alice_uw_sleeping_8

Alice：Rainy days are Amayori's favorite. "Rain" is in her name, after all, so I have to let her play.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_alice_uw_sleeping_9

Alice：A tale of two lovers torn apart... How sad. To think if that happened to me...  

## 场景 novel.home_event_alice_uw_sleeping_10

Alice：Looks like they're getting ready for the Summer Solstice Festival. Hehe, I wonder what you should eat.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_alice_uw_sleeping_11

Alice：My body will probably get a little out of shape on long breaks. I'll have to keep up my training!  

## 场景 novel.home_event_alice_uw_sleeping_12

Alice：There are rabbits on the moon? Your world sure has some interesting stories.   

## 场景 novel.home_event_alice_uw_sleeping_13

Alice：Of course I passed out the sweets. Even though I feel like I wanted to see those kid's trick...  

## 场景 novel.home_event_alice_uw_sleeping_15

Alice：It's Christmas Eve. I may not know the meaning of this day, but I'm happy I get to spend it with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_alice_uw_sleeping_3

Alice：Today's your birthday! So I'll go easy on you. You can sleep here to your heart's content.    

## 场景 novel.home_bd_alice_uw_sleeping_4

Alice：It's Eugeo's birthday... So let's go to Rulid and have a big celebration!  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_alice_uw_sleeping_6

Alice：It's Asuna's birthday... It wouldn't be a good idea if we went together, would it?  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_alice_uw_sleeping_7

Alice：If she's your sister, then that makes her my sister too. Let's celebrate! Give me a hand!  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_alice_uw_sleeping_8

Alice：Sinon is the one who told me about my mentor. I owe her my thanks. We must celebrate her birthday!   

## 场景 novel.home_bd_alice_uw_sleeping_11

Alice：Is it Ronie's birthday? What does she like? You're her senior, so surely you must know?  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_alice_uw_sleeping_12

Alice：Let's get a present for Tiese's birthday! I'm sure she'll be more than pleased with that!  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_alice_uw_sleeping_14

Alice：Lisbeth also comes from your world, right? Then you should decide on what to give her.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_alice_uw_sleeping_15

Alice：Silica's birthday is a perfect match for that cute wyvern. Let's get her something.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_alice_uw_sleeping_1

Alice：If you've completed your mission, then you don't need to wake up so early. You can relax here.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_alice_uw_sleeping_2

Alice：The present is here! Let's open it up right here. Don't worry, you're safe if you're with me.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_alice_uw_sleeping_3

Alice：The next mission just came in. Are you well rested? If so, let's get going.  

## 场景 novel.home_ronye_vc.home_wait_101

Ronie：Is it ok... to sleep next to you?  

## 场景 novel.home_ronye_vc.home_wait_102

Ronie：If you keep petting me, I'll fall asleep...  

## 场景 novel.home_ronye_vc.home_wait_103

Ronie：Aaaah! Y-You surprised me there! My drowsiness just went away...  

## 场景 novel.home_ronye_vc.home_wait_104

Ronie：Please stay will me all night!  

## 场景 novel.home_ronye_vc.home_wait_105

Ronie：Ah... Mhm... I saw your dream.  

## 场景 novel.home_ronye_vc.home_wait_106

Ronie：Ahhh... I'm getting a little sleepy...  

## 场景 novel.home_ronye_vc.home_wait_111

Ronie：Hey! I made snacks with Tiese. Would you like... some...?  

## 场景 novel.home_ronye_vc.home_wait_112

Ronie：I love you, Kirito ... Ah! Um, I said I love pies... !  

## 场景 novel.home_ronye_vc.home_wait_113

Ronie：W-Would you go to the Winter Solstice Festival, w-with me?  

## 场景 novel.home_ronye_vc.home_wait_114

Ronie：The Winter Solstice Festival is a very important day for women.  

## 场景 novel.home_ronye_swimsuit_wait_1

Ronie：A-Actually, I can't swim... Please teach me how...  

## 场景 novel.home_ronye_swimsuit_touch_1

Ronie：I-I'll practice some more...  

## 场景 novel.home_ronye_swimsuit_touch_4

Ronie：Ahh! S-Sorry! It's just a scratch.  

## 场景 novel.home_ronye_mc_long_bath_wait_1

Ronie：Lately, nothing beats going for a bath after a job!  

## 场景 novel.home_ronye_mc_long_bath_touch_1

Ronie：Is your hair still wet?  

## 场景 novel.home_ronye_mc_long_bath_touch_2

Ronie：Wearing this breastplate all the time has been a real pain, so taking it off is such a relief.  

## 场景 novel.home_ronye_halloween_wait_1

Ronie：Is it a costume party? That means I can bake some sweets with Tiese!  

## 场景 novel.home_ronye_halloween_wait_2

Ronie：Oh no, I'm going to be late to the party!  

## 场景 novel.home_ronye_halloween_touch_1

Ronie：I'll get tricked if I don't get any treats... right?  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_ronye_1st_1

Ronie：Cleaning your room is a crucial part of my duties as your page. And...I enjoy it, too.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_ronye_1st_2

Ronie：My little brother annoys me sometimes, but I do miss him when I'm away for too long.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_ronye_1st_3

Ronie：I can tell it's you, even from far away! I mean... because it's you...  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_ronye_1st_4

Ronie：How can I improve my attack speed like you? Please show me sometime!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_ronye_1st_9

Ronie：My mentor always looks so beautiful when he's deep in tho... Agh! D-Did you hear that?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_ronye_1st_1

Ronie：Frenica taught me how to keep my hair looking nice... H-How do I look?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_ronye_1st_2

Ronie：Um...may I touch your hair, too?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_ronye_1st_4

Ronie：Eek?! Is th-this also a part of Aincrad Style training?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_ronye_1st_5

Ronie：Oh, did I have dust on me? Thank you for cleaning it off.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_ronye_1st_6

Ronie：Why are you suddenly doing this...? Would you do it to any other girl?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_ronye_1st_1

Ronie：I've made your breakfast today! Eat to your heart's content!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_ronye_1st_4

Ronie：What lovely weather! Aren't you going to take a nap today, Mentor?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_ronye_1st_7

Ronie：Tiese and I are going to practice our Sacred Arts tonight. We have a test coming up.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_ronye_1st_10

Ronie：Nights are so quiet, and yet they still scare me. Won't you stay by my side?  

## 场景 novel.home_day_ronye_1st_1

Ronie：Knowing I'll see you the day after a day off gives me energy!   

## 场景 novel.home_day_ronye_1st_2

Ronie：Curfew for primary trainees is very strict... One second late and you're in big trouble.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_ronye_1st_3

Ronie：What sorts of things do you all talk about on your days off? We talk about... No, it's a secret.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_ronye_1st_4

Ronie：I eagerly await your lessons tomorrow!  

## 场景 novel.home_season_ronye_1st_1

Ronie：I love how warm spring is. It's wonderful seeing all the plants and wild animals out and about.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_ronye_1st_2

Ronie：Summers are hot, but I do love swimming on days like this.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_ronye_1st_3

Ronie：When Harvest is near, the town gets so lively. I hope this year is also bountiful.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_ronye_1st_4

Ronie：I always get excited when it snows. Tiese teases me for it, though. She calls me childish.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_ronye_uw_sleeping_1

I know I should call you "Sir Swordsman Delegate"...but to me, you'll always be my mentor.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_ronye_uw_sleeping_2

Will you teach me to swordfight some more? Like back at the academy... Just the two of us...  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_ronye_uw_sleeping_3

I'm your page. Being by your side at all times is my duty.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_ronye_uw_sleeping_4

When the time comes for you to return home to your world... I wish I could go with you...haha.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_ronye_uw_sleeping_5

I-Is it just me, or is it rather hot? Perhaps it's because I'm near you...  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_ronye_uw_sleeping_6

Ever since we were in school, I've dreamed of being next to you like this.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_ronye_uw_sleeping_7

Just for today, you're all mine. But tomorrow, we'll once again be the swordsman delegate and Ronie.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_ronye_uw_sleeping_8

Your eyes are always so kind... And when I'm in your kind gaze, I realize how truly blessed I am.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_ronye_uw_sleeping_9

Have you...fallen asleep? Then I suppose...it would be all right for me to do this.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_ronye_uw_sleeping_1

Ronie：My hair has grown quite a bit. So, um...do you like long hair?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_ronye_uw_sleeping_2

Ronie：When you pat my head like this, I feel like I'm a little kid again...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_ronye_uw_sleeping_3

Ronie：I-Is my face...hot? Well, that's because you're touching it...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_ronye_uw_sleeping_4

Ronie：Your hands... They're so hot. Or perhaps it's mine that are hot.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_ronye_uw_sleeping_5

Ronie：H-Have I grown since my days at the academy? I can't exactly tell...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_ronye_uw_sleeping_6

Ronie：Next time, I’ll help you relax. You call it a massage, right?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_ronye_uw_sleeping_7

Ronie：Were my shoulders exposed? Thank you... It would have been terrible to catch a cold.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_ronye_uw_sleeping_8

Ronie：Since I train every day, I suppose my figure isn't all that feminine...  

## 场景 novel.home_time_ronye_uw_sleeping_1

Good morning. Haha, your sleeping face was adorable.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_ronye_uw_sleeping_2

Look at us, sleeping in together. I feel like a spoiled princess.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_ronye_uw_sleeping_4

Do you not have delegate duties to tend to today? Then let's stay here together all day.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_ronye_uw_sleeping_5

My parents would always scold me if I slept in this late. "Get up at once!" they'd yell.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_ronye_uw_sleeping_7

I'll take care of supper! Your page is going to cook you a delicious feast to remember!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_ronye_uw_sleeping_8

I just love peaceful nights without clanging swords or yelling. I have you to thank for that.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_ronye_uw_sleeping_10

I dreamed you left me... Can I hold you tight? Just for now...  

## 场景 novel.home_time_ronye_uw_sleeping_11

The moonlight is so gentle. I wish I could shine on you just like that.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_ronye_uw_sleeping_1

Ronie：It's time for work again! Being the swordsman delegate sounds hard, but I believe in you!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_ronye_uw_sleeping_2

Ronie：Are you perhaps a little tired? Then please, come rest here with me.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_ronye_uw_sleeping_3

Ronie：The streets are so lively on Days of Rest now. I truly am happy the world is at peace.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_ronye_uw_sleeping_4

Ronie：Today was so much fun! I would love to go out with you again sometime, when you're free.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_ronye_uw_sleeping_1

Ronie：Since I got to spend New Year's Day with you, I'm certain the rest of the year will be good.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_ronye_uw_sleeping_2

Ronie：A bean-throwing festival would be fun at the Cathedral. Let's invite the city-dwellers here.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_ronye_uw_sleeping_3

Ronie：I made these sweets for you... Do you like them?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_ronye_uw_sleeping_4

Ronie：I never had dolls like these, but I think my mother and father still gave me a good childhood.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_ronye_uw_sleeping_5

Ronie：A present from you...? Thank you so much! May I open it now?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_ronye_uw_sleeping_6

Ronie：I can't quite distinguish between "lies" and "jokes"... It's just too difficult for me.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_ronye_uw_sleeping_7

Ronie：Today and the day after are Days of Rest...I'm so happy I get to be with you all that time.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_ronye_uw_sleeping_8

Ronie：When it rains, I remember my schooldays. Like, memorizing arts while listening to the rain...  

## 场景 novel.home_event_ronye_uw_sleeping_9

Ronie：I hope we can manufacture enough hemp paper for everyone to write down their wishes.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_ronye_uw_sleeping_10

Ronie：When I hear Summer Festival music, it always makes me excited. Come, let's go to the festival!  

## 场景 novel.home_event_ronye_uw_sleeping_11

Ronie：Our summer vacation has come to an end. I made so many wonderful memories with you this year.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_ronye_uw_sleeping_12

Ronie：Since my sword and dragon are both named after the moon...I feel much closer to Lunaria now.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_ronye_uw_sleeping_13

Ronie：Lady Asuna prepared a wide variety of candies for the children. I know they'll love it!  

## 场景 novel.home_event_ronye_uw_sleeping_15

Ronie：Santa must be a very kind old man, to leave presents on children's pillows while they sleep.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_ronye_uw_sleeping_2

Ronie：A birthday present from my mentor... Oh, I couldn't possibly be any happier!  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_ronye_uw_sleeping_3

Ronie：Happy Birthday! Is there anything you'd like? I would give you anything...  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_ronye_uw_sleeping_4

Ronie：It's Eugeo's birthday, I see. I would love to stay here all day and hear stories about you two.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_ronye_uw_sleeping_5

Ronie：Do you suppose we should make Lady Alice's birthday a holiday in this world?  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_ronye_uw_sleeping_6

Ronie：I will give Lady Asuna cooking tools for her birthday. I found some really cute ones.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_ronye_uw_sleeping_7

Ronie：Today is the Green Swordswoman Leafa's birthday. I wish I could have met her.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_ronye_uw_sleeping_8

Ronie：Since Sinon is the goddess Solus incarnate...perhaps we should throw her a festival?  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_ronye_uw_sleeping_12

Ronie：I want to celebrate Tiese's birthday alone with her. I won't even let you come.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_ronye_uw_sleeping_14

Ronie：Lisbeth is a blacksmith, yes? Then perhaps we should give her some rare metal ore.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_ronye_uw_sleeping_15

Ronie：For Silica's birthday, I think want to get her a treat for her cute little dragon.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_ronye_uw_sleeping_1

Ronie：A job well done, sir. Please, rest and recuperate here. Let me take care of you.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_ronye_uw_sleeping_2

Ronie：You've received a gift. Don't worry—I've checked it to make sure it's nothing dangerous.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_ronye_uw_sleeping_3

Ronie：Where is your next mission? If it's far away, please allow me to accompany you as your page!  

## 场景 novel.home_tiese_vc.home_wait_101

Tiese：Eugeo! I made snacks with Ronie... I hope it suits your taste...  

## 场景 novel.home_tiese_vc.home_wait_102

Tiese：Eugeo… I love… I'd love to know your favorite food. Can you tell me?  

## 场景 novel.home_tiese_vc.home_wait_103

Tiese：E-Eugeo, do you have any plans for the Winter Solstice Festival? If you're free, how about me and you...  

## 场景 novel.home_tiese_vc.home_wait_104

Tiese：Long ago, I used to bake sweets when it was the Winter Solstice Festival. That was my specialty!    

## 场景 novel.home_tiese_swimsuit_wait_1

Tiese：I'm pretty good at swimming! Do you want me to teach you how?  

## 场景 novel.home_tiese_swimsuit_touch_1

Tiese：I wonder if I should tie up my hair...  

## 场景 novel.home_tiese_swimsuit_touch_4

Tiese：Ahh! M-My clothes didn't get wet, did they?  

## 场景 novel.home_tiese_halloween_wait_2

Tiese：I baked some treats with Ronie for today's tea! Hope you like 'em!  

## 场景 novel.home_tiese_halloween_wait_3

Tiese：Tea time, everyone!  

## 场景 novel.home_tiese_contest_wait_1

Tiese：Eugeo, care to dance with me?  

## 场景 novel.home_tiese_contest_wait_2

Tiese：You don't have to be a pro. As long as I can just hold your hand and dance...  

## 场景 novel.home_tiese_contest_touch_1

Tiese：I had Ronie do my hair. It's different from my usual style, but which do you prefer?  

## 场景 novel.home_tiese_mc_bath_wait_1

Tiese：I occasionally hear people talking in the men's bath whenever I go for a dip. It's kinda exciting...  

## 场景 novel.home_tiese_mc_bath_touch_1

Tiese：Ronie's hair got longer, so last time I helped wash her hair.  

## 场景 novel.home_tiese_mc_bath_touch_2

Tiese：Taking this armor off after a mission is the best part of the day.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_tiese_1st_1

Tiese：Mentor, training again today? I would be honored if I could join you!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_tiese_1st_2

Tiese：Aincrad Style is different from any technique I've learned, but it's very intuitive.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_tiese_1st_3

Tiese：Higher-rank nobles are arrogant, even when they're in public... You should be more careful.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_tiese_1st_4

Tiese：I'd love for you to meet my father someday. I just know he would love chatting with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_tiese_1st_9

Tiese：You can take it slow, Mentor. Supporting you like this is a page's duty.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_tiese_1st_1

Tiese：Um, I j-just finished...training... so I'm, um, sweaty... and I'd rather you not...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_tiese_1st_2

Tiese：I don't mind if you touch my hair, Mentor. Your touch is... gentler than the others.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_tiese_1st_4

Tiese：Th-That tickles! But if you insist, I don't mind...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_tiese_1st_5

Tiese：My heart races when you do that to me... I feel like it's going to explode.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_tiese_1st_6

Tiese：Mentor... You're much more... forward than I took you for.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_tiese_1st_1

Tiese：Seeing you first thing in the morning makes me feel like it's going to be a fun day!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_tiese_1st_4

Tiese：I was thinking all four of us could have dinner together tonight.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_tiese_1st_7

Tiese：Ronie is going to help me study today. I'm not very good at Sacred Arts.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_tiese_1st_10

Tiese：Taking me out at night like this... You're rather bold, Mentor. Though I'm glad you did.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_tiese_1st_1

Tiese：Starting today, I vow to master at least one move from the Aincrad Style!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_tiese_1st_2

Tiese：Watching is part of the learning process... Understood! I'll gladly watch you for the next two days!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_tiese_1st_3

Tiese：The next Day of Rest, I'll be going to town with my roommates. I'll buy you something nice!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_tiese_1st_4

Tiese：Staying up late the night after a Day of Rest makes the next day difficult. I need to go to bed.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_tiese_1st_1

Tiese：Ooh, look at this, Mentor! What cute little buds...  

## 场景 novel.home_season_tiese_1st_2

Tiese：You always wear your uniform impeccably, even when it's hot. I...really respect that about you.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_tiese_1st_3

Tiese：Autumn skies have a special scent to them. Very crisp and comforting.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_tiese_1st_4

Tiese：How fascinating, the way water can freeze to ice. All without Sacred Arts...  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_administrator_1st_1

Administrator：A static world for all eternity. That is my utopia. Change is not necessary.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_administrator_1st_2

Administrator：Chudelkin is a hopeless man, but he never defies me. That is why I keep him close.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_administrator_1st_3

Administrator：Yes, love is precious. People will abandon everything else once they attain it.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_administrator_1st_4

Administrator：Why must your mind wander where it shouldn't? Life is much easier when you don't think.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_administrator_1st_9

Administrator：Why do you look so stunned? Perhaps you just learned some shocking information?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_administrator_1st_1

Administrator：Is your hand malfunctioning? Very well, allow me to give it another adjustment.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_administrator_1st_2

Administrator：Time will not ravage my body. I shall remain eternally in my current beautiful form.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_administrator_1st_4

Administrator：You seem quite ravenous. Are you really that starved?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_administrator_1st_5

Administrator：I dislike wearing clothes. The cloth is so rough against my skin.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_administrator_1st_6

Administrator：Your impudence is intolerable. Just how far do you think my mercy will stretch?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_administrator_1st_1

Administrator：Is it...morning already? If you have no business, then let me sleep.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_administrator_1st_4

Administrator：Solus... If only Solus were under my control, then I could extinguish this harsh light.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_administrator_1st_7

Administrator：Night is a time for evil. Tell the Integrity Knights to be vigilant.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_administrator_1st_10

Administrator：Very quiet indeed. How wonderful the world would be if it were quiet like this forever.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_administrator_1st_1

Administrator：I gave you a mission. So why are you here?  

## 场景 novel.home_day_administrator_1st_2

Administrator：I need no army. The Integrity Knights and my own powers are more than sufficient.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_administrator_1st_3

Administrator：The peasants become rowdy on Days of Rest. Watch out for those who break the Taboo Index.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_administrator_1st_4

Administrator：You can't sleep? Then stop thinking. Your kind don't need to think.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_administrator_1st_1

Administrator：Sacred Power increases in the spring. It is a season ideal for experimentation.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_administrator_1st_2

Administrator：I have organized the Cathedral for comfort. It was nothing but child's play for me.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_administrator_1st_3

Administrator：The wheat is blowing in the wind... I am not sure why, but this feels familiar to me.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_administrator_1st_4

Administrator：It's snowing... I thought it was oddly quiet. A bath on a day like this would certainly feel nice.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_administrator_nightwear_1

Administrator：Come to me, my dear child. I have everything your heart desires right here.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_administrator_nightwear_2

Administrator：You needn't think about anything. Just listen to my voice.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_administrator_nightwear_3

Administrator：Let me ask, are you happy? You must be happy, yes? Hehe, hehehe...  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_administrator_nightwear_4

Administrator：Your search is over. I shall give you all the love you need. Let me fill the emptiness inside you.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_administrator_nightwear_5

Administrator：You should feel honored. Sleeping beside me is a nigh unattainable experience.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_administrator_nightwear_6

Administrator：Offer everything unto me. I know you want to. So, come, take my hand.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_administrator_nightwear_7

Administrator：You may watch as long as you wish today. Though I can't guarantee you'll remember anything tomorrow.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_administrator_nightwear_8

Administrator：Now, close your eyes. Sharpen your other senses. Hehe... Doesn't it feel exhilarating?  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_administrator_nightwear_9

Administrator：Good. Let sleep take you. When you wake, you shall have returned to your former blissful state.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_administrator_nightwear_1

Administrator：Does my hair interest you? Then you may touch it. Just this once. It's your reward.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_administrator_nightwear_2

Administrator：Entranced by my face? Well... I don't blame you, but you must exercise a little restraint.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_administrator_nightwear_3

Administrator：You think I'll let you touch me? Then try it, if you dare.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_administrator_nightwear_4

Administrator：You wish to hold my hand? Very well, I shall hold your hand and give everything to you.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_administrator_nightwear_5

Administrator：Haha...somebody's certainly full of desire. A desire that cannot be quenched... Hehehe.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_administrator_nightwear_6

Administrator：You wish to take me, do you not? Well, you must complete your mission first.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_administrator_nightwear_7

Administrator：Yes. Place your hand there. Firmly. And now...recite the incantation I just taught you.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_administrator_nightwear_8

Administrator：My! So savage. If you are really that starved, I am confident you will serve me well.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_administrator_nightwear_1

Administrator：I am going to rest for a while. As a special reward, I shall allow you to lay beside me.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_administrator_nightwear_2

Administrator：Fetch me some cakes. I've no particular reason why, but I crave them today.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_administrator_nightwear_4

Administrator：Today, I shall let no one else enter this room. It will be a special place for you and me.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_administrator_nightwear_5

Administrator：I'll indulge you with a special reward before I readjust you. You'll forget about it anyway.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_administrator_nightwear_7

Administrator：I must arrange my memories again. If that pesky Prime Senator comes, make sure you drive him away.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_administrator_nightwear_8

Administrator：Chudelkin brought me an unusual wine. Come drink it with me.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_administrator_nightwear_10

Administrator：Everyone has gone to sleep. I suppose this means it is time for me to give you my love.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_administrator_nightwear_11

Administrator：Turn off those troublesome lights... Good. Doesn't it feel much nicer in the dark?  

## 场景 novel.home_day_administrator_nightwear_1

Administrator：Now, go attend to your mission today. I expect you to report to me immediately upon your return.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_administrator_nightwear_2

Administrator：Just another ordinary day in the Human Empire. As it should be.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_administrator_nightwear_3

Administrator：The Day of Rest's bustle does not reach us up here, which is what I prefer. I like the peace and quiet.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_administrator_nightwear_4

Administrator：I let you overindulge in your rest today. Be sure to work extra hard for me starting tomorrow.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_administrator_nightwear_1

Administrator：A new year is upon us. It shall be no different from the year before it...or the year after.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_administrator_nightwear_2

Administrator：I wish throwing beans could drive away the Dark Realm's forces. That would certainly be easier.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_administrator_nightwear_3

Administrator：I shall give you a gift only when you deserve one. Greed will destroy you.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_administrator_nightwear_4

Administrator：Children... Worthy of celebration. They shall bear more children, age, then die and return to the heavens.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_administrator_nightwear_5

Administrator：A gift for me? Well, set it down. I shall have a look at it later, if the mood strikes me.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_administrator_nightwear_6

Administrator：A day where deceit is forgiven? The outside world certainly is horrible. More so than here.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_administrator_nightwear_7

Administrator：If a person rests for too long, their mind might rot. I wish we could abolish such a holiday.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_administrator_nightwear_8

Administrator：If the rains continue for too long, we must manipulate the weather. The people mustn't starve.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_administrator_nightwear_9

Administrator：Not even I am capable of creating a river of stars. At my current level, that is.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_administrator_nightwear_10

Administrator：I understand that the people need their fun, but the racket of this Summer Festival is most annoying.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_administrator_nightwear_11

Administrator：Good morning. Did you enjoy your long slumber? Good. Now, get back to serving me.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_administrator_nightwear_12
