Administrator：I vastly prefer Lunaria's light over Solus. It is so much more soothing.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_administrator_nightwear_13

Administrator："Trick or Treat"... Interesting. Would you rather I "trick" your memories, or "treat" your soul?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_administrator_nightwear_15

Administrator：I do love a snowy landscape. It resembles my soul after I've arranged my memories.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_administrator_nightwear_3

Administrator：The day that boy was born... If I could, I would love to return his memory to his younger days.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_administrator_nightwear_4

Administrator：If time permitted, I would gift Eugeo my love to help him forget everything.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_administrator_nightwear_5

Administrator：Is it little Alice's birthday? Do you suppose she would like those two boys as a "gift"?  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_administrator_nightwear_1

Administrator：Have you finished your task? Then report to me at once. Your reward will come later.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_administrator_nightwear_2

Administrator：Oh, what is inside that box? Who gave it to you? I hope you can remember.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_administrator_nightwear_3

Administrator：Now, embark on your mission. If you are successful, I shall reward you with more love.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_administrator_nightwear_1

Administrator：Springtime always makes me uneasy. There are too many Integrity Knights. Are you all right?  

## 场景 novel.home_season_administrator_nightwear_2

Administrator：Stay back a little. It is far too hot to be close to you today.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_administrator_nightwear_3

Administrator：A storm is coming. Let us nap to the song of the wind.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_administrator_nightwear_4

Administrator：Oh, you poor thing. You're shivering. Fear not. I shall keep you warm.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_wait_1

Alice：Ooh, I knew seeing you would cheer me up. Come closer if you want to cheer me up even more!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_wait_2

Alice：Y'know, the Integrity Knights never got together to hang out before. How about we all have dinner later?  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_wait_3

Alice：I'm gonna go back to my sword training today—can't let you outshine me, can I? I'm the big sister, see.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_wait_4

Eydis：I dunno, I'm just not a big fan of the senior priests. Though Ayuha and Soness are pretty cute.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_wait_5

Eydis：Steer clear of the Prime Senator today. He's really upset because the High Pontifex is resting all day.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_wait_6

Eydis：Since we've both got nothing to do, let's spend the day together! Hm... How about a walk in the sky garden?  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_wait_7

Eydis：So, when're you gonna finally start calling me "big sister"? My ears are always ready.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_wait_8

Eydis：I wonder if there's a castle and cities full of people on the other side of the Wall at the End of the World?  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_wait_9

Eydis：Aww, you're so pretty when you stare off into the distance like that. I could gaze at you for hours.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_touch_1

Eydis：I love my hair color, if I do say so myself. But I kinda also wish I had your pretty golden locks.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_touch_2

Eydis：Hey, could you tussle my hair even harder? I actually kinda like how that feels.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_touch_3

Eydis：W-Whoa, there...I have ticklish spots too, you know. Geesh...your hands've gotten awfully strong.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_touch_4

Eydis：Why, you naughty little hands... Gotcha! Now you're mine!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_touch_5

Eydis：Come on, you don't have to be shy. If it's you, I won't get bad.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_touch_6

Eydis：I just hate wearing clothes! The cloth is so rough against my skin.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_touch_7

Eydis：Is it just me or have you gotten really needy lately? Hehe, well you're so adorable I just have to dote on you.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_touch_8

Eydis：When we touch each other like this, I feel so close to you. This must be what true happiness is.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_time_1

Eydis：Good morning! Seeing you first thing in the morning means this's gonna be a great day!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_time_2

Eydis：I sure love the smell of mornings. And I feel extra refreshed when I'm with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_time_3

Eydis：I'm so sleepy today... Hey, wanna just go back to bed? Come on. It'll feel really nice.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_time_4

Eydis：I was gonna go out to lunch with Kirimai today. Want to join us?  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_time_5

Eydis：I should really try to keep up with my Sacred Arts. Wouldn't want to forget an incantation when I really need it.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_time_6

Eydis：Yay, I'm done with work for the day! Went faster than I thought it would. Just look how high Solus still is!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_time_7

Eydis：Uh-oh, the Commander snatched the first bath right from me! That man loves his baths, too, doesn't he?  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_time_8

Eydis：Lying still in the darkness likes this makes me feel like I'm melting into it.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_time_9

Eydis：Another day in the books! Let's take a nice soothing bath to recharge. Together, of course!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_time_10

Eydis：If you can't sleep, don't force it. Your brain must've needed this time to think.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_time_11

Eydis：Oops, did I wake you? Sorry, I couldn't sleep. I was about to go on a walk.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_time_12

Eydis：Wow, you were out this late on duty? You must be exhausted. I'll go get you some warm milk.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_day_1

Eydis：Aw, I have to go back to work today. But I know our next day of rest will be just as fun as the last.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_day_2

Eydis：I hear you've got a busy week ahead. Don't kill yourself, okay? Get plenty of rest.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_day_3

Eydis：Any plans for your day off? Want to go somewhere with me?  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_day_4

Eydis：Did you enjoy the day of rest? Since I got to be with you, I had the best day ever, of course.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_event_1

Eydis：Happy New Year! Did you get to see Solus' first rise this time?  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_event_2

Eydis：Generate Metallic Element! Bean Shape! Now we can totally ward off all the demons!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_event_3

Eydis：Hey, wanna make and exchange chocolates together this year? I think it'll be fun!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_event_4

Eydis：When I first heard Hinamatsuri was a day for "chicks," I thought they meant "dragon chicks"...but it's "girls"!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_event_5

Eydis：Wow, don't you just love how they have not only a gift-giving, but a thank you gift-giving holiday, too?  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_event_6

Eydis：I know lying is wrong, but when I'm given a pass, I suddenly have the urge to prank the Prime Senator.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_event_7

Eydis：I got a few days off, so let's travel somewhere together! How about a nice long dragon ride?  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_event_8

Eydis：I don't hate the rain, but I could do without the humidity. Makes me wanna take off all my clothes.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_event_9

Eydis：I would wait for you always, even if you went far away from me. I know I would never forget you.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_event_10

Eydis：Let's go for a swim today! I made sure to get you a swimsuit, too! See?  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_event_11

Eydis：Our summer vacation may be over, but I'm not sad. I mean, I still get to see you in the fall!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_event_12

Eydis：Feeding the moon snacks is a funny custom, don't you think? Does Lunaria like cake?  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_event_13

Eydis：Look! There's a costumed person coming into the Cathedral! Oops, It's just the Prime Senator.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_event_15

Eydis：Today's the special day when the Integrity Knight in red flies in on his dragon sleigh to give us presents!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_season_1

Eydis：Gee, it's sure gotten warm. Now that winter's gone, Kirimai and her siblings are itching to fly.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_season_2

Eydis：Aw, why does food go bad so quickly in the summer? The pie I just bought is ruined...  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_season_3

Eydis：The farmers had a great harvest this year. And we have Terraria's generosity to thank!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_season_4

Eydis：No wonder it's cold... It's snowing! Hey, wouldn't it be nice to take a bath while watching the snow fall?  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_bd_2

Eydis：My birthday...? Sure doesn't feel like it. But I appreciate the gesture.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_bd_3

Eydis：It's Kirito's birthday today. Hey, let's throw him a party. A BIIIG party!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_bd_4

Eydis：Why don't you give Eugeo a present for his birthday? I'm sure he'd love anything.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_bd_5

Eydis：Today is the happiest day of the year. Why? Because it's your birthday! Of course it makes me happy!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_home_etc_1

Eydis：Good work out there! You handled that excellently, as always. But I won't let you outshine me!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_home_etc_2

Eydis：You've got a gift. Aww, it's okay to smile, you know? I'm sure it's something good.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_2nd_home_etc_3

Eydis：Your next assignment's arrived. Come on, let's get it done! I'll help.  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_swimsuit_wait_1

Eydis：It is HOT! I want to go for a swim so I took off my armor!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_swimsuit_touch_1

Eydis：Wh-What?  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_swimsuit_touch_2

Eydis：Hey! You gotta death wish or somethin'?  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_halloween_wait_1

Eydis：These ears catch your attention? Feel free to touch them!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_halloween_touch_1

Eydis：These ears catch your attention? Feel free to touch them!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_halloween_touch_2

Eydis：Hehehe... Watch out cuties, I'll eat you up!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_uw_2022_new_year_wait_1

Eydis：Just dropping by to wish you a happy new year! Hope you have a good one!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_uw_2022_new_year_wait_2

Eydis：MmmMmm! I've never had something this chewy before! Alice just HAS to try it!  

## 场景 novel.home_edith_uw_2022_new_year_touch_1

Eydis：How do I look? All dressed up with no where to go...unless you take me! Come on, let's go!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_wedding_1

Eydis：So this is clothing from the real world... A "dress," right? It's so cute!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_wedding_2

Eydis：Now you'll be able to make me your wife whenever you want. Then we can always be together!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_wedding_3

Eydis：If we lived together, what kind of house would you like? I'd definitely want lots of flowers.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_wedding_4

Eydis：What do you think the Commander'd say at the sight of us? Actually, we might get his blessing.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_wedding_5

Eydis：Why don't you try wearing it, too? Might be a bit hard to move, but it just makes me feel so stylish.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_wedding_6

Eydis：Wow, fancy making all these clothes I'd only get to wear once in a lifetime. The real world is so cool!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_wedding_7

Eydis：Well? How do I look?  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_wedding_8

Eydis：Sometimes I think just how lovely it'd be if you and I got married.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_wedding_9

Eydis：Come on, don't be shy! Look at me. I wanna have a look at you, too.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_wedding_1

Eydis：Easy, tiger! Once you cross that line, there's no turning back.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_wedding_2

Eydis：Your crystal-blue eyes and my scarlet orbs. Pretty nice combo, dontcha think?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_wedding_3

Eydis：Hey, don't do that unless you wanna get me worked up. Sheesh! You're just so forward.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_wedding_4

Eydis：Hey, come clean, you're actually pretty needy, aren't ya? It's okay. Come to Eydis.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_wedding_5

Eydis：Yeah, I can't help but wonder how these things were built. Go have a peek, okay?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_wedding_6

Eydis：Yeah, I heard in the real world, people exchange rings when they're engaged. Maybe we should, too?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_wedding_7

Eydis：It's okay. Be as needy as you want today. You'll make it up to me tomorrow.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_wedding_8

Eydis：The feel of your hand...it's so special. Completely unlike anything else.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_wedding_1

Eydis：Everything looks the same, but today is a very special day. Why? Well...because I'm with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_wedding_2

Eydis：Whew, I'm all sweaty! Let's go take a morning bath. Come on, please?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_wedding_4

Eydis：I just love these lazy moments with you. I feel like a queen! Wish every day could be like this.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_wedding_5

Eydis：Today, it's just gonna be you and me. All day. If someone comes along, I'm pushing 'em away!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_wedding_7

Eydis：We're having a feast tonight! A celebration, really. For you and me... For us.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_wedding_8

Eydis：Going to bed as I am always wrinkles my clothes...but oh well. Let's sleep together as-is!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_wedding_10

Eydis：I thought I was just having a good dream...but it wasn't a dream! You really are here with me.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_wedding_11

Eydis：I just can't sleep! How could I? When I'm with you, my heart just won't stop racing.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_edith_wedding_1

Eydis：Usually, I feel blah right after a vacation, but I'm energized today! Probably because I got to see you.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_edith_wedding_2

Eydis：A mission? Now? What lousy timing. Good thing I have you to help soothe my angry muscles.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_edith_wedding_3

Eydis：Hey, since we don't have to work today, wanna go flying? It'll be good for us and the dragons!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_edith_wedding_4

Eydis：Wait, isn't it too early to stop working? Did someone speed up time or something?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_wedding_1

Eydis：Happy New Year! Looking forward to lots of together-time with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_wedding_2

Eydis：I'm supposed to eat as many beans as my age, but I don't age, sooo... Should I go by how old I look?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_wedding_3

Eydis：So this is when winter solstice is celebrated in the real world. Don't worry, I've got some for you, too.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_wedding_4

Eydis：The sight of these tiny dolls makes me feel sentimental for some reason. I wonder why?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_wedding_5

Eydis：This thank-you gift custom must be a hassle for people who get a lot of candy. Like, say, Kirito...  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_wedding_6

Eydis：People in the real world made a holiday where it's okay to lie? Do they hate their Taboo Index, too?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_wedding_7

Eydis：I'd love to visit your hometown during our vacation. It sounds like it was a great place.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_wedding_8

Eydis：Guess we can't go out in the rain dressed like this. Nothing to it. Let's just stay inside.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_wedding_9

Eydis：Our prayers will be answered?! Oh, let's write ours together! What'll I pray for? Hehe. It's a secret.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_wedding_10

Eydis：If I go to the Summer Festival dressed like this, won't people think I'm an actor? Could be fun!  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_wedding_11

Eydis：I'm glad I get to spend the last day of vacation with you. I'd be bummed if I were alone.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_wedding_12

Eydis：Ooh, look! Lunaria is looking extra beautiful tonight, dontcha think?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_wedding_13

Eydis：I gave candy to some kids. They were so happy about it that I couldn't help but crack a smile.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_wedding_15

Eydis：Wanna try recreating that Krismis- tree thing with light elements and a giant pine? Think it might work?  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_edith_wedding_3

Eydis：You did get Kirito a present for his birthday, right? You can't let the other girls upstage you!  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_edith_wedding_4

Eydis：It's Eugeo's birthday, eh? Let's invite Kirito and some others and have a big party!  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_edith_wedding_5

Eydis：Happy Birthday! My life is way, waaay happier with you in it. I need to thank your parents.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_edith_wedding_6

Eydis：Asuna's birthday... She's a stylish lady, so maybe jewelry would be a good gift for her?  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_edith_wedding_7

Eydis：I'll bet even the Dark Realm will hold a big celebration for Leafa's birthday.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_edith_wedding_8

Eydis：Sinon would probably prefer an intimate gathering of friends over a big party.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_edith_wedding_11

Eydis：I figure Ronie would appreciate any gift, but maybe a ribbon for her long hair would be nice.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_edith_wedding_12

Eydis：So it's Tiese's birthday today. I heard Ronie was planning a party, so I'm gonna go help.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_edith_wedding_14

Eydis：Isn't Lisbeth a blacksmith? She'd probably love a tour of the Cathedral armory.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_edith_wedding_15

Eydis：Silica's dragon is so cute that I gave her some jewelry for it for her birthday.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_edith_wedding_1

Eydis：Good work today. Snuggle up to me and I'll make sure you rest well tonight.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_edith_wedding_2

Eydis：Something about opening presents is just so exciting! Hey hey, can I watch?  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_edith_wedding_3

Eydis：A new mission just came in. Too bad, I was having so much fun hanging out with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_1st_1

Eydis：What a beautiful day! Ooh, how about we feed Kirimai and Amayori some fish?  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_1st_2

Eydis：The Order sure has its fair share of pretty girls. You're number one, of course.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_1st_3

Eydis：Someday, when we have peace, I'd love to just fly off somewhere, with no destination in mind.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_1st_4

Eydis：I once met this really annoying girl in the Dark Territory. Wonder if I'll ever see her again.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_1st_9

Eydis：You're really cute when you get all serious with something on your mind.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_1st_1

Eydis：Isn't this hair ribbon cute? I've had it in my hair since the moment I was summoned.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_1st_2

Eydis：What delicate fingers... It just feels so soothing when you caress my hair.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_1st_4

Eydis：Armor makes me sweat, so I always want to strip it off as soon as possible.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_1st_5

Eydis：M-My, how assertive you are. Well, two can play at that game! Take this!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_1st_6

Eydis：People always want to cuddle when they're lonely... Hehe. It's okay, come closer.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_1st_1

Eydis：Good morning. Oh, you look so sleepy. Maybe a nice bath would wake you up?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_1st_4

Eydis：Been a while since we've had a day off together. Let's go on a picnic!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_1st_7

Eydis：I saw Solus pop behind a cloud to hide. That means tomorrow's sure to be auspicious!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_1st_10

Eydis：What a quiet night. It's actually the still nights that keep me awake.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_edith_1st_1

Eydis：I wonder if every day is a Day of Rest in Heaven? Hope not. That would get pretty boring.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_edith_1st_2

Eydis：You must be getting tired. You know the best remedy for that? Taking a bath with me!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_edith_1st_3

Eydis：On Days of Rest, I always make a lot of plans...then wind up just slacking off.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_edith_1st_4

Eydis：The night after a Day of Rest is always a little glum.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_edith_1st_1

Eydis：Another spring, another year gone by. Not that it matters when you're immortal like us.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_edith_1st_2

Eydis：Summer is all about swimming. Come take a dip with me! It's okay, don't be shy.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_edith_1st_3

Eydis：Seeing the wheat turn gold makes me so happy, knowing we'll have a comfortable winter.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_edith_1st_4

Eydis：I love watching it snow, but it's impossible to fly in. Too much brain freeze.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_nightwear_1

Eydis：Heyyy, come here for bit? Aw, come on.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_nightwear_2

Eydis：I didn't realize just how comforting body heat could be. Hehe, I could get used to this.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_nightwear_3

Eydis：Not doing anything and just... being...together. It's the most luxurious way to spend time.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_nightwear_4

Eydis：It's okay. You can be a little quieter. See? Can't you hear it?  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_nightwear_5

Eydis：Even up close, you're still so adorable. Fills me with joy just watching you.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_nightwear_6

Eydis：Sleeping with long hair is a bit of a pain. How do you handle it?  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_nightwear_7

Eydis：Oh, who cares about tomorrow? Today, it's just you and me. I want to savor this moment.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_nightwear_8

Eydis：I doubt I'll ever forget this day.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_nightwear_9

Eydis：Did you...fall asleep on me? Hehe. You're so cute when you're asleep.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_nightwear_1

Eydis：This is the reverse of what usually happens... But I love having you touch my hair.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_nightwear_2

Eydis：I rather like my hair color... But I still think yours is the prettiest.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_nightwear_3

Eydis：I'm not asleep yet. Don't worry. I'll stay up until you fall asleep.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_nightwear_4

Eydis：Did you have a bad dream? Aww, there, there. Let me hug away all your fears.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_nightwear_5

Eydis：My heart is racing right now... Do you hear it?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_nightwear_6

Eydis：Whenever you touch me, I feel so warm and fuzzy inside.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_nightwear_7

Eydis：Eek, that tickles! But...don't stop, okay?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_nightwear_8

Eydis：Give me your hand... Can we fall asleep holding hands like this tonight?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_nightwear_1

Eydis：Good morning... Did you sleep well?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_nightwear_2

Eydis：Seeing your cute face first thing in the morning gives me the energy to take on the day!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_nightwear_3

Eydis：Come on, can't we stay like this just a bit longer? Please? Just a li'l bit longer, I promise.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_nightwear_4

Eydis：I don't know how it happened, but...when I'm with you, I seem to lose track of time.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_nightwear_5

Eydis：It's no wonder you're hungry. But the bed's magic powers are restraining me...  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_nightwear_6

Eydis：Ahhh... All that lying down has made me sleepy again.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_nightwear_7

Eydis：It's still a bit early, but wanna just turn in?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_nightwear_8

Eydis：Lying in bed and talking to you like this is my favorite part of the day.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_nightwear_9

Eydis：I wish we could be in our own little world, sleeping together like this undisturbed every night.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_nightwear_10

Eydis：Can't sleep? Me too, actually. But I'm glad. Hehe.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_nightwear_11

Eydis：I know I should get some sleep, but I don't want to waste a moment together with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_nightwear_12

Eydis：Hey...come closer. Yes, closer... Snuggle right up against me.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_edith_nightwear_1

Eydis：Aw, our day off is over... Oh well, back to the grind! Thanks for recharging me.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_edith_nightwear_2

Eydis：That scouting mission tired me out! Please, could you let me... snuggle up to you? Please?!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_edith_nightwear_3

Eydis：Yay, it's a day off! Going out sounds fun and all, but I'm most excited just being with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_edith_nightwear_4

Eydis：Argh, time for work already. Feels like ages until my next day off.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_nightwear_1

Eydis：If the Vice Commander sees me sleeping through the New Year, she'll probably be very angry.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_nightwear_2

Eydis：Kirito showed me a demon mask... and it was very scary.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_nightwear_3

Eydis：Exchanging sweets like this is rather entertaining.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_nightwear_4

Eydis：That spinning paper lamp is called a "bonbori," apparently. It looks so mystical.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_nightwear_5

Eydis：Here, since I loved the gift you gave me so much, I thought I'd give a gift back.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_nightwear_6

Eydis：I could never lie to you, even if the Taboo Index permitted it.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_nightwear_7

Eydis：Since it's a long holiday, let's spend all of it together!  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_nightwear_8

Eydis：I actually really love dozing while listening to the patter of the rain like this.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_nightwear_9

Eydis：Even if you went far away, I would go visit you, and also eagerly await your return.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_nightwear_10

Eydis：On hot days like this, a bath is nice and all, but going for a swim feels much better.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_nightwear_11

Eydis：Since this is our last day off, I want to spend it together. Then I can conquer tomorrow.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_nightwear_12

Eydis：You can see the moon even all the way out here. So round and pretty...  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_nightwear_13

Eydis：Oh! You still have makeup on your face from your disguise. Sit tight, I'll wipe it off.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_nightwear_15

Eydis：They sneak in, leave presents, then vanish into the night... Must be an Integrity Knight.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_edith_nightwear_1

Eydis：Why does spring make me feel so sleepy? It's the season when everything comes to life.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_edith_nightwear_2

Eydis：Arrrgh, we're both so sweaty. Let's take a bath and refresh ourselves.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_edith_nightwear_3

Eydis：It's gotten a bit chilly. Makes getting out of bed a tad difficult.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_edith_nightwear_4

Eydis：Cold days like this remind me how fortunate I am to have a warm roof over my head.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_edith_nightwear_3

Eydis：It's Kirito's birthday? Then you'll have to get going! You'll lose if you get careless!  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_edith_nightwear_4

Eydis：Eugeo seems to like you. I'm sure he'd be happy to have you celebrate with him.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_edith_nightwear_5

Eydis：Happy birthday! Take it easy today, okay? I'll set up the party for you!  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_edith_nightwear_6

Eydis：Asuna's birthday, eh? I guess even pretend-goddesses have birthdays.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_edith_nightwear_7

Eydis：Leafa is Kirito's sister, right? Hehe. Ooh, if I had a cute sister I'd celebrate her every day.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_edith_nightwear_8

Eydis：It's Sinon's birthday, right? Let's figure out what we should give her.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_edith_nightwear_11

Eydis：I hear Ronie's celebrating her birthday with her family. Hehe, I'm kinda jealous.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_edith_nightwear_12

Eydis：I got invited to Tiese's birthday party. I wonder what I should give her...  

## 场景 novel.home_rank_edith_nightwear_1

Eydis：Is it just me, or did you get more muscly? Then again, I'm sure you can get even stronger.  

## 场景 novel.home_rank_edith_nightwear_2

Eydis：You sure grow fast. Guess I can't sit on my laurels.  

## 场景 novel.home_rank_edith_nightwear_3

Eydis：Is it just me, or have your shoulders gotten broader? Really ramps up your hero appeal.  

## 场景 novel.home_rank_edith_nightwear_4

Eydis：Wow, look how strong you've gotten while I wasn't looking... Hey, what's your secret?  

## 场景 novel.home_rank_edith_nightwear_5

Eydis：It's obvious just how strong you've gotten. Even I don't hold a candle to you now.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_edith_nightwear_1

Eydis：Good work on your mission. Let me reward you with a snuggle.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_edith_nightwear_2

Eydis：A present for you is probably in here. Wanna open it?  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_edith_nightwear_3

Eydis：Aw, my next mission is in. Can I please have a little hug for inspiration before I go? Squee!  

## 场景 novel.home_eldrie_vc.home_wait_101

Eldrie：By all odds, you will become an Integrity Knight and chase away those demons from the Dark Territory!  

## 场景 novel.home_eldrie_vc.home_wait_102

Eldrie：If you are knowledgable on the Sacred Arts of the Axiom Church, then you probably know a way to cure my mother's illness...  

## 场景 novel.home_eldrie_vc.home_wait_103

Eldrie：My father wanted me to continue serving in the military, but... I made up my mind to become an Integrity Knight.  

## 场景 novel.home_eldrie_vc.home_wait_104

Eldrie：I hope that you'll be able to sweep up that army from the Dark Territory and that people won't get sick like my mother...  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_wait_1

Eugeo：I feel like I've finally gotten the hang of this Blue Rose Sword.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_wait_2

Eugeo：You haven't changed a bit since we met. Haha, of course, I meant that as a compliment!  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_wait_3

Eugeo：That black sword suits you. With your black hair and clothing, black seems to be your color.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_wait_4

Eugeo：Somebody came by earlier, wanting to learn Aincrad Style. You think we could we teach her?  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_wait_5

Eugeo：I'd love to travel abroad someday. You definitely influenced me there.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_wait_6

Eugeo：Just how many attacks can you link together with Aincrad Style? I'd love to perfect a master attack someday.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_wait_7

Eugeo：I've become proficient in many Sacred Arts, so I hope I can help our village when we go back.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_wait_8

Eugeo：I do enjoy Centoria's hustle and bustle, but sometimes I miss Rulid's quiet forests.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_wait_9

Eugeo：If you're tired, you should tell me. No secrets between friends, okay?  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_touch_1

Eugeo：You say the word "chop" a lot when you touch my head... Is there some secret meaning behind that?  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_touch_2

Eugeo：Look, I know you're bored, but could you please stop playing with my hair?  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_touch_3

Eugeo：Hm? Was there something on my lip? Thanks for getting it.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_touch_4

Eugeo：Our sparring sure was intense today—now I'm sore all over. I imagine it's the same for you.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_touch_5

Eugeo：Yes, we both gained a little muscle. Though it's hard to notice when we see each other all the time.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_touch_6

Eugeo：If you're really that bored, maybe you should practice your Sacred Arts instead of poking me?  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_touch_7

Eugeo：Ah—sorry. Did you want something? I was just thinking... We've both come awfully far, huh?  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_touch_8

Eugeo：Stop, that tickles! Don't tickle me out of the blue! And no, warning me first won't absolve you either.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_time_1

Eugeo：Wait, are you going out for a morning practice? Haha, me too! Want to go together, then?  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_time_2

Eugeo：Back when I was a carver, I started the same time every morning, so I don't actually mind early mornings.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_time_3

Eugeo：Well, I tried to wake you up. Don't blame me if you fall asleep again and get up too late.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_time_4

Eugeo：So there you are! Taking a nap, huh? You sure sleep a lot.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_time_5

Eugeo：That early-morning practice sure got me hungry... Wanna go out to lunch?  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_time_6

Eugeo：The sky's so clear you can see far today. If we hadn't cut it down, maybe we could see the Gigas Cedar.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_time_7

Eugeo：How about letting me cook tonight? Believe it or not, I used to take care of my own meals back in Rulid.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_time_8

Eugeo：I love talking at night like this. Before I met you, I never knew it could be this much fun.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_time_9

Eugeo：Phew...I think I overindulged. I'm gonna go walk some of it off.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_time_10

Eugeo：Isn't it a little late for dinner? Gosh, when did you even get that? I hope there's enough for me.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_time_11

Eugeo：I used to be afraid of my dreams. I knew nothing but nightmares. But now...everything's different.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_time_12

Eugeo：You're going to hate yourself in the morning for staying up so late, you know? I'm guilty too, though.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_day_1

Eugeo：I actually kind of like watching everyone get back to business as usual after a day of rest.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_day_2

Eugeo：Time sure flies when you keep busy. I wish we had more time to just relax and chat.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_day_3

Eugeo：Even on my days off, I don't skip my training. I have to become stronger.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_day_4

Eugeo：Days of rest end all too soon, don't they? Though I guess you made the time fly.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_event_1

Eugeo：Happy New Year. I just know it will be a good one. We're starting it together, after all.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_event_2

Eugeo：If we're trying to expel evil spirits, we should probably throw the beans all the way out to the End Mountains.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_event_3

Eugeo：I bought these candies myself! They were neither a gift for you, nor were they from someone else!  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_event_4

Eugeo：I think praying for the happiness of girls is a beautiful custom. I hope the practice takes root here.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_event_5

Eugeo：Would it be too awkward to give someone a thank you gift if they never gave you a gift to begin with?  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_event_6

Eugeo：I won't be upset if you lie to me, but you shouldn't lie to others. Worst case, you'll be arrested.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_event_7

Eugeo：I never know what to do on long vacations... Wait, am I the only one?  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_event_8

Eugeo：Our spar in the rain was torture, wasn't it? I'm all sticky from it.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_event_9

Eugeo：Do you tie your wishes high in the tree so that Solus can see them?  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_event_10

Eugeo：I always think of Rulid when the Solstice Festival approaches. But Centoria's festival is much livelier.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_event_11

Eugeo：That long summer break might have weakened our muscles. Want to spar a little?  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_event_12

Eugeo：Staring up at the stars like this is pretty relaxing. I guess we have Lunaria to thank for that.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_event_13

Eugeo：Trick or treat... Is that another Aincrad saying? Well, I've never heard it before.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_event_15

Eugeo：Quietly leaving gifts for children... This Santa of yours sounds like a benevolent god.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_season_1

Eugeo：I bet the snow in Rulid is starting to melt. This is the time of year axe- swinging used to make me sweat.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_season_2

Eugeo：Training in the heat is hard, but the cold bath afterwards is downright heavenly.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_season_3

Eugeo：I've never heard the saying, "Autumn of Appetite" before, but I guess autumn does have many tasty foods.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_season_4

Eugeo：Ever since I started using the Blue Rose Sword, I've come to like the cold. I feel like it's a part of me.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_bd_2

Eugeo：I haven't celebrated my birthday much since receiving my Calling... so thank you. I appreciate it.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_bd_3

Eugeo：It's Kirito's birthday, huh? Since he's always pulling pranks, I'd love to give him a scare for once.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_bd_5

Eugeo：I want to give Alice a birthday gift, but I don't know what to get her... Please! Help me pick something!  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_home_etc_1

Eugeo：Good work on the assignment! But let's take a nice break before the next one.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_home_etc_2

Eugeo：You've got a present. Do you want to open it?  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_2nd_home_etc_3

Eugeo：And a new assignment has begun. I know you're up to the task, but still be careful out there, okay?  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_halloween_wait_1

Eugeo：If I don't get any treats...  I'll eat you right up!  Hehe, how does that sound?  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_halloween_wait_2

Eugeo：Isn't this costume scary?  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_halloween_touch_1

Eugeo：I'm giving treats out to everyone!  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_uw_2022_new_year_wait_1

Eugeo：Quite the rare outfit, huh? I dunno, I kinda like it. Makes me feel taller!  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_uw_2022_new_year_wait_2

Eugeo：I feel like this outfit would help me with my Sword kata.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_uw_2022_new_year_touch_1

Eugeo：I'm gonna go practice with it on.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_eugeo_1st_1

Eugeo：How did you get so good at the Sacred Tongue? May that's what your previous Calling was?  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_eugeo_1st_2

Eugeo：Could you spar with me again today? There's something I want to test out.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_eugeo_1st_3

Eugeo：Come to think of it, it feels like you and I have been together for ages now.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_eugeo_1st_4

Eugeo：I need to become stronger... So that I can protect what's precious to me from now on.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_eugeo_1st_9

Eugeo：Are you getting a bit tired? Let's take a break. I'll try loosening up those shoulders of yours!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_eugeo_1st_1

Eugeo：I wonder if I getting my hair short like Volo's will solve my bed hair situation?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_eugeo_1st_2

Eugeo：Hey, don't treat me like a child. You and I are practically the same age!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_eugeo_1st_4

Eugeo：Hmm...I think my pecs are just as strong as yours.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_eugeo_1st_5

Eugeo：Whoa there, what's wrong? You always seek attention when you want to talk.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_eugeo_1st_6

Eugeo：Well, I always guard my chest when I'm attacked. It's a vital area after all.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_eugeo_1st_1

Eugeo：Wow, I rarely see you up this early.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_eugeo_1st_4

Eugeo：It's getting late. What do you want for lunch? Want to eat with me?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_eugeo_1st_7

Eugeo：Just a bit more... I want to review everything I learned today so it sticks.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_eugeo_1st_10

Eugeo：Haha, it's pretty funny how we've woken up at the same time even though it's so late!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_eugeo_1st_1

Eugeo：We're starting a new class today. I can't wait to see what we're learning.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_eugeo_1st_2

Eugeo：Sometimes all the repetition makes me anxious... But I need to train to become stronger.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_eugeo_1st_3

Eugeo：On Days of Rest we're forbidden from swinging a sword... But maybe a practice staff doesn't count?  

## 场景 novel.home_day_eugeo_1st_4

Eugeo：Thanks to you, I had so much fun today that I'm inspired to do it all over again tomorrow.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_eugeo_1st_1

Eugeo：I'm always happier when you're around. It's like the sun finally coming out after a storm.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_eugeo_1st_2

Eugeo：On hot summer days, it's easy to lose track of time practicing and be surprised by nightfall.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_eugeo_1st_3

Eugeo：It's almost harvest time. When I lived in Rulid, all the villagers would help with the harvest.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_eugeo_1st_4

Eugeo：I don't mind the cold weather as long as it doesn't snow.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_eugeo_uw_sleeping_1

Eugeo：That reminds me, when we were caring for the horses in Zakkaria, we slept side by side like this.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_eugeo_uw_sleeping_2

Eugeo：Even when I lived in Rulid with my family, I always felt so alone. But now, I have you.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_eugeo_uw_sleeping_3

Eugeo：Maybe I always knew it would happen. That you would... change me for the better.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_eugeo_uw_sleeping_4

Eugeo：Hey, I hope you don't have mischief on the brain. You always do when you make that face.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_eugeo_uw_sleeping_5

Eugeo：I'll watch over you while you sleep. So stay in bed as long as you like.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_eugeo_uw_sleeping_6

Eugeo：Um...thank you. Er, well, I just felt like saying it. Wh-Who cares why!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_eugeo_uw_sleeping_7

Eugeo：Countless nights we've slept side- by-side like this. I'm shocked we haven't run out of stories to tell.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_eugeo_uw_sleeping_8

Eugeo：Ahh... Today sure was exhausting. Let's turn in early tonight so we can be well-rested for tomorrow.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_eugeo_uw_sleeping_9

Eugeo：Goodness, you always fall asleep so quickly. I'm starting to get jealous here.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_eugeo_uw_sleeping_1

Eugeo：When I was little, I remember having my hair tussled like this a lot... But wait, we hadn't met yet.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_eugeo_uw_sleeping_2

Eugeo：I know, it's getting long. I'd better cut it so it won't get in the way when I train.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_eugeo_uw_sleeping_3

Eugeo：Why are you touching my face? It can't be that amusing.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_eugeo_uw_sleeping_4

Eugeo：Have I bulked up a little? Golgorosso's training sessions really have been a godsend.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_eugeo_uw_sleeping_5

Eugeo：Don't worry, my wounds from that cave incident are gone now. I have you and Selka to thank for that.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_eugeo_uw_sleeping_6

Eugeo：Hey, stop! That tickles, you jerk! And I was just finally drifting off to sleep, too...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_eugeo_uw_sleeping_7

Eugeo：Whoa, there! Even I have boundaries and I'm not gonna let you cross them.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_eugeo_uw_sleeping_8

Eugeo：I hope you're not going to practice Serlut style on me.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_eugeo_uw_sleeping_1

Eugeo：Good morning. Come on now, get up or you'll be late.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_eugeo_uw_sleeping_2

Eugeo：You made me stay up too late last night...and I'm paying for it today.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_eugeo_uw_sleeping_4

Eugeo：Staying in bed all morning makes me feel a bit guilty... I think I'll go get a few swings in.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_eugeo_uw_sleeping_5

Eugeo：I've brought you some breakfast. You want to eat it in bed? Sheesh, you're like a spoiled child.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_eugeo_uw_sleeping_7

Eugeo：Are you prepared for the next Sacred Arts exam? I'm not bailing you out this time.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_eugeo_uw_sleeping_8

Eugeo：Whenever I sleep next to you, I have really strange dreams. Like...of us being kids together.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_eugeo_uw_sleeping_10

Eugeo：Sorry, did I wake you? I just had to practice everything Golgorosso taught me today.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_eugeo_uw_sleeping_11

Eugeo：When I look at the night sky, why do I always think of you? Is it because of your eyes and hair?  

## 场景 novel.home_day_eugeo_uw_sleeping_1

Eugeo：Wow, starting off the week by staying in bed all day? Now you're making it hard for me to get up.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_eugeo_uw_sleeping_2

Eugeo：Today was exhausting, wasn't it? I don't think I'll be able to get up once I lie down.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_eugeo_uw_sleeping_3

Eugeo：Do you have any plans to go out on the Day of Rest? Or would you rather just stay in?  

## 场景 novel.home_day_eugeo_uw_sleeping_4

Eugeo：I had a lot of fun today. And it's nice having someone to talk to while lying in bed.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_eugeo_uw_sleeping_1

Eugeo：Well, this is the first time I've ever rung in the new year while talking all night.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_eugeo_uw_sleeping_2

Eugeo：Does hanging fish heads in the doorway really ward off evil? I never know when you're joking.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_eugeo_uw_sleeping_3

Eugeo：February 14th is the day girls give boys candy, right? So why are you getting your hopes up?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_eugeo_uw_sleeping_4

Eugeo：It doesn't feel exciting as boys, celebrating a holiday for girls... but these snacks sure are tasty.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_eugeo_uw_sleeping_5

Eugeo：Oh, you have a thank you gift for me? Thanks... I'd better give you a thank you gift back later.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_eugeo_uw_sleeping_6

Eugeo：If I already know in advance that you're going to lie, I can't be fooled. So, go ahead.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_eugeo_uw_sleeping_7

Eugeo：I know that rest is a key part of training...but I still hate to waste such a long holiday resting.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_eugeo_uw_sleeping_8

Eugeo：When I used to be a carver, rainy season was always so dreary. I can still feel the slippery axe handle.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_eugeo_uw_sleeping_9

Eugeo：I'm supposed to write wishes on these papers? But...I don't want anyone else to see my wishes.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_eugeo_uw_sleeping_10

Eugeo：Solus's heat is brutal in the summer, but that's no excuse to slack on the training.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_eugeo_uw_sleeping_11

Eugeo：Summer vacation ends today... It feels like we wound up spending the whole vacation together.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_eugeo_uw_sleeping_12

Eugeo：I hear Lunaria is magnificent tonight. I don't usually stare at her, but I think I will tonight.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_eugeo_uw_sleeping_13

Eugeo：Trick or treat... Are you sure you didn't come up with this festival?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_eugeo_uw_sleeping_15

Eugeo：Snow is a hassle to deal with, but the sight of white on the ground makes me feel so calm.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_eugeo_uw_sleeping_1

Eugeo：Spring is when I met you and I'll never forget it. You're the one who made me the person I am.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_eugeo_uw_sleeping_2

Eugeo：You've worked up quite a sweat. You should probably rinse off before you get into bed.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_eugeo_uw_sleeping_3

Eugeo：Looks like there's an autumn storm coming. I hope everyone in Rulid will be okay.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_eugeo_uw_sleeping_4

Eugeo：Winters in Centoria aren't as cold as Rulid, so I guess we don't have to layer up so much.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_eugeo_uw_sleeping_2

Eugeo：Thanks for celebrating my birthday! I hope Alice can join us someday...  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_eugeo_uw_sleeping_3

Eugeo：Happy Birthday! I couldn't think of anything to give you, so do you want to go shopping?  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_eugeo_uw_sleeping_5

Eugeo：It's Alice's birthday... I remember it every year. The best I can do is wish her well from afar.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_eugeo_uw_sleeping_1

Eugeo：Have you finished your assignment? That was fast for you. Well, want to come hang out with me?  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_eugeo_uw_sleeping_2

Eugeo：You always get so many presents. It never ceases to amaze me.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_eugeo_uw_sleeping_3

Eugeo：Will you finish your assignment on time? You'd better get it done as soon as you can.  

## 场景 novel.home_fanatio_sweater_wait_1

Fanatio：There are many different kinds of strengths out there. You taught me that.   

## 场景 novel.home_fanatio_sweater_wait_2

Fanatio：Yes, women have a strength of their own.  

## 场景 novel.home_fanatio_sweater_touch_1

Fanatio：I've loved you ever since our first encounter...  

## 场景 novel.home_fanatio_sweater_touch_2

Fanatio：Whenever you pet my hair, I get so happy and can just fall asleep in your laps right there and then.  

## 场景 novel.home_fanatio_sweater_touch_3

Fanatio：Your hands feel so soft and warm. Can I touch them...?  

## 场景 novel.home_fanatio_sweater_touch_4

Fanatio：Even for the immortal Integrity Knights, we're just men and women when we take off our armor.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_wait_1

Kirito：We've gotten a bit too comfortable in this world. I don't know if we can handle the real world now.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_wait_2

Kirito：I wish we could've just experienced Aincrad normally instead of as a death game.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_wait_3

Kirito：Are you sure the esteemed Vice Commander of the KoB should be seen with a dirty beater like me?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_wait_4

Kirito：I don't let my guard down around anyone here—even people I know... But I can trust you.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_wait_5

Kirito：Hey, can you make me lunch again? I can't find anything here that tastes nearly as good as your cooking.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_wait_6

Kirito：So many people are good to me. The informant, the shopkeep, the blacksmith...and you.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_wait_7

Kirito：I'd resigned myself to being a solo player all this time, but I keep getting rescued...especially by you.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_wait_8

Kirito：If you've got time, do you want to go on a quest with me? It's too risky going alone.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_wait_9

Kirito：You look tired. I'm fine with taking another nap together, if you need it.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_touch_1

Kirito：When I started the game, my hair was actually down to my chin. Kind of embarrassed by that now.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_touch_2

Kirito：H-Hey...what's wrong? Why so close all of a sudden?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_touch_3

Kirito：Uh, won't we get in trouble if someone sees us like this? N-Not like I mind it or anything...  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_touch_4

Kirito：I like to build for agility. That's why I always pick the lightest armor possible.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_touch_5

Kirito：H-Hey, that tickles! A little warning, maybe?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_touch_6

Kirito：Y'know, I am a normal, healthy guy. So when you touch me like that... my mind goes somewhere else...  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_touch_7

Kirito：Don't get me wrong, I'm a huge fan of open communication. It's just... there's a time and place.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_touch_8

Kirito：Wait a minute, I hope you're not challenging me to a duel? I don't want to fight you.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_time_1

Kirito：Yawwwn... What time is it? I've got no plans today. Just lemme sleep in.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_time_2

Kirito：Oh, you brought me breakfast?! I'm always happy to eat what you've made!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_time_3

Kirito：Good morn...er, goodnight actually. I'm about to turn in. I was so focused on hunting, I couldn't stop...  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_time_4

Kirito：Mmm, what lovely weather. Just perfect for a nap! Wanna join me?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_time_5

Kirito：That alley is dangerous, even in daylight. I know too many people that never made it out.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_time_6

Kirito：The sun feels so good, even in VR. I wonder how they programmed it?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_time_7

Kirito：Going out of range at night is just too dangerous. If you're going out, I'm coming with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_time_8

Kirito：We sure got a great haul on today's hunt! This calls for a celebration. I know a tasty, cheap restaurant!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_time_9

Kirito：Sleepy already? But the night's still young! C'mon, let's talk some more.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_time_10

Kirito：Can't sleep? You should, though. It could mean the difference between life and death.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_time_11

Kirito：When it's night, my mind always conjures up bad memories. I try to stop them, but I just can't.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_time_12

Kirito：At the start, I used to wonder if I'd wake up in my own bed at home the next morning... I was so naive.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_day_1

Kirito：I guess one upside to being here is there's no school on Monday.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_day_2

Kirito：We've been in VR so long the days of the week no longer have meaning.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_day_3

Kirito：When I realize it's a holiday IRL, I always feel happy for some reason.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_day_4

Kirito：Not even staying up late on Sunday makes me feel guilty anymore... I don't think I can go back.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_event_1

Kirito：Happy New Year! Okay, this's the year we're finally making it out of this death game!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_event_2

Kirito：Setsubun doesn't seem to go with this world's aesthetic, but I guess I could picture a Samurai here.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_event_3

Kirito：So V-Day... Oh, no, I wasn't hinting at anything. It's just...are you sure you want to spend the day with me?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_event_4

Kirito：Hinamatsuri always makes me think of my sister. I hope she's doing okay.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_event_5

Kirito：Here's, uh...my thank-you gift. You know, since you gave me a Valentine, and all.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_event_6

Kirito：Thank heavens I finally met someone who won't play along on April Fools. Liars are everywhere today.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_event_7

Kirito：Long vacations are for grinding. So many high-reward quests and events to try out.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_event_8

Kirito：Who goes out of their way to program rainy season into a VR world? I wish they hadn't...  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_event_9

Kirito：I wonder if SAO has bamboo? If so, somebody could profit off a Tanabata event.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_event_10

Kirito：A swim in the lake sounds nice and all, but watch out for aquatic monsters, okay?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_event_11

Kirito：Even in a virtual world, summer's end still feels a little melancholy. I wonder why that is?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_event_12

Kirito：With this world as elaborately-built as it is, I wouldn't be surprised if the moon were also a field...haha.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_event_13

Kirito：Halloween... I was never really a fan IRL, but Halloween events in games are pretty fun.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_event_14

Kirito：This day always gives me flashbacks. Of the despair I felt being trapped and the determination to win.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_event_15

Kirito：I have a complicated history with Christmas...but I think it'll be a merry one, if I'm with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_season_1

Kirito：You know the saying, "In spring, one sleeps a sleep that knows no dawn?" Well, it's totally true.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_season_2

Kirito：I'm not a big bath-person, but I do appreciate a nice cold shower on a hot summer day.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_season_3

Kirito：With all these new restaurants popping up in Aincrad, we'll have our "Autumn of Appetite" after all.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_season_4

Kirito：I don't like winter, but I actually kinda like that first breath of crisp air in the morning.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_bd_2

Kirito：Aw, thanks! I didn't think anyone would remember my birthday... I'm really touched.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_bd_4

Kirito：It's Eugeo's birthday. I wonder, what kind of sword would he use in this world?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_bd_5

Kirito：It's Alice's birthday. Let's find a place with a great view and invite the gang to celebrate!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_bd_6

Kirito：It's Asuna's birthday. I got her a gift as a surprise... Hope I muster up the courage to give it to her.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_bd_7

Kirito：It's my sister's birthday today, but she's not here... Wanna celebrate with me anyway?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_bd_8

Kirito：Since this world doesn't have guns, what should I get Sinon for her birthday? What would she even like?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_bd_9

Kirito：It's Yuuki's birthday today, right? You should go be with her. I know she'd love that.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_bd_14

Kirito：It's Liz's birthday today, right? Want to go do something with her?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_bd_15

Kirito：It's Silica's birthday today. I want to get her a present, but I'm not sure what she'd like.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_home_etc_1

Kirito：You cleared the quest? That's great! What was your reward? Get anything good?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_home_etc_2

Kirito：You've got a present. Wanna open it and see what it is?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_2nd_home_etc_3

Kirito：Hey, somebody found a new quest! Let's go try it out right away!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_ggo_wizard_wait_1

Kirito：How in the world did I end up in this outfit...  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_ggo_wizard_wait_2

Kirito：Here I am... As a witch! ...Don't look at me like that!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_ggo_wizard_wait_3

Kirito：I wonder if my magic will double in power with my two-sword style.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_ggo_wizard_wait_4

Kirito：I can't seem to get a good footing. I wonder if my defense is also weak...  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_ggo_wizard_wait_5

Kirito：...I'm surprised nobody's recognized me. I was sure people would have started laughing as soon as I arrive into town...  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_ggo_wizard_wait_6

Kirito：...Whenever someone calls me cute, it's a mixed bag... It feels both frigid yet thrilling...  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_ggo_wizard_wait_7

Kirito：It's not fair that only I'm going through with this! Mark my words, Klein and Agil are next!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_ggo_wizard_wait_8

Kirito：Get a taste of this! Starburst Magical Stream!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_ggo_wizard_wait_9

Kirito：Hey! Don't leave me in this outfit!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_ggo_wizard_touch_1

Kirito：I think this hair accessory is cute. Of course not for me, but for an actual girl!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_ggo_wizard_touch_2

Kirito：Whenever someone pets my head in this outfit... I can't explain it, but I just feel...  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_ggo_wizard_touch_4

Kirito：S-Stop it! ...I can't do it... I can't do it anymore!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_ggo_wizard_touch_5

Kirito：Hey, hey! Don't even think about looking at my outfit like that! That's harassment, no?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_ggo_wizard_time_1

Kirito：Good morning! Kirito...the witch is here to wake up you sleepyheads!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_ggo_wizard_time_4

Kirito：Going into the battlefield in this outfit... At least give me a sword!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_ggo_wizard_time_7

Kirito：Having a meal with you makes me look more like a girl. What a weird turn of events.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_ggo_wizard_time_10

Kirito：I met a couple of people during the day in this outfit, but I wonder what they would say at night?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_wait_1

Kirito：Dressing like this in ALO is giving me a whole new perspective. Too bad I can't fly, though.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_wait_2

Kirito：There's a guy in Alphen's world who sounds like me. I'd kind of like to meet him.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_wait_3

Kirito：If Alphen's flaming sword can actually be used as a weapon, I'll bet everyone wants it.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_wait_4

Kirito：Alphen sure loves spicy food. Maybe I should take him on a spice tour.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_wait_5

Kirito：Feeling no pain is great in-game, but it would be horrible IRL. You'd keep walking on a broken leg.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_wait_6

Kirito：Alphen seems to be a real equipment junkie. He and Liz would probably get along great.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_wait_7

Kirito：When I first met Alphen, he scared me a little since his face is half-covered by a mask.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_wait_8

Kirito：There's a training hall in their world where you can practice sword fighting. I'd love to go.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_wait_9

Kirito：What's wrong? Is that new getup tiring you out? Then let's take a little break.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_touch_1

Kirito：I'm not used to dying my hair... Do I look weird?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_touch_2

Kirito：W-Whoa, there. Don't be so handsy. Alphen and Shionne are staring at us.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_touch_4

Kirito：The wolf on my shoulder sure looks cool!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_touch_5

Kirito：This seems like a weapon used with the Martial Art skill. It's so smooth, I actually like it.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_touch_6

Kirito：Is this design made of tails? These costumes are just full of wolf motifs—I love spotting them.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_time_1

Kirito：Now that it's a new day, my costume doesn't feel strange anymore. Now I can definitely fight.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_time_4

Kirito：Let's show Alphen and Shionne around Alfheim. This world sure has lots of pretty sights.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_time_7

Kirito：I'll have to take a rain check on spicy food today. Poor Shionne needs a break, too.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_time_10

Kirito：I saw Alphen and Shionne talking in hushed whispers late last night. I hope they make it home soon.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_day_1

Kirito：Do you think Alphen's world has days? I guess Mondays are a drag no matter where you're from.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_day_2

Kirito：Everything feels so fresh and fun when you're wearing a new outfit.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_day_3

Kirito：Good. It's finally vacation. Now I can spend the whole day helping Alphen and Shionne.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_day_4

Kirito：I hope we can manage to send Alphen and Shionne back to their world by next week.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_season_1

Kirito：Let's invite Alphen and Shionne on a Hanami picnic! We'll pack a sakura-themed lunch, of course!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_season_2

Kirito：Alphen always looks comfortable, no matter how hot it is. I wish I were him in the summer.   

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_season_3

Kirito：I hear their world is very woodsy. The views must be breathtaking in the fall.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_season_4

Kirito：Winters here are hard enough. But a world where it's always cold? Just the thought makes me shiver.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_home_etc_1

Kirito：Quest cleared! We're a step closer to getting Alphen and Shionne home!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_home_etc_2

Kirito：I think you've got a present. You should check it ASAP. It might be important.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_tales_home_etc_3

Kirito：I wonder what the next quest is? We should ask Alphen and Shionne to join us.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_alo_wedding_wait_1

Kirito：I can't stand still... Isn't there something a bit off?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_alo_wedding_wait_2

Kirito：...Ah, I'm sorry. I got a little nervous. I think it's because of this outfit.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_alo_wedding_wait_3

Kirito：Thank you for always being there by my side. Here's to more memories together.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_uw_2022_new_year_wait_1

Kirito：Japanese folks just love their Kimono. That's men and women alike! Makes you feel proper!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_uw_2022_new_year_wait_2

Kirito：Wanna go hatsumode? What's that? It's where you go pray at a shrine on new year's!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_uw_2022_new_year_wait_3

Kirito：Hagoita is harder than it looks. But I think I'm getting the hang of it? Let's play again!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_uw_2022_new_year_wait_4

Kirito：Heh. Drop the shuttle again and I'll paint your nose black!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_uw_2022_new_year_touch_1

Kirito：Not every day you get to wear a hat like this. Feels kinda like playing dress-up!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_uw_2022_new_year_touch_2

Kirito：My clothes are all messed up! Too much hagoita will do that though, I guess.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_kirito_1st_1

Kirito：We've been together for quite a while now. We're completely in sync.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_kirito_1st_2

Kirito：I wonder just how far beyond the End Mountains we can go? I'd love to head there someday.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_kirito_1st_3

Kirito：I never knew school could be this much fun! Or maybe it's just fun because you're here with me.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_kirito_1st_4

Kirito：I need to name my sword. But I'm having a hard time making a decision.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_kirito_1st_9

Kirito：Ahh... Being quiet like this sure makes me sleepy.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_kirito_1st_1

Kirito：Oh, do I have a cowlick again? Is it gone now?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_kirito_1st_2

Kirito：I wish I were a little taller. If I could use my Incarnation to grow, life would be so much easier.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_kirito_1st_4

Kirito：I think my muscles have gotten bigger. Wanna feel 'em?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_kirito_1st_5

Kirito：Oops, you heard my stomach growl? Yeah, I'm pretty hungry.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_kirito_1st_6

Kirito：Oh, you wanna rumble?! I'm always up for a good sparring session!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_kirito_1st_1

Kirito：Great, managed to get up nice and early today. It's not a bad thing every once in a while.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_kirito_1st_4

Kirito：Wow, noon already? Got caught up in my training... Didn't realize I was so hungry!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_kirito_1st_7

Kirito：When the night's quiet like this, all my old memories come back to me.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_kirito_1st_10

Kirito：Midnight snacks... Why are they so good? Come on, eat up!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_kirito_1st_1

Kirito：Let's put in another hard day's work and keep up that momentum through the rest of the week!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_kirito_1st_2

Kirito：I've got a lot on my plate, but I like to keep busy so my mind won't go to dark places.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_kirito_1st_3

Kirito：It's our day off, so let's take it easy. Lazy days are the best!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_kirito_1st_4

Kirito：It's sure hard to fall asleep the night after a day off. I'll pay for it tomorrow, though.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_kirito_1st_1

Kirito：I love seeing all the flowers in the spring. The sakura here in this world are just as pretty.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_kirito_1st_2

Kirito：Someday, I hope that I can go to the Summer Festival with Liena, Ronie, and the others.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_kirito_1st_3

Kirito：If you ask me, autumn is all about the feasting. Let's take a stroll and get some snacks!  

## 场景 novel.home_season_kirito_1st_4

Kirito：Winter skies sure are pretty. Probably because they really get dark at night.  

## 场景 novel.home_klein_vc.home_wait_101

Klein：I'm not the physical type to begin with, so it's nice to be able to move your body around in AR!  

## 场景 novel.home_klein_vc.home_wait_102

Klein：After this, I'll be going out for BBQ beef with a member of Fuurinkazan. Want to tag along?  

## 场景 novel.home_klein_vc.home_wait_103

Klein：Hey, I'll bring out the car! Let's go do a Boss Battle again!  

## 场景 novel.home_klein_vc.home_wait_104

Klein：Wait up! Our member isn't here yet.  

## 场景 novel.home_klein_alo_2022_new_year_wait_1

Klein：New year's ain't over yet! C'mon. Let's have a glass or two. You can drink this, right?  

## 场景 novel.home_klein_alo_2022_new_year_wait_2

Klein：Stripes are good luck right? Gonna make me look even cooler, I bet.  

## 场景 novel.home_klein_alo_2022_new_year_wait_3

Klein：Here's to the new year! And may we attack it with the fury of fuurinkazan!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_wait_1

Leafa：No turbulence for me, thank you. I'd much rather have a looong stretch of peace like this.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_wait_2

Leafa：VR games come in all sizes, but nothing beats the feeling of flying in ALO!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_wait_3

Leafa：C'mon, let's race! Last one to the opposite edge of the forest loses!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_wait_4

Leafa：You really do love to wear black, eh? A little color wouldn't kill you. Come on, let's dress you up!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_wait_5

Leafa：Here we are, together in VR while our bodies are also together at home... Wild, right?  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_wait_6

Leafa：Don't forget, we're having dinner with the parents today, so we're logging out together when it's time!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_wait_7

Leafa：Maybe I should put more points in my cooking skills, too? 'Cause... I'd love for you to eat my cooking.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_wait_8

Leafa：With the speed limit gone, I can fly much faster than before. Awesome update, right?!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_wait_9

Leafa：Are you tired? Let's go back to the real world, lie down, and eat junk food!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_touch_1

Leafa：Is the big ribbon a bit babyish? Well? What do you think? Do I look like an adult?  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_touch_2

Leafa：Ooh, more pats, please... Hey, you started it. You gotta see it through until I'm satisfied.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_touch_3

Leafa：One of the great perks of VR is not needing to worry about acne! I can eat all the chocolate I want.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_touch_4

Leafa：D-Dude! I know we're in ALO and all, but hands off!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_touch_5

Leafa：Hard to believe, I know, but they're actually a bit smaller in this world. It's much easier to move here.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_touch_6

Leafa：Hey, keep that up much longer, and you're gonna give me ideas. When we're IRL, it's payback time.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_touch_7

Leafa：Hey, I'm not a little girl anymore— so don't touch me like that! Unless... that was a grown-up touch?  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_touch_8

Leafa：So, do you do this to others? I'll cut you some slack, but the others might not be so forgiving.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_time_1

Leafa：Good morning—up early today, I see. Why don't you come practice some kendo with me!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_time_2

Leafa：I just love the morning air, don't you? Nice and chilly from the night— really clears my head!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_time_3

Leafa：Ah-hah! I knew you'd still be in bed! Come on, get up. You're going on that quest with me today.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_time_4

Leafa：I have a hard time practicing my kendo forms at school, so I was thinking of trying it here.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_time_5

Leafa：Aincrad is nice and all, but don't you want to go above ground? There's some pretty fun places.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_time_6

Leafa：Nice clear days like this just make me wanna fly straight up to the sun!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_time_7

Leafa：Logging into the game in the morning when it's night in-game sure throws off my internal clock.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_time_8

Leafa：Boy, do I love the nightscape in ALO! It's so magical and romantic.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_time_9

Leafa：The day time is nice, but the night sky sure gives flying a whole new level of fun.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_time_10

Leafa：Gaming? This late at night? Wait, I guess I shouldn't be the one to lecture you! Haha.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_time_11

Leafa：This world always makes me open up and say what's on my mind... especially late at night.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_time_12

Leafa：Wanna go see The World Tree with me? It's so pretty at night, that...c-couples like to go there.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_day_1

Leafa：I have a bad case of the Mondays... I wish they'd at least start class an hour later or something.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_day_2

Leafa：I can't do kendo at school anymore without thinking, "Ooh! I could use this in my next battle..."  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_day_3

Leafa：We haven't had time off in a while. Huh? It has sooo been a while! I've been waiting all week for this.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_day_4

Leafa：I never want Sundays to end, so I always stay up super late... even though I'll regret it on Monday.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_event_1

Leafa：Happy New Year! Let's make it a good one. With our friends and family alike.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_event_2

Leafa：Oh, shoot! Mom asked me to buy beans for Setsubun and I totally forgot! I'd better go now!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_event_3

Leafa：Here's some Valentine's chocolate! But I'll have some "real" chocolate for you when we get home.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_event_4

Leafa：I wish we could keep Hinamatsuri dolls up all year, instead of putting them away the next day.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_event_5

Leafa：Thanks for the White Day gift. Wanna share it with me? C'mon, I wanna eat it together.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_event_6

Leafa：Don't use April Fools' Day as an excuse to prank me when we get home. I don't wanna freak out.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_event_7

Leafa：It's a long vacation—time to party! I don't have kendo practice either, so I'll be free as a bird!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_event_8

Leafa：I hate wearing my kendo uniform in this humidity. I get sticky with sweat in like five minutes.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_event_9

Leafa：Do you remember what you wished for on Tanabata when you were little? Well...I do.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_event_10

Leafa：I miss our family trips to the beach. Plus, I think we'd have even more fun, now that we're older!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_event_11

Leafa：Summer vacation's almost over. Sucks, right? But at least we had plenty of fun times this year.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_event_12

Leafa：This just might be the first time we've moon-gazed together like this. Heehee...just you and me.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_event_13

Leafa：Where do you think they sell huge pumpkins like that? I'd love to wear one on my head at least once.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_event_15

Leafa：I get to eat Christmas cake in-game and at home, I've got a pretty tree— what'd I do to deserve such bliss?  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_season_1

Leafa：Forests in the spring are just as pretty in this world, aren't they? So many fragrant flowers.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_season_2

Leafa：It's been so hot lately that my kendo teacher stopped yelling at us for drinking water during practice.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_season_3

Leafa：In Japan, we often say "Autumn of Reading" or "Autumn of Appetite," but what's autumn to you?  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_season_4

Leafa：Blerrrg. If it keeps freezing so hard, I'll never want to go back to the real world. It's nice and warm here.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_bd_2

Leafa：Alone with you on my birthday? What a treat! Quality time with you... That's the best gift I could ask for.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_bd_3

Leafa：I'm gonna cook you a huge feast for your birthday. Cake and all— homemade, of course!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_bd_5

Leafa：It's Alice's birthday today, right? You should invite her over again! Mom and Dad would love that.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_bd_6

Leafa：Did you make sure to get Asuna a birthday present? Come on, please say you did...  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_bd_8

Leafa：I'm thinking of getting Sinon some weapon accessories for her birthday. She said she was looking for some.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_bd_9

Leafa：For Yuuki's birthday, let's round up the Sleeping Knights and throw a big bash!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_bd_14

Leafa：For Liz's birthday, we're gonna have a potluck. Don't forget to bring something!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_bd_15

Leafa：Since Silica's pigtails are so cute, I'm gonna get her some bows. I found some really pretty ones!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_home_etc_1

Leafa：Good job, trooper! That quest sure took a while. So just take it easy today, you hear?  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_home_etc_2

Leafa：A gift just came for you! Hurry and open it before it's too late!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_2nd_home_etc_3

Leafa：A new quest just started, so we'd better jump on it! I don't wanna be left in the dust.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_wait_1

Leafa：Dang, Kirito, you're always surrounded by eccentric folks. But I'm used to it by now.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_wait_2

I hear this costume is worn by magic users in Shionne's world.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_wait_3

Poor Shionne. Imagine having a thorn curse and not being able to touch the one you love...  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_wait_4

Shionne and I are gonna learn how to cook together. She wants to take our recipes back home with her.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_wait_5

Shionne and Alphen are Dahna and...Rena? Apparently they're both human, but different races.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_wait_6

Shionne's magic...Astral Artes, was it? It's so amazing. She can blast flying foes out of the sky.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_wait_7

We just have to get those two back to their world! You and I have to do it together, Kirito!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_wait_8

If I were blasted away to another world...maybe I'd be okay as long as you were with me.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_wait_9

Are you okay? I know, you don't usually dress like that. Why don't we take a little break?  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_touch_1

Leafa：Hee-hee, I think this's the first time I've used a hair decoration like this. Well? Is it me?  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_touch_2

Leafa：H-Hey! I know this is all exotic and new and stuff, but don't touch me like that...you startled me.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_touch_4

Leafa：What a cute outfit. It just screams “mage," doesn't it? Think I'd look good with a book of spells?  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_touch_5

Leafa：S-So I get that you're curious about my costume, but please don't touch me there...  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_touch_6

Leafa：This costume is so light. I feel like I'm just gonna float away!  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_touch_7

Leafa：Yeah, it's a butterfly motif. This is so cool! I never see equipment like this here.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_time_1

Leafa：When I woke up I was like, "Why am I dressed like this...?"  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_time_4

Leafa：Good morning, Kirito. Uh...it's me. Leafa. Don't you recognize me?! Jeez. All I did was change clothes.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_time_7

Leafa：Hey, Kirito, where are the good restaurants here? I want to take Shionne out to eat.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_time_10

Leafa：I know it's weird timing, but I'm glad I can share this event just with you. I feel really special.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_day_1

Leafa：Shionne asked me why I was so frazzled. Think she'd get it if I told her it was Monday?  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_day_2

Leafa：Another day, another fight to find a way to get Shionne and Alphen home! Let's get to it, Kirito.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_day_3

Leafa：Do you think Shionne's world has weekends? What if they didn't...? Man, that would suck.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_day_4

Leafa：Shionne thanked me for showing her around today. I'm happy to help.
