## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_season_1

Leafa：It's so nice and warm today. Let's take Shionne and Alphen on a picnic.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_season_2

Leafa：Y'know, this outfit is much more breathable than I expected. Might be good for the summer.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_season_3

Leafa：When I told Shionne autumn has good food, she got so excited. Guess she's a closet foodie.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_season_4

Leafa：They say their world has an area that's winter all year round. Maybe it's like the North Pole?  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_home_etc_1

Leafa：Congrats on clearing the mission! We'd better thank Shionne and Alphen for helping out.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_home_etc_2

Leafa：You've got a present. Will ya show it to me? C'mon, please?  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_tales_home_etc_3

Leafa：Hey, the next mission just came in. We'd better check it out, stat. Shionne and Alphen need our help.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_alo_swim_wait_1

Leafa：I wonder if I just practiced... I'd be able to swim, too?  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_alo_swim_touch_1

Leafa：I said I can't swim! So don't be making fun of me.  

## 场景 novel.home_leafa_alo_swim_touch_2

Leafa：Ack! H-Hey Kirito! Careful there...that just hit me!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_leafa_1st_1

Leafa：Come on, let's practice together for a change! It'll be fun, I promise.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_leafa_1st_2

Leafa：Maybe I should get a motorcycle license, too. Then we could ride together!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_leafa_1st_3

Leafa：You always overwork yourself and it seriously worries me. You should let me help out more.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_leafa_1st_4

Leafa：I really do think I prefer a world where you can fly.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_leafa_1st_9

Leafa：Hellooo? Are you awake? If you're tired, you should really let yourself rest.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_leafa_1st_1

Leafa：Jeez, why do you always have to treat me like that? I'm not a baby anymore, you know.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_leafa_1st_2

Leafa：Since my hair's short IRL, I like having it long in VR.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_leafa_1st_4

Leafa：Eek! J-Jeez... Seriously. Can you stop doing that?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_leafa_1st_5

Leafa：Oh, is it dirty? Unless...you touched it even though it's not dirty?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_leafa_1st_6

Leafa：Hm, you called me? Sit tight, just gonna get ready.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_leafa_1st_1

Leafa：I've gotten so used to morning practice that I always just wake up early.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_leafa_1st_4

Leafa：What do you want to do for lunch today? Wanna eat together for a change?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_leafa_1st_7

Leafa：Oooh, the stars are so pretty! Look! It's a shooting star!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_leafa_1st_10

Leafa：Whenever I wake up in the middle of the night, I always feel safe...when I see you.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_leafa_1st_1

Leafa：Yeah, Mondays wouldn't be so hard on you if you didn't party so hard on Sundays!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_leafa_1st_2

Leafa：I'm so busy with my after-school club that I can't log in much on weekdays.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_leafa_1st_3

Leafa：Weee, finally a day off! Got any plans today? I'm totally free!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_leafa_1st_4

Leafa：I had a lot of fun today. I'd better turn in early so I can wake up early again tomorrow.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_leafa_1st_1

Leafa：Now that it's warmer, training at the dojo has finally gotten a bit tough.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_leafa_1st_2

Leafa：When it's summer you have to make sure to stay hydrated! Especially while you're gaming.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_leafa_1st_3

Leafa：It's autumn, and the roasted sweet potatoes are calling to me... Maybe a li'l one as a treat after practice.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_leafa_1st_4

Leafa：Let's have hot pot today. Nothing warms the soul quite like hot pot with good company!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_leafa_alo_sleeping_1

Whenever I try to talk to Sakuya, she cuts me off and insists I bring you to Sylph territory.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_leafa_alo_sleeping_2

I actually go adventuring with the others a lot. Wanna come play with us sometime?  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_leafa_alo_sleeping_3

I sure wish I could've seen you in SAO... Though I do love your spiky hair here.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_leafa_alo_sleeping_4

Whenever we sleep next to each other like this, I feel like we're little kids again.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_leafa_alo_sleeping_5

Hey, can I scoot closer to you? My shoulders are kinda cold, you see...  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_leafa_alo_sleeping_6

Parties with different fairy races used to be a pretty rare sight around here, but not anymore.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_leafa_alo_sleeping_7

I think I'll buy myself a house in this world. And when I do, you're coming to visit, okay?  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_leafa_alo_sleeping_8

This feels so...real. Like we're not inside a game. You feel just like my brother IRL.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_leafa_alo_sleeping_9

Are you sleepy? You always were a sleepyhead, even IRL.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_leafa_alo_sleeping_1

Leafa：I rock the blond look, don't I? I'm not brave enough to dye it IRL, but I'm cool with it in-game.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_leafa_alo_sleeping_2

Leafa：Can I touch your hair now? I'm kind of really digging the spikes.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_leafa_alo_sleeping_3

Leafa：Ooh, do you like my ribbon? Maybe you should wear it— I think you could pull it off.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_leafa_alo_sleeping_4

Leafa：I wish I could have a slimmer body in-game. I already have to deal with these curves IRL...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_leafa_alo_sleeping_5

Leafa：Hey, I know we're in-game, but isn't that out of line? I mean, two can play at that game...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_leafa_alo_sleeping_6

Leafa：One thing I love about gaming is I can play with fashion without worrying about my body type.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_leafa_alo_sleeping_7

Leafa：Hey...I know we're in-game, but I can still feel you touching me. Jeez...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_leafa_alo_sleeping_8

Leafa：If you take this any further... then...I really will grab you and never let go. Got it?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_leafa_alo_sleeping_1

It's morning in this world, but IRL it's...evening, I think? So we can stay a bit longer...  

## 场景 novel.home_time_leafa_alo_sleeping_2

I just love snuggling in bed as the morning rays caress me... It's just, pure bliss.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_leafa_alo_sleeping_4

Aw, it's gotten pretty bright out. What do you wanna do? Guess we'd better get up.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_leafa_alo_sleeping_5

Is anyone else coming today? If not...then maybe I'll just sleep in with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_leafa_alo_sleeping_7

It's so dark and quiet...but I have nothing to fear because you're here with me.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_leafa_alo_sleeping_8

It's still a bit early, but wanna just turn in? We can always talk until we get sleepy.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_leafa_alo_sleeping_10

I just can't sleep... Hey, wanna go flying? The sky is a totally different beast at night.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_leafa_alo_sleeping_11

Are you still awake? Then...talk to me. We can talk about our childhood memories and stuff.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_leafa_alo_sleeping_1

Leafa：Look at me, starting my week off with ALO... I guess I really am your little sister.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_leafa_alo_sleeping_2

Leafa：Wanna go out for a bit? It's a bit of a waste spending all our time in this world sleeping.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_leafa_alo_sleeping_3

Leafa：Wanna just chill here in this world during vacation? I'd be cool with that.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_leafa_alo_sleeping_4

Leafa：Well, Sunday's over—guess I'll head back to the real world. Though I'd rather stay here...  

## 场景 novel.home_event_leafa_alo_sleeping_1

Leafa：Happy New Year! Looking forward to lots of quality time with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_leafa_alo_sleeping_2

Leafa：With that hairstyle, you make a perfect demon. Haha, it looks like demon horns.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_leafa_alo_sleeping_3

Leafa：I get to spend Valentine's Day alone with you. I'm such a lucky girl...  

## 场景 novel.home_event_leafa_alo_sleeping_4

Leafa：I always feel a bit glum when Hinamatsuri's close. It also means graduation and friends leaving.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_leafa_alo_sleeping_5

Leafa：I wonder what you're gonna get me for White Day? Teeheehee, I can't wait to see.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_leafa_alo_sleeping_6

Leafa：Everyone always falls for your April Fool's pranks. So I'm actually looking forward to it.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_leafa_alo_sleeping_7

Leafa：We're far apart IRL, but we get to be together when we game... I'm so glad I took up ALO.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_leafa_alo_sleeping_8

Leafa：In this world, I can even hear the sound of the rain hitting the leaves! It's so cool.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_leafa_alo_sleeping_9

Leafa：Did you hear? There's a Tanabata event where you tie wishes to The World Tree. Haha, sounds fun.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_leafa_alo_sleeping_10

Leafa：Remember how we used to build sand castles when we were little? Maybe we could build them here, too.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_leafa_alo_sleeping_11

Leafa：I hope that we can spend some quality time together even after summer vacation is over.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_leafa_alo_sleeping_12

Leafa：I hear you can get a good view of the moon in ALO, too. Let's go outside and check it out.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_leafa_alo_sleeping_13

Leafa：Autumn has so much yummy stuff to eat! So I'm gonna go light on the candy...for my figure.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_leafa_alo_sleeping_14

Leafa：I was a little worried when that castle showed up in ALO. Thought it might trigger you...  

## 场景 novel.home_event_leafa_alo_sleeping_15

Leafa：I just love Christmas. Everyone's so happy and full of life.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_leafa_alo_sleeping_3

Leafa：Happy Birthday! The others should show up soon, but can I give you a present first?  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_leafa_alo_sleeping_5

Leafa：I wish we could celebrate Alice's birthday in Underworld.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_leafa_alo_sleeping_6

Leafa：We're throwing Asuna a birthday party tonight at your lodge, right?  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_leafa_alo_sleeping_8

Leafa：It's Sinon's birthday today! We should probably throw her a party in GGO.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_leafa_alo_sleeping_9

Leafa：It's Yuuki's birthday today, huh. I'd love to watch her sword fight IRL sometime and take notes.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_leafa_alo_sleeping_11

Leafa：What're you getting Ronie for her birthday? No funny business, okay?  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_leafa_alo_sleeping_12

Leafa：I think a ribbon for Tiese's pretty hair would be just the thing for her birthday.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_leafa_alo_sleeping_14

Leafa：I guess Liz would probably like blacksmithing tools for her birthday.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_leafa_alo_sleeping_15

Leafa：Silica would rather we get Pina a gift. Maybe we should get Pina a ribbon?  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_leafa_alo_sleeping_1

Leafa：Congrats on clearing the quest! So, what was your reward? Can I see it?  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_leafa_alo_sleeping_2

Leafa：Hey, I hope you aren't letting gifts pile up in the Reward Box. You need to check it daily.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_leafa_alo_sleeping_3

Leafa：Did you hear about the next quest? It sounds like fun—so let's do it together!  

## 场景 novel.home_lisbeth_vc.home_wait_101

Lisbeth：Don't you know that you can get points from a variety of shops? We gotta get them before we miss out!  

## 场景 novel.home_lisbeth_vc.home_wait_102

Lisbeth：This will certainly come in handy. Plus, we can talk to Yui!  

## 场景 novel.home_lisbeth_vc.home_wait_103

Lisbeth：Whaaat is it? Are you really that bad at AR combat?  

## 场景 novel.home_lisbeth_vc.home_wait_104

Lisbeth：Alright! Let's show Yuna what we're made of!  

## 场景 novel.home_lisbeth_alo_swim_wait_1

Lisbeth：Hmmm... What do you think? Not too shabby, right?  

## 场景 novel.home_lisbeth_alo_swim_touch_1

Lisbeth：Hey, HEY! Don't treat people like kids!  

## 场景 novel.home_lisbeth_alo_swim_touch_4

Lisbeth：C-Come on now... Don't scare me like that...  

## 场景 novel.home_lisbeth_alo_2022_new_year_wait_1

Lisbeth：Happy New Year! Let's make it a good one.   

## 场景 novel.home_lisbeth_alo_2022_new_year_wait_2

Lisbeth：If you're blaming your laziness on New Year's, I'll bite-cha! ...Just kidding!  

## 场景 novel.home_lisbeth_alo_2022_new_year_touch_1

Lisbeth：If you pet my head, you might get something great in return! Go on, pet my head!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_lisbeth_1st_1

Lisbeth：I trust you, but just try not to worry everyone else, okay?  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_lisbeth_1st_2

Lisbeth：Mmm, the sound and feel of hitting this metal... Ahhh, it's the best feeling in the world!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_lisbeth_1st_3

Lisbeth：Even VR-world items have souls... I learned that from you.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_lisbeth_1st_4

Lisbeth：Haven't seen you around my smithy lately. You should visit sometime.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_lisbeth_1st_9

Lisbeth：Hey. What're you looking at? Eyes up here, pal.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_lisbeth_1st_1

Lisbeth：Why are you patting my head? Is that your way of telling me you want your head patted, too?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_lisbeth_1st_2

Lisbeth：I'll always have wild hair. Though I used to really envy girls with silky locks.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_lisbeth_1st_4

Lisbeth：I remember that one time... where we felt each other's warmth like this.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_lisbeth_1st_5

Lisbeth：Stop! That's against the rules! I am but your exclusive blacksmith. Don't you forget that!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_lisbeth_1st_6

Lisbeth：Eek! Jeez... Can't turn my back on you for a second!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_lisbeth_1st_1

Lisbeth：Have you eaten yet? What? You haven't? You should go eat something.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_lisbeth_1st_4

Lisbeth：You just caught me during some downtime. Let me do a little maintenance on your weapon.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_lisbeth_1st_7

Lisbeth：I need to close up shop soon. You look bored. Here, help me take down the sign.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_lisbeth_1st_10

Lisbeth：I always get some of my best ideas at night. Maybe the quiet air does me good?  

## 场景 novel.home_day_lisbeth_1st_1

Lisbeth：Thanks to a fun day off, I'll have a nice productive workweek ahead.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_lisbeth_1st_2

Lisbeth：I don't have much to do this week. Perhaps I should do some advertising...  

## 场景 novel.home_day_lisbeth_1st_3

Lisbeth：You're free this weekend, right? Come excavating with me. There's this material I want.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_lisbeth_1st_4

Lisbeth：Tomorrow's Monday, but I think I'll stay up just a bit longer... Come on, talk with me.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_lisbeth_1st_1

Lisbeth：I do love seeing the cherry blossoms in spring. Let's make a plan to go see them.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_lisbeth_1st_2

Lisbeth：Blacksmithing on a hot summer day? Just the idea of it makes me faint.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_lisbeth_1st_3

Lisbeth：Silica and I just ate some really yummy fruit. I'm sure you'd like it, too.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_lisbeth_1st_4

Lisbeth：I used to sword fight with icicles when I was a kid. Made my hands numb, though.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_1

Get that creepy look out of your eyes, pal. It's not what you think.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_2

It's not my fault we have to snuggle up close! It's a tiny bed!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_3

Reminds me of that night we fell into the dragon's pit. Remember? We held hands...  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_4

You are always so warm. You're like a living space heater.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_5

I'm gonna make some armor as well as weapons this time, so I've gotta take your measurements.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_6

My shop is closed today. So it's cool if I just take it easy.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_7

Even after we make it out of here, I'm still gonna be your personal blacksmith. Don't you forget that.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_8

The first time I broke a sword, I totally freaked out. But now, it's a happy memory.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_9

Hey. Could you maybe not... Like, fall asleep so quickly and be so damn irresistibly cute?!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_1

Lisbeth：Whenever you pat my head, I can't talk straight... Just like that one time.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_2

Lisbeth：H-Hey! A girl's hair is her life! You get a pass, but...I'm not letting anyone else touch it.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_3

Lisbeth：S-Stop it! My face is probably red as a tomato right now!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_4

Lisbeth：D-Dude! Not cool! Why? What do you mean, why?! Anyway...just stop it!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_5

Lisbeth：Hey, even I dress like this when I go to bed. Does it look...weird?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_6

Lisbeth：H-Hey! Wait a minute... Are you half-asleep? F-Fine... Guess I'll let this one slide.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_7

Lisbeth：If you keep that up much longer, I just might... Well, girls have urges, too, you know.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_8

Lisbeth：Back at that cave, we held hands like this too, remember? Of course you remember, come on.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_1

Getting up already? Come on, let's sleep a bit longer.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_2

I've gotta get up and set up the shop...but...this bed's magic is too powerful...  

## 场景 novel.home_time_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_4

I always feel restless this time of day unless I'm holding my hammer... Guess I'll get up.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_5

Aren't you getting hungry? I'm gonna make something light. You'll have a bite too, right?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_7

Another hard day's work in the books. Good work! I'm really glad you got back safe.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_8

Lying in bed together with you like this... It feels kinda... awkward? I guess.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_10

Oh...can't sleep? Well, not like I can talk.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_11

When it gets quiet like this, I'm reminded it really is just you and me and... No way can I sleep.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_1

Lisbeth：Better bring it today. You never want to slack off on Mondays— it ruins the whole week.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_2

Lisbeth：I've been so inspired by black- smithing lately. I think...I might have you to thank for that.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_3

Lisbeth：I used to think weekends were meaningless in-game, but work-life balance really is important, huh?  

## 场景 novel.home_day_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_4

Lisbeth：I had so much fun with you today. If you want to do that again, I'm always up for it.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_1

Lisbeth：Happy New Year! Since it's a special day, it's cool if you want to give me New Year's money!  

## 场景 novel.home_event_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_2

Lisbeth：I thought I'd make a demon-shaped helmet for Setsubun, so... Huh? What do you mean, it won't sell?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_3

Lisbeth：This' just a little valentine for being good in a fight! And y-you know, because I...want to...  

## 场景 novel.home_event_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_4

Lisbeth：It'd be fun if we could set up a Hina doll shrine in this world, huh.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_5

Lisbeth：What's this? A White Day present for me? Oh, thanks... That's really sweet of you!  

## 场景 novel.home_event_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_6

Lisbeth：Call me pretty all you want. I won't believe a word of it today. Tell me tomorrow instead.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_7

Lisbeth：Golden Week's a gold mine, isn't it? Everyone's gonna need new armor after their quests.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_8

Lisbeth：I can hear the thunder, but since you're with me...I just might not be that scared.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_9

Lisbeth：I guess it's okay if I don't make a wish this year. Since I'm content the way things are.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_10

Lisbeth：I can hear the festival noises. There's something really cozy about hearing them from indoors.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_11

Lisbeth：I used to hate going back to school after summer vacation, but...now I kinda miss it.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_12

Lisbeth：Here, a special dessert for the Moon Festival. Sheesh, is food the only thing on your mind?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_13

Lisbeth：We'd better get the candy ready. Seems like even SAO has little kids who want to trick-or-treat.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_14

Lisbeth：Today's a day that will haunt me forever. But you give me the strength to keep fighting.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_15

Lisbeth：Are you sure you're cool spending this special day with me? Way to get a girl's hopes up...  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_2

Lisbeth：A present for me? Oh, you shouldn't have... But thanks. I love it.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_3

Lisbeth：Happy Birthday! I'm glad I get to celebrate it with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_6

Lisbeth：It's Asuna's birthday. So you shouldn't be here—go on, go celebrate it with her.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_15

Lisbeth：It's Silica's birthday. Let's all have a big bash at my shop!  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_1

Lisbeth：Congrats! You cleared the quest. You must be tired. Feel free to rest up here.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_2

Lisbeth：I think you've got a present. You should check it ASAP. It might be important.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_lisbeth_sao_sleeping_3

Lisbeth：You're going to the next floor, right? Take plenty of equipment. Never know what you'll run into.  

## 场景 novel.home_sachi_sao_christmas_wait_1

Sachi：Merry Christmas!  

## 场景 novel.home_sachi_sao_christmas_wait_2

Sachi：I want to find the meaning... of our encounter...  

## 场景 novel.home_sachi_sao_christmas_wait_3

Sachi：Could you give me a hand with these Christmas tree decorations? My hand can't reach the top...  

## 场景 novel.home_sachi_sao_christmas_touch_1

Sachi：This hoodie... with the bear ears... it's cute... but a little embarrassing...   

## 场景 novel.home_sachi_sao_christmas_touch_2

Sachi：How about trying it on...? Hehe... You're always wearing black, so this will stick out!  

## 场景 novel.home_sachi_sao_christmas_touch_3

Sachi：Eek! I-I'm used to always wearing a breastplate... So not having one today is...  

## 场景 novel.home_silica_vc.home_wait_101

Silica：Lalala-lala-lalalala♪ Isn't Yuna's song just so catchy!  

## 场景 novel.home_silica_vc.home_wait_102

Silica：We did it! Game cleared! Let's go try out an AR game!  

## 场景 novel.home_silica_vc.home_wait_103

Silica：Ugh! Lisbeth doesn't understand! My song is not so opaque!  

## 场景 novel.home_silica_vc.home_wait_104

Silica：Next time, Yuna has a concert! Do you want to go together?  

## 场景 novel.home_silica_vc.home_wait_105

Silica：There's a big decorated tree in the town. Would you like to go see it when it gets dark?  

## 场景 novel.home_silica_vc.home_wait_106

Silica：Nothing says Christmas like a piece of cake!  

## 场景 novel.home_silica_vc.home_wait_107

Silica：Merry Christmas♪  

## 场景 novel.home_silica_vc.home_wait_108

Silica：You know, going on a date with the love of your life on Christmas is pretty romantic, don't you think?  

## 场景 novel.home_silica_vc.home_wait_109

Silica：I would be so happy if you thought I was cute... W-Wait! Never mind...  

## 场景 novel.home_silica_vc.home_wait_110

Silica：C-Could we... hold hands...? I-It's so we don't lose track of each other!  

## 场景 novel.home_silica_vc.home_wait_111

Silica：I made this cake with all my heart. Will you accept it?  

## 场景 novel.home_silica_vc.home_wait_112

Silica：What kind of cakes do you like? I want to take notes for next year, so please tell me!  

## 场景 novel.home_silica_vc.home_wait_113

Silica：P-please...from now on, let me stay by your side like this. That's all I need to be happy!  

## 场景 novel.home_silica_alo_swim_wait_1

Silica：D-Do you think this swimsuit makes me look like a child?  

## 场景 novel.home_silica_alo_swim_touch_1

Silica：D-Don't look at me like that... I'm getting embarrassed...  

## 场景 novel.home_silica_alo_swim_touch_2

Silica：Ahhh! Hey, don't touch that!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_silica_1st_1

Silica：I often dream about it... About that hill where we saved Pina.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_silica_1st_2

Silica：I sometimes think it would be great if we had something like a mentor-trainee relationship.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_silica_1st_3

Silica：Since I use a dagger, I have to get awfully close when I fight... Please help me improve!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_silica_1st_4

Silica：Liz and I went shopping the other day. Would you like to come with us next time?  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_silica_1st_9

Silica：Pina! Bad dragon! Don't peck at people when they're trying to think!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_silica_1st_1

Silica：You patting my head reminds me of the night we met...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_silica_1st_2

Silica：S-So... Do you like a girl in pigtails?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_silica_1st_4

Silica：Oh! Would you like to form a party with me? I'd love to have you!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_silica_1st_5

Silica：J-Just so you know, girls get very, um...excited... when you do things like that.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_silica_1st_6

Silica：N-Not now... Pina is watching...  

## 场景 novel.home_time_silica_1st_1

Silica：Seeing the morning dew reminds me of the drops from the Pneuma Flower. Pretty, just like glitter.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_silica_1st_4

Silica：Ooh, that lunch looks delicious! Maybe I should increase my cooking skill, too.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_silica_1st_7

Silica：It was a night like this the first time you rescued me, huh. You were so cool!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_silica_1st_10

Silica：Haha, guess you stayed up all night. Then again, I'm not one to talk.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_silica_1st_1

Silica：You have to try extra hard to get up early on Mondays. Otherwise, you'll be lazy all week.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_silica_1st_2

Silica：I think school would be much more fun if you were there.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_silica_1st_3

Silica：Any plans for your day off? W-We could always hang out, if you want...  

## 场景 novel.home_day_silica_1st_4

Silica：Wooo... I played so hard today I feel really sleepy now.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_silica_1st_1

Silica：Spring rain is really soothing somehow. I love how quiet the pitter-patter is.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_silica_1st_2

Silica：Beaches are fun, but I always feel insecure seeing all the sleek beach bodies...  

## 场景 novel.home_season_silica_1st_3

Silica：Collecting fallen leaves makes me want to have a bonfire. Bonfires sure are fun!  

## 场景 novel.home_season_silica_1st_4

Silica：Liz cooked a stew, so... Want to come eat with us?  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_silica_sao_sleeping_1

Thanks for saving me back then. Without you, both Pina and I wouldn't have made it.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_silica_sao_sleeping_2

Since the future is so uncertain in this world, I want to cherish this moment.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_silica_sao_sleeping_3

If I said I didn't want to join anyone else's party...would that be selfish of me?  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_silica_sao_sleeping_4

I know I may not be the best match for you...but...I swear my feelings are genuine!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_silica_sao_sleeping_5

You always give me courage. Courage to survive another day, so that I can be with you...  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_silica_sao_sleeping_6

This must be the safest place in SAO. And also the most relaxing one.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_silica_sao_sleeping_7

As painful as our imprisonment is, meeting you here...is one of my most cherished memories.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_silica_sao_sleeping_8

Pina says she also wants to go to bed... I've been pushing her too hard.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_silica_sao_sleeping_9

We don't even have to talk. Just sharing space with you puts me at ease somehow.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_silica_sao_sleeping_1

Silica：I remember you gently patted my head the day we met, too. Just like right now...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_silica_sao_sleeping_2

Silica：Don't worry, I won't cry anymore. I'll be all right. I've got both you and Pina on my side.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_silica_sao_sleeping_3

Silica：So, um...what do you think of my hairstyle? I'm not sure... Maybe it's too childish?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_silica_sao_sleeping_4

Silica：D-Due to my personal moral code... once you cross that line, there's no turning back.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_silica_sao_sleeping_5

Silica：I love being gently stroked like that. It's very reassuring. And it's making me a little sleepy...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_silica_sao_sleeping_6

Silica：May I...hold your hand? I just think it might help keep the nightmares at bay.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_silica_sao_sleeping_7

Silica：W-Well, two can play at that game. Now...you're my body pillow!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_silica_sao_sleeping_8

Silica：It's all right...we can stay close like this. Hug me tight, while you're at it.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_silica_sao_sleeping_1

Good morning. So, um...can we just spend the whole day together?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_silica_sao_sleeping_2

Mornings in Aincrad never felt this good before... It must be because you're here.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_silica_sao_sleeping_4

It's funny how we still get hungry, even though we're in a VR game. Want to get something to eat?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_silica_sao_sleeping_5

I keep thinking I really should get out of bed...but it just feels so cozy.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_silica_sao_sleeping_7

I used to find nights in Aincrad terrifying. But with you by my side, I'm not scared anymore!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_silica_sao_sleeping_8

You were supposed to sleep all day, remember? Your raids will be more successful if you're well rested.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_silica_sao_sleeping_10

Silica：Sorry I woke you. Pina was acting needy so I was just petting her.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_silica_sao_sleeping_11

Silica：Waking in the middle of the night next to you felt like a dream at first...but it's really you.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_silica_sao_sleeping_1

Silica：Another raid today? Please... be careful? And when you're done... come back here.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_silica_sao_sleeping_2

Silica：I love seeing you every day like this. I just feel so...relaxed.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_silica_sao_sleeping_3

Silica：Rest well today. If there's anything I can do to help, please let me know!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_silica_sao_sleeping_4

Silica：Did you have a restful day? I hope so. Try to go easier on yourself in the future.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_silica_sao_sleeping_1

Silica：Look at us, celebrating New Year's inside a game! It just feels... rather surreal.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_silica_sao_sleeping_2

Silica：If we exorcise all the devils on Setsubun, clearing SAO will be easy, won't it?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_silica_sao_sleeping_3

Silica：Look at me, spending Valentine's Day with you...I never imagined it could happen.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_silica_sao_sleeping_4

Silica：They were selling some sweets that looked like Hina dolls in town. See? Well-made, aren't they?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_silica_sao_sleeping_5

Silica：I don't need a White Day gift. Just being with you like this is chocolate for the soul!  

## 场景 novel.home_event_silica_sao_sleeping_6

Silica：Even though it's April Fools' Day, I still won't distrust you!  

## 场景 novel.home_event_silica_sao_sleeping_7

Silica：Everyone else is taking Golden Week off. We should take it easy, too.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_silica_sao_sleeping_8

Silica：I'm surprised by how realistic they got the rain moisture in this game. Your skin is glowing.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_silica_sao_sleeping_9

Silica：Even if you disappeared to the other side of the galaxy, I would fly on Pina to see you.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_silica_sao_sleeping_10

Silica：It's summer festival season... If we could slip away, I'd love to go eat candied apples with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_silica_sao_sleeping_11

Silica：If we were in the real world, I'd be dreading school tomorrow... But now, I wish I could go.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_silica_sao_sleeping_12

Silica：Even in this world, the moon is so pretty. Maybe it's extra pretty because...you're here with me.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_silica_sao_sleeping_13

Silica：Trick or Treat! Rather... trick or stay with me.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_silica_sao_sleeping_14

Silica：That day, I felt so lost and alone. But I'm okay now, because I have you and Pina.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_silica_sao_sleeping_15

Silica：I got you a Christmas present... Think of this as, my feelings in a box!  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_silica_sao_sleeping_2

Silica：I used to resent having a birthday inside SAO. But now...I'm really grateful for it.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_silica_sao_sleeping_3

Silica：Happy Birthday! It's really, um... an honor to celebrate such a special day with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_silica_sao_sleeping_6

Silica：It's Asuna's birthday today. So, um...do you think I should get her a present?  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_silica_sao_sleeping_14

Silica：It's Liz's birthday today. Let's have a party at her shop!  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_silica_sao_sleeping_1

Silica：You came back from another day of raids in one piece. What a relief.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_silica_sao_sleeping_2

Silica：You've received a gift. There might be one from me, too.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_silica_sao_sleeping_3

Silica：Off to another raid, I see. I'd love to help you someday, once I've gotten stronger.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_wait_1

Sinon：Some people still think we're both girls. Well, when you look like that, who could blame them?  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_wait_2

Sinon：ALO is fun and all, but nothing gives me the "I'm home" vibe more than the smell of gunpowder.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_wait_3

Eydis：Just buy a house here already. Then you can pop in whenever... We can share, if you want.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_wait_4

Eydis：This world also seems to have quests based off lost civilizations. We should go on one together sometime.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_wait_5

Sinon：I don't care how high I level up, I'm still using this gun. I couldn't bear to lose her.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_wait_6

Sinon：I feel kind of awkward doing it IRL, but sharing a bike here in this world isn't half bad.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_wait_7

Sinon：Wanna enter the next BoB with me? Not as a team, of course. As opponents. And I'll kick your butt.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_wait_8

Sinon：You want a spaceship armor plate? Where do we even find that? Well, I'll help you look if you need it.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_wait_9

Sinon：Hellooo? Anybody home? If you're tired, wanna take a break?  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_touch_1

Sinon：A sniper's eyes are her lifeline. So hands off. Next time, I'll break your fingers.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_touch_2

Sinon：Jeez...why are you so obsessed with my hair? I'm going to touch yours back if you don't stop.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_touch_3

Sinon：Uh, excuse me?! Why're you touching my lips...? What are you...trying to do here?  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_touch_4

Sinon：When I chose this outfit, I was prioritizing comfort and mobility— not touchability. Got it?  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_touch_5

Sinon：D-Dude, somebody might see! Argh, why are boys so touchy-feely...  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_touch_6

Sinon：M-My heartbeat is so fast because of you... Don't make me say more.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_touch_7

Sinon：I just hope you don't accidentally pull out a grenade pin and blow yourself up while you're fumbling.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_touch_8

Sinon：Okay, if you don't cut that out, I'm pulling out the big guns? And by that, I mean tying you up and tickling you!  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_time_1

Sinon：Hey... Why'd you have to wake up so early today of all days? You didn't...see me asleep, did you?  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_time_2

Sinon：Come on, sleepy head, wake up. Remember? You said you'd hang out with me today.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_time_3

Sinon：I don't think we have to get up yet. You look just as sleepy as me. Let's go back to bed.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_time_4

Sinon：Since I don't have to worry about sunburns in VR, I can go outside worry-free.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_time_5

Sinon：Wanna go shopping? It wouldn't hurt you to get yourself a gun, either. Sub-weapons are useful.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_time_6

Sinon：It's easier to catch enemies in the daytime, but the same applies to them. That's why I prefer night.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_time_7

Sinon：Risk-taking can pay off, but you give me heart palpitations. You really fly by the seat of your pants.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_time_8

Sinon：You do love your food, don't you? Whenever we eat together, I have to stop myself from pigging out.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_time_9

Sinon：Walking alone at night is scary IRL, but I feel safe in this world. The darkness is my ally here.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_time_10

Sinon：Are you tired? Sorry I kept you up. Let's turn in.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_time_11

Sinon：The quiet night air always quiets the noise in my head. Though part of that might have to do with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_time_12

Sinon：When we walk outside late at night, I feel closer to you. Maybe it's because I can't see anything else.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_day_1

Sinon：Oh, it's Monday. I always lose track of the day when I'm in-game.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_day_2

Sinon：I admire your work ethic, but go easy on yourself, okay?  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_day_3

Sinon：A lazy day off is a good thing. And it just so happens you've got someone cool to talk to.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_day_4

Sinon：I had fun all week. Well, guess I owe you some credit for that.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_event_1

Sinon：I never really played New Year's games before. Like badminton. Sounds like fun, though.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_event_2

Sinon：We'd better throw lots of beans to ward off those evil spirits this year.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_event_3

Sinon：Here's your in-game Valentine. I've got chocolate for you IRL, too, of course.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_event_4

Sinon：I don't drink much amazake, but I like it sometimes. Especially for Hinamatsuri. Ambiance, you know?  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_event_5

Sinon：Uh, another White Day gift? You already gave me one IRL... But thanks. That's so sweet.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_event_6

Sinon： Maybe I'll try out a Photon Sword... Yeah, no. Though I'll probably  carry one for insurance.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_event_7

Sinon：It gets so crowded here during the long break. Let's go find a quiet place to hunt.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_event_8

Sinon：I always love the little breaks in the rain during rainy season. Though the sun's too bright for me, normally.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_event_9

Sinon：Since we're both here, let's go look for Hikoboshi and Orihime in the sky. Hope it's a clear night.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_event_10

Sinon：Wanna light some fireworks? I love how fleeting they are... and the smell of gunpowder.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_event_11

Sinon：We wound up playing GGO all summer long, huh? We made some good memories.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_event_12

Sinon：The moon's up already. I'd love to play longer, but maybe we should head back.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_event_13

Sinon：Lots of people dress up their guns and body gear for Halloween. But...I think I'll pass.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_event_15

Sinon：Merry Christmas. Aww, you look cute in that Santa suit.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_season_1

Sinon：I used to be puzzled when folks said spring was "a season of farewells." But now I get it.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_season_2

Sinon：Heat, I can deal with. But sweat? Get me an AC, quick.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_season_3

Sinon：It's dry skin season. Better take good care of those nails so it won't throw off my sniping.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_season_4

Sinon：Isn't fall a bit cold for a bike? Here, use this scarf. Wouldn't want you catching cold.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_bd_2

Sinon：Thanks. Spending my birthday with you is the best present I could ask for.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_bd_3

Sinon：Should I invite...Kirito over on his birthday? I wanna spend the day with him, if possible...  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_bd_5

Sinon：If Alice could come to GGO, there's all sorts of gifts I could give her... but it looks like that's not happening.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_bd_6

Sinon：Asuna and I are having a chill day of girl-talk together for her birthday... and you're not invited.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_bd_7

Sinon：Make sure you get Leafa something for her birthday. I know she'd love a present from her cool big brother.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_bd_9

Sinon：Should we head out to Yuuki's birthday party now? Do you have her present?  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_bd_14

Sinon：What do you think Liz would like? Since she makes all my equipment, I want to get her something nice.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_bd_15

Sinon：I'd love to drag Silica to GGO and give her a high-caliber firearm for her birthday.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_home_etc_1

Sinon：Congrats on the mission. Great form, as always. I should take notes.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_home_etc_2

Sinon：Always check your gifts. Wouldn't want to lose out on something awesome.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_2nd_home_etc_3

Sinon：Did you check out the next mission yet? I think we've got this one in the bag.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_sinon_os_1

Sinon：The battlefield area in Ordinal Scale is pretty limited so I can't aim too far away.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_sinon_os_2

Sinon：I wonder if the Augma glasses have gone on sale?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_sinon_os_1

Sinon：This isn't the VR world so please try and keep yourself together.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_sinon_os_4

Sinon：W-wait! If you did that in this world then it really would be considered a crime!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_sinon_os_5

Sinon：Since this is an AR gun, I can't just pass it along to you.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_sinon_os_1

Sinon：Morning. Why are you up this early? Was there another battle on Ordinal Scale?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_sinon_os_4

Sinon：Even though we're in an AR, the texture of this gun is identical to the real thing...  

## 场景 novel.home_time_sinon_os_7

Sinon：Time sure does fly. It doesn't help that the time is always displayed neither.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_sinon_os_10

Sinon：Even in the middle of the night you're on Ordinal Scale, huh?  

## 场景 novel.home_day_sinon_os_1

Sinon：Being able to meet in the real world like this starting on Mondays doesn't seem too bad.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_sinon_os_2

Sinon：Wouldn't riding a bike with the Augma on lower your field of vision?  

## 场景 novel.home_day_sinon_os_3

Sinon：Looks like everyone plays Ordinal Scale, even on holidays.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_sinon_os_4

Sinon：It's because holidays are fun that gives us strength for tomorrow! That's how the cycle works.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_sinon_os_1

Sinon：Hey! You have a mark on your face. You took a nap with the Augma on, didn't you?  

## 场景 novel.home_season_sinon_os_2

Sinon：Just looking at the temperature displayed on Augma is enough to make me break a sweat.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_sinon_os_3

Sinon：Somehow, it smells like autumn. It's rare to experience something like that in a VR world.   

## 场景 novel.home_season_sinon_os_4

Sinon：Seeing snow in the AR world is a very weird sensation, isn't it? It looks dreamier than in VR worlds!  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_vc.home_wait_101

Sinon：Happy New Year! Let's make it a good one.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_vc.home_wait_102

Sinon：Since it's the New Year, why don't we go visit a shrine? We can go find out our luck!  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_vc.home_wait_103

Sinon：I wonder what kind of things you might get caught up with this year? Whatever it is, I'll always be by your side!  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_vc.home_wait_104

Sinon：Camellias are dignified yet fragile.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_vc.home_wait_105

Sinon：...Don't get the idea that I wore this just to show you.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_vc.home_wait_106

Sinon：B-Beautiful...! I meant...the kimono of course...  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_alo_swim_wait_1

Sinon：Haaaaa... I should have known I'd get all burnt up in this heat!  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_alo_swim_touch_1

Sinon：Bathing suits sure are breezy.  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_alo_swim_touch_2

Sinon：Kyaa! Y-Your hand is ice-cold! Watch where you're touching...  

## 场景 novel.home_sinon_alo_swim_touch_3

Sinon：Y-You're getting too close!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_sinon_1st_1

Sinon：Asuna has started calling me "Shino-non," but...don't you dare go calling me that!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_sinon_1st_2

Sinon：I enjoy being in VR a lot more now. I'm much more comfortable than I used to be.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_sinon_1st_3

Sinon：Why don't you try using a gun sometimes? It's actually quite a lot of fun.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_sinon_1st_4

Sinon：I just can't get the hang of magic. I wish spells were like items. I miss grenades.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_sinon_1st_9

Sinon：Asleep again? In a way, I'm almost impressed by how easily you can doze off.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_sinon_1st_1

Sinon：Wanna try on some glasses? They might actually suit you.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_sinon_1st_2

Sinon：Sometimes I wish I had long hair...but I think my style suits me best.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_sinon_1st_4

Sinon：Don't.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_sinon_1st_5

Sinon：You really get that big a kick out of touching me? Men...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_sinon_1st_6

Sinon：You'd better not be comparing my chest with someone else's.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_sinon_1st_1

Sinon：You're sure up early. Wanna head out? There's a full day ripe for the picking.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_sinon_1st_4

Sinon：I can tell you're hungry. Why don't we take a break? I've got some food.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_sinon_1st_7

Sinon：I used to be scared to be alone at night...but I'm okay now. I have an amulet to protect me.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_sinon_1st_10

Sinon：I always stay up all night when I'm with you. Hehe, wonder why?  

## 场景 novel.home_day_sinon_1st_1

Sinon：Don't slack off just because it's Monday, okay? I'm watching you.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_sinon_1st_2

Sinon：Everyone at school won't stop bugging me about you. Did you really have to make a scene?  

## 场景 novel.home_day_sinon_1st_3

Sinon：I feel like taking it easy today. Let's chill together.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_sinon_1st_4

Sinon：Time flies when you're having fun, eh? I hate to kill the mood, but we should turn in.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_sinon_1st_1

Sinon：It's a season of new beginnings. This sort of thing used to scare me, but now I'm excited.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_sinon_1st_2

Sinon：Man, it's hot. Doesn't dressing in black all the time make you sweat like crazy?  

## 场景 novel.home_season_sinon_1st_3

Sinon：I know autumn is said to be the season for eating...but I think you're overdoing it.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_sinon_1st_4

Sinon：I always prefer cold weather over hot... Oh, I mean, in the context of being a sniper.  

## 场景 novel.home_sortiliena_swimsuit_wait_1

Sortiliena：It sure is breezy... But I'm a bit embarrassed by how exposed I am...  

## 场景 novel.home_sortiliena_swimsuit_touch_1

Sortiliena：It's been awhile since I've let my hair down and just relaxed. What, you like it better this way?  

## 场景 novel.home_sortiliena_swimsuit_touch_2

Sortiliena：Stop that! Don't even think about laying another finger!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_sortiliena_1st_1

Sortiliena：I pressed the zephyria you gave me so that I can keep it as a decoration.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_sortiliena_1st_2

Sortiliena：You seem to surround yourself with like-minded individuals... most of whom are female.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_sortiliena_1st_3

Sortiliena：Everything changes, but not you. You're just like you were when I first met you.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_sortiliena_1st_4

Sortiliena：Being your mentor is one of my proudest life achievements.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_sortiliena_1st_9

Sortiliena：You're up to something again. When you get quiet, I know the wheels in your mind are spinning.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_sortiliena_1st_1

Sortiliena：No one has patted my head since I was a child... It is somewhat embarrassing.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_sortiliena_1st_2

Sortiliena：I may not act it, but I do consider myself a woman. I must take care of my hair.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_sortiliena_1st_4

Sortiliena：No matter how hard I train, I never seem to develop muscles like a male swordsman would.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_sortiliena_1st_5

Sortiliena：I do like and respect you... but stop doing things so lightly.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_sortiliena_1st_6

Sortiliena：You won't escape this time! I will make you pay dearly for your actions!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_sortiliena_1st_1

Sortiliena：You must be tired from your morning training. I've prepared a light breakfast, so eat up.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_sortiliena_1st_4

Sortiliena：Do you have some time? If so, might I suggest we go into town for some shopping?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_sortiliena_1st_7

Sortiliena：Still swinging your sword, eh? It really is commendable. Alright, I think I'll join you.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_sortiliena_1st_10

Sortiliena：I can't sleep either. Let's go for a walk. If we're caught, just say you were following orders.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_sortiliena_1st_1

Sortiliena：What's wrong? You look sleepy. Well, I know how to wake you up. Grab your sword!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_sortiliena_1st_2

Sortiliena：You're getting stronger by the day. And it brings me joy watching you grow.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_sortiliena_1st_3

Sortiliena：Don't train on a Day of Rest, you hear? If you're caught, that would be a hassle.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_sortiliena_1st_4

Sortiliena：Did you rest up? Good. Then we can get back to training hard tomorrow.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_sortiliena_1st_1

Sortiliena：When spring comes, children love to play outside. Seeing that brings me joy.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_sortiliena_1st_2

Sortiliena：Summer weather affects the autumn harvest, so I always pay attention to it.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_sortiliena_1st_3

Sortiliena：It's gotten a bit cold, so you need to keep yourself warm. Falling ill would be a nuisance.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_sortiliena_1st_4

Sortiliena：The sky always seems clearer in the winter. Strange, since it certainly is the same sky.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_yuuki_alo_1

Yuuki：Having our names engraved on the monument like this, feels like we'll always be together.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_yuuki_alo_2

Yuuki：Hey, let's try out air combat next time! I think it will be very fun!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_yuuki_alo_1

Yuuki：Hehehe! The color is different, but my hair length is about the same as yours! It's such an honor!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_yuuki_alo_4

Yuuki：...How is it? Can you feel my body heat?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_yuuki_alo_5

Yuuki：I wonder if it's ok to do this between girls...   

## 场景 novel.home_time_yuuki_alo_1

Yuuki：Morning! Hehe! I must be so lucky to see your face this early in the morning!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_yuuki_alo_4

Yuuki：Hey, let's participate in the next monthly tournament!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_yuuki_alo_7

Yuuki：Should we get ready to eat? Alright, let's call out all of the Sleeping Knights!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_yuuki_alo_10

Yuuki：Being like this in the middle of the dark with you feels like we are the only ones in the whole world.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_yuuki_alo_1

Yuuki：It's the start of a new week! Waking up to a new week like this makes me so happy!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_yuuki_alo_2

Yuuki：Hey, you don't look so great? Was school busy? Hey, cheer up!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_yuuki_alo_3

Yuuki：I like spending a whole vacation day with you like this!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_yuuki_alo_4

Yuuki：School starts tomorrow, right? Hey, tell me something funny about your school again!  

## 场景 novel.home_season_yuuki_alo_1

Yuuki：When the snow melts, new sprouts will grow! ...What amazing vitality!  

## 场景 novel.home_season_yuuki_alo_2

Yuuki：When it's summer, I want to do BBQ again! Let's invite a lot of people!  

## 场景 novel.home_season_yuuki_alo_3

Yuuki：I've never really experienced autumn flavors before, so could you make me some next time?  

## 场景 novel.home_season_yuuki_alo_4

Yuuki：I'm not that great with the cold, but I like snowy nights. Everyone feels calm and gentle.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuuki_halloween_wait_1

Yuuki：It's almost time for the costume party! Do you know what you're going to be?  

## 场景 novel.home_yuuki_halloween_wait_2

Yuuki：Trick or treat, smell my feet, give me something good to eat!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_yuuki_1st_1

Yuuki：Hehe, good to see you again today!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_yuuki_1st_2

Yuuki：Wanna go out somewhere today? I'm cool with anywhere as long as you're with me!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_yuuki_1st_3

Yuuki：I've been feeling really good lately. I mean, I'm just so happy every day! Hehehe...  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_yuuki_1st_4

Yuuki：Everyone has something they'd never surrender. For me, it's the memories I made with my friends.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_yuuki_1st_9

Yuuki：Just being with you quietly like this... For some reason, I don't mind it.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_yuuki_1st_1

Yuuki：Looks like I'm a little shorter. Argh, I've got some catching up to do!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_yuuki_1st_2

Yuuki：I've never really had my hair braided before. Will you do it for me?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_yuuki_1st_4

Yuuki：If you're gonna keep poking me like that... Yah! There, now you're miiine!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_yuuki_1st_5

Yuuki：My heart is racing right now... Gosh, I feel so silly... It's obvious, right?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_yuuki_1st_6

Yuuki：Where you touched me... It's tingling a little... and it feels kinda warm.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_yuuki_1st_1

Yuuki：You know what they say! If your breakfast tastes good, you're gonna have a great day!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_yuuki_1st_4

Yuuki：Time for lunch already? Okay then, let's climb that tree and spend our break there!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_yuuki_1st_7

Yuuki：Nights always feel kinda lonely. But not when you're around.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_yuuki_1st_10

Yuuki：Hey... Are you awake? Yeah, I can't sleep either.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_yuuki_1st_1

Yuuki：Most people hate going back to school on Monday, but that sounds like fun to me.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_yuuki_1st_2

Yuuki：I'm studying a little bit on my own, you know. Hehe. Learning sure is fun!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_yuuki_1st_3

Yuuki：You're spending all your time off with me today, okay? Pinky-swear!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_yuuki_1st_4

Yuuki：I don't want to fall asleep... But then again, I can't wait to see you again when I wake up.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_yuuki_1st_1

Yuuki：When spring comes, I always feel inspired to keep going.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_yuuki_1st_2

Yuuki：I'd love to go to the beach again this summer. I wanna smash watermelons and hit beach balls.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_yuuki_1st_3

Yuuki：I love autumn flowers. When I see them defying the cold to bloom, I can't help but root for them.  

## 场景 novel.home_season_yuuki_1st_4

Yuuki：Look, it's snowing! So pretty and white...  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_yuuki_alo_sleeping_1

Yuuki：Look at us, sleeping side by side... It's really a dream come true.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_yuuki_alo_sleeping_2

Yuuki：This is making me kinda nervous. I mean, I'm alone. With you. In a place...like this.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_yuuki_alo_sleeping_3

Yuuki：Hey, can I scoot closer to you?  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_yuuki_alo_sleeping_4

Yuuki：You're so pretty I don't recognize you...oh! I mean, you're always pretty, it's just...extra special.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_yuuki_alo_sleeping_5

Yuuki：Your voice, your scent, your warmth... I like it all. When it comes to you, anything goes!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_yuuki_alo_sleeping_6

Yuuki：I'll never forget the day we met. I've had a special place for you in my heart since then.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_yuuki_alo_sleeping_7

Yuuki：I've played my fair share of games, but I think it's fate that brought me to ALO.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_yuuki_alo_sleeping_8

Yuuki：Hehehe, not so close! You're making me blush. Can't you tell?  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_yuuki_alo_sleeping_9

Yuuki：I wish we could just stay like this forever and ever... Forever and ever and ever...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_yuuki_alo_sleeping_1

Yuuki：Your hand on my hair is so terribly gentle... I feel like... I'm gonna cry...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_yuuki_alo_sleeping_2

Yuuki：My cheek is so hot where you touched it...but I don't mind it at all.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_yuuki_alo_sleeping_3

Yuuki：We slept for so long that our hair got tangled together. But I don't wanna go back to normal.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_yuuki_alo_sleeping_4

Yuuki：Hey, can you feel it? The sounds, shapes, warmth, and softness of my body... I hope you can feel it.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_yuuki_alo_sleeping_5

Yuuki：If you keep doing that... I'll only crush harder on you. You okay with that?  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_yuuki_alo_sleeping_6

Yuuki：I used to wish I were big and muscular... But now I'm happy with the body I've got.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_yuuki_alo_sleeping_7

Yuuki：When we make a ring with our hands like this...I really feel connected to you.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_yuuki_alo_sleeping_8

Yuuki：Oh! No...I didn't mind. I'm cool with anything... as long as you are.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_yuuki_alo_sleeping_1

Yuuki：So it wasn't a dream... I really am sleeping with you. Hehehe. What a relief!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_yuuki_alo_sleeping_2

Yuuki：What do you wanna do for dinner? I'm hungry, but I don't want to leave.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_yuuki_alo_sleeping_4

Yuuki：As much as I hate to, we really should be getting up. It's already past noon.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_yuuki_alo_sleeping_5

Yuuki：Mmm... It's really hard to get up. Okay, how about we both get up on the count of three?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_yuuki_alo_sleeping_7

Yuuki：Are you cooking dinner tonight? Yay! I just love your cooking!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_yuuki_alo_sleeping_8

Yuuki：I used to think sleep was a waste of time, but sleep seems extra lovely when I'm with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_yuuki_alo_sleeping_10

Yuuki：Even if we're not snuggling, you still keep me warm. I think I'm gonna get a good night's sleep.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_yuuki_alo_sleeping_11

Yuuki：I've always wanted to have late- night pillow-talk with someone. Like on a school trip.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_yuuki_alo_sleeping_1

Yuuki：I've a feeling good things are gonna happen this week. 'Cause I got to spend all of day one with you!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_yuuki_alo_sleeping_2

Yuuki：Less people play ALO on school days, but I actually like the playing field when it's quiet.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_yuuki_alo_sleeping_3

Yuuki：Making vacation plans with someone I like... I never knew how blissful that could feel!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_yuuki_alo_sleeping_4

Yuuki：Hey, can I stay here a bit longer? Okay, then...let's cuddle and talk some more!  

## 场景 novel.home_event_yuuki_alo_sleeping_1

Yuuki：They say New Year's Day sets the tone for the year, so I'll get to spend the whole year with you!  

## 场景 novel.home_event_yuuki_alo_sleeping_2

Yuuki：I don't want to exorcise demons. I mean, not all demons are evil. Many have their reasons...  

## 场景 novel.home_event_yuuki_alo_sleeping_3

Yuuki：I love having a person to give Valentine's chocolates to. They're true-love chocolates, of course.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_yuuki_alo_sleeping_4

Yuuki：Since Hinamatsuri is a girls' festival, let's celebrate it together! Wanna like, wear kimono?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_yuuki_alo_sleeping_5

Yuuki：Look, we both gave each other White Day gifts! I'd sure love to do this every year...  

## 场景 novel.home_event_yuuki_alo_sleeping_6

Yuuki：It's no use... I've done a lot of thinking, but I just can't lie to you! Not even on April Fool's.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_yuuki_alo_sleeping_7

Yuuki：I wanted to do a lot of stuff on Golden Week, but I'm cool with whatever as long as it's with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_yuuki_alo_sleeping_8

Yuuki：You know, my skin always feels a little different on rainy days. Here, touch it.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_yuuki_alo_sleeping_9

Yuuki：If there were a VR game with a Tanabata setting, I'd love to play it with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_yuuki_alo_sleeping_10

Yuuki：I'd love to see what a summer festival is like. I'd eat all the yakisoba and shaved ice!  

## 场景 novel.home_event_yuuki_alo_sleeping_11

Yuuki：Summer vacation ends today... The next one can't come soon enough! I wanna chill with you again.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_yuuki_alo_sleeping_12

Yuuki：I've never seen the moon this big before. Maybe it looks extra big because I'm with you?  

## 场景 novel.home_event_yuuki_alo_sleeping_13

Yuuki：I've got candy on standby in case any kids show up. See? I've got tons of it!  

## 场景 novel.home_event_yuuki_alo_sleeping_14

Yuuki：I don't know much about what happened in SAO...but I really am glad you made it out.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_yuuki_alo_sleeping_15

Yuuki：I think Santa actually does exist. I mean, I have you! The best present I could ever ask for.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_yuuki_alo_sleeping_2

Yuuki：Look at me, with you on my birthday! Ugh… My heart is so full it hurts. Thanks. I love you.  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_yuuki_alo_sleeping_3

Yuuki：It's Kirito's birthday today. Do you think...I can come to the party?  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_yuuki_alo_sleeping_6

Yuuki：Happy Birthday! Is it just me, or are you even more beautiful than you were before?!  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_yuuki_alo_sleeping_7

Yuuki：For Leafa's birthday, we'd better go to Sylph territory! Let's throw a big bash for her!  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_yuuki_alo_sleeping_8

Yuuki：Hey, Sinon came all the way to ALO to hang out—let's throw her a big party with the gang!  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_yuuki_alo_sleeping_14

Yuuki：Wanna give Liz a birthday present from both of us?  

## 场景 novel.home_bd_yuuki_alo_sleeping_15

Yuuki：What should I get Silica for her birthday? Come brainstorm with me.

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_integrator_vc.home_wait_1

Integrity Knight Eugeo：That person will give me what I want. For me, that's all I need.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_integrator_vc.home_wait_2

Integrity Knight Eugeo：I don't know and I don't want to know. I hate... you and everybody...  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_integrator_vc.home_wait_3

Integrity Knight Eugeo：My head... it hurts... I get a terrible headache whenever I try to remember anything.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_integrator_vc.home_wait_4

Integrity Knight Eugeo：There's nothing more to discuss about. Because I will not fight you.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_integrator_vc.home_wait_9

Integrity Knight Eugeo：...Why do you have such a  sad look in your eyes? Even though you are my enemy.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_integrator_vc.home_touch_1

Integrity Knight Eugeo：...I'm sorry. I don't know who you are.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_integrator_vc.home_touch_2

Integrity Knight Eugeo：Don't touch me anymore! For some reason... I feel strange when you do.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_integrator_vc.home_touch_3

Integrity Knight Eugeo：A hole is open at the very back of my head. Whenever I try to peer into it, my chest tightens...  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_integrator_vc.home_touch_4

Integrity Knight Eugeo：...Remove Core Protection  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_integrator_vc.home_touch_5

Integrity Knight Eugeo：Strange... I don't feel like you are my enemy.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_integrator_vc.home_time_1

Integrity Knight Eugeo：It's morning again... I wish I could stay in a daze within the darkness.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_integrator_vc.home_time_4

Integrity Knight Eugeo：Shall we start? That's why you're here, right? To fight me.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_integrator_vc.home_time_7

Integrity Knight Eugeo：Nightfall... I must return to where that person is.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_integrator_vc.home_time_10

Integrity Knight Eugeo：I dream... That I'm together with a boy and girl whom I don't know...  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_integrator_vc.home_etc_1

Integrity Knight Eugeo：Looks like mission accomplished. Congratulations. That person will definitely praise you for sure.   

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_integrator_vc.home_etc_2

Integrity Knight Eugeo：It's a present. ...You are really are loved by many people it seems.  

## 场景 novel.home_eugeo_integrator_vc.home_etc_3

Integrity Knight Eugeo：A new order just came in. You need to complete your mission. Everything for that person.

## 场景 novel.home_suguha_vc.home_wait_103

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_darkside_1

Eydis：Are these really my memories? Then...why are they so fuzzy?  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_darkside_2

Eydis：I once had someone precious to me...but I couldn't protect her.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_darkside_3

Eydis：Stay away from me if you know what's good for you. I want to be alone.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_darkside_4

Eydis：I am Eydis Synthesis Ten. An Integrity Knight, and nothing more!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_darkside_5

Eydis：Baths... My refuge of bliss! Though they're even more blissful when I get to share them with someone...  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_darkside_6

Eydis：Deep underwater... Where not much life stirs... Nothing but darkness...  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_darkside_7

Eydis：A...spider? Yes. There was a spider. She always told me where to go...  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_darkside_8

Eydis：Can't you hear that voice? It's been calling my name. Calling my name for so long...  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_darkside_9

Eydis：Whenever I'm idle, I feel like I'm slipping into the darkness. But... that might be for the best.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_darkside_1

Eydis：This hair ribbon...there's another just like it. Someone...wanted to have one like mine...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_darkside_2

Eydis：There's this girl I see inside my head. But who is she? I...have no idea.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_darkside_3

Eydis：Long ago, someone patted my head like this and... No, it was me. I patted someone else's head...  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_darkside_4

Eydis：Hey...don't touch me right now. If you touch me again, I'll kill you.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_darkside_5

Eydis：Aw, you're worried about me? Thanks. But...I don't think I can do anything to stop this pain.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_darkside_6

Eydis：Did you...do something just now? I hope you weren't touching me.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_darkside_7

Eydis：I have this uneasy feeling in my heart that I just can't shake. No matter what I do...or where I go.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_darkside_8

Eydis：I need to get back...now. But... get back to...where, exactly? Please. Tell me.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_darkside_1

Eydis：Morning... I thought I heard someone calling me. Calling my name. Over and over.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_darkside_2

Eydis：I won't trust anyone, except the one who can tell me why I'm in so much pain.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_darkside_3

Eydis：I don't want to eat... I have no appetite. I'll be fine. It's okay to skip meals now and then.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_darkside_4

Eydis：Solus's light is so strong... But I feel like the darkness inside me grows even stronger in the light.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_darkside_5

Eydis：An Integrity Knight, summoned from the heavens to guard the Human Empire... Is this really who I am?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_darkside_6

Eydis：Those kids over there look so happy... So why does watching them make me cry?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_darkside_7

Eydis：Whenever I'm in a nice, warm bath, my mind seems to clear a little.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_darkside_8

Eydis：What a feast! And what a shame to eat this all by myself. I wish... I could share it with someone.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_darkside_9

Eydis：My heart is just like the full moon. A bright, white hole in the dark fabric of space.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_darkside_10

Eydis：This darkness... It's terribly familiar to me. Familiar and... somehow comforting.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_darkside_11

Eydis：I need to go to sleep... Otherwise, she'll yell at me.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_darkside_12

Eydis：Why am I in such an ornate room?! Oh, right...I'm an Integrity Knight.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_edith_darkside_1

Eydis：If I keep chasing my dreams I might reach her someday, the girl I see inside me.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_edith_darkside_2

Eydis：A gift... Who's it from? If I check the name, I might remember something.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_edith_darkside_3

Eydis：I have to go far away again? Well...let's get it done quickly.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_past_1

Eydis：Honey, I'm hommme! Were you good while I was gone? And I've got a souvenir for you!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_past_2

Eydis：I need to get stronger so that I can protect you. That's why I became a swordsman.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_past_3

Eydis：I hate to leave you alone, but if I find a cure for your foot, I'll never have to leave you again!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_past_4

Eydis：We sure worked hard today! It's a bit early, but let's take a bath!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_past_5

Eydis：I'll get the water ready. You just sit down inside and relax.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_past_6

Eydis：Your cooking is always so yummy! I really appreciate it.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_past_7

Eydis：Big news! There's this healer who can cure even the most serious of injuries!  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_past_8

Eydis：I'm gonna go out for a while tomorrow... Aw, don't pout. I'll be back before you know it.  

## 场景 novel.home_wait_edith_past_9

Eydis：Oh dear, are you asleep already? Hehe... Guess I'll have to carry you to bed.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_past_1

Eydis：Hm? It's okay, don't stop. Pat my head more. More!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_past_2

Eydis：Don't take out my ribbon! My hair will fly out everywhere.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_past_3

Eydis：Hehe, don't worry. Yours'll grow as long as mine soon enough. Then we can be twinsies!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_past_4

Eydis：Aw, you aren't usually this clingy, are you? There, there, I'll indulge you, just for today.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_past_5

Eydis：Did you revert to your baby-self? Haha. That's a cute look for you.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_past_6

Eydis：Of course I don't mind having my hair played with; it just startles me when you tug like that.  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_past_7

Eydis：Oops, was my belly showing? Better cover up. Don't wanna catch a cold!  

## 场景 novel.home_touch_edith_past_8

Eydis：Here, I'll hold you. Come on now, don't be shy. Just sidle on over!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_past_1

Eydis：Phew... You're awake already? Then I'd better get up too. I'm the big sister, after all!  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_past_2

Eydis：What a lovely morning. If your foot's feeling fine, want to go on a walk with me?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_past_3

Eydis：Thanks for always getting my breakfast for me. It always gives me the energy boost I need.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_past_4

Eydis：I'll be helping the guards in the village all day today. Travel is nice, but money is even nicer.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_past_5

Eydis：As long as I'm home, I need to take care of the heavy lifting, like fetching water.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_past_6

Eydis：The sky is so clear today! Look how far we can see... Are those the End Mountains?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_past_7

Eydis：It's gotten awfully late... I'd better hurry back. I don't want her to worry.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_past_8

Eydis：The bath's all readyyy! Won't you join me today? Pretty-please?  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_past_9

Eydis：Isn't it almost time for bed? Come on, off to bed with you. Get the covers warm for me.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_past_10

Eydis：Dear Stacia... Please make her foot feel a little better tomorrow.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_past_11

Eydis：You work so hard every day... You brave little thing... I want to cure your foot.  

## 场景 novel.home_time_edith_past_12

Eydis：Can't sleep? Want me to get us some warm milk?  

## 场景 novel.home_day_edith_past_11

Eydis：Even the first day back from vacation, you're full of energy. Glad it's rubbing off on me!  

## 场景 novel.home_day_edith_past_12

Eydis：When I leave on my quest, I won't see you for a while. So I want to see you as much as I can now.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_edith_past_13

Eydis：Finally, a Day of Rest! Let's lie around, do nothing, and recharge.  

## 场景 novel.home_day_edith_past_14

Eydis：Did you enjoy your Day of Rest? Let's put in another good week's work! In moderation, of course.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_past_1

Eydis：Happy New Year! I hope we get to spend a lot of time together, like last year.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_past_2

Eydis：I hear they throw beans this time of year in other countries. It's to ward off evil, apparently.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_past_3

Eydis：Let's make some candy together! Apparently, that's what you're supposed to do today.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_past_4

Eydis：It's a festival for girls today, apparently. We'll need to celebrate for the both of us, I guess!  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_past_5

Eydis：It's supposed to be a day of boys giving girls gifts, but I'm sure it's okay that we're both girls.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_past_6

Eydis：I just can't approve of lying. I don't want her to pick up any bad habits.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_past_7

Eydis：Want to travel someplace far away for our vacation? Don't worry, I'll carry you.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_past_8

Eydis：I don't mind the rain... I just wish it'd stop soon. I hope the wheat doesn't rot.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_past_9

Eydis：What's my wish? Isn't it obvious? Your health, of course.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_past_10

Eydis：The Summer Festival's almost here! Let's go together this year! I'm in the mood to party!  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_past_11

Eydis：Aw, summer vacation ends today. Arrrgh. There's still so many things I wanted to do with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_past_12

Eydis：Lunaria has a sort of quiet beauty that Solus lacks.  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_past_13

Eydis：You're supposed to give kids candy today. So here's some candy!  

## 场景 novel.home_event_edith_past_15

Eydis：Today's a day of feasting with the family! I'll get the ingredients if you take care of the cooking.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_edith_past_1

Eydis：Good work so far! Put your feet up and rest a bit...so that we can get out there again!  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_edith_past_2

Eydis：Ooh, a present! I'm sure it's something you've always wanted.  

## 场景 novel.home_etc_edith_past_3

Eydis：A new mission, eh? Well... don't kill yourself over it, okay? I'll be there if you need help.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_wait_1

Mito：Oh, good. You're here.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_wait_2

Mito：I'd love to go to the arcade when we get back. I want to scope out the new games.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_wait_3

Mito：Play a character with my real face showing? Too awkward...  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_wait_4

Mito：If you're free, wanna discuss how we might increase our hunting success rate?  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_wait_5

Mito：You focus on offense, okay? Sharpen your senses and look for your opportunity.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_wait_6

Mito：You okay? You look tired.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_wait_7

Mito：Even though we have to be in SAO, I'm still glad I met you.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_wait_8

Mito：Don't quit prematurely. That's the most important thing to remember.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_wait_9

Mito：Wanna hang out a bit more? I know this great restaurant.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_touch_1

Mito：You want me to touch your hair? Then sit down.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_touch_2

Mito：Jeez... What?  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_touch_3

Mito：This feels...weird.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_touch_4

Mito：Hey, pal...!  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_touch_5

Mito：If you don't cut that out, I'm logging out! Man...if only.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_touch_6

Mito：Again with the puppy dog eyes...  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_touch_7

Mito：Hey! Do I look like the affectionate type?!  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_touch_8

Mito：Ohhh, I get it. You're bored. Okay, let's go hunting.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_time_1

Mito：You awake? Then let's get right to hunting.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_time_2

Mito：Good morning. Aww, you've got sleepy eyes.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_time_3

Mito：Go wash your face. You won't even defeat a dire wolf, looking like that.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_time_4

Mito：Lunch? What a hassle. I wonder if SAO has cereal bars?  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_time_5

Mito：Are you hungry? Let me take you out to lunch. I know a great place.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_time_6

Mito：Hunting grounds clear up around lunchtime. And I'm counting on that.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_time_7

Mito：I love night time. Easier to hunt then.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_time_8

Mito：When it's pitch-black like this, it really makes you believe in ghosts...  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_time_9

Mito：I love how even SAO has stars. They're so pretty.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_time_10

Mito：You're going to sleep already? But aren't there more raids to do?  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_time_11

Mito：Sleepy? Okay, fine...  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_time_12

Mito：Argh, bedtime... I wish they'd install an update that makes us not need sleep.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_day_1

Mito：A new week, new beginnings. Let's level up by the weekend.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_day_2

Mito：You seem busy. You didn't have to come... But I still appreciate it.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_day_3

Mito：It's your day off? Oh, so that's why you showed up. Thanks. That's really nice.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_day_4

Mito：I just wanted to spend... a little more time with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_event_1

Mito：Happy New Year! Where do you want to make your first hunt of the year?  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_event_2

Mito：Do beans count as a ranged weapon?  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_event_3

Mito：Here. Chocolate. Though I must say, I'm more used to being on the receiving end.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_event_4

Mito：I remember Hinamatsuri dolls. When I was little, I thought their clothes looked very uncomfortable.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_event_5

Mito：Is that for me? Thanks...  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_event_6
