Mito：I cannot tell a lie... Yeah, that's a lie.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_event_7

Mito：We've got some time off. Wanna go hunting?  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_event_8

Mito：The ground's too marshy to plant my feet for a good shot. Don't you just love rainy season?  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_event_9

Mito：Orihime is Vega, Hikoboshi is Altair, and "Heaven's river" is the Milky Way.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_event_10

Mito：You're going to the beach? Hope your weapons don't rust in the ocean...  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_event_11

Mito：The end of summer always makes me feel a bit melancholy.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_event_12

Mito：What do you think the moon craters look like? 'Cause I just don't see a rabbit.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_event_13

Mito：That pumpkin looks hard. I wonder how much damage it would inflict if thrown?  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_event_14

Mito：If only I hadn't asked Asuna to come with me that day...  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_event_15

Mito：Are you sure you're okay with spending a special day like this with me?  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_season_1

Mito：Cherry blossoms... That's something I definitely hope to see again.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_season_2

Mito：I hate hot summer days. Too much slippery sweat.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_season_3

Mito：Do any of these levels have pretty red leaves in the fall?  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_season_4

Mito：I hate the cold. My fingers get all stiff.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_rank_1

Mito：You just ranked up. Congrats. You earned it.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_rank_2

Mito：That's good. Keep at it.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_rank_3

Mito：I hardly recognize you anymore.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_rank_4

Mito：I strive to be like you.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_rank_5

Mito：I'm going to keep pace with you. Hope you don't mind.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_home_etc_1

Mito：Mission accomplished. Not too shabby.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_home_etc_2

Mito：You've got a present. Open it before you forget.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_home_etc_3

Mito：I hear a new Quest just came in. Come on, let's check it out.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_wait_1

Asuna：Thank you. I do owe you that. If you hadn't helped me up back then, I wouldn't be alive today.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_wait_2

Asuna：We may be in a party together now, but our alliance is temporary. Don't forget that.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_wait_3

Asuna：I suppose control freaks exist everywhere, don't they. Though you don't seem like one.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_wait_4

Asuna：Not much time has passed since I've come here, but the real world already feels like a distant memory.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_wait_5

Asuna：I put so much care into calibrating my weapons because I value efficiency. In battle and in school.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_wait_6

Asuna：You need to eat to survive. You taught me that.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_wait_7

Asuna：Baths and cream... I guess I care more about that sort of news than battle intel.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_wait_8

Asuna：Everyone's happier than they were at first. Maybe because somebody's been helping us make progress.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_wait_9

Asuna：Wait... Did you honestly think that disguise would fool me? It's painfully obvious.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_touch_1

Asuna：H-Hey! Don't touch my hair! I know we're in a game, but that's no excuse.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_touch_2

Asuna：Wouldn't fighting in a heavy helmet just blind me and make me more vulnerable to sudden attacks?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_touch_3

Asuna：Not needing to worry about pimples or dry skin is one of the few perks of being trapped in this world.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_touch_4

Asuna：I suppose you've forgotten, but... you do realize this game has an ethics code, right?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_touch_5

Asuna：Wait... Do you want me to hit you? Or would you rather I cut you with my sword?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_touch_6

Asuna：I'll believe "the devil made you do it" and let that slide... But don't let it ever happen again.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_touch_7

Asuna：I can't use high-defense armor without increasing my strength first. But I don't want to get buff...  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_touch_8

Asuna：Truth be told, I'd rather wear something more fashionable. Think the higher levels have nicer clothes?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_time_1

Asuna：Even though we're trapped in a game, I'd still like some routine to my life. It just feels better.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_time_2

Asuna：The sun's so bright my eyes hurt. It's hard to believe they were able to replicate that with such detail.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_time_3

Asuna：Whenever I wake up, I instinctively reach for my school uniform. Do you think that will stop over time?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_time_4

Asuna：Without cooking skills, I can't even feed myself. Maybe I should change that.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_time_5

Asuna：I looked around, but there just aren't any schools in this game. Guess that makes sense, though.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_time_6

Asuna：I can't help but feel nervous whenever I go "out of range." I'm literally putting my life in danger.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_time_7

Asuna：I never used to walk at night IRL anyway, so I always feel unsafe.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_time_8

Asuna：Whenever I see people drinking, I wonder if it's possible to get drunk in this game.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_time_9

Asuna：Leaving the safe areas at night is terrifying. It's pitch black, for one thing... So try to avoid it.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_time_10

Asuna：We should get to bed. Even though our bodies aren't technically here, all-nighters are still bad for us.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_time_11

Asuna：I assumed online gamers all played late into the night, but most people are asleep right now.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_time_12

Asuna：You can't sleep? Neither can I. Want to chat for a bit?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_day_1

Asuna：In this world, Mondays just don't feel the same. Probably because there's no school or work.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_day_2

Asuna：Our daily life here is definitely much more intense than in the real world. Too intense at times, though...  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_day_3

Asuna：Unlike the real world, we don't get to take weekends off here. Another day, another raid!  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_day_4

Asuna：Another week begins tomorrow... Sunday nights always make me hyper-aware of that.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_event_1

Asuna：Happy New Year. I never pictured myself ringing in the new year inside a game.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_event_2

Asuna：I'd love to throw beans for Setsubun, but would the beans' DEF go down after they're thrown?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_event_3

Asuna：You can't make chocolate here without a high enough Cooking skill. In-game romance sure is tough.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_event_4

Asuna：There aren't many girls in SAO, but I'd love to get a group together to celebrate Hinamatsuri.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_event_5

Asuna：So, are you giving any girls thank you gifts for White Day? Not like that matters or anything...  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_event_6

Asuna：April Fools' Day, huh? I wish this game were just some big elaborate practical joke.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_event_7

Asuna：I used to spend Golden Week buried in homework, so being outside like this is actually new to me.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_event_8

Asuna：June is for brides, but not for me. I can't even think about marriage when I'm stuck in this game.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_event_9

Asuna：My only Tanabata wish is to get out of this game. But that's a wish we have to grant ourselves.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_event_10

Asuna：I wonder if Aincrad has beaches. If any of the shores are free of sea monsters, I'd love to go.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_event_11

Asuna：I didn't feel like I was on vacation this summer. I spent the whole time in-game...fighting for my life.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_event_12

Asuna：It's been ages since I've moongazed. It seems prettier than the real moon... I wonder why that is?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_event_13

Asuna：If we're in costume, don't you think we might get mistaken for monsters?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_event_14

Asuna：This date will always be a painful memory for me. If I'm ever able to forget it...it'll be if I ever escape.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_event_15

Asuna：I want to at least be festive on Christmas. It's important to recharge now and then.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_season_1

Asuna：Spring always makes me want to go pick flowers. I want to experience the seasons as much as I can.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_season_2

Asuna：I want to dress lightly on hot days, but if we're going "out of range," that's not such a good idea...  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_season_3

Asuna：Autumn is supposed to be a season for good eating, but is there even a harvest in this world?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_season_4

Asuna：I could see my breath this morning. Freaked me out for a minute— you don't usually see that in games.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_bd_2

Asuna：Thanks. My birthday really hits different this year... I'm just relieved to still be alive.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_bd_3

Asuna：It's your birthday, right? Here. Present for you. I'd feel wrong not getting you anything.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_rank_1

Asuna：Congratulations on leveling up. Looks like I've got some work to do. Help me level up, will you?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_rank_2

Asuna：You're leveling up nicely. Did you find a good hunting spot? Take me there and dinner's on me!  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_rank_3

Asuna：You've gotten much stronger while I wasn't looking. You okay? I hope you're not working too hard.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_rank_4

Asuna：If I really want to clear the 100th floor, I'll need to get as strong as you... Okay. Let's get to work!  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_rank_5

Asuna：If I get as strong as you, I'm sure I can clear this game. I wish you'd give us all a helpful push.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_home_etc_1

Asuna：Congrats on clearing the quest! So, what was your reward?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_home_etc_2

Asuna：You've got a gift. Better take your own advice and open it as soon as possible.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_sao_progre_home_etc_3

Asuna：I hear somebody found a new quest. We should check it out together. Safety in numbers, right?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_wait_1

Asuna：My brother bought this new game... I wonder if it's any good.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_wait_2

Asuna：My grandma's house in Miyagi got demolished. I wish I could have gone there one last time.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_wait_3

Asuna：I'm just not motivated today... but don't tell Mom I said that. She'll give me an earful.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_wait_4

Asuna：Mr. Sugo, this guy who works for my dad... He's really nice, but I just don't like him for some reason.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_wait_5

Asuna：Studying for high school is my entire existence right now. Will that change once I'm in high school?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_wait_6

Asuna：It's really exciting hearing other people talk about their crushes... but it's definitely out of my league.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_wait_7

Asuna：My plan is, get into a good college, then get a good job. When that happens, I'll finally be happy.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_wait_8

Asuna：I do love Kyoto, but I don't love the idea of seeing my relatives. It fills me with dread.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_wait_9

Asuna：Oh, shoot! I spaced out there... Next subject, next subject...  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_touch_1

Asuna：I love styling my hair. It would be easier if it were shorter, but I think I'll keep it long for a while.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_touch_2

Asuna：Eek! Wow, you startled me. Nobody really touches my hair anymore.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_touch_3

Asuna：Some of my friends are interested in makeup, but I'm not ready to go there yet. Maybe when I'm older.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_touch_4

Asuna：Um...if you think it's okay to treat girls like that, there's something seriously wrong with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_touch_5

Asuna：If you do that again, I'll scream.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_touch_6

Asuna：I think I've lost some weight. Strange, since I've been so busy studying I haven't had time to exercise.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_touch_7

Asuna：I hope my high school uniform is cute. A part of me wants to choose my high school based off the uniform...  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_touch_8

Asuna：I owe my good physique in part to our cook, for giving me a balanced diet.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_time_1

Asuna：I wake up at the same time every day, so my brain won't let me sleep in.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_time_2

Asuna：I don't have an appetite in the morning, but my mom always yells at me if I skip breakfast.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_time_3

Asuna：Studying first thing in the morning is a knee-jerk reaction to me. I wish I could sleep in sometimes.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_time_4

Asuna：Meals at school are always so lively—completely unlike at home. Is everyone else's family different?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_time_5

Asuna：My teacher yelled at me today for all my careless errors. I need to focus harder.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_time_6

Asuna：I have tests soon. I'd better go over all my notes again.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_time_7

Asuna：My brother and father are coming home late tonight. That means I'm having dinner alone with Mom.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_time_8

Asuna：I'm a little wiped out today. I'm just going to finish my homework and turn in early.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_time_9

Asuna：Bath time is the one time of the day where I can forget about school and my future and just relax.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_time_10

Asuna：Wow, do gamers really stay up this late playing? It's a little hard to believe.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_time_11

Asuna：Uh-oh...I can't sleep. And I have to get up early tomorrow, too.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_time_12

Asuna：I was going to do just one more page in the study guide...and now look at the time.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_day_1

Asuna：Oh. It's Monday. Another week just like the last.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_day_2

Asuna：School isn't much fun, but I don't mind it. As long as I'm studying, I don't get yelled at.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_day_3

Asuna：I wish I could take either Saturdays or Sundays off. But I have to study for my high school entrance exams...  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_day_4

Asuna：Back to school tomorrow. But I actually feel a little more at ease there than I do at home.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_event_1

Asuna：Happy New Year! I hope that this year... is a bit different from the last.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_event_2

Asuna：I haven't done the Setsubun bean toss at home in years. My parents say it's a pain to clean up.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_event_3

Asuna：I don't have anyone to give Valentine's chocolates to. And I know that's not changing anytime soon.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_event_4

Asuna：When I was little, my grandma and I decorated her house with dolls for Hinamatsuri. It was so much fun...  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_event_5

Asuna：I didn't give any boys Valentines, but some of them gave me White Day gifts. It was really awkward...  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_event_6

Asuna：I don't have anyone in my life who'd appreciate a practical joke. So it's Honest April again for me this year.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_event_7

Asuna：Spring Break is the best time to exercise your brain! Well...that's what my teachers say, at least.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_event_8

Asuna：Going to school in the rain is so bothersome. Can't we just go to class in VR?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_event_9

Asuna：This year, I wish for a passing grade into a good high school... The same wish as last year.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_event_10

Asuna：The beach sounds fun, but the idea of going with my parents isn't. I'd rather stay home and study.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_event_11

Asuna：With summer ending, I feel my high school entrance exam on my heels. I have to stay sharp.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_event_12

Asuna：Moon Viewing Festival... One of these years, I'd love to actually relax and celebrate it.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_event_13

Asuna：I've never dressed up for Halloween... but it does sound like fun.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_event_15

Asuna：Next Christmas...I hope I can spend the day partying with my friends.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_season_1

Asuna：Lots of changes happen in the spring...but I wonder if I've changed at all for the better?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_season_2

Asuna：I hate how I get sweat on my notes in the summer. I would use the AC, but then I'd get too cold.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_season_3

Asuna：My hands and feet always get cold at night in the fall. I need to get some fuzzy socks or something.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_season_4

Asuna：Winter came when I wasn't looking... which means I have to stay healthy for my high school entrance exams.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_bd_2

Asuna：You remembered my birthday? Wow, thanks... I'm kind of surprised.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_home_etc_1

Asuna：Good job out there today. Be sure to go to bed early and get your rest.  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_home_etc_2

Asuna：You've got a present. Hehe, I wonder who it's from?  

## 场景 novel.home_asuna_real_junior_high_school_home_etc_3

Asuna：A quest? I don't really know what that is— do you?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_wait_1

Kirito：Are you sure you're a noob? Hard to believe, from the way you swing that rapier.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_wait_2

Kirito：In an MMO, knowledge is your most valuable asset. Getting lost can mean the difference between life and death.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_wait_3

Kirito：Some people switch to whatever is strongest and some get attached to their weapons. It takes all sorts.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_wait_4

Kirito：This game has some pretty nice scenery... Too bad we're too busy fighting for our lives to enjoy it.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_wait_5

Kirito：Info-wise, map data is the most important, hands down. So I wouldn't feel right selling it for profit.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_wait_6

Kirito：Having a home base is important. Just having a place to call home does wonders for your mood.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_wait_7

Kirito：I think eventually, some big guilds or parties will rise up. And when they do, the fighting's gonna get intense.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_wait_8

Kirito：Don't just focus on your sword skills. You should make your stealth and tracking as high as possible, too.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_wait_9

Kirito：Hey...are you okay? If you're tired, you should turn in early. Unless you're hungry?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_touch_1

Kirito：Yeah, I don't like how long my bangs are. But I'm not sure if hair cutting is a thing in this world.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_touch_2

Kirito：Wh-Whoa, there. Is there something on my face? I did just eat some cream puffs...  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_touch_3

Kirito：Lots of players obsess over headgear. Some wear bandanas, and others like helmets with horns.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_touch_4

Kirito：I'm not a muscular guy, but the ripped characters in fighting games do always look more menacing.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_touch_5

Kirito：H-Hey, a little warning first? Don't think I'm used to this just because I'm a guy.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_touch_6

Kirito：Low-level armor definitely looks pitiful. I really want something sturdier.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_touch_7

Kirito：Oh, did you call me? If you're free, wanna go earn some cor with me?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_touch_8

Kirito：W-Well, I'm just...not used to talking to girls like this.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_time_1

Kirito：Waking up early was easy when I exercised in the morning. Guess that old habit is helping me in battle now.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_time_2

Kirito：Truth be told, playing video games from sunup to sundown is actually my dream-job. Just...not like this.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_time_3

Kirito：Gee, you sure are chipper this morning. I want to stay in bed a bit longer, so I'll see you later.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_time_4

Kirito：I'm hunting for enhancement materials today. Wanna come with? They might drop rapier materials.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_time_5

Kirito：The food here is good, but I really miss Japanese flavors like soy sauce and miso.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_time_6

Kirito：When you're checking into inns, it's clear how many groups have formed. You should join one yourself.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_time_7

Kirito：The stars are so bright here. I know they're not real, but they're still pretty.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_time_8

Kirito：Never thought I'd see the day where I'd eat dinner alone with a girl... N-Nothing! I didn't say anything.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_time_9

Kirito：Even without the stealth skill, I'd be practically invisible at night with these black clothes.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_time_10

Kirito：In gaming, there's a surprisingly high number of items you can buy only at night. Unlike IRL, though.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_time_11

Kirito：Out for a walk this late at night? Don't go "out of range," okay? If you must go, at least let me go with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_time_12

Kirito：This food is just data, really... so why does it taste so good when it's late at night?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_day_1

Kirito：When I hear the word Monday, I instinctively panic. It's silly, since days are meaningless in-game.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_day_2

Kirito：I was never much of a school-person, but even I'm starting to miss the mundane peace of it all.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_day_3

Kirito：I know days have no meaning here, but weekends always get me pumped. Let's finish that quest!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_day_4

Kirito：Don't you think it's funny how everyone gets all quiet on Sunday night here? I mean, we're in a game.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_event_1

Kirito：Happy New Year! Let's push ourselves to see how far we can go this year!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_event_2

Kirito：Let's eat our age in beans. I know it doesn't matter since we're in a game, but do it for morale.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_event_3

Kirito：Valentine's Day, huh... There might be some quests that can only be cleared with a partner, you know.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_event_4

Kirito：Would you believe it? They're selling the amazake drink for Hinamatsuri! It's actually pretty good.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_event_5

Kirito：Who even has a White Day gift to give? I guess if any couples logged in together, that's another story.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_event_6

Kirito：There'll probably be some silly boss today since it's April Fools'. And joke bosses actually have good drop rates.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_event_7

Kirito：If only we were free for Golden Week—oh, who am I kidding? I'd still be playing games all day.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_event_8

Kirito：It's always a drag to go out when it's raining. They got the sticky humid feel so realistic it's creepy.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_event_9

Kirito：My Tanataba wish is just to survive until next Tanabata, I guess. Then we can stargaze together again.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_event_10

Kirito：Why is festival food so good? Argh, now I'm hungry.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_event_11

Kirito：I always put off summer homework until the last day of vacation. Finishing it all felt oddly satisfying.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_event_12

Kirito：They've got rabbit-monsters out for the Moon Viewing Festival. I hear they're actually pretty tasty.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_event_13

Kirito：Instead of candy, can somebody get me some enhancement materials? Potions or crystals work, too.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_event_14

Kirito：I still have nightmares about that day. I'll wake up, realize it actually happened, and cry myself awake.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_event_15

Kirito：I've been seeing a lot of couples this Christmas... Strange, since the boy-girl ratio here is ten to one.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_season_1

Kirito：I love taking a picnic to the cherry blossoms in spring...but that's not happening anytime soon.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_season_2

Kirito：I just can't focus when it's hot. I need to take breaks, otherwise I make a bunch of careless errors.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_season_3

Kirito：There must be books about Aincrad's settings and lore and such. Now that would make good autumn reading.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_season_4

Kirito：Even the snow feels real in SAO. I wouldn't be surprised if we could play in it tomorrow.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_bd_2

Kirito：It's my...birthday? Doesn't feel like it when I'm stuck in this game. I'm glad you remembered, though.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_bd_6

Kirito：Today's your birthday, right? If there's anything you want... I wouldn't mind buying it for you.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_rank_1

Kirito：Congrats on leveling up. Your fighting style changes when you advance. Check it out!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_rank_2

Kirito：Ooh, leveled up already? That was fast! I've got some catching up to do.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_rank_3

Kirito：Wow, you got really powerful when I wasn't looking! More powerful than I could have imagined, to be honest.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_rank_4

Kirito：You've gotten really strong... too strong for me to beat, maybe? No...I've still got some fight in me!  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_rank_5

Kirito：Wow, did I didn't know levels went that high! Hats off to you. I know how hard you've worked for this.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_home_etc_1

Kirito：Congrats on clearing the quest! Are you going to win some more equipment?  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_home_etc_2

Kirito：You should check your gifts ASAP. There might be something really valuable.  

## 场景 novel.home_kirito_sao_progre_home_etc_3

Kirito：A new quest just arrived! Come on, let's go check it out! The early bird catches the worm!  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_wait_1

Anna：An idol in a fantasy VR game... It feels like I'm dreaming. Especially with Yuriko here.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_wait_2

Anna：I need to be fit to fight. I'd better do some training exercises. I just hope I don't die right away...  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_wait_3

Anna：Which skill should I raise first? Oh, this is stressing me out... I'll need a day to think...  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_wait_4

Anna：Flying...it's so much fun. Um, sir? Would you, uh... like to fly...with me?  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_wait_5

Anna：If "idoling" were a skill, what level do you think I'd be?  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_wait_6

Anna：The sky and trees look so real... Even now, I find it hard to believe that games like this really exist.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_wait_7

Anna：If I could raise my real life stats by studying, like in this world... I think I could actually make it.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_wait_8

Anna：I hear boss fights are done in groups. Oh, gee, I'd love to join one...  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_wait_9

Anna：Oh...are you tired, sir? Maybe I'll take a break, too. Do you...want to hang out with me?  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_time_1

Anna：Good morning, sir. Look at me, already gaming so early in the day. I feel kind of...guilty.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_time_2

Anna：Hey...want to eat breakfast with me? Even though we're in a game, we still need to eat to move, right?  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_time_3

Anna：Oh, wow, are you really going to spend the whole day with me? Thanks... That's so sweet of you.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_time_4

Anna：They're selling all sorts of equipment. Well, if I'm going to buy something, the cuter the better...right?  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_time_5

Anna：Wow... Everything looks so different from up in the sky. I wonder... Is this how birds feel?  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_time_6

Anna：I'd love to team up with Yuriko to raid a dungeon or something. That would be fun...  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_time_7

Anna：Wow, a fantasy world tavern... I've always wanted to go to one. Seems my dream came true.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_time_8

Anna：Night time...scares me a little. But...I also kinda like how realistic that is here.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_time_9

Anna：Look at that pretty light over there. Would you go check it out with me? I'm...scared to go alone.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_time_10

Anna：Oh, wow, look at us. Gaming together until morning... I feel so...funny inside.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_time_11

Anna：I'm enjoying our conversation... but we'd better get to sleep. Goodnight, sir.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_time_12

Anna：A late-night stroll... Now that's an experience I don't get to have in the real world.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_day_1

Anna：Starting the week off with a game... I like it. It really gives me energy for the week.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_day_2

Anna：You know, if we held class in VR... it would probably be much more fun.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_day_3

Anna：A whole day of gaming together on my day off with you... I'm the luckiest idol alive♪  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_day_4

Anna：I don't mean to, but... I always stay up too late on Sundays. I'm just having too much fun.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_event_1

Anna：Happy...New Year. I hope you have a good one, sir.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_event_2

Anna：We eat our age in beans for Setsubun. I wonder...does that have the same health benefits in VR?  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_event_3

Anna：So, um...do you like chocolate? You see...I, um...have some for you. Ta-daaah♪ Happy Valentine's Day.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_event_4

Anna：Since today is Girls' Day... could you maybe... be extra-nice to me?  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_event_5

Anna：Oh, wow... You got me a White Day gift? Thanks. That's so sweet.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_event_6

Anna：I hate April Fools' Day. I always fall for the pranks. So...can you help me pull one on someone?  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_event_7

Anna：Yuriko and I were talking... and we want to play this game during Golden Week vacation.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_event_8

Anna：It may be raining, but we don't actually get wet here. So... I kinda don't mind being out in it.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_event_9

Anna：The stars are so pretty in this world. If I make a Tanabata wish here... I have a feeling it will come true.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_event_10

Anna：Listen...I can hear festival music. Do you think this world also has summer festivals?  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_event_11

Anna：Summer vacation is almost over... I'm sad to see it end, but I'll carry my happy memories with me.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_event_12

Anna：Hey...do you think the moon in this world has rabbits living on it? I wish I could just fly there and see.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_event_13

Anna：I'm not good at tricks...but I do love treats. Can I share my candy with you?  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_event_15

Anna：There's a huge Christmas tree in the town square. Wanna go see it... with me?  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_season_1

Anna：Do you wanna have a picnic with me? In this world...or the real one... Either is fine with me.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_season_2

Anna：I love summer vacation. It's nice and long, and so much fun. Though... I do hate the homework.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_season_3

Anna：Autumn makes me feel melancholy... But when I game, I get to see my friends...even when they're far away.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_season_4

Anna：The winds are so bitter cold today... Even in-game, I just feel like shutting myself indoors all day.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_rank_1

Anna：Congrats on ranking up. Next time you go adventuring... can you take me with you?  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_rank_2

Anna：Wow, you just keep getting stronger and stronger. Last time you ranked up wasn't that long ago...  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_rank_3

Anna：You've become so strong now that... why, you could be our leader. Help us all reach our full potential.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_rank_4

Anna：I'm working hard to keep up, okay? Because...I want to belong in your party forever.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_rank_5

Anna：Wow, you're a master now... Oh, gee...I'd better put my work in too.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_home_etc_1

Anna：Congrats on clearing the mission. So...what was it like? Can you give me any hints?  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_home_etc_2

Anna：So...did you check the Gift Box? I think you got something... Come on, let's open it.  

## 场景 novel.home_anna_alo_home_etc_3

Anna：So...a new quest has begun. Do you...wanna do it with me? Come on, it'll be fun.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_wait_1

Yuriko：Oh, my goodness... I can't believe I'm in a VR world. It's just like my favorite books!  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_wait_2

Yuriko：So, is this a good look for me? Oh, it is?! W-Watch out, or I'll fall for you!  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_wait_3

Yuriko：Hey, I hear that in this world, there's so many screens it's like you can read several novels at once!  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_wait_4

Yuriko：This game is based off Norse mythology, apparently. Do you think we'll meet any Norse gods?  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_wait_5

Yuriko：Singing in a VR world... it feels kind of strange. But it definitely is my real voice!  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_wait_6

Yuriko：In this world, I really think I could finally become a real hero!  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_wait_7

Yuriko：It was disorienting at first, but I actually really like this world. Everyone's so nice.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_wait_8

Yuriko：If I were alone, I'd be scared right now. I'm so glad I have Miki and Anna with me here.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_wait_9

Yuriko：What's wrong? Haven't got your "sea legs" yet in this new VR world?  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_time_1

Yuriko：Good morning, sir! ...It sure does feel funny to sleep and wake up in a game.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_time_2

Yuriko：I love how in VR, the morning routine isn't as much of a drag.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_time_3

Yuriko：I can't get over how bright the morning sun is in this game. I like it, though.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_time_4

Yuriko：Seeing all those fairies walking around town is just so magical!  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_time_5

Yuriko：I love that we get to eat in this game! I'm amazed at how good VR food actually tastes.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_time_6

Yuriko：If I could beat this game with Anna... Ohhh, that would be so much fun!  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_time_7

Yuriko：I never thought I'd be in a dark forest of a fantasy world, but here I am.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_time_8

Yuriko：Night here is completely different from in the real world. It truly is pitch-black. It's actually kinda scary.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_time_9

Yuriko：It's already bedtime in the real world. But it's still light here. I feel like I could keep playing.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_time_10

Yuriko：Everything feels so real, it could be midnight and it'd still feel like noon.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_time_11

Yuriko：You're still up? Haha, guess that makes two of us.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_time_12

Yuriko：Hey...I'm having a hard time sleeping. Can we talk for a little while?  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_day_1

Yuriko：There seems to be a lot of people here for a Monday. That just goes to show how much fun this game is!  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_day_2

Yuriko：School would be fun in this world. Who doesn't love commuting by air?  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_day_3

Yuriko：I was planning on spending my day off at the bookstore. Do you think this world has one?  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_day_4

Yuriko：Another week starts tomorrow. Let's crush it together again!  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_event_1

Yuriko：Happy New Year! Let's make it a good one together!  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_event_2

Yuriko：When you throw Setsubun beans in-game, does that send the demons "outside" into the real world?  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_event_3

Yuriko：W-Wait, sir! Here... A Valentine. I-It's for you!  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_event_4

Yuriko：You know the white sake in the Hinamatsuri song? I wanted to drink it so bad when I was a kid.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_event_5

Yuriko：A White Day gift? For me?! Watch out, I might fall for you!  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_event_6

Yuriko：I've been thinking over some possible fibs to tell for April Fools' Day... but then I got liar's block.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_event_7

Yuriko：I'd sure love to spend all of Golden Week beating this game with Anna.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_event_8

Yuriko：It's interesting how rain in this game actually makes you wet. The sheer attention to detail is remarkable.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_event_9

Yuriko：If only Orihime could fly. Then she could cross the Milky Way and see Hikoboshi anytime.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_event_10

Yuriko：Would you like to go camping by the sea? This world has lovely beaches.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_event_11

Yuriko：Summer vacation ended too soon. But I'm excited to get back to the grind tomorrow!  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_event_12

Yuriko：If the rabbits on the moon are anything like Anna, then they must be really cute.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_event_13

Yuriko：I wonder if I could dress like this for Halloween in the real world without feeling embarrassed?  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_event_15

Yuriko：Christmas in this world is like something right out of a fairytale. I just find it all so enchanting.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_season_1

Yuriko：I love how the seasons are so direct in this game. Just look at all the blossoms!  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_season_2

Yuriko：I suppose more people will play this game in the summer. I can't wait. The more the merrier!  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_season_3

Yuriko：Do you think autumn makes food taste better in this world, too? I can't wait to find out.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_season_4

Yuriko：My hands are numb, it's snowing... It's so easy to forget this is a game.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_rank_1

Yuriko：Congratulations on the rank-up! I knew you could do it! Now I've got some catching up to do.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_rank_2

Yuriko：You've already ranked up again? Wow, you're fast. Give me some tips!  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_rank_3

Yuriko：Wow, you're such a go-getter— even in this world! I hope I can rank up fast enough to catch up to you!  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_rank_4

Yuriko：You've sure gotten strong! No matter what world you're in, I always strive to be like you, sir!  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_rank_5

Yuriko：I might as well just call you the Strongest Warrior of All Time! Ah! Don't look at me or I'll fall for you!  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_home_etc_1

Yuriko：Good work on the mission. You should take the day off. Read a book and put your feet up.  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_home_etc_2

Yuriko：There's a present for you, sir! Don't you just love presents? They're so heartwarming♪  

## 场景 novel.home_yuriko_alo_home_etc_3

Yuriko：A new job came in? If you get stuck, call on me for help anytime!  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_wait_1

Miki：It'd be like so much fun to use magic during our concerts, you know? We'd be like, pow! And poof!  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_wait_2

Miki：What's this game again? Elf...chime? Wait, it's not?  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_wait_3

Miki：Ooh, look! Your girl's got ears! And get this, it tickles when you touch 'em!  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_wait_4

Miki：Ooh, look at all the people with pets! Haha! Now I kinda wanna bring Hamuzo the hamster here!  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_wait_5

Miki：Wow, this game has so, so, so many cool places to go! The beach, the sky, the mountains—it's all super pretty!  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_wait_6

Miki：You knowww, wouldn't it be great if we went flying togetherrr? I mean, we don't get many chances to fly IRL.  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_wait_7

Miki：So, like, can't we explore this VR world with just the two of us? I'd totally come with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_wait_8

Miki：Hey, I hear we can change costumes all we want here! Guess who's having ten costume changes in the concert?  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_wait_9

Miki：What's wrong, Mr. Producer? Are you sleepy? Wanna take a nap with me?  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_time_1

Miki：*Yawn*...morning already? Well, since this is just a game... Nighty-niiight.  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_time_2

Miki：Mmm...breakfast ready? 'Kay-kay. I'll get up. So what's on the menu, Mr. Producer?  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_time_3

Miki：It feels like, sooo weird sleeping in-game. I mean, is IRL-Miki asleep? Or is she awake, gaming?  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_time_4

Miki：Good morning, Mr. Producer! Your girl made breakfast, see? Huh? It's lunchtime already?  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_time_5

Miki：It sure feels good to fly. A girl could get used to this, y'know? I wish we could fly for real.  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_time_6

Miki：I think an airborne concert would be like, crazy-fun, you know? Our fans would be flying, too, of course.  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_time_7

Miki：Heyyy, d'you think this world also has onigiri? Haha! I wonder what kinda stuff they'd put inside?  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_time_8

Miki：Flying at night must feel sooo good. But, it also sounds kinda scary. Oh, I know! Let's fly together!  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_time_9

Miki：So, heyyy, a day isn't twenty-four hours in this world. Guess who's  sleeping in!  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_time_10

Miki：Oh...can't sleep? How about you snuggle up with yours truly? Haha!  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_time_11

Miki：It's like, sooo quiet here. Totally not like the real world. I'll definitely sleep well here.  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_time_12

Miki：As much as I'm enjoying talking, I'm kinda really sleepy now. Time for Miki's beauty sleep. 'Night!  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_day_1

Miki：Man, why can't school just be in this world? I could fly and Mondays wouldn't be so hard.  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_day_2

Miki：It's so cool seeing everyone having so much fun on a weekday.  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_day_3

Miki：Hello, weekend! We're gonna have lots of Miki-time, okay, Mr. Producer?   

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_day_4

Miki：*Yawn*...I played way too hard today. Now I'm all sleepy... Nighty-niiight.  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_event_1

Miki：Happy New Year! I hope it's a banger!  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_event_2

Miki：Wouldn't the Setsubun bean-toss be like, so much fun in the air?! Don't ask me to clean up, tho...  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_event_3

Miki：Here ya go! V-day chocolates! They're from the heart, of course.  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_event_4

Miki：Hinamatsuri is so much fun! I'm expecting a box of those yummy strawberry cream cookies, okayyy?  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_event_5

Miki：Sooo, just a hunch... but do you have a present for Miki on this special day?  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_event_6

Miki：I'm seeing April Fool's events all over. I wonder what people do here?  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_event_7

Miki：This Golden Week, I wanna take ALL the naps! It's just the right weather for it.  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_event_8

Miki：I used to hate rainy season 'cause it made my hair all frizzy, but that's not a problem here!  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_event_9

Miki：Sooo, what's your Tanabata wish? You wanna know mine? Tooo bad!  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_event_10

Miki：Ever notice how yummy yakisoba tastes at the beach? Just add onigiri and you've got a perfect meal!  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_event_11

Miki：Aw, is summer vacation over already? Gee, time sure flies in this game.  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_event_12

Miki：Dontcha just love how cute and chubby Moon Viewing dango are? You eat your age in them, right?  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_event_13

Miki：Everyone looks soooo cool on Halloween! Aw, I wanna go to a costume party.  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_event_15

Miki：Merry Christmas! I hear this game really goes all-out for the holidays. Let's go check it out!  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_season_1

Miki：Naps are great, but springtime naps are even greater!  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_season_2

Miki：I hate the heat, but I don't mind it so much in this game. Maybe 'cause I don't get sweaty?  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_season_3

Miki：As much as I hate the heat, I wish it didn't have to get cold. Why can't the temperature always be just right?  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_season_4

Miki：Winter is always full of events, huh. New Year's, Christmas, V-Day... So many yummy holidays.  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_rank_1

Miki：Congrats on the rank-up! Somebody's getting a special present from Miki soon.  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_rank_2

Miki：Wait, didn't you like, just rank up? Wow, hardcore.  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_rank_3

Miki：Everyone's talking about you. Sayin' you're really powerful now. I'm just so proud of you!  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_rank_4

Miki：Whoa, can you get any stronger? You're gonna take good, good care of your Miki from now on, I hope?  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_rank_5

Miki：Jeez, you're like, so strong now! Looks like I've got my work cut out for me if I wanna be a top idol!  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_home_etc_1

Miki：Another job in the books! You must be hungry. So let's eat onigiri!  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_home_etc_2

Miki：Hey, you got a present! C'monnn, open it! I can't wait to see what's inside!  

## 场景 novel.home_miki_alo_home_etc_3

Miki：New job came already? Always grinding, eh? Want a little help?  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_wait_1

I can't make nice with the filthy Human Empire dwellers... They killed my parents.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_wait_2

I may be in the Human Empire, but it is purely for study. Not for pleasure.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_wait_3

At last, I am able to wield this sword. But secrets still remain locked inside it...  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_wait_4

I hate to admit it, but the Human Empire is more bountiful than the Dark Realm. Especially in food.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_wait_5

How was Shirley able to forgive the Human Empire for what they did to Lady Lipia?  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_wait_6

I won't believe in peace with the Human Empire...but if there's another war, innocents will die.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_wait_7

I shall steal every sword skill and Art from you! Say your prayers.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_wait_8

Not even in my realm was there a dual-wielder... However, he still seems like an easy opponent.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_wait_9

The Human Empire is quite peaceful. So peaceful... and I'm bored to tears.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_touch_1

Sylvie：I got this hairpin from Mother. Keep your grubby hands off it!  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_touch_2

Sylvie：Wh-What are you doing?! You have no excuse for touching my hair!  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_touch_3

Sylvie：Do you just...go around touching everyone? Could you show a little restraint?  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_time_1

Sylvie：Good morning, Moth...er?! F-Forget what I just said! Got it?!  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_time_2

Sylvie：My, what a lazy oaf you are. And you call yourself the strongest person in the Human Empire?  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_time_4

Sylvie：I must admit, Human Empire cuisine can't be beat... Unless food in the Cathedral is special somehow?  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_time_5

Sylvie：Clear waters and lush forests... If only the Dark Realm were like this. Then it would be more prosperous.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_time_7

Sylvie：I stole many Human Empire tricks today. Just a bit more training, and I shall be better than you.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_time_8

Sylvie：The moon is the same in both lands. So why is it that the Human Empire and Dark Realm are so different?  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_time_10

Sylvie：I dreamed about Mother and Father. They left me...and disappeared far, far away... What a sad dream.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_time_11

Sylvie：I'm just having a hard time sleeping. You needn't concern yourself. Begone at once.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_day_1

Sylvie：The day of rest is long gone. Why the relaxed smile?  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_day_2

Sylvie：I live to strengthen myself every day. I must do so to avenge my mother and father.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_day_3

Sylvie：I will not rest, even on a day of rest. While you lot are resting, I'll be besting you!  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_day_4

Sylvie：I suppose I did exhaust myself a little... I will rest up tonight and start again...tomorrow...  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_event_1

Sylvie：A new year is upon us. I swear that this will be the year I avenge you, Mother, Father!  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_event_3

Sylvie：Why do you assume these sweets are for you? These are for Asuna.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_event_8

Sylvie：Rain always makes me so happy. Drinking fresh rainwater is the height of luxury!  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_event_9

Sylvie：If I got to see the ones I loved most, even just one day per year would suffice.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_event_10

Sylvie：My goodness...clean water as high as my own head... I wish my parents could have bathed in this manner.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_event_12

Sylvie：I had thought tonight would be a good time to train, but the moon is so huge.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_event_13

Sylvie：Understood. So, I am supposed to dress as a monster so that I may harness its powers, yes?  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_event_15

Sylvie：This feast is a dream come true. I hope that someday children of the Dark Realm might eat like this.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_season_1

Sylvie：Does springtime always have this many flowers? I had no idea...  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_season_2

Sylvie：Dressing in black is the reason you're overheated. Drop a layer or two and perhaps you'll feel better?  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_season_3

Sylvie：Asuna's cooking is indeed delicious. But Mother's fare was far superior.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_season_4

Sylvie：The weather is cold out, but Human Empire buildings are warm enough to dress light. Ideal for training!  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_bd_3

Sylvie：Kirito's birthday... Humph. I'm not giving him anything.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_bd_6

Sylvie：Asuna's birthday... Perhaps I should give her a little present, to thank her for all those meals.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_rank_1

Sylvie：You've grown a little, I see. But I hope you aren't satisfied where you're at.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_rank_2

Sylvie：Hmmm... You've bulked up quite a bit in my absence. Let's spar a little.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_rank_3

Sylvie：Your daily training has paid off, I see. I'm rather impressed.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_rank_4

Sylvie：Agh! How did you get so strong? I mustn't be complacent... Yes, allow me to train with you!  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_rank_5

Sylvie：I am no longer a match for you. But don't get cocky—I will catch up to you before you realize it!  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_home_etc_1

Sylvie：Did you complete a mission? My, that was fast. I knew you were a worthy rival!  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_home_etc_2

Sylvie：Well, you've got a present. You should check it immediately. You wouldn't want it to rot.  

## 场景 novel.home_sylvie_home_etc_3

Sylvie：The next mission has arrived. Come, we must prepare to depart at once!  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_wait_1

Alphen：Everyone must be worried about us, so we want to get home as soon as possible.  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_wait_2

Alphen：I would have gone mad with worry if I were alone. I blush saying this, but I'm glad you're with me, Shionne.  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_wait_3

Alphen：The lords here are different from the ones in our world. I suppose they're more like leaders?  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_wait_4

Alphen：Each and every one of Kirito's sword moves are exemplary. Everything would serve us well in battle.  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_wait_5

Alphen：Kirito reminds me a little of Law. Their fighting styles may differ, but their voices are identical.  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_wait_6

Alphen：Kirito's sandwiches were so tasty they gave me a shock. Would you like to try some, Shionne?  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_wait_7

Alphen：Everyone who lives here seems so happy. What a lovely sight.  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_wait_8

Alphen：I've never seen so many lady fighters. Maybe it's a feature of this world...or is it just Kirito?  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_wait_9

Alphen：Would you like to have a rest? They told me of a nice eatery.  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_time_1

Alphen：Good morning... I couldn't sleep very well. The bed was too soft.  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_time_4

Alphen：I never imagined I'd have to fight corrupt lords in this world, too.  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_time_7

Alphen：Suppers in this world are quite tasty, but I really do miss eating around an open fire.  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_time_8

Alphen：All of these weapons are completely foreign to me. What are they made of?  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_time_9

Alphen：Kirito does resemble Law. Though their personalities are completely different...their auras, too.  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_time_10

Alphen：Can't sleep? Fear not, we'll return home someday. Let's both do our best to ensure that day comes soon.  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_rank_1

Alphen：You never miss a target, do you, Shionne? I could learn from your precision.  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_rank_2

Alphen：Now we're one step closer to defeating the lords.  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_rank_3

Alphen：You're always rescuing me, Shionne. That won't do. I must get stronger so that I can return the favor.  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_rank_4

Alphen：Law and Rinwell will certainly be surprised when we return much stronger than before.  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_rank_5

Alphen：I know you can reach every target you set your sights on, Shionne. Let's do this together.  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_home_etc_1

Alphen：Congratulations on reaching your goal. I always believed in you.  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_home_etc_2

Alphen：A present seems to have arrived. If it's a weapon, will you show it to me?  

## 场景 novel.home_alphen_home_etc_3

Alphen：Our next mission has arrived. Let's get ready to depart at once!  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_wait_1

People-pleaser this, mother hen that. The people of this world all remind me of a certain someone.  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_wait_2

We have a mission we must accomplish. And we must return home quickly.  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_wait_3

I wonder what flight feels like. I must admit I am a little curious.  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_wait_4

That Asuna girl is quite the accomplished cook. As good as Kisara, even.  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_wait_5

It truly has been ages since I've settled in one spot for a while.  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_wait_6

It seems my thorn curse and your sense of pain have returned to normal. What a strange feeling.  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_wait_7

Fairies, people flying to and fro— reminds me of Rinwell's stories.  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_wait_8

Even here, I can still use Astral Artes... What a curious feeling.  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_wait_9

What's wrong? Your head is in the clouds. If you're feeling ill, please say so.  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_time_1

Shionne：Come on, out of bed. It's rude to keep someone waiting when they've made breakfast for you.  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_time_4

Shionne：Water and earth, wind and fire, light and dark... this world is quite balanced.  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_time_7

Shionne：How long has it been since I had a soothing soak in the bath? I must thank Asuna later.  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_time_8

Shionne：What was that food I saw earlier? N-Not that I'm curious about it...  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_time_9

Shionne：Is there something wrong with my clothing? Somebody was staring an awful lot at me.  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_time_10

Shionne：When we first got lost here, I thought we would be stuck forever... It's nothing. Forget I said that.  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_rank_1

Shionne：It seems you've learned how to use the swords here. Keep at it and improve your technique.  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_rank_2

Shionne：You've been hitting your targets well. Perhaps you've learned to read enemy movements?  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_rank_3

Shionne：I see you've been training hard for our fight with the lords. Perhaps I underestimated you.  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_rank_4

Shionne：I am certain your strength comes from your drive to protect others.  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_rank_5

Shionne：I am confident that together, you and I can defeat all the evil lords. I'm counting on you.  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_home_etc_1

Shionne：Mission accomplished. No matter what world you're in, your nature stays the same, doesn't it?  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_home_etc_2

Shionne：There's a gift addressed to you. I think you should open it as soon as possible.  

## 场景 novel.home_shionne_home_etc_3

Shionne：Our next orders have arrived. Let's head out immediately.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_wait_1

Oh, good. You're here.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_wait_2

If I have to be in VR, I know it would be a lot of fun as long as I was with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_wait_3

I hope I get to see that black swordsman from the beta test after launch. He must still be playing.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_wait_4

You and I are completely different... Maybe that's why we get along so well.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_wait_5

Am I perhaps the only person who has seen you at your giddiest? Haha...I don't think I mind that.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_wait_6

You okay? You look tired.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_wait_7

Moving your fingers in-game is supposed to be good for the brain... Not! Did you really believe that?  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_wait_8

Don't quit prematurely. That's the most important thing to remember.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_wait_9

Wanna hang out a bit more? I know this great restaurant.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_touch_1

Misumi：You want me to touch your hair? Then sit down.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_touch_2

Misumi：Jeez... What?  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_touch_3

Misumi：This feels...weird.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_touch_4

Misumi：Hey, pal...!  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_touch_5

Misumi：The uniforms here are kind of cute, I guess.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_touch_6

Misumi：Again with the puppy dog eyes...  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_touch_7

Misumi：Hey! Do I look like the affectionate type?!  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_touch_8

Misumi：We're in-game right now— could you save that for later? I'm almost done.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_time_1

Misumi：I'm running on low sleep today. I was just making so much progress in the game last night...  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_time_2

Misumi：Good morning. Aww, you've got sleepy eyes.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_time_3

Misumi：Do you have some time after school today? If so...meet me on the roof.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_time_4

Misumi：We have volleyball for P.E. today? I'd better not jam my fingers. Harder to handle the controller.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_time_5

Misumi：Are you hungry? Let me take you out to lunch. I know a great place.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_time_6

Misumi：I don't get lonely eating by myself. Besides, eating is no fun when you have to be mindful of your company.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_time_7

Misumi：I should hit the books after dinner. Unless I maintain my good GPA, I won't be allowed to play the game.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_time_8

Misumi：When it's pitch-black like this, it really makes you believe in ghosts...  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_time_9

Misumi：As much as I love bathing, it is so annoying drying my hair... Don't you agree?  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_time_10

Misumi：When it's just you and me late at night like this, it feels like we've snuck into another world...  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_time_11

Misumi：Sleepy? Okay, fine...  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_time_12

Misumi：Oh, would you look at the time... But I still need to memorize a few more things before it's SAO time.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_day_1

Misumi：And another week begins. I don't mind Mondays, but I am a little sad to lose my free time.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_day_2

Misumi：You seem busy. You didn't have to come... But I still appreciate it.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_day_3

Misumi：I think I'll go to the arcade today. There's this game I like there and I'll lose my edge if I slack off.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_day_4

Misumi：I just wanted to spend... a little more time with you.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_event_1

Misumi：Happy New Year! Let's make it a fun one together. Having fun is one thing that's in our control, right?  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_event_2

Misumi：As old as I am now, eating my age in beans is enough to get me full.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_event_3

Misumi：Here. Chocolate. Though I must say, I'm more used to being on the receiving end.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_event_4

Misumi：I remember Hinamatsuri dolls. When I was little, I thought their clothes looked very uncomfortable.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_event_5

Misumi：Is that for me? Thanks...  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_event_6

Misumi：I cannot tell a lie... Yeah, that's a lie.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_event_7

Misumi：Even a long vacation flashes by when you fill it with studying and gaming. That's student life.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_event_8

Misumi：The ground's too marshy to plant my feet for a good shot. Don't you just love rainy season?  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_event_9

Misumi：Orihime is Vega, Hikoboshi is Altair, and "Heaven's river" is the Milky Way.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_event_10

Misumi：Once we're free from studying for entrance exams, let's take a nice trip to the beach.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_event_11

Misumi：The end of summer always makes me feel a bit melancholy.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_event_12

Misumi：What do you think the moon craters look like? 'Cause I just don't see a rabbit.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_event_13

Misumi：I've noticed that every Halloween, more and more people dress up as video game characters.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_event_15

Misumi：Are you sure you're okay with spending a special day like this with me?  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_season_1

Misumi：My games and studies change every year...but the cherry blossoms never stop blooming in the spring.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_season_2

Misumi：I hate hot summer days. Too much slippery sweat.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_season_3

Misumi：Sickles were originally used in the grain harvest, right? Oh, it came up in a game I'm playing.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_season_4

Misumi：I hate the cold. My fingers get all stiff.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_home_etc_1

Misumi：Mission accomplished. Not too shabby.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_home_etc_2

Misumi：You've got a present. Open it before you forget.  

## 场景 novel.home_mito_real_junior_high_school_home_etc_3

Misumi：I hear a new Quest just came in. Come on, let's check it out.  

## 场景 home（未引用文本，按编号排列）

Alice：You remembered my birthday? Ⅰ am...very honored.  
Alice：It's Kirito's birthday? I doubt he needs a present, but I'll have one ready...just in case.  
Alice：It's Eugeo's birthday. Let's go give him some good cheer.  
Alice：Asuna's birthday... She'll probably want to celebrate alone with Kirito.  
Alice：It's Leafa's birthday. I know she would love to receive a sword as a gift.  
Alice：For Sinon's birthday... I suppose a bow would be the best gift.  
Alice：It's Ronie's birthday. Her favorite dessert is honey pies, correct? Let's go buy her some.  
Alice：Tiese's birthday, you say? A party with all her friends would probably be best.  
Asuna：Thanks. Celebrating with you is the best gift of all.  
Asuna：Hehe. How many times is it now? I hope we get to spend every birthday together from now on!  
Asuna：Today is Alice's birthday? Do you know what she likes?  
Asuna：It's Leafa's birthday! You have a present for her, right?  
Asuna：Let's throw Shino-non a birthday party. We'll have the gang over at the Dicey Café.  
Asuna：Since it's Yuuki's birthday today, the Sleeping Knights and I are throwing her a night party.  
Asuna：Oh, it's Ronie's birthday today? What did you get her?  
Asuna：It's Tiese's birthday today, eh? Let's throw her a party.  
Asuna：It's Liz's birthday, right? I thought a party with just us girls would be great.  
Asuna：It's Silica's birthday today. Come on, let's go wish her a happy birthday!  
Eydis：It's that boy's birthday? Wait, do you have a thing for him?  
Eydis：The boy with the blue rose sword is having a birthday today. Are you going to get him a gift?  
Eydis：Since today is Alice's birthday, we need to celebrate!  
Eydis：The Lady Asuna's birthday? Why, that is surely a huge festival for the Human Realm.  
Eydis：It's the green swordswoman's birthday? Would you tell me what sort of gift she would like?  
Eydis：It's Sinon's birthday... Let's all celebrate the way we do at the Solus festival!  
Eydis：So it's Ronie's birthday today. I know a great gift for her: that black-haired boy.  
Eydis：So it's Tiese's birthday today. I'm sure she would be honored if you threw her a party.  
Eugeo：Huh? It's my birthday? Oh. You're right... I can't believe I forgot.  
Eugeo：Today is Kirito's birthday! Order anything you'd like today. My treat!  
Eugeo：Alice's birthday... Even though I know I won't see her, I always plan for it every year.  
Eugeo：I heard that today is Ronie's birthday. I think she'd love it if you went to see her.  
Eugeo：Tiese's birthday, huh... I did get her a gift, but I wonder if I should hand it to her in person.  
Kirito：You remembered my birthday? Thanks! You're a great friend.  
Kirito：It's Eugeo's birthday today. Maybe I should treat him to a special dinner or something.  
Kirito：I hear it's Alice's birthday today. Eugeo said he's doing something special for her. I wonder how she'll react...  
Kirito：It's Asuna's birthday today. Yui and I should do something special for her.  
Kirito：It's Sugu's birthday today. I think I remember her talking about something she wanted...  
Kirito：We're throwing Sinon a birthday party IRL today.  
Kirito：It's Yuuki's birthday today. We should all throw her a party.  
Kirito：It's Ronie's birthday today. When I told Eugeo I was going as a present, he yelled at me. Hmmm...maybe I should give her a real present instead.  
Kirito：It's Tiese's birthday today. Eugeo said she'd be flattered to get a present from me, no matter what it is... I think I'll get her a special pie, too.  
Kirito：It's Liz's birthday today... I hear Silica and Asuna have something special planned.  
Kirito：It's Silica's birthday today. Come to think of it, how old is she?  
Leafa：Hehehe, thanks for the birthday wishes. That was so sweet!  
Leafa：Today is Kirito's birthday... Maybe I should throw him a party.  
Leafa：It's Alice's birthday? Well, we should all celebrate!  
Leafa：It's Asuna's birthday. We should do something special for her, too.  
Leafa：It's Sinon's birthday today. Hmm...I wonder what I should get her.  
Leafa：We're all throwing a birthday party for Yuuki, right? I'm bringing the flowers.  
Leafa：I was thinking of baking a pie with honey as a sweetener for Ronie's birthday  
Leafa：We're having a girls' night out for Tiese's birthday. No boys allowed!  
Leafa：It's Liz's birthday today. Come on, let's go wish her a happy birthday.  
Leafa：I was thinking of getting Silica a dress for her birthday. I'm sure she'll look adorable!  
Lisbeth：You remembered my birthday? ...Thanks!  
Lisbeth：It's Kirito's birthday today. Oh well, I guess I might as well get him a present.  
Lisbeth：Today is Alice's birthday? Since she already has a sword, maybe she'd like some defensive gear?  
Lisbeth：Do you have plans for Asuna's birthday yet? I can help if you need any ideas.  
Lisbeth：Wanna throw Leafa a birthday party with the gang?  
Lisbeth：What would Sinon like for her birthday? I need to get her something.  
Lisbeth：Asuna is throwing Yuuki a birthday party. We should all go!  
Lisbeth：It's Silica's birthday! And of course I got her a present already.  
Mary：Ohh, we're gonna have a big feast today! Hehehe. Well, it's your birthday, so eat up, Sister!  
Ronie：You remembered my birthday? I am very honored, sir!  
Ronie：I hope Kirito has a wonderful birthday! Look...I got him a present.  
Ronie：It's Eugeo's birthday?! We need to celebrate!  
Ronie：Today is Lady Alice's birthday. I do admire how majestic she is!  
Ronie：The goddess Asuna also has a birthday? Oh, that is, if Stacia had a human incarnation, I imagined she would be like Asuna.  
Ronie：The green swordswoman's birthday... She's related to you, isn't she? What is she like?  
Ronie：Today is Lady Sinon's birthday, I hear. Her weapon is very...mystical.  
Ronie：It's Tiese's birthday today, sir! Want to throw her a surprise birthday party?  
Silica：Huh? You have a gift for me, too? Oh...thank you so much! I could just cry...  
Silica：It's Kirito's birthday... I need to be brave and give him a present!  
Silica：It's Alice's birthday today, right? Hmm... I was thinking a ribbon for Amayori? That might be a good present.  
Silica：It's Asuna's birthday! And I got her a present!  
Silica：It's Leafa's birthday today. Hmm...I wonder what I should get her.  
Silica：People are talking about throwing a party for Sinon. Want to go with me?  
Silica：A birthday party for Yuuki? Do you think I'm invited?  
Silica：It's Liz's birthday! I'm giving her some cute jewelry.  
Sinon：You remembered my birthday... Thanks. That's so sweet of you.  
Sinon：It's Kirito's birthday today. I want to do something special for him, so I'd better get him a present.  
Sinon：It's Alice's birthday today. She probably doesn't want something edible... Maybe a cute dress?  
Sinon：Today is Asuna's birthday, so I'm going out for a bit. Or you can come with me, whatever.  
Sinon：Oh, it's Leafa's birthday. Has she mentioned anything she wants? Like kendo gear?  
Sinon：You're going to Yuuki's birthday party, right? I'll see you later. I'm going early to help decorate.  
Sinon：It's Ronie's birthday? We should throw her a party.  
Sinon：If you didn't invite Solus to Tiese's birthday party, I'd feel much more comfortable going.  
Sinon：What did you get Liz for her birthday?  
Sinon：I'm getting Silica concert tickets for her birthday. I think she'd like that.  
Sortiliena：It's Kirito's birthday? Then I'll take him to the city as a special treat.  
Sortiliena：It's Eugeo's birthday? Then Golgorosso and I shall throw him a party!  
Sortiliena：It's Lady Alice's birthday... What sort of gift would please her?  
Sortiliena：It's Lady Asuna's birthday... I suppose a woman of her caliber would want a sword as a gift.  
Sortiliena：It's the green swordswoman's birthday? Would you tell me what sort of person she is?  
Sortiliena：Today is Solus...I mean, Sinon's birthday? I know. I'll get her a bow.  
Sortiliena：Ronie's birthday, huh... How about we get her a present together?  
Sortiliena：So, today is Tiese's birthday... Let's invite Eugeo and the others, and throw her a dinner party.  
Suguha：Hehehe, thanks for the birthday wishes. That was so sweet!  
Suguha：Today is Kirito's birthday... Maybe I should throw him a party.  
Suguha：It's Alice's birthday? Well, we should all celebrate!  
Suguha：It's Asuna's birthday. We should do something special for her, too.  
Suguha：It's Sinon's birthday today. Hmm...I wonder what I should get her.  
Suguha：We're all throwing a birthday party for Yuuki, right? I'm bringing the flowers.  
Suguha：I was thinking of baking a pie with honey as a sweetener for Ronie's birthday  
Suguha：We're having a girls' night out for Tiese's birthday. No boys allowed!  
Suguha：It's Liz's birthday today. Come on, let's go wish her a happy birthday.  
Suguha：I was thinking of getting Silica a dress for her birthday. I'm sure she'll look adorable!  
Tiese：I'm so honored that you would celebrate my birthday with me. I will cherish this memory, always!  
Tiese：I hear it's Kirito's birthday today. Are we having a party?  
Tiese：Happy Birthday, Mentor! I'm preparing you quite a feast today!  
Tiese：Today is Lady Alice's birthday, isn't it? You're getting her a gift, I assume?  
Tiese：The Lady Asuna's birthday... Perhaps we should invite her to a party at the Academy?  
Tiese：Today is the green swordswoman's birthday?  
Tiese：Today is Lady Sinon's birthday. We should brighten up her day just like Solus’s rays!  
Tiese：It's Ronie's birthday today. Would you help me throw her a party?  
Yuna：Today is Eiji's birthday! To celebrate, let's sing a birthday song for him!  
Yuuki：Whoa, you remembered my birthday?! I think I need a moment!  
Yuuki：Tell Kirito I said happy birthday!  
Yuuki：I might forget my own birthday, but I'd never forget Asuna's birthday!  
Yuuki：It's Leafa's birthday today, eh? Let's throw her a party.  
Yuuki：It's Sinon's birthday today. Do you know what she wants?  
Yuuki：Since it's Liz's birthday today, I'm sure the gang is throwing her a party. Let's go too!  
Yuuki：Silica's birthday, eh? Do you think she'd like it if I got Pina's favorite food or something?  
Alice：Come to think of it, I haven't gone north on missions much.  
Alice：Comparing forms is rather meaningless... But your sword was rather good.  
Alice：The Swordcraft Academy? The idea has always appealed to me. I would love to have cohorts with whom to study.  
Alice：The Taboo Index is absolute. However...I believe you did the right thing.  
Alice：I was honestly surprised when you bested Eldrie. It would seem I underestimated you.  
Alice：It would seem that Cardinal and the High Pontifex are keeping many secrets from us.  
Alice：You defeated Deusolbert and didn't fall for the scheme... I continue to be awed by your dynamism.  
Alice：Your moves surprised me a little. However, you are too reliant on your weapon.  
Alice：I never expected that Uncle would be defeated. Even now, I still cannot believe it.  
Alice：Now that I have discovered the Prime Senator's scheme... I no longer know whom to trust.  
Alice：I will defeat the High Pontifex. Not for anyone but myself!  
Alice：I have faith that you will rise. Come, have courage!  
Eugeo：Rulid Village... I have good memories and bad memories there, but I can't wait to go back there someday with Alice.  
Eugeo：Taking care of the horses sure was tough. But on the bright side, I think my muscles are stronger for it.  
Eugeo：I never dreamed I'd be a student at the Swordcraft Academy!  
Eugeo：Taboo Index or no, as a human being, there are certain acts I can't let slide!  
Eugeo：Eldrie? I've heard that name before...  
Eugeo：I can read about the origins of this world? Wow. What an impressive library.  
Eugeo：That's an Integrity Knight, alright. No one stands a chance.  
Eugeo：Integrity Knight Alice... Is she really our Alice?!  
Eugeo：Even though I'm up against a hero of legend, I can't let him defeat me!  
Eugeo：I was...searching for love.  
Eugeo：The people of this world need saving... We can't run away and abandon them!  
Eugeo：Don't worry. I know you can do it. And you know it, too.  
Kirito：Seeing the pretty vistas reminds me of Rulid. I wish you could see it...  
Kirito：Straw beds are actually pretty warm. Wanna try it out?  
Kirito：True will from the heart... "True will"...huh... Oh, sorry. Was just doing some thinking.  
Kirito：Don't forget to keep your peace of mind. Let's make "stay cool" our mantra.  
Kirito：Can you look at my bangs for me? Whenever I'm stuck making a decision, I feel a tug on them.  
Kirito：Even if I'm stuck, going nowhere, I'll never stop struggling to break free. I have to live like my authentic self, you know?  
Kirito：I never pull my punches, even against a woman. I've lost to many a powerful swordswoman before.  
Kirito：Hey, is there something on my back? Like, a spider or something?  
Kirito：When I tapped my pocket, I found two buns.  
Kirito：Some feelings can only be communicated through the sword... So, will you listen to my sword?
