Kirito：Hey, why'd you hide that from me? I thought we were friends!  
Kirito：My memories live on inside of me, forever.  
Administrator：Today is the start of the work-week for ordinary civilians. I will catalogue that.  
Administrator：Diligence is a virtue, my sweet little doll.  
Administrator：A day of rest... Yes, perhaps you should rest sometimes.  
Administrator：I will carefully readjust you. Don't worry. There's nothing to fear.  
Agil：Customer turnout is always pretty meh on Mondays. Maybe I should take inventory or something.  
Agil：You're saying I never have customers? No, no, the shop just happens to be empty right now!  
Agil：Weekends are my bread and butter at the bar, so I don't have much time for gaming then.  
Agil：I'm grateful to see you here, even this late. As long as you're here as a customer, that is.  
Alice：Starting today, you need to seriously get back to your work. Don't worry...I'll be here for you.  
Alice：When I see the people, living their daily lives, I feel a very strong urge to protect them.  
Alice：A day of rest? Well then, let us go somewhere with Amayori.  
Alice：You have more vigor now, I see. Let's both have a productive week.  
Alice：It's back to work tomorrow! I hope you like the lunch I'm packing you.  
Alice：How is your work coming along? I'm rather enjoying mine.  
Alice：Let's all go out somewhere together on the day of rest.  
Alice：Today's day of rest was lots of fun! Now I feel energized for the rest of the week.  
Argo：Are you going to work right now? I've got the day off.  
Argo：Days of the week have no meaning for an informant. You never know when some juicy intel will show up.  
Argo：Well, a day of fun might be called for...  
Argo：You look tired, man. Are you partying too hard?  
Asuna：Another week starts today. Let's crush it together again!  
Asuna：I'm sad that I don't get to see you much on weekdays, but I'm grateful for every minute.  
Asuna：I wish I could spend my entire day off with you.  
Asuna：Thanks again. I had a lot of fun today.  
Bercouli：Hm? I don't see the problem. We'll just call this another day of rest.  
Bercouli：You'd better get in a good day's work, otherwise I won't let your arrogant ass have a rest tomorrow!  
Bercouli：Come on, it's a day of rest. Rest.  
Bercouli：Today was rather enjoyable. Let's do it again tomorrow.  
Cardinal：The day of rest has ended, and the world is back on the move.  
Cardinal：I like how the people of this world work. They live healthy, earnest lives.  
Cardinal：I, too, enjoy days of rest. The actions of those I'm observing change.  
Cardinal：Only one day of rest per week? Humans on the outside certainly work hard.  
Deusolbert：Now then. Guarding the Human Empire today again.  
Deusolbert：There are no abnormalities in the Human Realm at present. I am grateful for the peace.  
Deusolbert：Humans such as yourself should rest up on your day of rest.  
Deusolbert：To us Integrity Knights, days of rest have no meaning.  
Eydis：I'll be returning to my post, starting today. I'm feeling lonely. Can I hold your hand?  
Eydis：I may not look it, but I'm rather busy. What with spying and patrolling, and all.  
Eydis：Finally, a day off! Even Integrity Knights get exhausted.  
Eydis：This day of rest truly rejuvenated me. I'm ready to face another week.  
Eldrie：If at a controlled site, I naturally strive to work hard, but there is no impetus to work...  
Eldrie：Merely working hard is not my style. I would rather reserve my energy for when my skill is actually needed.  
Eldrie：It is a day of rest? I see. So, citizens work every day.  
Eldrie：It was a rather fulfilling day. I would love to spend my next day of rest with you.  
Entokia：Back on duty today. Let's put in a good day's work!  
Entokia：Everyone was really angry that you suddenly went missing. Haha, even you play hooky, Master?  
Entokia：Can I really take the day off?! In that case, I'll take Iwahaku out for a ride. It's been a while.  
Entokia：Nights on days off are strangely quiet, don't you think? Sometimes it's even harder to sleep.  
Eugeo：The day after a day of rest is always tough.  
Eugeo：Life is so much more fun now, compared to my days of axe-swinging.  
Eugeo：Since it's a day of rest, we should go out shopping or something!  
Eugeo：Thanks again. I had a lot of fun today!  
Eugeo：Okay, here's the challenge this week: Which one of us makes the better sounds?  
Eugeo：I can understand why you want to slack off, but you need to be dedicated to your job.  
Eugeo：Since it's a day of rest, want to go out somewhere?  
Eugeo：Today's day of rest was lots of fun! I can't wait until next week's!  
Fanatio：Well. Back to work.  
Fanatio：Dark forces have been spotted nearby. I must do something about it.  
Fanatio：Even on days of rest, we must remain vigilant.  
Fanatio：Was your day of rest meaningful? Glad to hear it.  
Fizel：Oh dear. Studying again? I wish I could go flying on a dragon.  
Fizel：But I really am studying! I swear!  
Fizel：Finally, a day of rest! I hope I can go out today.  
Fizel：The day of rest is ending! I don't want to go to bed just yet.  
Iskahn：I wonder what kind of training I should do this week... You. Any good ideas?  
Iskahn：Looks like practice is coming along! At this pace, not even a Human Soldier nor Dark Knight will be able to stand in your way!  
Iskahn：Day of rest? That has no meaning here. Everyday is for training. Everyday is a battlefield!  
Iskahn：I have to figure out what next weeks training course will be. Maybe for the next course I should add a little more weight...  
Kirito：That was a great weekend! Okay. Let's crush it this week!   
Kirito：Is it Saturday yet?  
Kirito：Wanna go out someplace?  
Kirito：Man, doesn't it suck that the day after vacation,you suddenly get all sorts of ideas for things you want to do?  
Kirito：Back to work again today, huh. The next day of rest can't come soon enough.  
Kirito：You're doing really good today! You made the Gigas Cedar's life go down three whole points!  
Kirito：Do you wanna go somewhere on our next day of rest? How about we go marigot picking?  
Kirito：Aw, our day of rest is over. Back to the grind tomorrow.  
Klein：Monday already? It's always so hard to have energy right after a weekend.  
Klein：I feel like I'm just going through the motions until the weekend gets here.  
Klein：Finally, a break! Wanna go out for a drink?  
Klein：We should really turn in early, but I don't wanna waste the night either.  
Kureha：Mondays are generally gloomy.. I would be great if we could stay in this world forever...  
Kureha：I wonder if we scream out for the weekend to come faster, will it really happen?  
Kureha：The long awaited weekend is here! Where should we go hunting today? Or should we go do a battle?  
Kureha：Time sure does fly by when you're having fun... I'm already looking forward to next week.  
Leafa：Mondays suck, but complaining only makes it worse. So, I always try to stay positive!  
Leafa：I'm used to morning practice by now. Wanna try it? It's actually kinda fun.  
Leafa：Since we have some time off for once, I'd like to spend it together.  
Leafa：Vacation is over already? Man, time does fly when you're having fun!  
Linel：Whenever a day of rest ends, I feel rather gloomy.  
Linel：I studied the Taboo Index today. Though I've already memorized the whole thing.  
Linel：Today is a day of rest! I would love to take it nice and slow.  
Linel：Hehe. What a fun day of rest that was.  
Lisbeth：Aaargh. Back to the grind again. Don't over do it though, okay?  
Lisbeth：Weekdays sure are stressful, huh? Then again, when I have time off, I get bored.  
Lisbeth：As much as I'd love to take it easy on weekends, I always wind up doing a bunch of stuff.  
Lisbeth：Going to bed early on Sundays always feels like a missed opportunity to me.  
Mary：Back to work today. Let's be productive!  
Mary：I hope to contribute to the world as much as everyone else. That's why I sew and study.  
Mary：I wanted to splurge a little since it's a Day of Rest, so I bought some fish that were on sale!  
Mary：I'm so happy we got to spend our Day of Rest together! Hehehe. Well, goodnight.  
Medina：It's the start of a new week. This is the week that I'll overtake you. You better be ready!  
Medina：I don't know how tired you are, but everyone needs a break from time to time. I'll have some of that Siral water.  
Medina：There's no day of rest for me. Even if all of my training was to get banned, there's still so many things I could do in my room.  
Medina：I need to get myself motivated. Because the academy is going to be starting up again as of tomorrow.  
Nergius：The Day of Rest is over. Swordsman Delegate, I ask that you get back to your duties.  
Nergius：Why are you so fidgety?! You must take your duties seriously!  
Nergius：Seeing the peasants with smiles on their faces reinvigorates my sense of duty to protect them.  
Nergius：Why are you returning so late? Well, I suppose I should commend you on returning at all.  
Philia：I have this strong feeling that I need to try my best since it's monday. But on the contrary, it's exhausting.  
Philia：It would be fun if I could be with everyone, everyday... I can't wait for the weekend.  
Philia：Not having to worry about tomorrow and just playing along is such a wonderful thing.  
Philia：Today was so much fun! I hope that next week will be just as fun as today.  
Premiere：I guess monday is tiring for anyone regardless of the person. Even though it's the start of the week, I wonder why we would be tired...  
Premiere：I'm looking forward to the weekend since I'll be able to talk to everyone again.  
Premiere：Being able to meet everyone... to meet you, makes me so happy. Let's talk a lot.  
Premiere：Too bad the weekend is over, huh. So that's what they mean when they say they feel lonely...  
Rain：I love mondays. It's the start of a new week. Isn't that a great feeling?  
Rain：Days worth of fatigue really do pile up during the middle of the week, eh... No, I have to keep pushing forward!  
Rain：Yes, I'll be taking a break today. I need to get some rest.  
Rain：I got plenty of rest, so I should be able to do my best from tomorrow onwards!  
Renly：It's going to be a little difficult waking up on the next day of rest.  
Renly：Can't wait for the next day of rest. It would be great to go somewhere with you again...  
Renly：Do you have any plans for today's day of rest? I... I'm free, so...  
Renly：We'll have to train hard again, starting tomorrow. In order to have a fun day of rest.  
Ronie：Starting today, it's back to working hard on my studies and my training!  
Ronie：My schoolwork is stressful, but I enjoy my sword training.  
Ronie：Sir, how are you going to spend your day of rest?  
Ronie：I'm a little tired from overexerting myself, but I had a lot of fun!  
Sachi：When you're in the virtual world long enough, you lose track of what day it is.  
Sachi：Oh, I miss my peaceful life before this... I hope I can get my old life back someday.  
Sachi：Since we have the day off, I want to do something fun.  
Sachi：I always feel uneasy at night... But since I had such a great day, I might be able to sleep easily tonight.  
Sheyta：Looks like today will mark the beginning of another week. Since everyone seems to be so busy.  
Sheyta：I look observing from afar to check if people are doing their jobs.  
Sheyta：Day of rest... That doesn't really apply to Integrity Knights.  
Sheyta：The day of rest has ended.  See you tomorrow, then.  
Selka：It's back to the grind today. I'd like to learn another Sacred Art by the next day of rest.  
Selka：I enjoy looking after the children. Though it can be a stressful job sometimes.  
Selka：Let's all go out on the next day of rest. I'll make us a big picnic lunch.  
Selka：I need to thank the Goddess Stacia for everything I've accomplished today.  
Seven：It doesn't matter weather it is monday or tuesday. To a researcher, they're busy any day of the week.  
Seven：I feel pretty good this week. Until the weekend comes, looks like it will calm down a bit.  
Seven：Researchers need their breaks. That way, they can spread their wings on the weekend.  
Seven：...Again, let's try our best from tomorrow... Good night...  
Shasta：Looks like the beginning of the week is tough for everyone. I think a little effort is going to be needed here.  
Shasta：How are the men doing? Is morale high?  
Shasta：Day of rest? When there's no more barren struggles, I would be open to that kind of day.  
Shasta：I'm counting on you tomorrow. I thank you for your service.  
Silica：It’s the start of a new week... Let's push through!  
Silica：Thinking about the weekend always gives me energy.  
Silica：Since we have the day off, let's really use it to relax.  
Silica：Since I got to spend so much time with you this weekend, I have plenty of energy to face the week.  
Sinon：It's the start of a whole new week. Hope you have a good one.  
Sinon：School is pretty tough for me, but I'm okay now. Sometimes I have fun, too.  
Sinon：Do you have any plans this weekend? If not, wanna go somewhere with me?  
Sinon：What a great weekend. I'll make it through next week okay, knowing I'll have another fun weekend to look forward to.  
Sortiliena：I'm expecting good things from you again today. We're focusing on martial arts today.  
Sortiliena：All my days are filled with fun, now that I get to spend them with you.  
Sortiliena：For the day of rest, I was thinking of going shopping. Would you like to come with me?  
Sortiliena：What a fulfilling day of rest that was. Now I'm recharged for the rest of the week.  
Sortiliena：Okay. Back to sword training today.  
Sortiliena：It's crucial to never skip a day of training.  
Sortiliena：A day of rest... Yes. Taking time off is also important.  
Sortiliena：I rested up well today, which means I have to get back to training that much harder tomorrow.  
Strea：I don't really have a sense for days...but everyone seems to be a little tired today?  
Strea：It used to be that when it was the weekend everyone would play together...but it seems like everyone is busy. How sad.  
Strea：Looks like everyone is having fun today! If everyday was the weekend, that would be amazing.  
Strea：I still haven't played enough...but that can't be helped. I guess I'll leave things like that for today!  
Suguha：Mondays suck, but complaining only makes it worse. So, I always try to stay positive!  
Suguha：I'm used to morning practice by now. Wanna try it? It's actually kinda fun.  
Suguha：Since we have some time off for once, I'd like to spend it together.  
Suguha：Vacation is over already? Man, time does fly when you're having fun!  
Tiese：This starts another week of me tending to your every need. It's an honor to serve.  
Tiese：Time spent with you after training is my favorite time.  
Tiese：I'm thinking of going shopping today with my roommates.  
Tiese：I would be so honored if we could go out together on our next day of rest.  
Tiese：We need to give it our all starting today! Like a noble!  
Tiese：The Sacred Tongue is so difficult. I need to remember these by tomorrow...  
Tiese：There's no training today since it's a day off. Hehe. What a relief.  
Tiese：I got plenty of rest, so I'll be able to do my best starting from tomorrow!  
Vassago：Monday? I honestly do not care what today is or what day it is in general.  
Vassago：Don't work so hard everyday.  
Vassago：Weekend? That has nothing to do with me.  
Vassago：Isn't it great that we still have tomorrow to look forward to?  
Subtilizer：I guess this is the day in which tiny souls go out to work...  
Subtilizer：Do their souls wear out by working that hard?  
Subtilizer：A peaceful soul? Where is the fun in that?  
Subtilizer：Next time, I want to find a brighter spirit... Next time for sure...  
Yui：Too bad the weekend is over. But, I wish you well this week!  
Yui：Aren't you tired? If you are, go ahead and take a nap.  
Yui：Are you going out with Mom on your day off?  
Yui：Today was so much fun! Hehe, I'd love it if you took me out again.  
Yuna：It's monday! I hope that there will be a lot of interesting things this week too!  
Yuna：We can play when it's the weekend, right? I can't wait...  
Yuna：It's finally the weekend! Let's hangout! As for where... let's decide when we hangout!  
Yuna：...Today was really fun. Thank you, as always.  
Yuuki：I don't mind Mondays. I always feel pumped to make it a good week!  
Yuuki：I love being able to see you every day like this.  
Yuuki：Since we have the day off, that means we can see each other from dawn 'til dusk!  
Yuuki：Another week in the books. Time sure flies!

Administrator：Something has arrived for you. Make sure you check its contents.  
Administrator：Your next mission is in. Make sure you confirm it in a timely manner.

Agil：A present arrived for you. You're a guy no one should underestimate.  
Agil：You've got a new Mission.

Airy：A new mission has arrived.

Alice：You have received a new mission. I am confident that you will pass with flying colors.  
Alice：Your work went well, didn't it? Good for you!  
Alice：A gift arrived for you. Opening gifts is always exciting, isn't it?  
Alice：A new task arrived, I see. Don't worry, I'll help!

Alicia：You've got a present. Hurry and open it!  
Alicia：I hear a new Quest just came in.

Argo：You got a gift. You should check who sent it to you.

Azurika：It is time for your next drill. Please read the details carefully.

Cardinal：A new task has arrived! Now, you must go complete it!

Dakira：A gift arrived for you. Would you like me to go fetch it?  
Dakira：The next mission has arrived. I would love to accompany you, if I may!

Deusolbert：A new mission has arrived. Are you ready for it?

Dee Eye Ell：Have you decided on what the next target will be? Please leave the command to me.

Eydis：A gift arrived for you. Won't you tell me what it is?  
Eydis：A new mission arrived. Let's go take care of it.

Eugeo：Looks like you have a reward coming your way! It's because you finished your job.  
Eugeo：You got a gift. Look who's popular!  
Eugeo：Looks like we have a new job. Let's check it out.

Sachi：You've got a present. Hehe, I wonder who it's from?

Sheyta：Looks like the next mission is here.

Selka：Did you hear about the new task? I wonder what sort of task it is?

Seven：You should always try and open your presents as soon as you get them. That is the proper way of doing things.  
Seven：Isn't there a new Mission out? Let's go take a look!

Shasta：There's a gift waiting for you. It will surely bring a smile to your face.

Silica：Congrats on clearing that mission!  
Silica：You got a gift. You're so popular...

Siune：Let's check to see if there are any new Quests!

Sortiliena：The next mission is in. Let's go see what it is!

Strea：You got a present. Hurry up and open it!  
Strea：I new quest just came in! Would you mind if we both went and checked it out?  
Suguha：Congrats on clearing that Mission. Let's keep pushing through!  
Suguha：You've got a present. Wanna open it?  
Suguha：A new Quest came out! I wonder what's in it.

Tiese：Sir, a gift has arrived for you. Let's open it!  
Tiese：A new mission has arrived for you. I can tell many people depend on you.  
Tiese：Looks like you've completed the mission. Please let me assist you next time!  
Tiese：It's a present addressed to you. Um...should we hurry up and check what's inside?  
Tiese：A new mission! Please do your best and don't over do it!  
Vassago：Tch. Mission accomplished, eh? How dull.  
Vassago：You better be careful opening up presents. Who knows, it might be a Bomb!  
Vassago：Hurry up and get to the next mission! That's where I'll kill you!  
Subtilizer：Report to me immediately once the job is finished.  
Subtilizer：You can't even take care of your own presents?  
Subtilizer：Don't you have another job? What are you dazing off about?  
Volo：You accomplished a mission, eh? Then I suppose it wasn't a difficult task.  
Volo：This gift is addressed to you, apparently.  
Volo：The next mission is in? Let's compete to see which of us finishes first.  
Yui：You cleared the Mission! I knew you could do it.  
Yui：You've got a present. Who could it be from?  
Yui：A new Quest came out! I wonder what's in it.  
Yuna：Congrats on clearing that Mission! Any mission thrown your way is no biggie for you!  
Yuna：Looks like a present addressed to you arrived. What could be inside?  
Yuna：I heard a new quest just came in! How about we go see together?  
Yuuki：You cleared the Mission! I knew you could do it.  
Yuuki：Come on, look, you got a present! I'm sure there's something awesome inside.  
Yuuki：I hear a new Quest is out. You're gonna go check it out, right?  
Zink：You accomplished a mission? Good job, I guess.  
Zink：Hey, you got a gift.  
Zink：What's the next assignment? Hurry up and tell me!  
Alice：This soft, stretchy food... It's rather delicious. It’s called mochi?  
Alice：Ah, so it is a ceremony to ward off evil. Is that why you are wearing that mask?  
Alice：I'm supposed to give chocolate...to my beloved... D-don't get cocky! It isn't you! However, if you insist...  
Alice：Amazake...you say? It's rather tasty. May I have another cup?  
Alice：If this is a token of your feelings, then it would be rude to refuse it. Thank you...  
Alice：You lied again. I will not listen to another word you have to say today!  
Alice：I am spending my vacation training to surpass your level. How do you plan on spending it?  
Alice：Thanks to the rain, the trees and flowers are full of life.  
Alice：The milky way, you say? Is it a "way" that cannot be crossed, even by flying dragon?  
Alice：If you insist, I will attend the Summer Festival with you. Now that’s settled...we must make haste!  
Alice：You're stressed over your impending deadlines... Actually, this behavior is very like you.  
Alice：The moon is so bright tonight. I've never seen a moon so big...so divine.  
Alice：Trick or treat... A competition for candy, perhaps?  
Alice：This thing you call "cake" is delicious. I could eat it all day.  
Asuna：Happy New Year! Let's make it a good one.  
Asuna：Even though clean-up can be really annoying, I still want to throw beans for Setsubun.  
Asuna：I made this year's chocolates myself, just like last time. I put in my secret ingredient... Love!  
Asuna：I remember I used to play with Hinamatsuri dolls a lot when I was little.  
Asuna：I'm happy with any gift, as long as it's from you.  
Asuna：Hmmm...I can't think of a fun April Fool's prank to pull.  
Asuna：Where should we go for our week off this year? I vote for a place away from all the crowds.  
Asuna：The rain is like soothing music when you're inside.  
Asuna：If we only got to see each other once a year, I’d miss you so bad.  
Asuna：Want to go to the beach with the gang this summer? I think we'd have a lot of fun!  
Asuna：Today's the last day of summer vacation... I had a lot of fun spending so much time with you.  
Asuna：Good thing it's a clear night. Look how clearly you can see the moon!  
Asuna：I'm sure everyone will show up, so I'll get some candy.  
Asuna：Even though the experience wasn't 100% good... today still marks a special anniversary for me.  
Asuna：You're my Santa.  
Eydis：A new year is upon us. This day always feels poignant to me, no matter how many years pass.  
Eydis：If throwing beans really did make all evil in this world be gone, I can't imagine anything finer.  
Eydis：Oh, is this for me? Actually, I got some for you, too.  
Eydis：Many of the Integrity Knights are female, so I think this custom might catch on.  
Eydis：A gift for the gift-giver? Hehe, I like this custom, too. I wish it happened every month.  
Eydis：Telling lies really does pain my heart, so I don't think I'll do it.  
Eydis：I haven't had a long vacation in a while. So, I'd like to relax in the Cathedral.  
Eydis：Since the rain erases my footprints, it's ideal weather for spying.  
Eydis：The lovers only get to meet once a year? I would die with loneliness.  
Eydis：I can hear the Summer Festival all the way from here. Shall we slip out and take a look?  
Eydis：The break is over, is it? Oh well. Back to work.  
Eydis：I've never stared at the moon at length before. I like it, I could get used to this.  
Eydis：The strange costumes are a bit shocking. But upon closer examination, they're also rather adorable.  
Eydis：So, he's a gentleman in red who flies on horses, to give gifts to children... Are you sure he isn't an Integrity Knight?  
Eugeo：A new year is already here. It seems like only yesterday that we left Rulid.  
Eugeo：Wouldn't the effects of these beans be more powerful if we threw them using Sacred Arts?  
Eugeo：Look at what Tiese gave me. I don't know what to do about this!  
Eugeo：If this is a festival for girls, shouldn't we invite Tiese and Ronie?  
Eugeo：Oh, so you give gifts back to the girls who gave you gifts? Candy would be the best gift, right?  
Eugeo：It's a day where lying is okay? I wonder how that holiday came into being?  
Eugeo：Even if it's a long holiday, I don't want to slack off on my training.  
Eugeo：The life span of foods depletes quickly on rainy days. Be careful.  
Eugeo：Even though it only happens once a year, I'm sure Orihime and Hikoboshi are happy to see each other on Tanabata.  
Eugeo：I can hear the sounds from the summer festival all the way out here. It sounds like so much fun.  
Eugeo：I'm not helping you with your assignment. You knew today was the last day of vacation!  
Eugeo：You're supposed to offer rice cakes to the moon? I guess their life span would last a while in this weather.  
Eugeo：I'm a little too embarrassed to go out in public dressed like this.  
Eugeo：Christmas, huh... It has a nice ring to it. And would you look at that feast!  
Kirito：Happy New Year! Let's make it a good one.  
Kirito：They say if you eat as many beans as your age, you'll have a healthy year.So eat up!  
Kirito：So today is Valentine's Day... I'm getting hungry.  
Kirito：Hinamatsuri is for girls. So how should guys like me celebrate it?  
Kirito：White Day, huh... I always kind of dread it. I never know what to give people.  
Kirito：Hehe. What kind of pranks should I pull today?  
Kirito：Wanna go on an overnight trip?  
Kirito：Ugh... When it's this hot and humid, I just feel like taking off, like, all my clothes.  
Kirito：I was searching for my Orihime. That's why I'm here.  
Kirito：I hear there's a festival.  Let's check it out!  
Kirito：So, hey... Wanna do homework in my room with me?  
Kirito：The moon sure is pretty tonight. It's so romantic... Er, I didn't mean anything by that!  
Kirito：Trick or treeeat!  
Kirito：Today is the day it all began.  
Kirito：Merry Christmas! Let the feast begin!  
Leafa：Happy New Year! I hope everyone has a happy and healthy year!  
Leafa：Setsubun sushi rolls are always impossible to eat in one bite.  
Leafa：I made you a chocolate cake this year. This way, you can easily sneak in bites while you're studying or gaming.  
Leafa：Today is the day where girls are the star, so shower us with affection, okay?  
Leafa：Hehe, thank you. I do love getting thank-you gifts.  
Leafa：I was trying to come up with a good April Fool's prank, and before I knew it, I'd stayed up all night.  
Leafa：I'd love to go away somewhere on vacation with the gang. You know, like Asuna and Liz.  
Leafa：It's really humid today, huh? This laundry won't dry on the line. Can it be summer already?  
Leafa：I hear there's going to be fireworks tonight for Tanabata! Let's go see them!  
Leafa：Hehe, I bought a new swimsuit, so let's all go swimming!  
Leafa：It's sad summer vacation is almost over. Agh. I have to go to school tomorrow.  
Leafa：Remember when we were kids and ate all the rice cakes and got yelled at? Those were the days, huh?  
Leafa：I remember wandering around the neighborhood dressed as ghosts when we were little. That was so much fun.  
Leafa：Today is a day I'll never forget. But...you came back home in the end. So I don't have to cry this year.  
Leafa：Merry Christmas! We've got a big feast, so loosen your belt!  
Lisbeth：Happy New Year! Let's both make it a good one.  
Lisbeth：Wow, you threw those beans like you meant it! Fine. Two can play at that game!  
Lisbeth：Look who's got lots of chocolate again. If you eat aaall of those, you’ll get so big!  
Lisbeth：The box we keep the hina dolls in, doesn't it remind you of a treasure chest?  
Lisbeth：So, for White Day, I'd like cute little accessory or something.  
Lisbeth：Everyone seems to be scheming up some prank. I guess I'd better come up with my own plan.  
Lisbeth：Wanna go somewhere for Golden Week?  
Lisbeth：Eek! Was that thunder?! That sound always freaks me out.  
Lisbeth：If I were Orihime, I'd build a bridge across the Milky Way so I could see Hikoboshi every day, instead of just once a year.  
Lisbeth：I always feel like going to the tropics in the summer. I love how clear the water is down there.  
Lisbeth：You still haven't finished your summer homework? But I thought you said you made yourself a schedule.  
Lisbeth：Every year, I always somehow miss the Moon Festival. Good thing I didn't forget this year.  
Lisbeth：Maybe I'll hold a Halloween event at my shop? Make pumpkin helmets or something.  
Lisbeth：Today is the anniversary of that launch of that game, huh? Looking back now, I actually have some fond memories of that world.  
Lisbeth：You can't have Christmas without a big Christmas cake! Hehe. Now I'm hungry.  
Mary：A new year starts today. Happy New Year! I'm so glad we get to spend it together.  
Mary：Use the fish head as decoration? I don't really mind, but we're eating that for dinner later.  
Mary：You're supposed to make candy? Then we'll need milk, sugar... Oh, why didn't you say so sooner!  
Mary：I'm too young to drink... Oh? I'm allowed to drink this liquor? It's sweet and delicious, you say?  
Mary：I get candy again today? Even though you just gave me some last month? Well, I'm still thankful!  
Mary：A day where it's okay to lie... Isn't that idea itself a lie? It's just wrong!  
Mary：For this extended holiday, all I want is for you and me to spend all our time together.  
Mary：Rain, both yesterday and today! That's bad... Bread and meat have shorter lifespans on rainy days.  
Mary：So we write our wishes on these papers and Stacia grants them? We should place ours at the top.  
Mary：The Summer Festival is the biggest of the year. Food just tastes better when you eat it outdoors!  
Mary：We sure had a lot of fun this summer vacation. I hope we have as much fun together next year.  
Mary：Can we stay outside a bit later than usual tonight? Lunaria is just so beautiful.  
Mary：We're supposed to dress up as monsters today? I'm scared. What if Vecta catches us?  
Mary：There will be a present under my pillow when I wake? I can see why everyone loves this holiday.  
Ronie：Happy New Year! This is an Aincrad Style seasonal greeting, right?  
Ronie：Exorcising evil spirits by throwing beans... An intriguing ceremony indeed.  
Ronie：I see you have a lot of sweets already. Still...here! Have mine, too!  
Ronie：What adorable little dolls. Is this a bridal doll?  
Ronie：You got sweets...for me? Thank you so much! I will cherish this memory always!  
Ronie：You must never tell lies, sir. A day where lies are not taboo? No such day exists!  
Ronie：Just imagine...consecutive days of rest. It feels like a dream.  
Ronie：I don't mind rainy days much, because that means you're in your room.  
Ronie：We write our wishes on the slips of paper? Hehe, this does feel like a fairytale.  
Ronie：It's almost the Summer Festival. Oh, you'll love all the food stalls!  
Ronie：The school assignments just never end... I'll be spending the entire day doing school work.  
Ronie：What a beautiful moon. I'm glad I get to see it, even if I had to break curfew.  
Ronie：Costumes...how amusing! But will you please stop scaring me?  
Ronie：Wow, look at that feast and that cake! I've never attended a banquet so elaborate!  
Silica：Happy New Year! I'm really happy I get to ring it in with you.  
Silica：That demon's mask... It looks real. Too real...  
Silica：I have chocolates, too. Have some, if you'd like!  
Silica：Oh, sure! I could have a little amazake. I love it! So sweet and warm.  
Silica：Today is White Day... So, uh, I have a present for you.  
Silica：Eek, I'm not letting anyone fool me today!  
Silica：I hope we can get the gang together for Golden Week. We could meet up in ALO or something.  
Silica：It's really pouring out there. Let's spend the day indoors.  
Silica：The Tanabata legend... It's very romantic, don't you think?  
Silica：If we're going to the beach, maybe I should get a new swimsuit.  
Silica：Summer homework? Sorry, I already finished mine.  
Silica：You know, there might actually be a virtual world where the moon is made of cheese.  
Silica：I was thinking of dressing up Pina for Halloween this year.  
Silica：Even though I had a lot of traumatic experiences, I'm still glad I lived through SAO.  
Silica：I put reindeer antlers on Pina. How does she look?  
Sinon：Happy New Year! I hope it's an uneventful year this time.  
Sinon：So, the beans you throw on Setsubun are supposed make demons blind? That makes sense. Kind of like tear gas or smoke.  
Sinon：Jeez, stop staring. I can take a hint. Here. Happy Valentine's Day.  
Sinon：I prefer the really tiny Hina dolls. They may not look very powerful, but they're very carefully made.  
Sinon：Thanks. I'll treasure this.  
Sinon：I don't think you need to lie just because it's April Fools Day. I'm up for a fun prank, though.  
Sinon：For Golden Week, let's spend our time in a not-so-crowded place.  
Sinon：Isn't it hard to ride a bike in the rain? Be careful out there.  
Sinon：Orihime and Hikoboshi keep loving each other even though they only meet once a year? That's some strong love right there.  
Sinon：I won this prize at a festival shooting booth. I feel kind of like I scammed the booth, though.  
Sinon：I finished my summer homework ages ago. And I'm not helping you with yours.  
Sinon：The moon looks extra-pretty this year. I wonder why that is...  
Sinon：I don't want any tricks, so here's your treat.  
Sinon：Merry Christmas. Celebrating indoors where it's nice and warm... Can life get any better than that?  
Sortiliena：Have you chosen your New Year's resolution yet? Setting and achieving goals is very important.  
Sortiliena：What an odd custom... Odd, but fun. I don't suppose we could use it in wars, though.  
Sortiliena：You certainly have lots of candy. Was all of it because of that "Valentine's" day of yours?!  
Sortiliena：A festival to celebrate girls growing up? I'm sure I would have loved such a festival as a child.  
Sortiliena：So, this is your thank-you gift? I would have been satisfied seeing you fight again, but...I like this gift, too.  
Sortiliena：We aren't accustomed to lying. It goes against the Taboo Index.  
Sortiliena：A long vacation, you say? Then, perhaps we should travel somewhere to train? We can duel all day, without worrying about time.  
Sortiliena：There is a special way of fighting on rainy days. Come, let's go outside to practice.  
Sortiliena：The lovers only meet once a year? When separated by a wall, I suppose that is inevitable.  
Sortiliena：Swimming in the ocean... That still terrifies me a little. There's no telling what's under the water.  
Sortiliena：The long vacation is coming to an end. I think it's vital to make the most out of one's time off.  
Sortiliena：When I stare at the moon like this, I start remembering things from my past.  
Sortiliena：I am not wearing that...thing! If you want to dress up, dress up alone!  
Sortiliena：So, you exchange gifts? One can read a lot into another person's feelings by examining their gift...  
Suguha：Happy New Year! I hope everyone has a happy and healthy year!  
Suguha：Setsubun sushi rolls are always impossible to eat in one bite.  
Suguha：I made you a chocolate cake this year. This way, you can easily sneak in bites while you're studying or gaming.  
Suguha：Today is the day where girls are the star, so shower us with affection, okay?  
Suguha：Hehe, thank you. I do love getting thank-you gifts.  
Suguha：I was trying to come up with a good April Fool's prank, and before I knew it, I'd stayed up all night.  
Suguha：I'd love to go away somewhere on vacation with the gang. You know, like Asuna and Liz.  
Suguha：It's really humid today, huh? This laundry won't dry on the line. Can it be summer already?  
Suguha：I hear there's going to be fireworks tonight for Tanabata! Let's go see them!  
Suguha：Hehe, I bought a new swimsuit, so let's all go swimming!  
Suguha：It's sad summer vacation is almost over. Agh. I have to go to school tomorrow.  
Suguha：Remember when we were kids and ate all the rice cakes and got yelled at? Those were the days, huh?  
Suguha：I remember wandering around the neighborhood dressed as ghosts when we were little. That was so much fun.  
Suguha：Today is a day I'll never forget. But...you came back home in the end. So I don't have to cry this year.  
Suguha：Merry Christmas! We've got a big feast, so loosen your belt!  
Tiese：It is an honor to ring in the new year with you, sir.  
Tiese：Why are you wearing that mask? Is it an Aincrad tradition?  
Tiese：Do you like the candy I made you?  
Tiese：The clothing on these dolls... It looks Eastern. Is this an Eastern custom?  
Tiese：This gift is...for me? Oh! You're thanking me for the candy. Well, thank you, too, Mentor!  
Tiese：I'm not comfortable with lying, lies hurt others. The Taboo Index says so.  
Tiese：Even on a day of rest, a page must still take care of her master.  
Tiese：The rain just keeps pouring. I hope it doesn't damage the crops.  
Tiese：You write down wishes on slips of paper and offer them to the gods? That's an odd custom, but I like it.  
Tiese：The Summer Festival is upon us. The Academy is taking a holiday then, so... would you like to go to the Festival with me?  
Tiese：I already finished my school work. I knew I needed to be systematic, or I would never finish on time.  
Tiese：What a big moon! Looking at it with you like this, it seems even more beautiful.  
Tiese：You want me to wear this? W-well, at least it doesn't break the Taboo Index.  
Tiese：Today is a day of exchanging gifts, yes? Is it okay if we exchange gifts, too?  
Yuuki：Hey, how much mochi are you putting in the New Year's soup?  
Yuuki：Okay, let's eat all the beans! They're supposed to make you healthier, right?  
Yuuki：All the girls are so hyper today.  
Yuuki：The prince doll is holding a sword in this display, but don't you think the princess should also have a sword?  
Yuuki：I've given gifts for White Day, but I've never really received them.  
Yuuki：Since it's April Fool's Day, do you wanna reverse our advance-guard and rear-guard as a joke?  
Yuuki：Do you mean it?! Are we really going away on vacation together?!  
Yuuki：It’s raining in every world during this season. I like the rain. You can meet all different kinds of enemies.  
Yuuki：Can I write down as many Tanabata wishes as I want? There are so many things I want to wish for!  
Yuuki：I wanna go to the beach and play the watermelon smashing game! Do you think I can use a Sword Skill for that?  
Yuuki：Everyone will be starting school soon. I want to go, too...  
Yuuki：Wow, I didn't know the moon could be so bright. Look at how shiny it's making your hair!  
Yuuki：If you come to my door, there's gonna be nothing but tricks!  
Yuuki：Ooh, what a huge Christmas tree! I've always wanted to have a big tree like this.  
Administrator：I see you're starting to look proud of yourself. Good. Keep encouraging yourself to do better.  
Administrator：You are still not powerful enough to combat forces from the Dark Territory.  
Administrator：You are finally worthy of becoming an Integrity Knight.  
Administrator：You have become quite strong indeed... It's a bit concerning.  
Administrator：Perhaps you've become too strong... Maybe it's time to go back to the drawing board and readjust you.  
Agil：Congrats on ranking up.  
Agil：You got a lot stronger, didn't you? Well, you are a former Assault Team member.  
Agil：You leveled-up again? Hehe, now I can feel safe at night.  
Agil：I don't think I wanna fight you now. You'll probably beat me.  
Agil：Jeez, just how much stronger are you gonna get?  
Alice：You have gotten a bit stronger. That is cause for celebration.  
Alice：Hm...let us have a duel. I suddenly want to test your abilities.  
Alice：The strength with which you strike... I can feel the determination in your heart. I am impressed by how hard you've trained.  
Alice：Victory will no longer come easily to me. Your self-improvement is worthy of praise.  
Alice：You are indeed a knight who will save the Human Realm. I shall assist you in any way I can.  
Alice：You got stronger while I wasn't looking. And I used to be able to beat you in the past!  
Alice：Heeey, you grew another inch! No fair! Don't leave me behind!  
Alice：Hehe, as strong as you've become, I know you'll protect me no matter what.  
Alice：You've gotten so heroic, I almost didn't recognize you...  
Alice：Even the Dark Territory's minions don't stand a chance against you now!  
Argo：You leveled-up. Cool. Did my intel help you out?  
Argo：Good. Keep getting stronger and gather great info for me.  
Argo：So, hey... You must have some info on high level bosses by now, right?  
Argo：You sure got a lot stronger while I wasn't looking. You surprised me.  
Argo：Hehe. You sure got strong. I'm really proud of you.  
Asuna：Congrats on ranking up! Now I've got to catch up.  
Asuna：You got stronger yet again. Don't get too much better than me, okay?  
Asuna：Let's celebrate your rank increase! Come on, it's a special day.  
Asuna：You're always just a couple steps ahead of me, pushing me to better myself.  
Asuna：I knew it all along. You're the strongest guy in the world!  
Bercouli：Great form. I can tell you're training.  
Bercouli：I thought your form was improving... And it is. At an alarmingly fast rate.  
Bercouli：You just keep getting stronger and stronger. Keep it up and you’ll surpass me one day.  
Bercouli：I never imagined you'd actually become this powerful. I'm truly happy for you.  
Bercouli：Heh! There's nothing more for me to say. At your current level, even I can't beat you.  
Cardinal：You're proving to have potential. Keep at your training.  
Cardinal：Have you gotten a bit stronger? This is why I love humans. So amusing.  
Cardinal：The look in your eye is much more earnest now. Now you just might stand a chance against an Integrity Knight.  
Cardinal：I, too, can see how strong you have become. I am proud of you.  
Cardinal：Of all the humans I've encountered, you are now the strongest.  
Deusolbert：You've become rather strong. I will have to keep on my toes.  
Deusolbert：Your sword movements are sharp and difficult to read. It is clear that you have trained well.  
Deusolbert：What power... I hope you intend to use it to serve the Axiom Church.  
Deusolbert：Humph... Though it pains me to admit it, your powers are true. I can't compete.  
Deusolbert：Perhaps you have climbed to the top of human potential.  
Eydis：Not bad. But, I know you can do better.  
Eydis：To be honest, I really respect your dedication and hard work.  
Eydis：You've come a long way... Would you like to spar with me?  
Eydis：Impressive. Just as I knew you would be. Look at how far you've come.  
Eydis：Describing you as the ‘strongest’, it fits perfectly! Looks like I'll have to work hard to catch up to you.  
Eldrie：Looks like your training is paying off.  
Eldrie：I see your skills have improved.  
Eldrie：I must train harder to keep up... As your mentor, I am embarrassed.  
Eldrie：Even if I use Perfect Weapon Control, you could still hold your own in a fight for a while. That is just how much power I sense from you.  
Eldrie：You have now surpassed me greatly. However, I will be your equal once again someday.  
Entokia：Interesting fighting stance, eh, Master? I always get a kick out of watching you fight.  
Entokia：An impressively sharp attack. You could knock out a whole horde of enemies in one blow.  
Entokia：If I were as strong as you, maybe I'd be able to wield my Roaring Thunder Spear properly.  
Entokia：Whoa, Master! What impressive swordsmanship! For a moment, I didn't recognize you.  
Entokia：Even if the Integrity Knights ganged up against you, you still might defeat them, Master.  
Eugeo：We're both really getting into shape, huh? But, we need to keep getting stronger!  
Eugeo：Wow! Those were some impressive moves! I need to keep training.  
Eugeo：You're strong, just like I thought. I'd like to at least win 1 out of 3 matches!  
Eugeo：Oof...since when did you get this strong? The gap between our levels has widened a lot.  
Eugeo：That's why you're my trainer! But I promise I'll catch up to you someday.  
Eugeo：Wait a minute, have you been practicing sword fighting in secret? Your axe swinging is a bit weak.  
Eugeo：You got a lot stronger while I wasn't looking. I'd better step up so I don't get left behind.  
Eugeo：Your muscles have gotten a little bigger. Hmmm...I'd better train harder.  
Eugeo：How can I get stronger like you? I'll need to think about that.  
Eugeo：I've been struggling to keep up with you all this time, but I can never fully catch up.  
Fanatio：You still aren't taking this seriously, are you? I want to see your full potential soon.  
Fanatio：Very sharp attacks indeed. I could learn a thing or two from you.  
Fanatio：I am impressed. Not many knights have your skill.  
Fanatio：Your strength cannot be matched... even by myself... But someday, I will catch up to you!  
Fanatio：You must have trained your body so hard that your mind reached a meditative state I truly admire your strong will!  
Fizel：I see you've gotten a bit stronger. But I'm still more powerful than you.  
Fizel：Oooh, you look pretty tough. I'm sure you're stronger than the average Integrity Knight.  
Fizel：Hmph! Don't pose and strut just because you've put on a little muscle!  
Fizel：Yeah...you're even stronger than I am now. Grrr, how vexing!  
Fizel：Yes, now I could never defeat you. But the Lady Administrator could.  
Iskahn：Ha, at that rate... I can take you down with one punch! You need to get even stronger!  
Iskahn：This is still far from enough! You need to aim even higher levels of strength.  
Iskahn：Looks like you can see a little better. If I were to be your opponent, you could probably take a couple of punches from me.  
Iskahn：I knew you had it in you! There was no doubt in my mind you would make it this far.  
Iskahn：I acknowledge your strength. But, I will still get stronger! When that happens, we will fight! You can count on that.  
Kirito：Whoa! Is it just me, or have you been getting jacked?  
Kirito：Your form is getting really good. Guess I need to up my game.  
Kirito：You've been looking like a real swordsman lately! That's a compliment, believe me!  
Kirito：You totally won the last match. But I'm not letting that happen again today!  
Kirito：I have nothing left to teach you. You should be proud of yourself.  
Kirito：You've gotten stronger recently. Okay! Looks like I need to up my game!  
Kirito：Don't you think we've gotten strong enough to hold our own against the beasts out there?  
Kirito：Hey...did you get taller? Darn it! You're beating me!  
Kirito：If we keep training like this, cutting down that big tree won’t just be a far-fetched dream!  
Kirito：You really have gotten really strong. I'm impressed! You're just like a real swordsman.  
Klein：Ooh! You leveled-up? Not too shabby, dude.  
Klein：Dang, you're strong! But not as strong as me!  
Klein：Whoa, dude, you leveled up again? A little fast, don't you think?  
Klein：Jeez, already so strong? Even in this world, you're just as stoic as ever.  
Klein：Congrats, man. I'll never joke about how weak you are again.  
Kureha：Looks like you ranked up! But, even at that level you won't beat me.  
Kureha：Such an accurate attack... I must learn that move.  
Kureha：...Yes, you've gotten quiet strong, I see. I have confidence in myself, but in terms of if I'll win...  
Kureha：You really are an amazing one... But that doesn't I'll give up so easily!  
Kureha：No surprise there! I'll make sure to have you on my side in the next battle!  
Leafa：Your rank went up! Way to go! All that training paid off.  
Leafa：Wow! How'd you get so strong so fast?  
Leafa：You moved really well today. I need to up my game.  
Leafa：Man, no one can hold a candle to you anymore. You're totally our leader!  
Leafa：Is this what a master looks like? Your moves are almost like dancing... O, teach me your ways!  
Linel：You've gotten stronger, I see. I hope to become stronger, too, so I can become a proper Integrity Knight.  
Linel：At your current level, you can certainly hold your own against an Integrity Knight.  
Linel：How many more must I kill to become as strong as you?  
Linel：My mistake. You surely trained very hard to become that strong.  
Linel：At your level, you might even be able to defeat a guardian dragon. Oh, how I envy you.  
Lisbeth：You climbed up in rank? Okay then, wanna test out this equipment?  
Lisbeth：Hmmm. Well, look at you. Got all strong while my back was turned.  
Lisbeth：Oh, yes, yes, you've gotten much stronger. Funny, seeing as how this quest just popped up that I want to go on, but the enemies are too powerful for meee...  
Lisbeth：Well, after all that hard training, of course you got stronger. Congrats.  
Lisbeth：Let me make you a custom sword again someday. Promise me.  
Medina：Your swordsmanship has improved a little. But you still have things you need to work on. You got that, right?  
Medina：Hmm. This sword's not too bad. Next time we face each other, it would be smart to sharpen up those skills!  
Medina：All of a sudden, where did that power come from...? There's no way I can lose! Hurry up and challenge me!  
Medina：It's unfortunate, but your power is the real deal. That means your sentiment is also real.  
Medina：There is no swordsman of your caliber in this world. Let's take a moment to seriously admire that.  
Nergius：Do you honestly take pride in your mediocre sword skills? You cannot be serious.  
Nergius：Your fighting stance has indeed improved. A testament to hours of dedicated practice.  
Nergius：Swordsman Delegate... At last, you have received a sword worthy of your title.  
Nergius：To be honest, it still surprises me, even now, just how strong a person can become.  
Nergius：I see. Forgive me. I, Nergius, shall pledge my sword to you.  
Philia：Fufu, I just know that you'll get even stronger.  
Philia：I do think you've gotten quiet strong. However, you're not quiet there yet.  
Philia：I guess I can count on you afterall. But, I don't need someone to only rely on.  
Philia：You've really been training this whole time. That strength of yours, is proof of it.  
Philia：Since the time when you rescued me, you've been a part of me... A fragment of me.  
Premiere：Congratulations on ranking up. Let's keep doing our best for the next round.  
Premiere：Congratulations on ranking up. What kind of meal would you like to celebrate this special occasion?  
Premiere：I've never heard of anyone ranking up that high before. You're quiet the strong one, aren't you.  
Premiere：Seeing you get this strong, makes me a bit proud ...I wonder why?  
Premiere：I can't measure your strength anymore. That makes... me so happy.  
Rain：Hey, when did you get that move? Would you mind teaching it to me next time?  
Rain：Just as I suspected, you leveled up pretty fast. I'll make sure I won't lose!  
Rain：Wow! How'd you get so strong so fast?   
Rain：As anticipated, you're a strong one... There's no way that I can do one-on-one combat with you.  
Rain：Not only do you have physical strength, but a strong heart as well.  
Renly：You've gotten really strong, lately! Everyone is surprised. Especially how fast you've become.  
Renly：That's amazing! I'm surprised that you've gotten this strong!  
Renly：To think that you've gotten this strong! I also need to get stronger so that I don't fall behind!  
Renly：Wow...  Just how much stronger are you gonna get?  
Renly：I probably won't be able to win anymore... But I will try my best to not leave you behind!  
Ronie：I learned firsthand just how powerful you are!  
Ronie：You've become even stronger, I see. Congratulations!  
Ronie：I...can't even see your sword! It's moving so swiftly.  
Ronie：I would love to learn from you, and gain even a fraction of your strength.  
Ronie：I wonder if there is an opponent who is truly worthy of you?  
Sachi：You're just getting stronger and stronger. I need to take a leaf from your book!  
Sachi：Since you've leveled-up, I guess that means you're venturing to more dangerous areas. Be careful, okay?  
Sachi：Someday, I'll have to get as strong as you.  
Sachi：Congrats on leveling up! Can I train with you for a bit? I want to become stronger, too.  
Sachi：You must've worked harder than I could imagine. I really do respect you.  
Sheyta：I will cut you down if you stand in front of me. Toughen up if you dare to do so.  
Sheyta：You've toughened up a bit. But you're still soft in a lot of spots.  
Sheyta：Looks like you've gradually grown stronger in some places.  
Sheyta：...Whenever I am with you, I really can fight with all my strength.  
Sheyta：You don't have seem to have any weak spots. ...I will fight you, properly. Show me what you're made of.  
Selka：Well, I'm quite impressed. If you keep it up, you might be able to become a knight.  
Selka：You just might be the strongest in Rulid now!  
Selka：Will you protect everyone in Rulid, now that you've gotten stronger? Thank you. That's so sweet of you.  
Selka：You've gotten even stronger. I feel so distant from you now.  
Selka：No matter how powerful you get, you're still a slob when you eat. That's actually reassuring.  
Seven：I see you've gotten a bit stronger. But I'm still more powerful than you!  
Seven：Fuun, so you do got it! Don't get cocky though!   
Seven：Fufu, you're going to get stronger, as I suspected.   
Seven：You wouldn't be able to get this strong if you hadn't trained for it. You're quiet a stoic one.  
Seven：I have nothing else to say to you. I need to learn from you and get stronger myself.  
Shasta：Your sword skills are promising. Come, try and strike at me!  
Shasta：Hm.. As I thought. There is quality in there. However, it's still not enough. You can still get stronger.  
Shasta：This strength is different from what I saw before. You truly are an interesting one.  
Shasta：I don't think there is anyone as well-trained as you even among the Dark Knights! I'm impressed.  
Shasta：You are probably stronger than even me now. You are more than worthy to continue on my will.  
Silica：You got a higher rank already? It's gonna take me a little while longer.  
Silica：Wow, I'm impressed! Look how quickly you improved!  
Silica：You got stronger again, I see. Would you help me practice my form sometime?  
Silica：Wow, your rank is so high already! I need to put in more effort.  
Silica：You've always been the strongest swordsman to me!  
Sinon：Not bad. But you're going at a good pace.  
Sinon：You've gotten stronger. I knew I saw potential in you.  
Sinon：That was a display of your true skills, I guess. Hehe. But I'm sure you can get stronger.  
Sinon：If we fought 1-1, I'd lose. But I've got some fight in me yet...  
Sinon：It's like we're on whole different levels now. I'm honored to fight with you.  
Sortiliena：That was a rather sharp hit. Keep it up and you're sure to become stronger.  
Sortiliena：I hear you've been training relentlessly. And it shows.  
Sortiliena：I knew you could do it. But this is not your limit, you can aim even higher!  
Sortiliena：Splendid. And I thought I was disciplined.  
Sortiliena：I think I would be hard pressed to find someone as skilled as you, in all the world. I am truly proud to be your friend.  
Sortiliena：You've been working hard lately. I can't let you out-perform me!  
Sortiliena：You look so dashing when you train. It's very inspiring to me.  
Sortiliena：Your form...is very intriguing. Would you teach it to me?  
Sortiliena：I may no longer be able to defeat you now. Would you give me a workout?  
Sortiliena：I hope that someday, I can stand beside you as an equal.  
Strea：This is great! I feel like I've gotten stronger!  
Strea：You're quiet the sharp one! It's amazing how much you've honed your skills!  
Strea：You got a lot stronger while I wasn't looking. I'd better step up so I don't get left behind.  
Strea：Uwa. I conceed! I won't be your enemy! You're quiet the strong one.  
Strea：You'll only continue to get stronger going forward. I want to see that through to the end.  
Suguha：Your rank went up! Way to go! All that training paid off.  
Suguha：Wow! How'd you get so strong so fast?  
Suguha：You moved really well today. I need to up my game.  
Suguha：Man, no one can hold a candle to you anymore. You're totally our leader!  
Suguha：Is this what a master looks like? Your moves are almost like dancing... O, teach me your ways!  
Tiese：I find it difficult to believe that you rank below the First and the Second Seats.  
Tiese：You never neglect your daily training, I see. I need to be more like you.  
Tiese：Your attacks are so swift! I can't block them.  
Tiese：Now I see how powerful Aincrad style is. I would love to be able to master your moves!  
Tiese：Please, let me keep striving to be as skilled as you!  
Tiese：Your sword is so fast... You'll definitely get even stronger!  
Tiese：What technique! Looks like you've been honing your sword skills this whole time!  
Tiese：You are probably already stronger than my teacher...  
Tiese：If someone like you entered the Imperial Tournament, you would definitely get the title!  
Tiese：I've been watching this whole time. Seeing you get stronger makes me want to hurry up and catch up!  
Vassago：There's no point in ranking up. Because you'll quietly be killed by me.  
Vassago：Tch. Looks like you ranked up. Not such a big deal, but it'll be a problem later on.  
Vassago：If you're going to kill me, better do it now than never. Because I can't guarantee that you'll still have hands to use!  
Vassago：Sorry, but I advise that you stop doing 1 on 1 matches. That is, if you get the chance.  
Vassago：Very interesting. You've gotten so strong! I can't wait to kill you with these hands!  
Subtilizer：Your strength still has a ways to go.  
Subtilizer：Well, well. Things just got interesting. If you keep training at that rate, your soul will surely be delicious.  
Subtilizer：Perfection. Adjust the taste of your soul with care.  
Yui：How impressive! You just keep getting stronger and stronger!  
Yui：Wow...I can't even see your sword, it's moving so swiftly!  
Yui：Now you're definitely strong enough to protect Mom from anything!  
Yui：I've watched you struggle all this time. I'm glad your efforts finally paid off.  
Yui：Hehe, no matter how strong you get, you're still always so gentle with me. I love you so much!  
Yuna：Congrats on ranking up! You're quickly getting stronger, eh.  
Yuna：Looks like you ranked up again! Fufu, I can count on your for the next step!  
Yuna：You're so strong now! Any enemy that pops out won't stand a chance!  
Yuna：Seeing you get this strong, makes me happy as though it's me who's done the work. Strange... isn't it?  
Yuna：I'm sure that you had many difficulties and challenges making it this far ...but in the end you overcame all of them.  
Yuuki：Looks like you just ranked up. But you’ve still got a long way to go!  
Yuuki：Oh, you got stronger again? But you still don't hold a candle to me!  
Yuuki：Judging by your sword's speed, it looks like you raised your rank again. Okay, I challenge you to a duel!  
Yuuki：When I see how strong you've become, I can't stop fangirling! But I'm gonna be just as strong as you!  
Yuuki：You're out of my league now. But someday I'll catch up to you!  
Dee Eye Ell：Did you find it? I did not think that you could detect my technique...
