Iskahn：I finally found you! Come on over here! Fight me!  
Iskahn：It's been awhile since we've last met, but you haven't changed one bit. You need to eat more meat!  
Lipia：Long time no see, my Lord. I am always glad to see that you have not changed one bit.  
Renly：Oh...s-sorry! Looks like I just got lost.  
Renly：It's been awhile since I've talked like this. ...It's a good feeling.  
Sheyta：...I apologize for intruding.  
Sheyta：Who is it? ...Was that you?  
Tiese：My apologies for the suddenness... Did I get in the way?  
Tiese：Um...Could you do sword practice with me?  
Vassago：...What are you looking at? Or are you interested in getting a taste of my 'Mate Chopper'?  
Vassago：Wow! You noticed my presence... How did you know?  
Subtilizer：...Careful. Next time you show your face in front of me, I'll chop you up without a warning.  
Subtilizer：Tch. I wasn't planning on coming to a place like this, but...  
Administrator：Whenever I watch the baby golden flying foxes in the spring, I start to feel sentimental.  
Administrator：How inconvenient to feel the heat. I wish I could make some weather adjustments.  
Administrator：A storm is coming. This tower should be all right, but I want you to check and make sure.  
Administrator：Another year is ending. But this world will continue to cycle through the same time, year after year.  
Agil：Wanna go on a picnic with the gang soon?  
Agil：My hairstyle is nice and cool for hot days. You should try it.  
Agil：Autumn does have lots of tasty produce. Maybe I should come up with some new menu items.  
Agil：On cold days like this, business sure is slow.  
Alice：I love the young, fresh new buds of spring. I hope they grow up big and strong.  
Alice：Don't cry over a little summer heat. Shake it off with a vigorous spirit!  
Alice：So you have Harvest Festivals in autumn. I heard that the fruit in particular is quite delicious.  
Alice：Whenever I see ice on the water's surface, I feel a pain in my heart. Perhaps it is from the cold air...  
Alice：I love spring. It's so pretty, with all the flowers.  
Alice：Oooh, the water looks so cool. I wish I could just jump right in!  
Alice：I'm testing out a new pie recipe tomorrow! Lots of fruit is in season right now, so it's going to be a good one!  
Alice：Haaah... Oooh, look at how white my breath is!  
Argo：Spring feels like the start of a new year. So, Happy New Year, I guess?  
Argo：Whenever the weather gets hot, I get a lot of occult intel for some reason.  
Argo：You wanna see the autumn leaves? Not really my thing. But some juicy intel just came in...  
Argo：I'm really grateful for my hood on cold days. Wanna try it on for size?  
Asuna：What do you want me to put in our cherry blossom picnic lunch?  
Asuna：Just because it's summer, that's no excuse to go full-slob on me.  
Asuna：Why does food have to taste so good in the fall? Now I want to eat all the things!  
Asuna：Aren't you cold riding a bike in the winter? I'd freeze my butt off.  
Bercouli：It's the perfect season for flying. The views are great, and it's not too chilly.  
Bercouli：It’s too hot to get anything done. Want to go swimming?  
Bercouli：It's harvest season, I hear. Any good fruit out there?  
Bercouli：I'm not a big fan of cold weather. My dragon's wings get weak, too.  
Cardinal：Animals born in the spring... Why are they so adorable?  
Cardinal：This library is never too cold nor too hot. Though at times...it can feel empty.  
Cardinal：I shouldn't have any memories from my past life, but I always feel melancholy during this season. A surreal feeling indeed.  
Cardinal：I would love to be able to touch snow just once, with my own hands.  
Deusolbert：Whenever I see spring's colors, my heart aches for no reason.  
Deusolbert：Integrity Knights have no time to waste complaining about the weather.  
Deusolbert：When the nights are long, one must be vigilant.  
Deusolbert：When the world is white, beholding the landscape from a dragon's back is a lovely thing indeed.  
Eydis：When little flower buds pop out, it truly feels like spring is here.  
Eydis：Never go outside on hot days. That's the best policy.  
Eydis：During this time of year, the eastern mountains turn red. Would you like to go see them with me?  
Eydis：The lake's surface is frozen. That's just how cold it is today.  
Eldrie：I do like the spring custom of drinking wine whilst admiring the blossoms.  
Eldrie：Why am I sweating, you ask? I was just taking some practice-swings. A knight's training is never finished.  
Eldrie：The cattleya really shine this time of year. Cattleyas are the prettiest among the Four Holy Flowers.  
Eldrie：It is because we are in this season that we must remain ever vigilant with the Dark forces.  
Entokia：What a nice, warm day. Days like this are just perfect for napping. Wouldn't you agree, Master?  
Entokia：I actually thrive on hot days. Something about moving my body and sweating really hits the spot!  
Entokia：Ack, it's getting colder. On days like this, it's best to eat something warm and go straight to bed.  
Entokia：I'd love to just skip work when it snows...but I know I can't. Let's just get this over with.  
Eugeo：When the fields are full of flowers, that's a sign that warm weather is finally returning.  
Eugeo：When the weather gets hot, I always remember that day. The day where I was paralyzed with fear and hated myself for it...  
Eugeo：We should go out fruit-picking. A lot of fruits get ripe this time of year.  
Eugeo：Swinging my axe in the winter was always tough, but swinging a sword feels good.  
Eugeo：Whenever this season rolls around, I always think about a new year coming to life.  
Eugeo：Wow, I'm soaked in sweat. Let's take a little break.  
Eugeo：It's finally gotten more comfortable outside. But the wind is a bit chilly.  
Eugeo：I always get excited for the first snowfall. I wonder why that is?  
Fanatio：A season filled with rebirth... How it does lift the spirit.  
Fanatio：No matter how hot the weather is, I refuse to remove my armor!  
Fanatio：Listening to the sound of the wind makes life feel rather bleak.  
Fanatio：When it snows, the roads are slippery with ice. We must train further for this terrain.  
Fizel：Now that the weather is warmer, it's easier to get up in the morning.  
Fizel：These nun's robes get hot in the summer. I wish the fabric were thinner.  
Fizel：The leaves change color in the fall, don't they? It's not because they’re...poisoned, is it?  
Fizel：Stone buildings are so cold! I want to warm up in a nice, hot bath!  
Kirito：It's prime napping season right now. Let's go have a nap over there.  
Kirito：You know the perfect thing for a hot day like this? Ice cream.  What's your favorite flavor? I'll buy you some.  
Kirito：The red and orange leaves sure are pretty. Want to go out and look at them?  
Kirito：Did you know you can see the stars the best in winter? Wanna go stargazing tonight?  
Kirito：I love this time of year. Not too cold, not too hot... Why can't it be spring all the time?  
Kirito：The life of food depletes quickly on days like this. We'd better eat quickly before it goes bad.  
Kirito：Aw, it's gotten cold again. I don't exactly hate the cold air, but...  
Kirito：My hands sure hurt when I use the axe on cold days like this. When it hits the tree, my hands sting.  
Klein：I like how warm spring is... But it always makes me so sleepy.  
Klein：Man, it sure is hot. I wish we had air conditioning.  
Klein：Hey, how about we exercise outside today? I think people do sometimes say "Autumn is for sports"?  
Klein：Brrr, so cold! On days like this, it's best to just stay inside.  
Kureha：Spring is the season for meeting people and breakups... It's a season that really stirs the heart.  
Kureha：It's pretty hot, ain't it. A day like this makes me want to go play in the river or lake.  
Kureha：Looking at the leaves turn color makes me a little happy. It's because I'm a 'Kureha'.  
Kureha：Winter means being confined to your house, like in a cage. My skills will get dull!  
Leafa：Today is a perfect day for a picnic under the blossoms! Let's all get there before all the petals fall!  
Leafa：I used to hate summer, but now that I can swim, I love it!  
Leafa：Whenever this season rolls around, I can't help but think that this was when you left and also when you came home.  
Leafa：The dojo is always freezing in the winter. Especially in the morning. I feel like my toes will freeze off.  
Linel：Many flowers bloom during the spring, yes? I would love to go outside just once to see them.  
Linel：I've gotten a little sweaty. I'll just go change my clothes.  
Linel：In autumn, meals at the Church are a bit tastier. I look forward to that.  
Linel：When the weather is cold, summoning a thermal element helps keep you warm.  
Lisbeth：I hate how spring is warm one day, cold the next. It's a struggle to figure out which uniform to wear. Boys don't know how easy they have it.  
Lisbeth：When summer rolls around, I always crave shaved ice, the fluffy kind!  
Lisbeth：Autumn always makes me feel kinda melancholy. Wow, eew. That sounded cheesy.  
Lisbeth：Nothing says winter better than hot pot! Silica and I are having a hot pot party. You should come!  
Medina：I notice a lot of cats in the spring. Because that's when the kittens start to get bigger. It's something I enjoy looking at every day.  
Medina：Will it get cooler with chill and wind elements? You must be at a high level to think of something like that!  
Medina：The scattered leaves will help become nourishment for the flowers to bloom next season. I also want to be like that...  
Medina：It's cold so don't you want to step outside? What are you going to do with something so cowardly? Hurry up and get to the front!  
Nergius：The weather has finally warmed up. The peasants will likely become more active now.  
Nergius：It is so hot that even Shionade is exhausted. Perhaps I should take them swimming.  
Nergius：Solus is starting to set much earlier now. The peasants will likely begin the harvest soon.  
Nergius：Green onions are indeed ripe in the winter, but please, stop drooling over my hair.  
Philia：Looking at the cherry blossoms in the spring... Sure is a calm season. Ehehe...Doesn't suit me, don't you think?  
Philia：This summer heat makes me sweat so much that I want to jump straight into the sea!  
Philia：There are a lot of delicious food in the fall! I love it! I wonder what I should have today.  
Philia：I don't do well with the cold, but I do like the snow. Somehow, the world covered in white is such a beautiful thing.  
Premiere：Spring signals the start of a new year. Same meaning as my name: Premiere... Somehow that makes me happy.  
Premiere：I heard that you wear less layers when it's hot. Should I also do the same?  
Premiere：I hear 'seasonal' a lot during this tim. I thought it was some sort of delicious food when I first heard it.  
Premiere：Even though snow is just frozen water crystals, it really makes me recall many emotions. I feel a little strange...  
Rain：I kind of like spring showers. Don't you think it's like listening to a gentle song?  
Rain：I want to go to the beach, but I'm not the sportive type... Would it be ok if I don't swim?  
Rain：In Russia, we can see the our own breath in this temperature. Thinking of that cold makes me feel a little nostalgic.  
Rain：In the winter time, there are days where you can see a huge rainbow. ...Someday, I hope to go see it with you.  
Ronie：Now that the weather is warm, I would love to have another picnic with our friends.  
Ronie：Mentor, don't you find your black clothes a little too hot for today?  
Ronie：Even fallen leaves serve a purpose. They will feed next year's new leaves.  
Ronie：The winter sky is so high... I would love to fly up there someday.  
Sachi：I'd love to have a picnic under the blossoms again sometime.  
Sachi：It sure is hot today! I'm all sticky with sweat.  
Sachi：Autumn's winds are so cold. So...can I come a bit closer?  
Sachi：Winter is cold, but I don't mind. The world is beautiful when it's covered in white.  
Selka：Calves are born in the summer, so it's a busy season for me.  
Selka：It's almost time for the Solstice Festival. Hehe, I can't wait to see what booths they have this year.  
Selka：I'm glad the weather is cool enough now that food has a longer shelf life.  
Selka：Everyone else seems to love the snow, but with frozen roads, it has its own share of problems.  
Seven：I wonder why spring showers feel so gentle.  
Seven：It's pretty hot, isn't it? I can't operate in this heat... Let's call it a day already!  
Seven：I heard that there are a lot of delicious food in the fall in Japan! I wonder what I should eat today?  
Seven：The cold is a bitter thing, but I feel like it clears your mind. That's why, I like winter.  
Silica：Spring is filled with all sorts of hellos and goodbyes. It makes me feel so young, like I have to grow up.  
Silica：Wow! Now that's a big watermelon. I can't even lift it.  
Silica：The fruit is so tasty in the fall, that me and Liz will go out to eat some.  
Silica：Oh, man...this blanket is so warm, I can't get out from under it.  
Sinon：Seeing you looking so sleepy is making me sleepy, too. Is it the spring air, I wonder?  
Sinon：When each day is hot like this, I feel like retreating into the virtual world.  
Sinon：Do you not have a coat? It's supposed to be chilly today. Here, take this. I don't want you catching cold.  
Sinon：Cold days remind me of the incident... But I'm over it now. You keep me present.  
Sortiliena：Whenever spring rolls around, I remember meeting you for the first time. I don't think I'll ever forget it.  
Sortiliena：Phew, I worked up quite a sweat. Helping me change clothes... is not in a page's job description. Got it?  
Sortiliena：We attempt to slice falling leaves with our swords? Amusing. I like this challenge.  
Sortiliena：Whenever I train in the cold like this, I feel both my heart and mind tighten up.  
Sortiliena：Time really does fly! I blinked and spring had already arrived.  
Sortiliena：Training on hot days does make me self-conscious about my sweat.  
Sortiliena：Slash a falling leaf? Easy! All I need is a sharp sword...  
Sortiliena：Many people skip practice on cold days, which means it's just an opportunity for me to get in extra training!  
Strea：It's gotten warm outside. Let's go view the cherry blossoms with everyone next time! That will be fun, for sure.  
Strea：Summer means going to the beach! Let's all put on some swimsuits and head to the sea!  
Strea：Looking at the leaves fall off the trees is a little sad, don't you think?  
Strea：Uhh... Today is pretty cold. Hey, could we get just a little closer?  
Suguha：Today is a perfect day for a picnic under the blossoms! Let's all get there before all the petals fall!  
Suguha：I used to hate summer, but now that I can swim, I love it!  
Suguha：Whenever this season rolls around, I can't help but think that this was when you left and also when you came home.  
Suguha：The dojo is always freezing in the winter. Especially in the morning. I feel like my toes will freeze off.  
Tiese：Whenever this season rolls around, I'm reminded of the day we first met.  
Tiese：Solus' rays are so harsh today. I feel as though I'm going to faint.  
Tiese：Whenever I see the naked trees, I feel a little lonely.  
Tiese：What a cold day! Just feel how icy my hands are.  
Tiese：Finally, the snow is gone! It would be great if this meant that it gets warmer.  
Tiese：Swinging a sword in this heat is tough...  
Tiese：Looking at the leaves fall off the trees is so sad...  
Tiese：Things get harder when it starts snowing, but I can't help but get a little excited.  
Yui：Let's go see the cherry blossoms when they bloom! Mom will make us a picnic.  
Yui：Summer is indeed the season for beaches. Does the Alicization world have oceans?  
Yui：The weather is getting cold, so bundle up when you go out, okay?  
Yuna：A lot of flowers blossom in the spring, huh. Doesn't it seem like everyone is singing?  
Yuna：Today's a scorcher, isn't it. Try not to get too caught up in the game and stay hydrated.  
Yuna：For some reason when it's the fall, I want to read books. I guess it's called 'fall reading'!?  
Yuna：Since snow is the same color as my hair, I like winter. It's a little bit troublesome if it snows a lot, but...  
Yuuki：Whenever I see fresh, new buds on the trees in spring, I just feel warm and fuzzy inside.  
Yuuki：I love summer because that's when the cotton flowers bloom!  
Yuuki：You know how in autumn, the wind kinda goes "wooo" through the house? Just hearing that makes me shiver.  
Yuuki：On cold days like this, I always feel like being around people.  
Administrator：I need to sleep still. You understand, don't you?  
Administrator：Come. Wake up. I hope the...adjustments...I made worked out well.  
Administrator：Don't wake me unless it’s truly important.  
Administrator：My, my. Isn’t someone rebellious? Hehe... just what kind of boy are you?  
Administrator：Being awake today after a long sleep was very tiring.  
Administrator：What do you want at this ungodly hour?  
Administrator：You bad boy, barging in on me at night. Somebody needs to be disciplined.  
Administrator：This world is mine. I won't surrender it to anyone. Neither to the outside world nor the forces of Darkness.  
Agil：I don't like mornings. Running this bar, I have to stay up late by necessity.  
Agil：The best cure for sleepiness is a shower. It really clears the head.  
Agil：Have lunch at my place.  
Agil：I've got some new menu items. Wanna taste them for me?  
Agil：This is the time of day when my job really gets busy.  
Agil：Be careful when you're out walking at night.  
Agil：Well, it's about time to close shop. You should call it a night, too.  
Agil：If you go to bed this late, you'll be a tired wreck tomorrow.  
Operator：Good morning.  
Operator：It is almost time for lunch. Make sure you have your rest.  
Operator：It is almost time for bed. Please, don't stay up too late.  
Operator：Did you also come out here for a little night air?  
Airy：Good morning.  
Airy：It is almost time for lunch. Make sure you have your rest.  
Airy：It is almost time for bed. Please, don't stay up too late.  
Airy：Did you also come out here for a little night air?  
Alice：Good morning. What a lovely morning it is. And I'm full of energy.  
Alice：Why don't you get out of bed already? Sloth is one of the deadly sins.  
Alice：Breakfast is very important. Especially if you have hard work to do.  
Alice：I was going to wash Amayori today. The warmth feels so soothing.  
Alice：You never cease to frustrate me. How can you sleep in the middle of the day?  
Alice：Since the sun is so bright today, I will let my sword bathe in her abundant light.  
Alice：Good work today. Make sure you rest up well tonight.  
Alice：Roses seem even more fragrant at night. Though, perhaps it's just my imagination.  
Alice：Sometimes I realize how surreal it is to spend an entire day with you like this.  
Alice：You can't sleep? Serves you right for napping during the day.  
Alice：I was dreaming. I don't remember much about it, but it was a warm dream about days past...  
Alice：I'm going out for a little night air. Would you...care to join me?  
Alice：Wake up, wake up, wake up! It's already morning.  
Alice：Any plans today?  
Alice：Here, I've brought your lunch. It's delicious...I hope.  
Alice：We used to pick flowers a lot when we were little... I really miss it.  
Alice：Wow, were you really up this late working? You're a trooper.  
Alice：So, how was your day? Wanna talk about it?  
Alice：Being up this late at night is kind of exciting, huh?  
Alice：We really should get some sleep now. I don't want to ruin tomorrow's fun.  
Alicia：Good! Morniiing! What a great day!  
Alicia：Mmm, I'm getting sleepy. Think I'll take a cat nap.  
Alicia：Wanna go to bed? It's gotten pretty late.  
Alicia：Hehe, seeing you at night like this feels kinda naughty. I like it.  
Argo：No rest for the informant.  
Argo：Hey. 'Morning. Here to buy some juicy gossip?  
Argo：Info gathering takes a lot of leg work. Nah, it's cool. I can go by myself.  
Argo：Look at you, napping out in the open... That's ballsy.  
Argo：Lots of people are after me, so I have to be on my guard at night.  
Argo：Nights always bring out everyone's secrets. That's what an informant like me aims for.  
Argo：Why'd you come to me so late at night? Were you worried about me?  
Argo：You wanna know when I find time to sleep? If I told you my secret, some informant I'd be.  
Asuna：Good morning. Hehe...I watched you sleep.  
Asuna：Boys are lucky. It takes them no time to get ready in the morning.  
Asuna：Breakfast is the most important meal of the day. You're too thin, regardless.  
Asuna：The weather sure is lovely today. Let's eat lunch outside.  
Asuna：Are you hungry? I brought us a picnic lunch. Wanna share?  
Asuna：The sun sure is bright today. It's kind of hard to see.  
Asuna：Good work today! Dinner's ready.  
Asuna：I usually don't notice it, but look at how dark night actually is!  
Asuna：Walking in the moonlight sure feels nice.  
Asuna：The night sky has a tranquil feel to it.  
Asuna：I'm having a hard time sleeping tonight... Can I stay up with you a bit longer?  
Asuna：Look how late it is. We'd better go to sleep or we'll be zombies tomorrow.  
Azurika：Good morning. It's time to get up.  
Azurika：Take your time when you eat. Eating too quickly causes indigestion.  
Azurika：It's almost curfew. Let's wrap things up for the day.  
Azurika：You aren't asleep yet? Well, it can't be helped. Shall I bring you something to drink?  
Bercouli：I'm always sleepy in the morning.  
Bercouli：Ooh, you got me breakfast? Thanks. You're always so helpful.  
Bercouli：Well, then. Since the weather is nice, I think I'll go for a dragon ride. Would you like to come along?  
Bercouli：Oh, forget about training for one day. If you train on a day like this, the gods will punish you.  
Bercouli：Okay. Time for a bath! I always love a bath before dinner.  
Bercouli：You spend your nights training, too? I admire your dedication, but try to get some sleep.  
Bercouli：You can't sleep? Wanna have a nightcap?  
Bercouli：Since there's no missions lined up, I'll be able to sleep in tomorrow morning.  
Cardinal：Morning already, eh? I must have gotten quite absorbed in observing the outside world.  
Cardinal：Come, wake up. You should already be well rested.  
Cardinal：You want breakfast? Well, I suppose I could prepare something... You have no shame, do you.  
Cardinal：Solus' rays... It has been years since I've felt them.  
Cardinal：You must be careful at night. Their insects are buzzing.  
Cardinal：There's a bath down the hall. Be sure to dry off well.  
Cardinal：My body does not need sleep. So your thoughtfulness is wasted.  
Cardinal：Whenever I view the moon through another's eyes, it looks small and faded.  
Dakira：Good morning! I’m looking forward to working together today.  
Dakira：If you're hungry, would you like me to fetch you a meal?  
Dakira：You must be tired. Please, warm yourself in the bath.  
Dakira：Rest assured, I will guard your room at night. I won't let anyone come near you.  
Death Gun：Heh heh heh. This will probably be the last morning I see.  
Death Gun：Even if it's bright out, it makes no difference for me. I will never be able to see again.  
Death Gun：Heh, heh, heh. Tonight we will be able to see the black star. It's a good night for someone to die.  
Death Gun：Would it be alright for me to rest my eyes? Even though I probably won't be able to open them up again.  
Deusolbert：What kind of cad sleeps in this late?  
Deusolbert：Another day of hard work to protect humanity.  
Deusolbert：One must also feed one's dragon.  
Deusolbert：Solus's powers are great. The skies are filled with divine energy.  
Deusolbert：Well. Let us rest.  
Deusolbert：I must continue with my duties for the rest of the day. There will be little time for rest.  
Deusolbert：Are you also out on a mission? Night missions must be difficult for ordinary humans.  
Deusolbert：Rest well when you are given the chance. That is a fighter's duty.  
Diavel：It's a brand new day! Where shall we go today?  
Diavel：Are you hungry, too? Let's eat together.  
Diavel：Good work today. Make sure you rest up well tonight.  
Diavel：If you don't sleep, you'll be off your game tomorrow. Taking care of your physical health is essential for someone who's on the front lines.  
Dee Eye Ell：Good morning, your majesty. The battalion of Dark Sorcerers is ready to depart at a moments notice.  
Dee Eye Ell：Gah! Drive away these pigs! More of them should die so that I don't have to waste my Sacred Arts...  
Dee Eye Ell：Your majesty, may I pour you a drink?  
Dee Eye Ell：I was thinking perhaps when he was asleep... But the general's room surely must be well protected.  
Eydis：Good morning... Well, goodnight, technically. I'm about to go to sleep.  
Eydis：I have to return to my post soon. Let's break our fast together before I leave.  
Eydis：You certainly are up early again today. I should follow your example.  
Eydis：So you were in the Cloudtop Garden. I don't blame you. It is so relaxing up here.  
Eydis：I ran into the Prime Senator today. Eew. So gross! I need to get that visual out of my mind.  
Eydis：Sometimes I make a trip home to the Church, just to eat the delicious food.  
Eydis：Night is the time for Dark agents, so one must always be on guard.  
Eydis：Work is done for the day! Wanna take a bath with me?  
Eydis：I'm at my best this time of day.  
Eydis：Mm, you're still awake? Wait a minute, did you miss me?  
Eydis：The hidden sides to a person's character are often revealed at night. The good, the bad, and the ugly...  
Eydis：Did you wake up? But you should still stay in bed. Even lying awake helps rejuvenate you.  
Egome：It's later than I thought. We'd better get up.  
Egome：What, you want lunch? Well, I'm not sharing mine.  
Egome：It's almost dinnertime. Anything available?  
Egome：Oh, shut up. Can't it wait until tomorrow?  
Eiji：Morning. Listening to your singing in the morning is a good way to start the day.  
Eiji：Well, shall we go out somewhere? The excitement of this world is sure to become an asset of yours.  
Eiji：Excuse me, let's call it a day here. Looks like I've gotten a little tired. Guess I am not used to this sensation.  
Eiji：What's up? Can't sleep? No point and trying to sleep. How about staying up with me?  
Eldrie：Good morning. You look lovely today, as always.  
Eldrie：Awakening to the songs of the birds is a truly beautiful thing.  
Eldrie：Today's meal was passable. Difficult to call perfect.  
Eldrie：The perfect day for a ride on a dragon. Would you like to join me?  
Eldrie：It's nothing much, but it is a 100-year-old red. Have some, if you'd like.  
Eldrie：The flowers, moist with the dewy night air, are quite charming.  
Eldrie：Having trouble sleeping? Then please, have a milk.  
Eldrie：Patrolling this late at night... I admire your strength of character.  
Entokia：Oh, you're just now returning too? You must be hungry. Let's eat!  
Entokia：Another lovely day! When Solus is in high spirits, so too are the townsfolk.  
Entokia：Master, you can hold your liquor, can't you? Come drink with me! Just you and me, man to man!  
Entokia：It's nights like this that make Iwahaku howl the most. Such a needy dragon.  
Eugene：We should get going. The sun is way up.  
Eugene：What's in your lunch? That looks good.  
Eugene：Today was another good day. See ya again tomorrow.  
Eugene：Attack under cover of night? I'm not stupid enough to get my head cut off while I'm in bed.  
Eugeo：Come on, wake up. It's already time for breakfast.  
Eugeo：I'm going out for some morning practice swings. Want to come with me?  
Eugeo：Well, I woke you up. Don't say I didn't try.  
Eugeo：Ordinarily, I'd be swinging my axe right now... This feels really surreal.  
Eugeo：I'm getting hungry. Let's have some lunch.  
Eugeo：Lunch break is over. We'd better get back to class.  
Eugeo：I'm really tired again today. How about you? How was your day?  
Eugeo：We need to take care of our swords, otherwise they lose endurance.  
Eugeo：Spending time together at night like this feels so natural now.  
Eugeo：What's wrong? It's the middle of the night... You're not sneaking out, are you?  
Eugeo：I couldn't sleep, so I thought I'd exercise a little.  
Eugeo：I could see the same stars back in Rulid, but they look different here somehow. I wonder why that is?  
Eugeo：Good morning. It's already past time to wake up.  
Eugeo：Breakfast is the most important meal of the day.  
Eugeo：Whoa, there! Isn't it a little early for lunch?  
Eugeo：What a nice day... Days like this make me want to go exploring further than I ever have before!  
Eugeo：Let's call it a day here. We don't want to be sore tomorrow.  
Eugeo：We can have dinner together too, if you want.  
Eugeo：Are you still awake? You'll be really tired tomorrow, you know?  
Eugeo：It's really easy for your mind to wander at night. Funny how that works, huh?

Fanatio：Good morning. Time to get up!  
Fanatio：Today, we will once again receive Solus' bountiful blessings.  
Fanatio：If you will excuse me. I must check on my subordinates.  
Fanatio：If you wish to eat, preparations have been made over there.  
Fanatio：Are you drinking again?  Although you're an Integrity Knight,  please don't over do it.  
Fanatio：The bath is ready. You may go first.  
Fanatio：The moonlight is very pretty indeed. I do love quiet nights.  
Fanatio：Please get up early tomorrow. Don't oversleep.  
Fizel：Good morning. Hope there's something fun to do today.  
Fizel：It's always dreary getting up in the morning when you know there's nothing but studying to do.  
Fizel：Lunch? Sure, I'll eat with you!  
Fizel：Why do you think we get sleepy right after we eat?  
Fizel：Ahhh, another exhausting day. I'm just so sick of Sacred Arts!  
Fizel：Truthfully, I would love to stay up late, but I don't like being yelled at.  
Fizel：Mmm...morning already?  
Fizel：Can't sleep? Neither can I. Oh, I wish Linel were here.  
Frenica：Good morning! What a lovely morning it is.  
Frenica：Did you have lunch yet? If not, wanna join me?  
Frenica：Look how late it's gotten. Time does fly when you're having fun.  
Frenica：You can't sleep? Neither can I. Hehe.  
Geero：Good morning. Um... If it’s not too much trouble, can you train with me later?  
Geero：You should come prepared and with a full stomach.  
Geero：The night breeze feels so nice.  
Geero：Have a good rest and we’ll get ready tomorrow.  
Golgorosso：Oh, awake already? Let's get to our morning training!  
Golgorosso：Make sure you eat up! A strong body needs ample nourishment.  
Golgorosso：It's already gotten dark. Let's call it a day.  
Golgorosso：I appreciate your dedication... But don't overwork yourself at night.  
Heathcliff：Morning. Seeing your face this morning is a sign that today will be a lucky day.  
Heathcliff：Your evenings look somewhat busy. Even the guild doesn’t seem so relaxed.  
Heathcliff：I wonder if it's ok for me to stay out this late? Don't you have a lovely person waiting for you?  
Heathcliff：What is the purpose of midnight? I wonder if it's for foul play.  
Hoveren：Good morning! Did you have have a good rest?  
Hoveren：Ready for afternoon duties!  
Hoveren：I’m going to work on my Sacred Arts skills just a little more.  
Hoveren：Leave patrolling to us. Please get some rest.  
Humbert：Damn it, I'm still sleepy... Solus’s rays are burning my eyes.  
Humbert：That pair of country bumpkins are sure eating a meager lunch.  
Humbert：I think I'll call upon my page again tonight...  
Humbert：The night is young. The fun is yet to come.  
Iskahn：Oi, you're pretty late. Morning practice just wrapped up.  
Iskahn：Make sure you're eating properly in the morning. You won't be able to build up your body if you don't.  
Iskahn：A new warrior will be joining us today. This brat has been training hard since he was young to be a strong warrior.  
Iskahn：Oi, don't get tired on me! Give it another shot, starting from the head!  
Iskahn：I can care less about territory. I just want to get stronger, simple as that. That is our wish and our way of life.  
Iskahn：Being too tired to eat is what novices say. No matter how tired you get, you have to make sure to eat.  
Iskahn：Are you always up this late? No wonder you always look tired.  
Iskahn：Oh, shut up. The night is for sleeping...  
Jayce：Good morning! I look forward to training with you today!  
Jayce：Let’s grab a bite before we’re too hungry to move!  
Jayce：Great work today! Should we get something to eat?  
Jayce：My... My apologizes! It was a mistake... I’ll return to my own room now!  
Kagemune：Good morning. The weather looks nice.  
Kagemune：Wanna eat lunch with me?  
Kagemune：You wouldn't think it, but nights are ideal for fighting against strong opponents.  
Kagemune：Okay, even I'm sleepy now. Let's turn in.  
Kibaou：Oh. So, you finally decided to wake up? The sun rose hours ago.  
Kibaou：Well, I don't mind eating with ya.  
Kibaou：I'm gonna take a nap. You should stop farting around and do the same.  
Kibaou：I'm not having trouble sleeping. I was standing guard!  
Kirito：Mmm...sleepy...  
Kirito：Man, you have some serious bed head. Want me to fix it?  
Kirito：Okay! Let's put in another day's work!  
Kirito：Did you have lunch yet? If not, wanna join me?  
Kirito：The weather is so nice today. Too bad we have to spend the day training.  
Kirito：Ugh... I ate too much lunch.  
Kirito：Good work today! Let's take a nice, long bath.  
Kirito：It's getting pretty dark. Want to go on a little night walk?  
Kirito：It's still a bit too early to go to sleep. Wanna do something?  
Kirito：Can't sleep? Wanna talk some more, then?  
Kirito：Wake me up at eight,  tomorrow, okay? Goodnight.  
Kirito：If you can't sleep, wanna go outside with me? I'm in the mood for some stargazing.  
Kirito：Ahhh... I think I'll go back to sleep.  
Kirito：Oh well. Guess I'd better get up. Don't wanna get yelled at.  
Kirito：It's almost dinnertime. I wonder what's on the menu tonight?  
Kirito：The weather is nice and warm. Time for a nap!  
Kirito：Boy, am I hungry! Is it dinnertime yet?  
Kirito：Aw, can't I stay up? I'm not sleepy yet.  
Kirito：What's wrong? Bad dream?  
Kirito：Did you wake up, too? Wanna go outside then?  
Klein：Oh, you're up. Well, let's head out!  
Klein：Ahhh... I'm gonna feel like a zombie all day.  
Klein：Munch, munch... Wow, even eating feels real!  
Klein：Napping again? Wow, you really do love sleeping.  
Klein：Good work, trooper! I think we both deserve a medal today.  
Klein：A night walk? Cool, I'm coming with!  
Klein：Dude, stop it. It's late! Just go to sleep.  
Klein：If you can't sleep, a little exercise sometimes does the trick.  
Kuradeel：Good morning! I eagerly await my itinerary!  
Kuradeel：I have prepared your lunch. Come, this way.  
Kuradeel：Nights are dangerous. You can count on me to protect you!  
Kuradeel：Wow! I'm honored that you would come to see me so late...  
Kureha：Morning. What kind of quest will we be going to today?  
Kureha：Hehe... You always seem sleepy in the morning.  
Kureha：Napping again...? Do you always sleep when the weather is nice?  
Kureha：It's about that time of the day. How about joining us for some lunch?  
Kureha：How are today's results looking? We're a little on the so and so side of things.  
Kureha：How about we do a party with everyone today? I'll go and invite everyone!  
Kureha：...Looks like you caught me at a weird place. Sometimes, even at night I can't sleep.  
Kureha：You're awake at this hour? I'm also the same... but we should both probably head off to bed early.  
Leafa：Good morning! And what a lovely morning it is. Wanna do some training?  
Leafa：Hahaha! Whoa, look at that cow lick! Come here, I'll fix it.  
Leafa：I may be awake, but I'm still sleepy... I just might fall asleep again...  
Leafa：I have no plans this afternoon, so let's go out somewhere!  
Leafa：Wanna have lunch together? Mom made enough for both of us!  
Leafa：Oh! Look who's taking a nap... Fine! Two can play at that game.  
Leafa：I spent the whole day training, and I'm exhausted. I could fall asleep standing up!  
Leafa：Ahhh, nothing is more refreshing than a good bath!  
Leafa：I'll be polishing my bamboo sword tonight. I have to give it some love, since it's so special to me.  
Leafa：I always feel anxious when I wake up in the middle of the night. That's why I always come to see you...  
Leafa：We'd better go to sleep soon, or tomorrow's gonna suck. Well, goodnight.  
Leafa：Sorry, did I wake you? Sorry, I was just watching you sleep, that's all.  
Linel：Good morning. You're sure up early.  
Linel：I braid my hair every morning. Will you braid my hair for me?  
Linel：Let us take a lunch break. Would you...care to join me?  
Linel：I love Solus's warmth. It feels quite...soothing.  
Linel：Another day is done. A nun-in-training's life certainly is monotonous.  
Linel：If we don't fall asleep quickly at night, the Prime Senator punishes us.  
Linel：Zzz...zzz... Hm?! What is it?  
Linel：Sometimes I'm kept wide awake by past memories. Whenever that happens, I snuggle against Fizel.  
Lipia：Good morning. I have sorted out our preparations for departure. I am always at your service.  
Lipia：I have prepared some food. It's not the most glamorous, but if you don't mind...  
Lipia：We need to be on the lookout for Assassins and Dark Sorcerers roaming around in the darkness of the night. Those vermin are probably lurking around somewhere.  
Lipia：My Lord, why are you up this late in the night... If you call for me, I will be there.  
Lisbeth：Morning. Okay. Better get ready to open shop.  
Lisbeth：Are you still in bed? Let me guess. You pulled another all-nighter?  
Lisbeth：No matter how early I wake up, time always disappears so quickly in the morning.  
Lisbeth：You stayed in bed so long this morning, and you wanna nap again? In a way, that's a talent.  
Lisbeth：Ooh, perfect timing! I'm kinda busy, can you lend me a hand?  
Lisbeth：Blacksmithing in this world is fun! I just might become obsessed.  
Lisbeth：Good work today. Make sure you rest up well tonight.  
Lisbeth：Sometimes I get customers even at night. But, I have to give them good service, too.  
Lisbeth：Spending nights together like this... It reminds me of that one night in the cave.  
Lisbeth：I just couldn't sleep. Maybe I should make some warm milk.  
Lisbeth：Oh, why am I up so late? Well, I was researching weapons, and I guess I lost track of time.  
Lisbeth：Phew. Okay, now I'm definitely tired. Thanks for staying up with me.  
Mary：Come on, Sister, wake up! Solus is already high in the sky!  
Mary：It's time for breakfast. I need to cook up something that will give my sister the energy she needs!  
Mary：My foot doesn't really hurt today. So I need to go to town and do as much shopping as I can.  
Mary：What a lovely day. I know! Let's give all the beds a good airing!  
Mary：The villagers were kind to fetch me food since I can't walk well. I should thank them.  
Mary：I must bring my sister lunch! I think she's working in the village today.  
Mary：Sister sure is late getting back... Ooh, here she is! Good evening, Sister!  
Mary：You can go ahead and bathe without me, Sister. I need to wash the dinner dishes.  
Mary：I know it's rather late, but I'd sure love to stay up a little longer and talk with you.  
Mary：Sister, were you awake all this time? Please, you need to come to bed. Come on, let's sleep together.  
Mary：May the gods bless my sister... and bring her home safe.  
Mary：I'm having a hard time falling asleep... Promise you'll be with me when I wake up. Please?  
Medina：Looks like you overslept. So what is it? Did you wake up to confront me?  
Medina：What do you do in your free time? I'll catch up to you in no time.  
Medina：I reminiscence whenever it gets dark outside. About happy days and of those words that can't be taken back...  
Medina：You should be able to understand by looking at the way one handles a sword. Even though I don't want anyone to look at me...  
Nergius：Did you just now awaken? Are you...perhaps regretting staying up so late last night?  
Nergius：It's almost noon. I need to get Shionade some lunch. And a bath.  
Nergius：Now, tell me. Just where do you think you are going this late, Sir Swordsman Delegate?  
Nergius：Ah...I see you've caught me. Well, I was practicing. There's no guarantee peace will last forever.  
Oberon：Morning. Are you well? Lady Titania.  
Oberon：Take a look on the ground! Everyone is so tiny! Like an insect!  
Oberon：Even idiots get tired. In terms of relaxation, her form is by far the best.  
Oberon：If you're tired now, then don't push yourself. If you have anything to do, you can save that for later.  
Philia：Morning! Did you eat breakfast? No? That's no good! You need to eat.  
Philia：Today is the perfect weather for an exploration! I think we'll find some great treasure!  
Philia：Today's weather is pretty nice and warm, isn't it? I totally get those who would want to take an afternoon nap.  
Philia：Hey, I made a lunch box today... Do you want to try it with me?  
Philia：Fufu, seeing the sunset cast it's warm glow over the whole world is one of my favorite sights  
Philia：Anyhow, shall we get in the bath? D-Don't you dare peak...!  
Philia：Staying silent in the middle of the night by yourself makes you remember things from the past... So can you stay with me?  
Philia：Ehehe... If you can't sleep, how about taking a walk at night?  
Premiere：Good morning. Shall we do breakfast? What's on the menu today?  
Premiere：Did you oversleep today? Hehe... I guess that makes two of us. I also managed to get a lot of shut eye.  
Premiere：Let's eat cookies together! Cookies are always delicious.  
Premiere：Napping... I've heard that rest is an important thing to have. I will also take a rest.  
Premiere：Lots of stars dot the night sky. Um... How about we go see them together?  
Premiere：Looking at the stars reminds me of that time. Fufu... A very special memory.  
Premiere：Shouldn't you be getting some rest?  
Premiere：If you're tired, then come sleep by my side. That way, you'll be fully rested.  
Rain：Morning. Ehehe, I made some breakfast. Care to join me?  
Rain：Ah, ah♪ If you practice your vocals in the morning, then your voice will be pitch perfect all day!  
Rain：What great weather... This is the sort of day for soaring in the skies or even going out for a walk.  
Rain：Hey, if it's ok with you how about we go out somewhere? I'll also invite Seven, so it'll be the three of us!  
Rain：Before we knew it, it's already pitch black outside. Let's start heading back.  
Rain：If it is alright with you, would you mind having dinner together? Seven will also be joining today.  
Rain：You're still awake? ... I also can't talk about a person's business.  
Rain：If you aren't tired, how about I sing you a lullaby?  
Raios：It appears I've overslept. Perhaps I carried on a little too heavily during last night's banquet.  
Raios：I like day-drinking. It has a certain elegance to it.  
Raios：Phew. At last, some time to relax.  
Raios：I've gotten sleepy. I would love to drink some more, but I think I'll call it quits.  
Renly：Good morning! Meeting you this early in the morning means that good things will happen today.  
Renly：The weather is nice today. If it's fine with you... Would you like to get a drink somewhere?  
Renly：Did you happen to make that lunch yourself? It looks pretty delicious...  
Renly：The Twin Edged Wings will get stronger depending on how you use it. I need to get better...  
Renly：Good job today! I hope I can see you tomorrow too.  
Renly：I'm think of doing a little training  after dinner. I need to get stronger, even if it's just a little.  
Renly：...I had a dream that made me feel nostalgic. But, I don't know why I feel that way...  
Renly：You know, sneaking around talking like this gets me a little excited.  
Ronie：Good morning, sir! It's time to get up!  
Ronie：May I accompany you on your morning training?  
Ronie：Mentor, are you still resting? Then, as your page... I shall watch over you.  
Ronie：I wish I had my own private quarters, like the elite disciples.  
Ronie：I'm honored! I wasn't expecting to see you in the dining hall.  
Ronie：Let's have a picnic today. Tiese and I will make the lunch!  
Ronie：When did it get so late... Ms. Azurika might scold me.  
Ronie：Sometimes I accidentally stay up all night talking with Tiese and Frenica.  
Ronie：Tiese and I play-wrestle at night. I think we've both gotten stronger from it.  
Ronie：If we go outside this late, our teachers will be livid.  
Ronie：I understand that staying up all night makes the next day difficult. However...  
Ronie：I'm sleepy. Goodnight, Mentor.  
Rosalia：Mm...morning already? I'm gonna stay in bed. You go on ahead without me.  
Rosalia：Where is my lunch? You have cooked me lunch, I hope?  
Rosalia：Well, I'll have the first bath. You stand guard.  
Rosalia：Intruding on a lady at night? You've got some nerve.  
Sachi：Good morning! Did you sleep well?  
Sachi：Whenever I see the sun's morning rays I always feel cozy and safe. It means I made it to see another day.  
Sachi：Want to eat lunch soon? I can join you, if you'd like.  
Sachi：Napping in the middle of a field... I'd be too scared to try that.  
Sachi：I managed to come home in one piece again! Hehe, thanks, everyone.  
Sachi：Stronger monsters come out at night, so you should stay indoors.  
Sachi：Hey, um...I can't sleep. Can I, uh, hold your hand?  
Sachi：Thanks for being with me. Now I know I'll sleep soundly.  
Sakuya：Good morning. Mmm, what a lovely morning.  
Sakuya：It's just about lunchtime now. I just got in some great wine. Wanna have a drink with me?  
Sakuya：Going to bed already? Well, rest up.  
Sakuya：I had some odds and ends I wanted to take care of... And before I knew it, the moon was out.  
Sheyta：...Good morning. Everyone is already awake.  
Sheyta：You aren't going to eat breakfast?  
Sheyta：Am I sleepy? ... Not at all.  
Sheyta：You're a strange one. Stubborn, yet gentle...  
Sheyta：This Black Lily... Is specialized to cut.  
Sheyta：Make sure to eat a proper dinner. Otherwise you won't be able to fight tomorrow.  
Sheyta：...What do you want at this hour?  
Sheyta：Don't let down your guard... even at night. That's how you end up getting cut.  
Selka：Come up, wake up. It's time for prayers.  
Selka：Well, you're sure up early! Hehe. Good boy.  
Selka：Here's your lunch. Let's eat with the others over there.  
Selka：There you go, napping again! I swear, the minute I turn my back...  
Selka：Welcome back. The bath is ready. You go on in ahead of me.  
Selka：We should turn in soon, I don't want to waste lamp oil.  
Selka：You can't sleep? Then...I'll sing you a lullaby.  
Selka：You may stay up, as long as you stay quiet. I don't want you waking the others.  
Seven：Good morning! You're still asleep? Sleepyhead!  
Seven：The brain works the most in the morning! Don't go dazing off, let's go get some food!  
Seven：Looks like a couple of interesting people have gathered around you. This is a sight to be seen.  
Seven：Sometimes you gotta stretch out your wings! What about it - let's go hang out!  
Seven：You did quiet a lot today. So you must be tired - how about we take a rest?  
Seven：Anyways, what should we do about dinner tonight? Rain will be cook for us!  
Seven：....What happended? If you aren't... sleepy, then come over here by me.  
Seven：If you don't get any sleep, then you won't be able to do anything tomorrow!  
Shasta：Just as I thought, the light is weaker here. I hear that the Human Empire is brighter...  
Shasta：You wake up early and train hard, but don't over do it. Got it?  
Shasta：There haven't been any serious contenders lately. That's one way your skills can get sloppy.  
Shasta：Having food is something to rejoice about. Someday, I want to live in a world where nobody has to worry about when their next meal is...  
Shasta：Lighten up. It's only me and you here. You can talk to me more casually now.  
Shasta：What do you say? A glass for yourself as well? The wine is on the cheaper side of things but the taste isn't bad.  
Shasta：You're still working at this hour? Your intel really is useful, but your safety comes first.  
Shasta：Rest when you have the chance. Human Soldiers aren't the only enemies we have to worry about.  
Silica：Good morning. Pina always wakes me up, you know.  
Silica：Hehe. You have a cowlick. Here, I'll fix it.  
Silica：Want to have breakfast with me? I'm having sandwiches.  
Silica：You're napping... You look so peaceful.  
Silica：I'm going to go walk Pina. Would you like to come with me?  
Silica：Earlier today, someone asked me if I didn't have my "Calling" yet. Do I really look that young?  
Silica：Welcome back! Dinner's ready... Hehe, I've always wanted to say that.  
Silica：The trees in the forest are rustling. It almost sounds like birds.  
Silica：So, how was your day? Wanna talk about it?  
Silica：Zzz...zzz...mm... Pina, stop...  
Silica：I don't think I can sleep tonight. Want to go for a little walk?  
Silica：Is it okay for you to be up this late? Please, don't overwork yourself.  
Sinon：Good morning. Come on, get up! You're being lazy, you know?  
Sinon：What an epic yawn... Some people cover their mouths, you know?  
Sinon：Mm, nah... I was up late last night hunting, so I'm gonna take a little nap.  
Sinon：It's noon already? Want some lunch?  
Sinon：The sun sure is bright today. My eyes hurt.  
Sinon：Sleeping again? Wow. I'm impressed.  
Sinon：Any plans tonight? Wanna stay in with me?  
Sinon：I love night time because it's nice and quiet. I don't do well in noisy places.  
Sinon：Yeah, it's still too early to go to sleep. Let's stay up and talk a bit more.  
Sinon：I had a bad dream...but I'm okay now. Seeing you made all the scary thoughts disappear.  
Sinon：Wow, look how late it is. I'd like to stay up more, but we really should sleep.  
Sinon：You can't sleep? Neither can I. Wanna talk for a bit?  
Siune：Good morning, sleepy-head.  
Siune：Let's eat lunch outside today.  
Siune：Today was another good day. See you again tomorrow?  
Siune：You're still awake? Here...you can sleep with me.  
Sortiliena：It's morning. Get up. Do you want to be late?  
Sortiliena：I'm about to go to my morning training. If you have time, come with me.  
Sortiliena：You're still half-asleep. Hehe, you're just asking to be clobbered.  
Sortiliena：This ends your afternoon tasks for the day. I'll see you again in the evening.  
Sortiliena：I just finished preparing lunch. Care to join me?  
Sortiliena：How are your studies going? Make sure you practice the Sacred Arts as well as the sword.  
Sortiliena：Don't let today's exhaustion carry over to tomorrow. Rest is a very important part of work.  
Sortiliena：Whenever we talk at night like this, I always feel a strange sensation.  
Sortiliena：Going out at this hour? But it's well past curfew!  
Sortiliena：You can't sleep? I would admonish you... but as I can't sleep myself, that would be hypocritical.  
Sortiliena：Pity to wake up at a time like this. I think I'll go get in a few practice-swings.  
Sortiliena：I used to have trouble sleeping, too. So don't force it. If we just talk for a while, you will easily drift off.  
Sortiliena：Okay, let's begin our morning training!  
Sortiliena：I do get sleepy sometimes... But I can't let myself focus on that!  
Sortiliena：I was thinking I'd spend the afternoon studying weapons.  
Sortiliena：I was thinking I'd go into the forest today to study how one should fight amidst the trees.  
Sortiliena：I want to practice just a bit more. I must get stronger as quickly as possible.  
Sortiliena：Now that was incredibly tiring. I must make sure I eat my supper.  
Sortiliena：Sometimes I wake up in the middle of the night like this. There are just too many things on my mind.  
Sortiliena：I must sleep. I have another early morning tomorrow.  
Strea：Good morning... You're up early, eh? I think I want to rest just a little longer...  
Strea：I was up late last night... Please let me sleep a little longer...  
Strea：Hey, hey, let's go get some lunch together! I found a really good restaurant!  
Strea：Ah, are you going to take a nap? If you are, I'll join you then.  
Strea：Eh, it's still pretty early. How about we hang around a little longer?  
Strea：The night time ambince is great. Makes me feel like an adult.  
Strea：You're still awake at this hour? Fufu, looks like that makes both of us. Come over here.  
Strea：Shall we get some sleep? I wanted to talk a little longer, but there's always tomorrow for that.  
Suguha：Good morning! And what a lovely morning it is. Wanna do some training?  
Suguha：Hahaha! Whoa, look at that cow lick! Come here, I'll fix it.  
Suguha：I may be awake, but I'm still sleepy... I just might fall asleep again...  
Suguha：I have no plans this afternoon, so let's go out somewhere!  
Suguha：Wanna have lunch together? Mom made enough for both of us!  
Suguha：Oh! Look who's taking a nap... Fine! Two can play at that game.  
Suguha：I spent the whole day training, and I'm exhausted. I could fall asleep standing up!  
Suguha：Ahhh, nothing is more refreshing than a good bath!  
Suguha：I'll be polishing my bamboo sword tonight. I have to give it some love, since it's so special to me.  
Suguha：I always feel anxious when I wake up in the middle of the night. That's why I always come to see you...  
Suguha：We'd better go to sleep soon, or tomorrow's gonna suck. Well, goodnight.  
Suguha：Sorry, did I wake you? Sorry, I was just watching you sleep, that's all.  
Tiese：Good morning, sir! Were you already awake?  
Tiese：Hehe, oh, what a huge yawn that was.  
Tiese：Would you like to have breakfast with everyone this morning?  
Tiese：I love your blue uniform. You look very nice in it. Maybe I should also wear blue...  
Tiese：We have Sacred Arts lessons today. So many incantations to remember...  
Tiese：Ronie and I made your lunch. Hope you like it!  
Tiese：It's past curfew, but I had to press on. I couldn't disobey you.  
Tiese：When we talk late at night, sometimes we annoy Ms. Azurika.  
Tiese：Ronie and I are having little mock-fights, the way you taught us. We try to keep them quiet, though.  
Tiese：You can't sleep either? Then...would you like to chat for a while?  
Tiese：If you have a difficult time waking tomorrow, I'll come wake you up.  
Tiese：You look sleepy, Mentor. Thanks for staying up with me.  
Tiese：Good morning. Breakfast is ready. I made it myself.  
Tiese：The studies from yesterday were tough... I still feel tired.  
Tiese：I've been learning ever since I was little, so I can kind of use a sword. Not that well but...  
Tiese：It'll feel nice to play outside! Let's go!  
Tiese：Let's like it's already dark out. Before you knew it, another day gone.  
Tiese：I'll instantly get tired when it gets dark out. Even though I want to stay up to talk some more...  
Tiese：It's the hour when monsters come out... But, if that monster is nice it's not so scary.  
Tiese：We need to go to bed early tonight! Tomorrow's training is going to be a tough one!  
Vassago：Looks like it's daybreak... I'm having a little trouble moving.  
Vassago：It's that time of the day when prey are on the move. I wonder what kind of hunt I will do today.  
Vassago：This one is a little showy in the daylight...  
Vassago：My head hurts... Does anyone... have... an aspirin?  
Vassago：Looks like it's finally nightfall. We'll be able to move around easier now.  
Vassago：This alcohol and food won't be enough to satisfy this hunger... I need blood and screams!  
Vassago：If you go on a walk at this hour you do know that demons will pop out, right? ...For example, someone like me...  
Vassago：Only a couple of hours before the sun rises. Until then, let's get to it!  
Subtilizer：Even in the morning, the foul stench of this world doesn't change.  
Subtilizer：The Dark Territory is just as the name implies. It's a place where even at daybreak, the skies are dimly lit.  
Subtilizer：Wine is a better substitute to satisfy your belly, rather than a skimpy meal.  
Subtilizer：The Dark Territory is vast. When I remember that this world is in the palm of my hands, I feel a little better.  
Subtilizer：I want to taste the Fluctlight of this world as soon as possible.  
Subtilizer：Looks like there's some decent alcohol laying around. Not bad...  
Subtilizer：They say that man has a fear for the darkness of the night. I don't quiet feel that.  
Subtilizer：Even if I see it with my own eyes, the speed in which this world moves compared to the real world is truly amazing.  
Volo：I hope you're not seriously sleeping in this late?  
Volo：Well, time for me to get back to my voluntary training.  
Volo：A day is a short period of time. Too short to train oneself.  
Volo：You like practicing at night, too? We might be more alike than different.  
Yui：Good morning! What a lovely morning it is.  
Yui：Hehe. You have a cowlick.  
Yui：The meals we had in Aincrad were so delicious! I can just taste it now...  
Yui：A nap? Let me join you!  
Yui：Good work, Dad! Are you finished for the day?  
Yui：If Mom catches you snacking between meals, she'll give you an earful.  
Yui：If you're having trouble sleeping, we could talk for a while.  
Yuna：Morning! Hey, hey, where are you going to take me today?  
Yuna：The morning sun feels great! I feel like my body and voice are fully awake!  
Yuna：Ehh, napping? That's such a waste. Come on, let's go out.  
Yuna：I heard that a huge monster appears over there! Hey, hey, let's go and take a look!  
Yuna：Looks like it'll start getting dark soon. You're not tired yet, are you?  
Yuna：Today was pretty fun! I wonder where you'll take me tomorrow.  
Yuna：Amongst the silent night skies doesn't it just make you want to sing? I guess it would make people mad...  
Yuna：Yeah, it's a bit of a waste, but we have to get some sleep for tomorrow. Good night.  
Yuuki：Good morning! I'm bright-eyed and bushy-tailed!  
Yuuki：Whenever I wake up in the morning, I always feel really excited. That’s because I know it'll be another great day!  
Yuuki：Phew, I really am sleepy today. I think I overexerted myself yesterday.  
Yuuki：What a nice day, huh. I can understand why you'd want to take a nap.  
Yuuki：Just taking a walk under the sun makes you feel so giddy, doesn't it?  
Yuuki：I'm getting hungry. Let's have some lunch.  
Yuuki：Come on, let's take a bath!  
Yuuki：I'm already running out of energy. I probably overdid it during the day.  
Yuuki：See, relaxing and hanging out like this after a nice big meal? I live for these moments.  
Yuuki：I don't mind quiet nights. Though I sometimes feel a little lonely.  
Yuuki：Did you wake up? Hehe. Same. Wanna talk for a while?  
Yuuki：Haha, you sure look sleepy. Okay, let's turn in.  
Zink：Oh, you're awake already? You're sure up early.  
Zink：Well, let's have some lunch.  
Zink：I'm going to bed now. Goodnight.  
Zink：What is it? It's in the middle of the night!  
Administrator：Stop that. The mere thought of touching me is already a sin.  
Administrator：Very well, then. Shall I show you another "dream"?  
Administrator：I understand that you are enchanted by my visage. But laying hands upon me is unforgivable.  
Administrator：A pity. Metal weapons are ineffective against me.  
Administrator：Very well, then. Fetch me something to wear.  
Administrator：You certainly are needy. Would you like some more "love"?  
Administrator：You aren't like the other little boys, are you?  
Administrator：What is it? You don't really need my judgement, do you?  
Agil：I'm not really bald. I just shave every day.  
Agil：So that's why you stood on tiptoe? Hey, kid, if you wanna touch my head, grow a couple feet first.  
Agil：The only person who's allowed to touch my head is my wife.  
Agil：You have to keep up with your exercise one way or another. Even VR-fighting requires real strength.  
Agil：You should check your armor, too. It can save your life.  
Agil：Wanna buy something? I'll give you a fair price on any item.  
Operator：Hm...? Something wrong?  
Operator：Oddly enough... I don't mind what you're doing.  
Operator：Is there something on my head?  
Operator：I don't understand why you're doing that. Is it fun?  
Operator：If you go any further... you may be in defiance of the Taboo Index.  
Operator：Do you require my services? I will gladly take on any task you have.  
Airy：Hm...? Something wrong?  
Airy：Oddly enough... I don't mind what you're doing.  
Airy：Is there something on my head?  
Airy：I don't understand why you're doing that. Is it fun?
