Sheyta：...What do you want?  
Sheyta：...Hands to yourself.  
Sheyta：Are you amused by touching my chest?  
Sheyta：...I don't want to cut you so hands off.  
Sheyta：What is so amusing? I don't understand, but...  
Selka：I hope you're not planning on doing something that goes against the Taboo Index again.  
Selka：Could you maybe pat some of the others? They don't have mothers or fathers and they're very lonely.  
Selka：Is patting heads a customary greeting where you come from?  
Selka：Where do you think you're touching?  
Selka：Even when you're a guest, some behaviors do cross the line.  
Selka：If you break the Taboo Index, you'll be gravely punished for it.  
Selka：What is it? If you're looking for something, why not check the shelf first?  
Seven：Wait a minute! Can you not treat me like a kid? I'm a fully grown adult.  
Seven：Isn't my hair beautiful? Hehe, it's my pride and joy.  
Seven：W-why are you suddenly petting my head...? Could it be that's how you greet each other in Japan!?  
Seven：What, you mean you'll even hug me? I'm flattered, but you think I’m just some kid, don’t you!  
Seven：Hey, you promised me, didn't you? You wouldn’t do anything weird when we’re alone. Was that all a lie?  
Seven：Either you need to grow up or this was plain 'ol sexual harassment! Sexual harassment!  
Seven：I want to get bigger too, but if my body changes, so will my voice.  
Seven：What is it? Do you want to hold hands? Hehe, I don't mind. Will you dance with me during the katyusha?  
Shasta：If we're talking about size, I don't intend to be outdone by those ogres!  
Shasta：What's wrong? Is there something on my face?  
Shasta：That's kind of out of place for you doing that for me.  
Shasta：Strength can't be defined with body alone, but a well-trained body is what supports a strong heart and technique.  
Shasta：I won't understand anything even if you touch me like that! Come closer!  
Shasta：Are you worried about cracks on my armor? There are many out there who would want new armor, but I'm used to wearing this so it's the best option for me.  
Silica：What do you think of this hair pin? I hope it looks okay.  
Silica：So, uh, you're patting my head...  
Silica：I feel like a kid again... But it still feels nice.  
Silica：So, uh, hey... You're freaking me out a little.  
Silica：Eek?! You remind me of Pina! Rough-housing with me like that...  
Silica：So, uh, you can't touch me there. Because, I, you know...  
Silica：Um...even I have a breaking point.  
Silica：Yes, what is it? Say anything!  
Sinon：Hey! Hands to yourself!  
Sinon：My glasses are non-prescription. I wear them for protection...  
Sinon：Hey, that tickles! You don't treat other girls like this, do you?  
Sinon：Yeah, I did rest against your chest once... But that was inside a VR game!  
Sinon：Eek?! Hands off the stomach!  
Sinon：I'm not warning you again.. Next time you do that, I'm slapping you.  
Sinon：Why don't you just touch your own chest? You’re a real creep!  
Sinon：Don't tug. I can hear you just fine!  
Siune：What's wrong? Somebody sure is needy.  
Siune：Hey! That tickles!  
Siune：I'll pet you too. Come here.  
Siune：Hehe. Want me to hold you?  
Siune：Don't worry. I'm here for you.  
Siune：Hm? Did you call me?  
Sortiliena：I like my hair tied back because it's easier to fight this way.  
Sortiliena：When you treat me like that, it’s like you don’t see me as your mentor. Show a little more respect.  
Sortiliena：If other disciples saw this... I would be a laughing stock!  
Sortiliena：Just what do you think you're doing? What do you mean, you want to have a stop short duel?  
Sortiliena：One look at your hands, and I can tell how long you've been swinging a sword.  
Sortiliena：I know what it means to be touched there. I wasn't born yesterday... So please, stop it!  
Sortiliena：That's enough. No more touching.  
Sortiliena：What is it? If you've finished your work, then you may go home now.  
Sortiliena：I've never had my head patted like this before.  
Sortiliena：That tickles, stop it!  
Sortiliena：Attacks from above are powerful, but they're easy to dodge.  
Sortiliena：Hah! Even a kid could dodge your flimsy attacks!  
Sortiliena：I don't think your hand is dirty, but... this is weird.  
Sortiliena：What in the world are you doing?  
Sortiliena：Are you having inappropriate thoughts? You have a devious grin on your face.  
Sortiliena：A duel, you say?! I would love to fight you, anytime!  
Strea：Hm? Could you pet me?  
Strea：Hehe... Having your head pet feel's pretty good, doesn't it?  
Strea：Thank you! Next up will be me then. Hey, stick out your head!  
Strea：Heheh, as I suspected. You would be curious about something like that.  
Strea：Kyaa?! ...Tell me before you touch me!  
Strea：You're awfully bold today. Hehe, go ahead. I won’t stop you.  
Strea：Even like this, I wonder if you hear... the beating of my heart.  
Strea：What is it? Is this an invitation for a quest? I'm also open to those!  
Suguha：Huh? What?! J-jeez. You startled me.  
Suguha：When I was a baby, you used to pat my head like this a lot.  
Suguha：Hey! That tickles! Aha! The tables have turned!  
Suguha：Uh...could you, like, not stare at me? Unless...do I really stand out that much?  
Suguha：Eeep! You know that's my weak spot, don't you?!  
Suguha：Whoa, dude?! I may not look it, but I'm pretty self-conscious. So, don't tease me.  
Suguha：Hey! Even though we're family, there's still "good touch" and "bad touch"!  
Suguha：What's up? Going out somewhere?  
Tiese：Oh, Mentor?! How...bold of you!  
Tiese：Ronie's black hair is so pretty. To be honest, I've always been a little jealous.  
Tiese：M-may I also touch your hair?  
Tiese：What's the matter? If something is troubling you, please tell me.  
Tiese：Where do you think you're touching?  
Tiese：I appreciate the sentiment, but this is neither the time nor the place.  
Tiese：I think...you're misinterpreting the situation.  
Tiese：There! I've finished cleaning your room. Sorry it took so long.  
Tiese：I-I'm not a kid anymore!  
Tiese：If...I grew out my hair, that would be more lady-like, right?  
Tiese：I...don't hate... having my head patted...  
Tiese：A sword contest? It would be a pleasure!  
Tiese：Does your hand hurt? I'll take a look at it for you.  
Tiese：Is there something on my shoulder? I wonder if it got dirty during training?  
Tiese：I was taught that a body's midline is a fatal spot.  
Tiese：I don't like it when other people do that to me...but for some weird reason, you're an exception  
Vassago：Do you want to look under the hood? No problem. All I ask for is your life in exchange.  
Vassago：Aiming for the head is harder than I expected. Because it's always moving around. Aim for the stomach if you can.  
Vassago：Even if you kill me here, it's pointless. No matter how many times you dive at me, I will be there to confront you!  
Vassago：Splendid! Now I can aim at your weak point precisely... That's the rule of combat!  
Vassago：If you're going to do it, aim for the heart. Because that's where I'll be aiming.  
Vassago：Very well, I will be your opponent! In exchange, don't think for a minute that your death will be painless!  
Subtilizer：What are you doing? Put those filthy hands away!  
Subtilizer：What do you want? Who is giving you orders?  
Subtilizer：Did you honestly think that an attack like that would take me down?  
Volo：You touched my head. How do you wish to apologize for your rudeness?  
Volo：That slashing attack will not work on me. I wish you wouldn't underestimate me.  
Volo：If you think of dodging, you will. That's why you couldn't dodge it.  
Volo：If you wish to duel, then that is inevitable.  
Volo：So, your attacks are "Aincrad style," you say?  
Volo：I am a busy man. If you want something, spit it out.  
Yui：Hehe... More pats, please?  
Yui：Hey! That tickles!  
Yui：Maybe I should change my hairstyle like everyone else.  
Yui：Is my chest-area really that intriguing?  
Yui：Your hands are so big... Just being in your arms like this makes me feel sleepy.  
Yui：You should do this to Mom instead. She would love it.  
Yui：A tickle-fight? You asked for it!  
Yui：Any plans?  
Yuna：Hehe, isn't this ribbon cute? It's my favorite.  
Yuna：What is it, are you going to pet my head? Yes, it's fine! Yes, stick out your head as well!  
Yuna：Hehe... Well it's not like you disapprove of it, do you?  
Yuna：Hehe, can't you hear my heart beating through my chest?  
Yuna：Ahaha, I'm ticklish there! Ah, take it back!  
Yuna：This all feels so dirty... Shouldn't we stop?  
Yuna：...Touching me there, is a little embarrassing, but...  
Yuna：You are actually quiet bold. But that's no good. Since everyone is watching.  
Yuuki：Hehehe. I feel like a big sister.  
Yuuki：Do I look okay in this costume?  
Yuuki：I wish I were as tall as you.  
Yuuki：I can feel your heartbeat more clearly than I expected.  
Yuuki：Your hands are so warm.  
Yuuki：I'm not very big, so touching me might be boring.  
Yuuki：Stop! Haha! That tickles!  
Yuuki：Hm? What's wrong? Let me guess, you want to stay with me longer?  
Zink：Haha! Hey! That tickles!  
Zink：If you need anything, call my name!  
Zink：Is there some lint on me? Um...no thanks?  
Zink：What? You want a sword-fight?!  
Zink：All you need to protect your chest from attacks is a little armor.  
Zink：Again, call my name if you need anything!  
Administrator：You stand before the High Pontifex herself. Come and kneel.  
Administrator：If you stay still, I will bestow upon you my special love.  
Administrator：Keeping the Human Realm as it is forever... That is my mission as the High Pontifex.  
Administrator：To resist me would be a fool’s errand. After all, resistance is impossible.  
Administrator：I do love the quiet. Quietness keeps unnecessary feelings at bay.  
Administrator：I have been looking for you. Fancy! Dashing away on your own. I think some readjusting is in order.  
Administrator：Who's there? How dare you appear before me without introducing yourself!  
Agil：Man, trouble seems to just follow you wherever you go.  
Agil：I'd love to open shop here as well... But it seems like you really need one of those "Callings" here.  
Agil：I can live anywhere as long as there's people, goods, and money.  
Agil：Don't get too crazy, okay? Consider the many people who worry about you every day.  
Agil：If you have some time on your hands, wanna help me with the shop?  
Agil：I haven't seen ya around much. Did you find a place you like better than mine?  
Agil：Oops, sorry to bother you. But it's a relief to see you're doing well.  
Operator：Do you require my services?  
Operator：I am truly honored to lay eyes on you.  
Operator：Sometimes I think back fondly on the scenery that leads to the Cloudtop Garden.  
Operator：The breeze in the outside world must feel very nice.  
Operator：If you have any commands for me, then please tell me.  
Operator：Truly an honor to see you again. How may I serve you?  
Airy：I am truly honored to lay eyes on you.  
Airy：Sometimes I think back fondly on the scenery that leads to the Cloudtop Garden.  
Airy：The breeze in the outside world must feel very nice.  
Airy：If you have any commands for me, then please tell me.  
Alice：Sometimes I have this dream... I'm in a peaceful village, surrounded in green.  
Alice：If you call me stupid again, I can't guarantee your head will stay connected to your shoulders.  
Alice：If I might be frank...I don't like the Prime Senator. I dislike his extravagant way of dress, and I think he has a screw loose.  
Alice：Curious... You often say what others would not normally say.  
Alice：I...am not a good cook. I devoted all my studies to the sword, or to Sacred Arts.  
Alice：A proper appearance is the minimum requirement of a polite person. Please, shut it away.  
Alice：P-please, don't stare at me like that. You're embarrassing me!  
Alice：You are quite ridiculous... But I do enjoy watching.  
Alice：Why are you staring into space? I thought you were the sort of man who acted before he thought.  
Alice：It has certainly been a while since I last saw you. You were probably slacking off somewhere.  
Alice：I don't have a particular reason to see you... I merely came by to make sure you weren't up to something foolish again!  
Alice：A new year is upon us. So I came to pay my respects, since it's proper.  
Alice：Were you throwing...beans? Even against children, that isn't an effective attack.  
Alice：Why are your eyes filled with anticipation? I don't have any candy to give to a person I like!  
Alice：What incredible detail on those dolls. A highly skilled craftsman must have built them.  
Alice：You have a thank you gift...for me? Well...thank you. I accept it. It would be rude to refuse.  
Alice：April Fools? You called me a fool again, didn't you? Fix that bad habit of yours at once!  
Alice：A long vacation? I am not that tired, however... I'll take advantage of the opportunity and rest up.  
Alice：Rain is a blessing from the heavens. However, excessive rain is a problem in and of itself.  
Alice：This plant is called bamboo? What a strange plant. I've never seen it before.  
Alice：Summer Festivals...I've heard commoners enjoy them. It does indeed sound enjoyable.  
Alice：I would love to sit on Amayori and fly beneath that moon.  
Alice：I witnessed a strangely-dressed intruder! He must be a Dark Agent! ...Oh, he isn't?  
Alice：When I see red and orange on the trees, I feel melancholy. The poor leaves have served their purpose and now they are dying.  
Alice：How very festive. What are we celebrating?  
Alice：Hey, how was the pie today? Was it yummy?  
Alice：You're looking lively today. Try not to play too hard.  
Alice：But the Sacred Arts are useful, too. Depending on how you use it, of course.  
Alice：My task today is to watch over you. I'm not slacking off, okay?  
Alice：Why the long face? Overthinking things doesn't suit you.  
Alice：There you are! I've been looking all over! Why do you always run off like that?  
Alice：Long time no see. Don't tell me you've forgotten who I am?  
Alice：From now on, I will fight for what I desire.  
Alice：... I'm fine. I will move forward. For what you and I desire.  
Alice：Can you hear my determination?  
Alicia：I would love to research the source of your power. Come closer! Come on!  
Alicia：The mercenary deal isn't entirely off the table yet, FYI. I'm always game for it, whenever you are!  
Alicia：As long as I have Mr. Secret Weapon by my side, I can hold my own in any fight!  
Alicia：Come on, let's go on a walk! We might find something fun!  
Alicia：Arrrgh, I'm so bored I'm getting sleepy!  
Alicia：Howdy! I haven't seen you in, like, forever!  
Argo：Looks like you're pushing yourself too hard here, too. Just come back alive, will you?  
Argo：This world is too mysterious for me to gather intel.  
Argo：Being able to look up map data was a really useful game function.  
Argo：No matter what world I'm in, I'm still the same. I'm Argo the Informant.  
Argo：You look deep in thought. Something worrying you?  
Argo：If you need intel, make sure you come to me.  
Argo：Oopsie. Not this one. I'm looking up intel right now. Catch ya later.  
Asuna：Honestly! I was so, so worried about you!  
Asuna：That sword...it really suits you.  
Asuna：This world is very beautiful... If not for all the wars, I'd love to travel here.  
Asuna：I wonder if I can use the materials in this world to create food from the real world?  
Asuna：I'm not a god... But I doubt anyone would understand if I told them that.  
Asuna：No matter where you go or who you’re with, you never change.  
Asuna：D-don't stare at me like that. You're making me blush.  
Asuna：It's been a while since you and I sat down for a nice long talk. Let's change that.  
Asuna：Hehe, it's been a while since you and I have had the chance to take it easy like this.  
Asuna：Hey, where are we going next? Think it through first, okay?  
Asuna：I haven't seen you around lately. Hey...I missed you.  
Asuna：Happy New Year! Want to go to the shrine with me?  
Asuna：It sure is cold today. According to the calendar, it should already be spring...  
Asuna：Good job! Since today's Valentine's Day, do you want to, um, spend it with me?  
Asuna：Since there's a festival today, I baked some chestnut cakes. Want to eat them with me?  
Asuna：Hehe, I came to get you. Remember? You promised we'd spend White Day together.  
Asuna：So, how are you going to lie to me today?  
Asuna：Since we've got a few days off, want to go away and stargaze again?  
Asuna：Man, I just hate the rain. Even with an umbrella, my feet still get wet.  
Asuna：Here's your slip of paper for Tanabata. Now we can both write down our wishes to tie on the bamboo branch.  
Asuna：Phew! Just walking outside for, like, 5 minutes gets me sweaty. I hope the weather cools down soon.  
Asuna：I finished putting all the rice cakes in the display. Don't eat any until we've watched the moon for a while, okay?  
Asuna：Trick or treat!  
Asuna：I hear the autumn leaves are beautiful in the park. Want to check them out with me?  
Asuna：Merry Christmas! I made a whole roasted chicken for the first time. Want to come to my room?  
Azurika：Is your training proceeding well? You mustn't grow complacent.  
Azurika：I know that you are capable of overcoming this world's laws of nature.  
Azurika：You want to see...me sword-fight? I am not skilled enough for an audience.  
Azurika：My one true wish is to help raise as many skilled sword fighters as I can.  
Azurika：Being calm and quiet isn't a bad thing, but it seems a little bit out of character for you.  
Azurika：Oh, there you are. Be sure not to break your curfew.  
Bercouli：That High Pontifex does seem to have ulterior motives.  
Bercouli：If you defeat me, you may have my position as Commander.  
Bercouli：Come on, don't be so formal. Just relax.  
Bercouli：The Prime Senator? Be wary of him. I know he's up to something.  
Bercouli：I've never been that good at "being quiet."  
Bercouli：Oh, were you busy? Sorry about that.  
Bercouli：Long time no see. I thought you'd forgotten about me.  
Cardinal：Oh, are you interested in books? You may read whatever you'd like, but the Axiom Church wrote those books.  
Cardinal：I have waited all this time for a human such as you to appear.  
Cardinal：Stop this! Don't swing your blade here! It's too dangerous.  
Cardinal：My, what a glutton you are! Here. Have another bun.  
Cardinal：You are unusually quiet. Is there something on your mind?  
Cardinal：Hmm... It would seem that meddling from afar is my forte.  
Cardinal：Long time no see. Have you memorized the incantation?  
Dakira：As one of the Four Whirling Blades, I am ready to die at any instant.  
Dakira：I don't mind if you don't call me by name.  
Dakira：The four of us shall carry out any command, as a unit.  
Dakira：If you are going out somewhere, please let me come with you!  
Dakira：I will always be by your side. Call on me if you ever need anything.  
Dakira：What is it? Do you require the services of the Four Whirling Blades?  
Death Gun：Me and my gun are called... "Death Gun". My existance will be the death of you all.  
Death Gun：Noone will ever be able to catch me.  
Death Gun：This isn't, over. This tragedy will never end... It's show time.  
Death Gun：I don't believe in killing people. I do not use my true strength on people.  
Death Gun：Heh heh. Feel frightened? A-Afraid of m-me?  
Deusolbert：Inconceivable. That even I could not grasp your skill with the sword...  
Deusolbert：Even an army of ten thousand is mere ash when faced with my Conflagration Bow.  
Deusolbert：I, too, am eager to receive a new post from the High Pontifex.  
Deusolbert：You are indeed alien... But that is not all. There's more than meets the eye.  
Deusolbert：If you have no business with me, then I shall return to my post.  
Deusolbert：Long time no see. I look forward to the day when we stand together on the battlefield.  
Deusolbert：Hmm...I received word that someone was breaking the Taboo Index in this general vicinity.  
Diavel：Wanna go on a quest?  
Diavel：I was just thinking I'd like to talk to you.  
Diavel：Sometimes I wish I could live like you.  
Diavel：Call on me anytime! I promise I'll help.  
Diavel：Sometimes I don't mind a little peace and quiet.  
Diavel：Do you remember me? It'd be nice if you thought of me, once in a while.  
Dee Eye Ell：Eventually, I will be the queen of this world. I will do whatever it takes to make that come true...  
Dee Eye Ell：His majesty the Emperor, the armies of the Human Empire... I will sweep them all away with my technique. You just wait and see.  
Dee Eye Ell：These giant pigs, and these goblins... All these sub-beings! I want them out of my sight!  
Dee Eye Ell：Don't get careless with that Dark Territory general. That man is possessed by a strange idea...  
Dee Eye Ell：When there's an order, make sure to call out my name. I will be there at a moments notice.  
Eydis：Would you like to go out today? You have no other duties, do you?  
Eydis：Just the person I wanted to see! You know, you cheer me up!  
Eydis：Is that stupidly dressed Prime Senator not around today? So, that explains why the atmosphere is so good!  
Eydis：Mine is a sword of darkness. No one shall be permitted to touch it! Haha. Just kidding.  
Eydis：Fancy, infiltrating the great Axiom Church! You must have a death wish.  
Eydis：Osmanthus is perfect for you. Those flowers are so sweet and fragrant.  
Eydis：Oh, there you are. I wanted to see you for some reason...  
Eydis：If you ever need a shoulder to cry on, mine is always available.  
Eydis：Why the long face? Come on, think positively!  
Eydis：Oh dear, I'm sorry. But don't worry, I wasn't spying on you.  
Eydis：So good to see you again! I missed you.  
Eydis：I came to wish you a Happy New Year.  
Eydis：Why are you wearing a mask? Ohhh, I see. It's a ceremony. You gave me a little fright there!  
Eydis：I hear today is the day where one gives candy to their beloved! So, here you go!  
Eydis：What delicate, pretty dolls. Oh, now I see it. This is set up like a wedding.  
Eydis：What?! A gift...for a gift? Thank you. That was so sweet of you.  
Eydis：A day of lying, eh? If I could tell carefree lies, that might not be a bad thing indeed.  
Eydis：I wish the Axiom Church had long vacations. But we can't rest while the Dark Territory exists.  
Eydis：Don't force yourself to go outside; it's raining. Would you like to stay in with me?  
Eydis：What are you doing with those papers? You're offering wishes to the heavens? Then we should display them on the tower's roof!  
Eydis：I thought I'd go to the Summer Festival, so I came to take you with me. Don't worry, I'll just hide my identity as an Integrity Knight.  
Eydis：Spying is always most difficult when the moon is out. It's easy for the enemy to spot me.  
Eydis：Alert! Alert! Strange-looking creatures spotted outside the gate!  
Eydis：The mountains, dyed in red leaves, look so beautiful from a dragon's back. Would you care to join me for a ride?  
Eydis：It's started to snow. I only hope Dark agents don't use it as cover to invade.  
Egome：I wouldn't mind making a special case and teaching you Zakkarite style.  
Egome：Some day, I will unmask the secret behind your so-called "combo-attacks."  
Egome：Don't you feel much safer having me as an ally?  
Egome：I will do what it takes to win. That's what it means to be strong.  
Egome：You're looking awfully bored, especially considering you're in my company.  
Egome：Whoa, there. Do you have business with me?  
Eiji：This world... Isn't any different from reality. Am I really in a VR?  
Eiji：I... I will absolutely protect you. I'm different than when I was weak.  
Eiji：Your voice will absolutely charm the people of this world.  
Eiji：The sensations in the AR world truly are something. I need to hurry up and get used to these movements.  
Eiji：...Such an endless interest in this world. It looks like I don't catch your attention anymore.  
Eldrie：A fine wine from the West just arrived. Would you like to try some with me?  
Eldrie：Why are you so preoccupied with that child? I honestly don't understand.  
Eldrie：May we have a practice duel?  
Eldrie：I'm going to go check on my dragon. Caring for one's dragon is a very important part of an Integrity Knight's duties.  
Eldrie：If you have no business with me, then please let me return to my sword practice.  
Eldrie：Pardon if I am intruding. I just wanted to see you.  
Eldrie：I haven't seen you for a while. I am glad to see you are hard at work.  
Entokia：Please, just call me Enki! It suits me much better.  
Entokia：You sure are lucky to be free, Master. I wish we were all free to travel abroad like you.  
Entokia：Where did you study that Aincrad style of yours? I've never even heard of it before.  
Entokia：My Roaring Thunder Spear is heavy, you know. I'm not sure that even you could wield it easily, Master.  
Entokia：Master, you've got something amusing on your mind, haven't you? Won't you share it with me?  
Eugene：How's it going? Wanna have a practice-duel?  
Eugene：Know where there're any strong people to fight? I've been coming across nothing but weaklings lately.  
Eugene：I never thought the day would come when you and I would join forces.  
Eugene：Look at your sword... I'd like to see how my Demonic Sword Gram fares against it.  
Eugene：Standing still doesn't suit me. I'm gonna go on a quest or something.  
Eugene：Ooh! Let's have a practice-duel. I'm not letting you back out of this one!  
Eugeo：I don't know when it happened, but I can't imagine life without you now.  
Eugeo：Why do you always have to be so reckless? Put yourself in my shoes - I have a terrible time stopping you.  
Eugeo：I'll teach you the Sacred Arts. And I won't let you weasel out of it.  
Eugeo：I never really noticed apprentices and mentors much back in Rulid... But I think I like it.  
Eugeo：I never even considered that I could chop down the Gigas Cedar. I was putting limitations on myself without even realizing it.  
Eugeo：Stay cool, huh? I like that Aincrad style saying. Are there any other sayings?  
Eugeo：Your eyes are really pretty.  
Eugeo：Want to go some place quieter? I want us to be alone...  
Eugeo：You don't have ulterior motives, do you?  
Eugeo：Not seeing you for even a little while feels like ages. Because we've been together all this time.  
Eugeo：Haven't seen you in a while. Where've you been?  
Eugeo：Looks like we made it to another New Year. Hope it's a good one!  
Eugeo：I see there's a fish head displayed outside. Is it some kind of curse?  
Eugeo：Is it just me, or have the girls been giving us lots of attention today?  
Eugeo：I remember playing with dolls when I was little, but they weren't this elaborate.  
Eugeo：Where are you going with such a big bag? Huh? Are all of those gifts for the girls?  
Eugeo：Lying is wrong. Nothing good can come from deceiving others.  
Eugeo：I'd like to spend my long holidays training. I have to get much stronger and better with the sword.  
Eugeo：Rainy days made my work as a "carver" really miserable... So rain always makes me feel gloomy.  
Eugeo：You write your wish on a slip of paper and tie it to a tree? Then I know what I want to wish for. I only have one wish.  
Eugeo：Summer Festival, eh? We had those in Rulid, too. Though they were really small in scale.  
Eugeo：Oh, look! When's the last time you saw the moon so big?  
Eugeo：Trick or treat...? Is that another Aincrad saying?  
Eugeo：Back in Rulid, I never considered sitting and looking at the trees in autumn.  
Eugeo：Oh. So it's the birthday of a very important man... But what's with the red costume?  
Eugeo：Do you think the Silver Dragon Knight actually exists?  
Eugeo：Okay, I'm not losing this time! This time for sure, you're treating me to siral water!  
Eugeo：I'm not exactly a star pupil. You're just a major slacker!  
Eugeo：I wonder if the day will come... when I also leave this village.  
Eugeo：Let's wrap up our break and get back to work!  
Eugeo：Oh! I'm sorry. I was just wondering how you're doing.  
Eugeo：I know you're probably busy, but come see me again if you want.  
Integrity Knight Eugeo：欠番  
Integrity Knight Eugeo：欠番  
Integrity Knight Eugeo：欠番  
Integrity Knight Eugeo：欠番  
Fanatio：Whenever you go out, please inform me first.  
Fanatio：If you have the time, may we have a practice-duel?  
Fanatio：I'm about to go observe the Four Whirling Blades at practice.  
Fanatio：Please don't think of me as a woman. I abhor pity!  
Fanatio：You seem quite tired. Why don't you take a rest?  
Fanatio：Excuse me! Fanatio Synthesis Two has arrived!  
Fanatio：Oh, there you are. Come, we have lots of work to do!  
Fizel：I preferred being stabbed in the heart. It feels like you just get colder and colder while you die.  
Fizel：I'm sick of all this studying of law and Sacred Arts! I just wanna fight Dark forces.  
Fizel：It's insulting when humans weaker than us pretend to be Integrity Knights!  
Fizel：The Lady Administrator gave us this poisoned sword! Isn't it lovely?  
Fizel：Goodness, what's wrong? You're so quiet.  
Fizel：It's Fizel! Don't tell me you've forgotten?  
Fizel：I am intrigued by the source of your power. So you should come visit us from time to time.  
Frenica：Since it's a day of rest, want to go out somewhere?  
Frenica：I hope I can become just as strong as you someday.  
Frenica：Sacred Arts... I think I'm more cut out for that than for Sword Arts.  
Frenica：I need to learn how to say no when I don't like something.  
Frenica：You seem tired. Want to take the day off?  
Frenica：Oh...s-sorry! I just got lost. Excuse me!  
Geero：I, Geero Synthesis Twenty-Four of the Four Whirling Blades, will show you the extent of my power!  
Geero：I’m only doing this to avoid any regrets.  
Geero：I wonder if we’ll ever be bestowed divine weapons...  
Geero：Follow the path you believe is right. We’ll always be here to assist you.  
Geero：We, the Four Whirling Blades, are always with you.  
Geero：How may I be of assistance?  
Golgorosso：Have you kept up with your training? Knowing you, I'm pretty sure the answer is yes.  
Golgorosso：You've been training pretty hard. That's reassuring!  
Golgorosso：I'm sure you have it in you to master the secrets of Baltio style.  
Golgorosso：Okay, take off your clothes. Let me see those muscles.  
Golgorosso：Meditation? That is also an important practice for developing a strong body and mind.  
Golgorosso：Pardon the interruption. Won't you come train with me from time to time?  
Heathcliff：I want to try and compete against you. Please show me your "Two-sword Syle" up close.  
Heathcliff：We're always looking to recruit those with a fighting spirit. Especially players as strong as you.  
Heathcliff：Even if detailed instructions are not provided, our guild members won't have any problems.  
Heathcliff：However, your reaction speed is abnormal This is faster than my calculations...  
Heathcliff：You're not an easy one to catch. Why are you so against joining the guild?  
Hoveren：The Four Whirling Blades are prepared to fight till death.  
Hoveren：Fighting alongside you is but the greatest honor.  
Hoveren：This is as far as I can go. But I will become stronger!  
Hoveren：Let it be day or night, I will forever be at Lord Fanatio’s disposal!  
Hoveren：Is something the matter...? Come to us if you ever need our help.  
Hoveren：Do you know some effective training to make Whirling Sword Dance stronger?  
Humbert：I would never lose to the likes of those two country scum!  
Humbert：This kingdom is held up by noblemen like us!  
Humbert：The words of the low-born are of no concern to me.  
Humbert：No one will lay a finger on me. Falling back respectfully is our only course of action.  
Humbert：You're unusually quiet today. Have you forgotten how to speak?  
Humbert：My name is Humbert. Don't you ever forget it!  
Iskahn：Though you look like the elegant type, you're just like me. There's only one thing that I can think of.  
Iskahn：Do you think you've bested me? No, right? Because the fight has only just begun!  
Iskahn：Oi, if you're free join me for practice.  
Iskahn：Whenever I see you... My mood just gets messed up!  
Iskahn：Hey, you! Don't you dare ignore me!  
Jayce：As a knight, I vow to carry out my mission!  
Jayce：I’m ready to go at any time!  
Jayce：There should be no limit to our swords’ power. I must throw away my pride and apply myself!  
Jayce：We’re evenly matched. I’m always glad to have an partner with equal skill.  
Jayce：Are you tired? Should I bring you some siral water?  
Jayce：You can come to me if you ever need anything.  
Kagemune：I feel safer knowing you're on my side! Thanks for joining me, buddy.  
Kagemune：Will you go on a quest with me soon? My magic skill is about to increase.  
Kagemune：It sure feels good to fly. I wish we could fly for real.  
Kagemune：If you surround yourself with the right people, you can beat enemies that are even stronger than you! That's why you should treat your allies well.  
Kagemune：Ahhh. We’ve got nothing to do.  
Kagemune：Oops, did I interrupt something? Sorry, my bad. I was just a bit lost.  
Kibaou：What? You have business with me?  
Kibaou：Hmph. I'll do my own thing.  
Kibaou：Don't get the wrong idea. I'm not here to help you.  
Kibaou：I'll beat ya someday. Wipe your ass, 'cause it's gonna get kicked!  
Kibaou：Why dontcha talk about something, instead of being quiet?  
Kibaou：Why're you ignoring me?!  
Kirito：Hey, there! I was hoping you'd show up.  
Kirito：Let's make a bet. Whoever gets in the first hit gets a free drink from the loser. How does that sound?  
Kirito：Ooh, perfect timing! Wanna go work up a sweat in the practice room?  
Kirito：I'm no hero. I just...want to protect people.  
Kirito：Whoa, there! I'm not hiding anything, I swear! Well, then, I guess I'll clean the room or something...  
Kirito：Hi! Whatcha doin’?  
Kirito：Are your fingers cold? Let me help keep you warm.  
Kirito：Wanna go out somewhere? It's been a while.  
Kirito：Okay. What should I do now?  
Kirito：Hey, it's been a while. At least remember me from time to time, will ya?  
Kirito：Hey! I wanted to see you, so I swung by.  
Kirito：Let's go to Hatsumode together! Oh, Hatsumode is where you go to a shrine on New Year's Day to pray.  
Kirito：Time to celebrate Setsubun by throwing some beans! Demons out! Good fortune in!  
Kirito：All the girls seem nervous today. I wonder why?  
Kirito：These are bride and groom dolls for Hinamatsuri. We use these dolls to wish girls an auspicious journey to adulthood.  
Kirito：Okay. Who should I go see next?  
Kirito：It's not a lie, it's a joke. Just joking to lighten up the mood. That's not a sin, last I checked.  
Kirito：Hey, there! We haven't had a day off in ages. Let's go out somewhere special!  
Kirito：Ooh, perfect timing! I forgot my umbrella. Can I walk home with you?  
Kirito：Write your wish on this piece of paper for Tanabata. I just know your wish will come true.  
Kirito：Dude, it's so hot out! Makes me wanna go to the beach.  
Kirito：Hellooo! It's a full moon tonight. Let's watch it together!  
Kirito：Hey, let's dress up! This isn't a minion costume, FYI!  
Kirito：The leaves are starting to turn red for autumn, so we should go for a drive to look at them! It's called Momijigari.  
Kirito：Merry Christmas! I got us a Christmas tree. Come on, let's put on some ornaments!  
Kirito：Hi. Where're we going today?  
Kirito：Let's go out and see if we can find something fun!  
Kirito：Hehe. We'll be best friends forever!  
Kirito：Grrr. I'm just not very good at Sacred Arts.  
Kirito：Hey, what's wrong? Got a tummy ache?  
Kirito：How've you been? I haven't seen you in a long time.  
Kirito：Hey, now! Call on me once in a while, will ya?  
Klein：Man, the Alicization world is just weird and mysterious all around.  
Klein：If something is troubling you, tell me right away.  
Klein：There's no level or stats displays? Man, how can you tell how powerful anyone is?  
Klein：You always push yourself too hard, buddy. Jeez, high-maintenance, much?  
Klein：Whoa, dude, why're you so quiet? Didn't you want something from me?  
Klein：Sup! Haven't seen you in a while. Glad you're lookin' good.  
Klein：Hey! At least say hi once in a while?  
Kuradeel：My mission is to protect you. And I will do so, at any cost.  
Kuradeel：Goodness. That person is just a little too selfish.  
Kuradeel：Hehe... You should always keep your guard up. There's no telling when danger might strike.  
Kuradeel：I am Kuradeel! A proud member of Knights of the Blood!  
Kuradeel：Hm...no movement. I will continue to observe.  
Kuradeel：Oh, there you are. I can't turn my back for a minute...  
Kureha：It seems that there's an unusual amount of girls around you... How bizzare.  
Kureha：I must get stronger in the game.  
Kureha：I want you to invite me if there are any quests that you are interested in! I want to get even stronger!  
Kureha：Relax...? Speaking of which, I recently can't take it easy.  
Kureha：Busy as always... But, you're popular so it can't be helped?  
Leafa：I'd rather not see you in bed all day. I want to spend time with you like this.  
Leafa：This virtual world is less like a game and more like a realistic fantasy, right? That's the sense I get.  
Leafa：If only you'd practiced kendo in the real world as faithfully as you trained in VR. Then you'd be much stronger than me!  
Leafa：Promise me we'll eat Mom's cooking together again at the table... Promise me!  
Leafa：I wasn't roped into this. I came here of my own free will.  
Leafa：I'd like to try fighting with my own "Leafa style."  
Leafa：Hey...just where are you looking???  
Leafa：Remember? I told you I'd follow you anywhere. So we'll stay together. No matter where you go.  
Leafa：Wait, did you drift off? Hehe, okay, let me tuck you in.  
Leafa：Good work today. Hey, wanna relive the good old days and have a little duel?  
Leafa：I got worried, since I haven't seen you in a while. But I'm glad to see you're doing well!  
Leafa：Happy New Year! I brought some New Year's Osechi dishes.  
Leafa：Setsubun is all about exorcising evil spirits, so we have to make sure we toss those beans.  
Leafa：Here... It’s Valentine's chocolate from me. Be sure to eat it, okay?  
Leafa：Hey, can you help me arrange these dolls in the mini-shrine for Hinamatsuri?  
Leafa：Huh? You have a White Day present for me? Aw, you didn't have to! But, thanks!  
Leafa：Lie away, dude! You'll never fool me.  
Leafa：Hey, if you don't have any plans, wanna go on a family vacation like we used to?  
Leafa：Agh, it's pouring out there! Lemme share your umbrella! Hehe...oops. We got wet.  
Leafa：I wrote down my wish and tied it on the bamboo branch in the shopping district for Tanabata. But my wish is a secret!  
Leafa：Well, la-dee-da! I learned how to swim, so bring on the beach days!  
Leafa：Hey, wanna look at the moon tonight? I found a good spot.  
Leafa：Trick or treat! Hehe. Gimme candy!  
Leafa：Here, I got you a red leaf! It's so little and cute, isn't it?  
Leafa：Merry Christmas! Don't you just love hearing all the Christmas music on the radio?  
Linel：Being stabbed in the stomach hurt dreadfully. The cleanest way to die is by beheading.  
Linel：I don't mind staying in my room and studying, however...I want a flying dragon.  
Linel：Everyone patronizes me because I'm a trainee. So we need to prove our merit.  
Linel：If poison is used to kill, it often becomes impossible to resurrect the victim. So, my poison merely paralyzes.  
Linel：What's wrong? Having doubts?  
Linel：I'm Linel... You remembered me?  
Linel：I want to know the source of your power... Won't you come visit us again?  
Lipia：My Lord, please be careful about those Dark Sorcerer. They're a cowardly bunch...  
Lipia：If it's intelligence that you're looking for, please leave that to me. I will show you that I can get the info you seek.  
Lipia：My Lord, if you happen to have time... Could I talk with you, even if just for a little bit?  
Lipia：If it helps make my Lord's dream come true, then my life isn't worthless after all.  
Lipia：...If there is anything, please let me know. I will always be at your service.  
Lisbeth：Is your sword really made of wood? It's so hard and heavy... I've never seen wood like this before.  
Lisbeth：So, this world also has blacksmiths. I'd love to talk shop with them!  
Lisbeth：Let me guess. You want me to do some weapon maintenance. Oh, fine. If I must, I must.  
Lisbeth：Hey, you free? Wanna come to my shop?  
Lisbeth：Can I sit next to you?  
Lisbeth：You know, just being near you really relaxes me.  
Lisbeth：Whoa, man! I was thinking about you, and there you were! Way to scare a girl.  
Lisbeth：I am your exclusive blacksmith. So, I have a duty to check on your weapon. Not just your equipment either, everything.  
Lisbeth：Why are you staring off into space? If you're bored, wanna go somewhere with me?  
Lisbeth：Hi there, trooper! Thought I'd stop by and say hi, since it's been a while.  
Lisbeth：Hey, are you free right now? A good quest just came in. Wanna do it with me?  
Lisbeth：Happy New Year! Let's make it a good one.  
Lisbeth：Where' my demon homies at?! Haha, that's not how the Setsubun saying goes, huh.  
Lisbeth：Here. Happy Valentine's Day. Purely out of obligation, FYI.  
Lisbeth：Since it's Hinamatsuri, I brought special rice crackers for the shrine.  
Lisbeth：So, do you have something to give me?  
Lisbeth：Hey, some hot chick is asking for you! Heh. April Fools!  
Lisbeth：It's crowded everywhere. That's Golden Week for ya.  
Lisbeth：Aw, dang. I forgot my umbrella! Maybe we should find a place to wait out the storm.  
Lisbeth：I hope it doesn't rain. Today's Tanabata!  
Lisbeth：Man, is it hot! Let's go somewhere to cool off.  
Lisbeth：Okay, I got the silver grass. Where should we put it in the display?  
Lisbeth：Why don't we all dress up, then? I have enough materials to make costumes.  
Lisbeth：Oh, look! You've got a leaf on your shoulder. Wow, look at how red it is.  
Lisbeth：Merry Christmas! Since it started snowing, we’re in store for a White Christmas.  
Mary：Welcome back. ...Good, looks like you aren't hurt anywhere.  
Mary：I wonder what my Calling will be? Especially since my lame foot limits my ability to move.  
Mary：It's okay, don't worry about me. I'm just content to live here with you.  
Mary：Oh, Sister, you really do love baths, don't you? Well, I love them too, of course.  
Mary：My foot is feeling better today, so I went to the forest to forage for fruit. Let's eat some later!  
Mary：A hunter gave me some meat today! I'm going to cook up a big feast for us. Hey! No snacking!  
Mary：I've been looking into learning Sacred Arts, since I can do so without needing to move much.  
Mary：I'll be fine on my own. But... come back as soon as you can, okay? I get lonely sometimes.  
Mary：What's wrong? You look glum. If you're tired, be sure to rest.  
Medina：Why do you care about me like that? I don't get you. At all.  
Medina：I will wash away the disgraced name of the Orthinanos family with this hand.  
Medina：This is getting in the way of my training. Don't get in the way!  
Medina：No one can defeat my family's techniques. They've been passed down from generation to generation. If I lose, it's because of my lack of training.  
Medina：If that person wasn't here, it would be awfully quiet around these parts... It would be great.  
Nergius：Call me Negi one more time, and you will pay dearly.  
Nergius：If you knew your place, you wouldn't act so thoughtlessly or so recklessly.  
Nergius：You are indeed skilled. I have to admit that your skills qualify you to be our swordsman delegate.  
Nergius：My Budding Storm Spear never falls, and neither shall I.  
Nergius：I look everywhere for you only to find you here, in a daze. What an elusive fellow you are.  
Oberon：Even if I am forced to obey, I won't be discouraged. She may dislike me now, but over time... What's wrong with that?  
Oberon：Kings are especially suitable fiancés for a queen... More so than those nobodys.  
Oberon：She's so beautiful... I won't let anyone lay eyes on her. She's mine, and mine only...  
Oberon：Kayaba is a genius, but a moron as well. He couldn't see reality at all.  
Oberon：To ignore someone such as me. Oh dear! You're also such a fool.  
Philia：For today, how about we try going somewhere new? There's probably some interesting things there.  
Philia：Now that I think about it, we've accompanied each other for a long time... Do you remember the first time we met?  
Philia：Hey, do you have any plans for today? If it's ok with you, would you like to form a party with me?  
Philia：Hey hey! Rumor has it, there's treasure! Do you want to go looking for it together?  
Philia：...You seem pretty busy, huh. I'm open to it.  
Premiere：If you happen to have any time, could you teach me some battle tricks again?  
Premiere：You always came whenever I felt lonely. Just like now. Hehe...  
Premiere：I'm kind of hungry. Would you like to eat some lunch box?  
Premiere：I get to learn so much whenever I am with you. That's why I always want to be with you.  
Premiere：I mean... No, perhaps are you feeling ill?  
Rain：What's going on? Could it be that you're looking for me?  
Rain：Hehe. Having company isn't such a bad thing!  
Rain：Next time when you have time, can you listen to my song? I want you to hear it.  
Rain：The life of an idol... Of course I'm working hard! It's what I've always wished for!  
Rain：...Are you thinking about something difficult? If you want to lighten up your mood why not try singing!  
Raios：You're a fresh-off-the-boat bumpkin, so your ignorance is inevitable. You need our guidance.  
Raios：Oh, bother. I don't want to waste my time on a bastard with questionable lineage.  
Raios：You need to practice your form a little. Though I doubt I'll fall behind you.  
Raios：I have the right to pass judgement on impudence.  
Raios：Well, I think I'll have a drink. I just got in some nice wine.  
Raios：My name is Raios Antinous. Don't insult me by saying you forgot it?  
Renly：It's you. Looks like it's my lucky day meeting you here.  
Renly：A failure like me needs to get better, even if it's just by a little. So that I don't leave everyone behind.  
Renly：I-If... it's ok with you. How about we get some tea next time? That is, if you're fine with it.  
Renly：Just as I thought. Going up against enemies is scary. But, I won't give in to that fear...   
Renly：When it's quiet like this I can feel a big hole within the darkest reaches of my heart. It's large and very deep.  
Ronie：I have finished today's cleaning, without delay!  
Ronie：Where did you learn Aincrad Style?  
Ronie：Those honey pies from the Prancing Deer were so delicious!  
Ronie：I am so honored to be your page!  
Ronie：So, you're teaching me Norkian Style today! I look forward to learning from you.  
Ronie：I've never seen a black sword before. What's it made of?  
Ronie：Excuse me! Will you please train me again today?  
Ronie：Lately I've been nervous whenever I enter your room. N-no, I'm not scared, exactly. I'm, uh...  
Ronie：Don't worry! Waiting like this is also a page's duty!  
Ronie：Ronie Arabel, reporting for duty!  
Ronie：I haven't seen you in a while. I would love some more Aincrad Style training, please!  
Ronie：Your Ronie Arabel formally wishes you a Happy New Year!  
Ronie：You requested beans, so I brought many!  
Ronie：I heard today is a day where we give candy to someone special. So...here you go!  
Ronie：Bonbori? This paper lamp is called a "Bonbori"?  
Ronie：Oh, I couldn't! I don't deserve a return gift...  
Ronie：Mentor! Fighting whilst facing backwards... Surely that isn't an Aincrad Style move!  
Ronie：Do you have any plans for the coming days of rest? If you don't mind, you and I could, uh...  
Ronie：It's suddenly started to rain. My shoes are soaked through!  
Ronie：What did you wish for, Mentor?  
Ronie：What hot weather we're having. Would you like to cool off at the river?  
Ronie：The moon sure is beautiful tonight. Isn't it romantic... Huh? Why are you blushing?  
Ronie：I got a lot of candy, so I'd like to share it with you, Mentor!  
Ronie：Everything is so delicious during harvest season! Let's go out and eat something.  
Ronie：Mentor, look! It's snowing! Want to go play in the snow?  
Rosalia：Have you found any delicious prey?  
Rosalia：There's no room for weaklings in my party. Please, take this more seriously.  
Rosalia：Don't make me get blood on my hands. I have to stay a green player.  
Rosalia：Justice? Law? What a load of crap. I hope you're not into that stuff.  
Rosalia：Why are you spacing off? Hurry up and earn your keep.  
Rosalia：Oh, hi. Maybe don't ghost me next time?  
Sachi：Wow, I get to be with you again... It feels like a dream.  
Sachi：Will you teach me how to use a sword? I want to pull my own weight, in any way I can.  
Sachi：When monsters show up, I do get scared, but I'll be fine as long as you're with me.  
Sachi：Someday, I'd love to meet up with you in the real world... Haha. Silly, I know.  
Sachi：Hehe...sorry. Didn't mean to stare.  
Sachi：H-hello! So good to see you after all this time.  
Sachi：Hehe, no matter where I am, I'm always able to find you.  
Sakuya：Are you finally up for having a drink with me?  
Sakuya：I haven't given up on you yet. When you change your mind, give me a holler.  
Sakuya：Maybe I should take a cue from you and wander around freely for once.  
Sakuya：I'm happy to fight by your side again. Naturally, it's partly because you're so powerful... But I also just appreciate your company.  
Sakuya：Something on your mind? Let me know if there's any way I can help.  
Sakuya：Hey, kid. Long time no see. Good to see you.  
Sheyta：...Do you need something from me?  
Sheyta：I don't want to look. Their mangled bodies will haunt my thoughts...  
Sheyta：I don't mind myself, but how about talking to someone else?  
Sheyta：Victory...? You seem soft, just now...  
Sheyta：...Keeping your distance from me will be the biggest help.  
Selka：Awww, I wonder when my trainee period will be over?  
Selka：Tell me, is sword-fighting fun? I've never really tried it...  
Selka：Sometimes, I'm not sure whether you're a genius or a fool.  
Selka：I'm not as good as my sister... But I'd at least like to get closer to her level.  
Selka：So, what's next?  
Selka：I brought your lunch! I'm really proud of this one.  
Selka：I haven't seen you in a while. So, why don't you come live at the Church? Then I could see you all the time.  
Seven：Privyet! What perfect timing! Would you mind helping me out a little with my research?  
Seven：Yes, I'm also continuing research. The VR world is so interesting, don't you think?  
Seven：Quest? I'll have to pass. I decided that I'll be taking it easy today.  
Seven：It really is fun to be able to do this and relax with someone you can trust.  
Seven：In time, meditation is also necessary... but when I'm by your side don't you think you're fine without?  
Shasta：Apologies. I need to swing my sword at least once a day or I won't feel right. It's my nature to do so.  
Shasta：I can only say this to you, but if one day we can reach a compromise with the Human Empire... Life would be easier in this country.  
Shasta：The commander of the Integrity Knights... His sword transcends human intellect. Following it requires extraordinary effort.   
Shasta：Dark Sorcerers have Goblins, Ogres... Their existence is more troublesome than the humans.  
Shasta：Something on your mind? If I can be of any assistance, don't be afraid to ask for help.  
Silica：Swordcraft Academy... I wonder if attending will make me stronger than now...  
Silica：When Pina gets big, do you think she'll let me ride her?  
Silica：I still can't believe this is a virtual world.  
Silica：If there's a Taboo Index, I wonder if there's no Orange Guild here... I can't help but wonder it, you know?  
Silica：Can I, um...come a bit closer?  
Silica：In every world, there are people who bend the rules.  
Silica：So...how do I look? You see, I cut my bangs today. Do I look...weird?  
Silica：So, yeah, like, anytime you want to go shopping... I'm totally cool with that.  
Silica：N-never mind! Forget I said anything.  
Silica：Hello! Is...this a bad time?  
Silica：I haven't seen you in a while. How have you been?  
Silica：Happy New Year! Just came to tell you that.  
Silica：It's Setsubun! Would you like to throw beans with me?  
Silica：Um, here, I got you some chocolates!  
Silica：I had a lot of fun with Hinamatsuri when I was a little girl, since it's a festival for little girls.  
Silica：Huh? You have a gift for me, too? Oh...thank you so much!  
Silica：Hehe, I'm seeing April Fool's events all over the place.  
Silica：So, um...do you have plans for Golden Week?  
Silica：Oh! Uh, if you don't have an umbrella, want to share mine?  
Silica：Wait, Pina! Don't fly away with my wish!  
Silica：So, uh...you're really sweaty. Crouch down and I'll towel you off.  
Silica：I got lots of rice dumplings! I really love them.  
Silica：So, uh, trick or treat? Please, don't stare. I'm not very comfortable in this costume.  
Silica：Let's get the gang together for a picnic under the autumn leaves!  
Silica：Merry Christmas! I got you a present.  
Sinon：Jeez, it's one annoying task after another...  
Sinon：It has a mysterious smell to it. It's not like the real world, or the world of gas and gunpowder.  
Sinon：I guess my "Calling" in this world would probably be a "hunter." Too bad "sniper" isn't an option.  
Sinon：I came here to find you to make sure I don't lose you. "BoB" would be tough without you.  
Sinon：Just a theory, but can you repel Sacred Arts attacks with your sword?  
Sinon：I wonder if they have a shooting tournament as well as a sword tournament.  
Sinon：Oh, there you are. G-good to see you...  
Sinon：Hey, can you take me out again like last time? With us on the same bike...  
Sinon：Oh, are you tired? Wanna take a break together?  
Sinon：Long time no see. Good to see you're doing well.  
Sinon：A lot of the GGO folks miss you. You should pop in there too every once in a while.  
Sinon：Happy New Year. Let's stay in today. It's always so crowded on New Year's.  
Sinon：I'm cool with celebrating Setsubun, but I hope you don't expect me to wear that hideous mask.  
Sinon：Since I bumped into you, I might as well give you some Valentine's chocolate.  
Sinon：My favorite part of Hinamatsuri is probably the clam soup.  
Sinon：Hmmm, White Day, eh? Well, if you insist, I'll take that present off your hands.  
Sinon：I still haven't forgiven you for the first time you fooled me. Haha, just kidding!  
Sinon：It's a long vacation, so there's no rush to go out.  
Sinon：I thought you wouldn't have an umbrella. So I brought you one.  
Sinon：I did bring my wish with me to tie to the bamboo branch... But don't you dare look at it!  
Sinon：I don't like the crowds at festivals, but watching the fireworks from a distance is nice.  
Sinon：It's really nice to sit in a quiet place and stare at the moon for hours.  
Sinon：Haha, look at all the monsters outside It's kind of fun watching everyone trick-or-treat.  
Sinon：The leaves are already changing color. Want to go see the trees with me?  
Sinon：Do you have any Christmas plans? If you don't, then, uh... would you like to eat Christmas cake with me?  
Siune：I'm happy that you and I are still adventuring together like this.  
Siune：We don't have to spend all our time together. As long as we're together when it counts most.  
Siune：Hey, wanna go out somewhere today?  
Siune：Talking to you like this is making me excited.  
Siune：Have you organized your thoughts? Be sure to take your time.  
Siune：Hehe. How have you been?  
Sortiliena：I had my doubts in the past... But now I can say with confidence that Serlut is the style for me!  
Sortiliena：I always considered myself rather unconventional, but I think you've brought new meaning to the word.  
Sortiliena：A "walking tactics manual," eh? Thanks. I'll take that as a compliment.  
Sortiliena：I've learned a lot by teaching you. I do owe you my thanks.  
Sortiliena：In the name of House Serlut, I will rise to fight any dark forces that attack.  
Sortiliena：I've always wanted to fight an actual duel with you. Though, I would probably lose.  
Sortiliena：Hehe. It's good to see you.  
Sortiliena：I would love to fight with you again. But not against you, alongside you...as a friend.  
Sortiliena：Something on your mind? Heh. That's a good look for you.  
Sortiliena：Long time no see! Has your training in Aincrad Style advanced since the last time we spoke?  
Sortiliena：Meeting you here must have been fate. Want to have a duel? For old time's sake!  
Sortiliena：Happy New Year. I don't feel right until I start off the new year by thanking everyone who's helped me.  
Sortiliena：Why are you throwing beans? Is it...some kind of magic ceremony?  
Sortiliena：Oh, it's a day where you give chocolates to people you care about? Then I'd better get some chocolates. For you, of course.  
Sortiliena：Even from a distance, those Hina dolls really are pretty.  
Sortiliena：Is this for me? Oh, I see. It's a thank you gift for the Valentine I gave you. Well, thank you.  
Sortiliena：A harmless lie, you say? But, betraying trust is never harmless. That is too heavy a burden for me.  
Sortiliena：A vacation for the next few days? Then...I'll spend it on my training.  
Sortiliena：I know rain is vital for our farmers, but I do hate when it drags on like this.  
Sortiliena：Why are there slips of paper tied on that bamboo branch? Oh...people write their wishes on the papers?  
Sortiliena：I too am looking forward to the Summer Festival. So many unusual sights, and they're lively, at that.  
Sortiliena：My heart has always felt drawn to the moon, rather than the sun. I just love her soft, quiet glow.  
Sortiliena：When you chant the words dressed like that, you remind me of a wizard from a fairytale.  
Sortiliena：So you travel far just to admire the red leaves? Your people think up some rather stylish customs.  
Sortiliena：Christmas...is that similar to the Solstice celebration? But it seems much more festive.  
Sortiliena：I don't care how I am mocked, I will continue to strengthen myself with the Serlut Style!  
Sortiliena：Next time, we're learning how to use a whip. Sounds difficult, and quite different from sword-fighting.  
Sortiliena：I'm going to ask Father to buy me a weapon arsenal.  
Sortiliena：I can't let all these drills of working just on my form get me down!  
Sortiliena：Being quiet and calming my mind... That's tough.  
Sortiliena：I'm s-sorry! I came here without meaning to.  
Sortiliena：I know I'm not much, but call on me anytime.  
Strea：Hey, where should we go today?  
Strea：I wonder if something interesting has dropped.  
Strea：I feel like I'm in the mood for some shopping today. Would you like to join?  
Strea：Hehe. Where are you looking? Would you happen to be interested in me?  
Strea：Hey, this is boring! Let's do something and play!  
Suguha：I'd rather not see you in bed all day. I want to spend time with you like this.  
Suguha：If only you'd practiced kendo in the real world as faithfully as you trained in VR. Then you'd be much stronger than me!  
Suguha：Promise me we'll eat Mom's cooking together again at the table... Promise me!  
Suguha：I wasn't roped into this. I came here of my own free will.  
Suguha：I'd like to try fighting with my own "Leafa style."  
Suguha：Hey...just where are you looking???  
Suguha：Remember? I told you I'd follow you anywhere. So we'll stay together. No matter where you go.  
Suguha：Wait, did you drift off? Hehe, okay, let me tuck you in.  
Tiese：Reporting in! Today's cleaning is finished!  
Tiese：Being of the nobility means having responsibility. The responsibility to protect the common man.  
Tiese：I am truly honored that you would choose me as your page.  
Tiese：Truth be told, there are many girls who wanted to be your page.  
Tiese：Mentor, are there any foods in particular that you like or dislike? Just curious.  
Tiese：Your blue sword is beautiful. Its icy shine is quite captivating.  
Tiese：Mentor! Will you please train me again today?  
Tiese：While you train to become a proper knight, I will train to be a girl worthy of you. Strong...and pretty...  
Tiese：Aren't you tired? You should rest.  
Tiese：Tiese Schtrinen, reporting in!  
Tiese：I'm so glad to see you like this! Whenever you have the time, please teach me some more Sword Arts!  
Tiese：Tiese Schtrinen formally wishes you a Happy New Year! Please take care of me this year, too.  
Tiese：I heard people crying, "Demons, begone!" Is that a spell to curse demons to the Dark Territory?  
Tiese：I heard this is a day where you give candy to the ones you love... So I made this for you, sir.  
Tiese：I heard a cute little melody, so I came to see what it was. Are these your dolls, sir?  
Tiese：I am so honored that you summoned me today!  
Tiese：I heard this is a day where we are supposed to tell lies... But I could never lie to you.  
Tiese：It seems like there is a long vacation coming up! It’d be great if we could spend a day and go out somewhere together.  
Tiese：It's really pouring out there. Could I stay at your place until the rain stops?  
Tiese：My wish is...a secret!  
Tiese：What a hot day! Here, I brought you some siral water. Be sure to hydrate!  
Tiese：The moon is so pretty tonight, so I came here to watch it with you.  
Tiese：Trick or treat? If I gave you that ultimatum, which one would you give me?  
Tiese：The forest is beautiful dyed in red. Would you like to come admire it with me?  
Tiese：I, um, brought you a present! Do you like it?  
Tiese：What ever could be the duties of a nobleman...  
Tiese：It's fun to swing around a sword, but studying makes me so tired...  
Tiese：If I can enroll into the Swordcraft Academy, I wouldn't have to study the Sacred Arts.  
Tiese：I'm glad we could meet today!  
Tiese：I get antsy standing still... Let's move around!  
Vassago：That Blacky is here... My body is trembling just thinking about it!  
Vassago：It's show tiiime!  
Vassago：Those who die become energy... Isn't that amazing! It's a world like my 'Mate Chopper'!  
Vassago：These humans of Underworld... They have the unthinkable capacity to judge AI. They must be killed.  
Vassago：...Waiting is one of my strong suits. Because what I want to do in the future is wait for my prey, just like this.  
Subtilizer：Hmph. Seems like I've gotten a little used to moving around in this body.  
Subtilizer：I feel nothing when absorbing a soul. As I suspected, prey are but simple containers.  
Subtilizer：Well, where is this so-called Alice anyways? I hope I can taste her soul...  
Subtilizer：Alice... If only I can taste her soul one more time. That would be a real treat...!  
Volo：Someday, I'll have to settle the score with you for good.  
Volo：Your swordsmanship is keen... But I will learn to see through it.  
Volo：I devote every spare moment to my sword training. I guess you're that kind of person too.  
Volo：Let's call off the duel... for my sake. I need to strengthen my weaknesses.  
Volo：What's wrong? Is there mud on my chest?  
Volo：Long time no see. It seems you've gotten stronger, but you still don't hold a candle to me.  
Yui：Don't you dare touch any girl besides Mom!  
Yui：The world of Alicization... Is it really a virtual world?  
Yui：If something is troubling you, please tell me.  
Yui：If only we could find a computer, we could use it to gather information.  
Yui：Did you fall asleep? Please, make sure you cover up.  
Yui：You summoned me? If it's research you want, I'm your girl!  
Yui：Hehe, I missed you, so I flew here to see you!  
Yuna：Good timing. I was just thinking about you. Could it be, you heard my voice?  
Yuna：Theres so many weird things here! Hey, how about we go take a look?  
Yuna：I wonder if I can become a songstress whom people look up to - even in this world? That would be nice.  
Yuna：If you have time next time, do you want to sing with me? I think you'll probably be really good!  
Yuna：What are you thinking about? Your gaze was so distant. It was pretty beautiful.  
Yuuki：Everything looks so real. The trees, the ground, the sky... Hehe, how intriguing!  
Yuuki：You can ride on dragons in this world, can't you? I wanna ride a dragon!  
Yuuki：I wanna try gripping my own sword, too!  
Yuuki：Hehe, what a perfect day! Come on, let's go out somewhere.  
Yuuki：There's a sword tournament here?! I have to enter that!  
Yuuki：Hi! Just the guy I've been wanting to see!  
Yuuki：Hehehe...it smells like you. When I'm wrapped up like this, I feel so safe and calm.  
Yuuki：Don't just lie around, that's a waste of time! Let's go have some fun!  
Yuuki：Hehe, long time no see. How've ya been?  
Yuuki：Wanna go on an adventure with the Sleeping Knights sometime?  
Yuuki：Happy New Yeeear! Hey, let's go have our fortunes told!  
Yuuki：So, hey, are the beans all ready to be tossed?  
Yuuki：Happy Valentine's Day! Here's your chocolate!  
Yuuki：Whoa, what a huge Hina doll! He kinda looks like you.  
Yuuki：Chocolate on Valentine's Day, gifts on White Day... I've always wanted to do stuff like this.  
Yuuki：I've become a healer, too! Haha, was it really that hard to believe?  
Yuuki：This is a long vacation, right? Then let's take on an expedition quest!  
Yuuki：Sorry, but can ya let me share your umbrella? Mine broke this morning.  
Yuuki：Ooh, look at this big bamboo branch I found!  
Yuuki：I hear there's a festival today! Wanna put on yukata and go out somewhere?  
Yuuki：Hey, why do you think we put silver grass out?  
Yuuki：Trick or treat! Whoever gets the most candy wins!  
Yuuki：The red leaves were so pretty, I wound up picking a bunch!  
Yuuki：Wanna gather the gang to go look at Christmas lights?  
Zink：If you ever need my services as a guard, let me know.  
Zink：Well, you're not a bad fighter yourself.  
Zink：I didn't realize how big the world was, outside of Rulid.  
Zink：I don't need you, exactly! I just...don't mind if you stick around.  
Zink：Why're you so silent? I'm going to go take some practice swings.  
Zink：Wh-what the hell are you doing?  
Yuna：Today's MVP is you!
